     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const {
     4:   computeStatisticalTop3V3,
     5: } = require("./scoreEngineUnified");
     6: 
     7: const GROUPS_K = 25;
     8: const LAPLACE_ALPHA = 1;
     9: 
    10: function safeArray(value) {
    11:   return Array.isArray(value)
    12:     ? value
    13:     : [];
    14: }
    15: 
    16: function normalizeHour(value) {
    17:   const raw = String(
    18:     value ?? ""
    19:   ).trim();
    20: 
    21:   if (!raw) {
    22:     return "";
    23:   }
    24: 
    25:   const match = raw.match(
    26:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?/
    27:   );
    28: 
    29:   if (!match) {
    30:     return "";
    31:   }
    32: 
    33:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
    34:   const minute = Number(
    35:     match[2] || 0
    36:   );
    37: 
    38:   if (
    39:     !Number.isFinite(hour) ||
    40:     hour < 0 ||
    41:     hour > 23 ||
    42:     !Number.isFinite(minute) ||
    43:     minute < 0 ||
    44:     minute > 59
    45:   ) {
    46:     return "";
    47:   }
    48: 
    49:   return (
    50:     String(hour).padStart(2, "0") +
    51:     ":" +
    52:     String(minute).padStart(2, "0")
    53:   );
    54: }
    55: 
    56: function pickDrawHour(draw) {
    57:   return normalizeHour(
    58:     draw?.close_hour ??
    59:     draw?.closeHour ??
    60:     draw?.hour ??
    61:     draw?.hora
    62:   );
    63: }
    64: 
    65: function groupFromDezena(value) {
    66:   const digits = String(
    67:     value ?? ""
    68:   )
    69:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
    70:     .padStart(2, "0")
    71:     .slice(-2);
    72: 
    73:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(digits)) {
    74:     return null;
    75:   }
    76: 
    77:   const rawDezena = Number(digits);
    78: 
    79:   if (!Number.isFinite(rawDezena)) {
    80:     return null;
    81:   }
    82: 
    83:   const dezena =
    84:     rawDezena === 0
    85:       ? 100
    86:       : rawDezena;
    87: 
    88:   const group = Math.ceil(
    89:     dezena / 4
    90:   );
    91: 
    92:   return (
    93:     group >= 1 &&
    94:     group <= GROUPS_K
    95:   )
    96:     ? group
    97:     : null;
    98: }
    99: 
   100: function pickPrizePosition(prize) {
   101:   const candidates = [
   102:     prize?.position,
   103:     prize?.posicao,
   104:     prize?.pos,
   105:     prize?.colocacao,
   106:   ];
   107: 
   108:   for (const value of candidates) {
   109:     const position = Number(value);
   110: 
   111:     if (Number.isFinite(position)) {
   112:       return position;
   113:     }
   114:   }
   115: 
   116:   return null;
   117: }
   118: 
   119: function pickPrizeGroup(prize) {
   120:   const directCandidates = [
   121:     prize?.grupo,
   122:     prize?.group,
   123:     prize?.grupo2,
   124:     prize?.group2,
   125:     prize?.animal_grupo,
   126:     prize?.grupo_animal,
   127:     prize?.grupoAnimal,
   128:     prize?.g,
   129:   ];
   130: 
   131:   for (
   132:     const value
   133:     of directCandidates
   134:   ) {
   135:     const group = Number(value);
   136: 
   137:     if (
   138:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
   139:       group >= 1 &&
   140:       group <= GROUPS_K
   141:     ) {
   142:       return group;
   143:     }
   144:   }
   145: 
   146:   const numberCandidates = [
   147:     prize?.milhar,
   148:     prize?.milhar4,
   149:     prize?.numero,
   150:     prize?.number,
   151:     prize?.value,
   152:     prize?.resultado,
   153:     prize?.result,
   154:     prize?.premio,
   155:   ];
   156: 
   157:   for (
   158:     const value
   159:     of numberCandidates
   160:   ) {
   161:     const digits = String(
   162:       value ?? ""
   163:     ).replace(/\D+/g, "");
   164: 
   165:     if (!digits) {
   166:       continue;
   167:     }
   168: 
   169:     const group = groupFromDezena(
   170:       digits.slice(-2)
   171:     );
   172: 
   173:     if (group != null) {
   174:       return group;
   175:     }
   176:   }
   177: 
   178:   return null;
   179: }
   180: 
   181: function pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw) {
   182:   const prizes = safeArray(
   183:     draw?.prizes
   184:   );
   185: 
   186:   const firstPrize =
   187:     prizes.find(
   188:       (prize) =>
   189:         pickPrizePosition(prize) === 1
   190:     ) ||
   191:     prizes[0] ||
   192:     null;
   193: 
   194:   if (firstPrize) {
   195:     const group =
   196:       pickPrizeGroup(firstPrize);
   197: 
   198:     if (group != null) {
   199:       return group;
   200:     }
   201:   }
   202: 
   203:   const directCandidates = [
   204:     draw?.grupo1,
   205:     draw?.group1,
   206:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
   207:     draw?.first_grupo,
   208:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
   209:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
   210:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
   211:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
   212:     draw?.grupo_1,
   213:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
   214:     draw?.g1,
   215:   ];
   216: 
   217:   for (
   218:     const value
   219:     of directCandidates
   220:   ) {
   221:     const group = Number(value);
   222: 
   223:     if (
   224:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
   225:       group >= 1 &&
   226:       group <= GROUPS_K
   227:     ) {
   228:       return group;
   229:     }
   230:   }
   231: 
   232:   return null;
   233: }
   234: 
   235: function extractCandidateGroup(item) {
   236:   const group = Number(
   237:     item?.grupo ??
   238:     item?.group
   239:   );
   240: 
   241:   return (
   242:     Number.isFinite(group) &&
   243:     group >= 1 &&
   244:     group <= GROUPS_K
   245:   )
   246:     ? group
   247:     : null;
   248: }
   249: 
   250: function collectSameHourFrequency(
   251:   drawsRange,
   252:   targetHour
   253: ) {
   254:   const counts = new Map();
   255: 
   256:   for (
   257:     let group = 1;
   258:     group <= GROUPS_K;
   259:     group += 1
   260:   ) {
   261:     counts.set(group, 0);
   262:   }
   263: 
   264:   let samples = 0;
   265: 
   266:   for (
   267:     const draw
   268:     of safeArray(drawsRange)
   269:   ) {
   270:     const hour =
   271:       pickDrawHour(draw);
   272: 
   273:     if (
   274:       !targetHour ||
   275:       hour !== targetHour
   276:     ) {
   277:       continue;
   278:     }
   279: 
   280:     const group =
   281:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw);
   282: 
   283:     if (group == null) {
   284:       continue;
   285:     }
   286: 
   287:     samples += 1;
   288: 
   289:     counts.set(
   290:       group,
   291:       Number(
   292:         counts.get(group) || 0
   293:       ) + 1
   294:     );
   295:   }
   296: 
   297:   return {
   298:     counts,
   299:     samples,
   300:   };
   301: }
   302: 
   303: function computeProbability(
   304:   count,
   305:   samples
   306: ) {
   307:   const denominator =
   308:     Number(samples || 0) +
   309:     (
   310:       LAPLACE_ALPHA *
   311:       GROUPS_K
   312:     );
   313: 
   314:   return denominator > 0
   315:     ? (
   316:         Number(count || 0) +
   317:         LAPLACE_ALPHA
   318:       ) / denominator
   319:     : 1 / GROUPS_K;
   320: }
   321: 
   322: function sameGroupSet(
   323:   first,
   324:   second
   325: ) {
   326:   const a = safeArray(first)
   327:     .map(extractCandidateGroup)
   328:     .filter(Number.isFinite)
   329:     .sort(
   330:       (x, y) => x - y
   331:     );
   332: 
   333:   const b = safeArray(second)
   334:     .map(extractCandidateGroup)
   335:     .filter(Number.isFinite)
   336:     .sort(
   337:       (x, y) => x - y
   338:     );
   339: 
   340:   return (
   341:     a.length === b.length &&
   342:     a.every(
   343:       (value, index) =>
   344:         value === b[index]
   345:     )
   346:   );
   347: }
   348: 
   349: function computeStatisticalTop3E08Experimental(
   350:   input = {}
   351: ) {
   352:   const baseline =
   353:     computeStatisticalTop3V3(
   354:       input
   355:     );
   356: 
   357:   const baselineTop =
   358:     safeArray(baseline?.top)
   359:       .slice(0, 3);
   360: 
   361:   if (baselineTop.length < 3) {
   362:     return {
   363:       ...baseline,
   364:       experimental: {
   365:         enabled: true,
   366:         experiment: "E08",
   367:         applied: false,
   368:         reason:
   369:           "BASELINE_WITH_LESS_THAN_3",
   370:       },
   371:     };
   372:   }
   373: 
   374:   const targetHour =
   375:     normalizeHour(
   376:       input.targetHourOverride
   377:     );
   378: 
   379:   const frequency =
   380:     collectSameHourFrequency(
   381:       input.drawsRange,
   382:       targetHour
   383:     );
   384: 
   385:   const rerankedTop =
   386:     baselineTop
   387:       .map(
   388:         (item, index) => {
   389:           const group =
   390:             extractCandidateGroup(
   391:               item
   392:             );
   393: 
   394:           const count = Number(
   395:             frequency.counts.get(
   396:               group
   397:             ) || 0
   398:           );
   399: 
   400:           const sameHourProbability =
   401:             computeProbability(
   402:               count,
   403:               frequency.samples
   404:             );
   405: 
   406:           return {
   407:             ...item,
   408:             __baselineRank:
   409:               index + 1,
   410:             __sameHourCount:
   411:               count,
   412:             __sameHourProbability:
   413:               sameHourProbability,
   414:           };
   415:         }
   416:       )
   417:       .sort((a, b) => {
   418:         if (
   419:           b.__sameHourProbability !==
   420:           a.__sameHourProbability
   421:         ) {
   422:           return (
   423:             b.__sameHourProbability -
   424:             a.__sameHourProbability
   425:           );
   426:         }
   427: 
   428:         return (
   429:           a.__baselineRank -
   430:           b.__baselineRank
   431:         );
   432:       })
   433:       .map(
   434:         (item, index) => {
   435:           const {
   436:             __baselineRank,
   437:             __sameHourCount,
   438:             __sameHourProbability,
   439:             ...original
   440:           } = item;
   441: 
   442:           return {
   443:             ...original,
   444:             rank: index + 1,
   445:             title:
   446:               index === 0
   447:                 ? "Mais provável"
   448:                 : index === 1
   449:                   ? "2º mais provável"
   450:                   : "3º mais provável",
   451:             meta: {
   452:               ...(original?.meta || {}),
   453:               experiment: "E08",
   454:               baselineRank:
   455:                 __baselineRank,
   456:               sameHourCount:
   457:                 __sameHourCount,
   458:               sameHourProbability:
   459:                 __sameHourProbability,
   460:               sameHourSamples:
   461:                 frequency.samples,
   462:               targetHour,
   463:             },
   464:           };
   465:         }
   466:       );
   467: 
   468:   if (
   469:     !sameGroupSet(
   470:       baselineTop,
   471:       rerankedTop
   472:     )
   473:   ) {
   474:     throw new Error(
   475:       "E08 alterou o conjunto de candidatos da V3."
   476:     );
   477:   }
   478: 
   479:   return {
   480:     ...baseline,
   481:     top: rerankedTop,
   482:     ranking: rerankedTop,
   483:     baselineTop,
   484:     experimental: {
   485:       enabled: true,
   486:       experiment: "E08",
   487:       version: 1,
   488:       applied: true,
   489:       strategy:
   490:         "RERANK_V3_TOP3_BY_SAME_HOUR_FREQUENCY",
   491:       preservesCandidateSet: true,
   492:       targetHour,
   493:       samples:
   494:         frequency.samples,
   495:       baselineGroups:
   496:         baselineTop.map(
   497:           extractCandidateGroup
   498:         ),
   499:       rerankedGroups:
   500:         rerankedTop.map(
   501:           extractCandidateGroup
   502:         ),
   503:     },
   504:     meta: {
   505:       ...(baseline?.meta || {}),
   506:       scenario:
   507:         "E08_V3_TOP3_RERANK",
   508:       experimental: {
   509:         experiment: "E08",
   510:         preservesCandidateSet:
   511:           true,
   512:         targetHour,
   513:         samples:
   514:           frequency.samples,
   515:       },
   516:     },
   517:   };
   518: }
   519: 
   520: module.exports = {
   521:   collectSameHourFrequency,
   522:   computeStatisticalTop3E08Experimental,
   523: };

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const path = require("path");
     4: 
     5: const TOP3_ROOT = path.resolve(
     6:   __dirname,
     7:   "../../src/pages/Top3"
     8: );
     9: 
    10: let publicApi = null;
    11: 
    12: /**
    13:  * Carrega o núcleo ESM do TOP3 dentro do backend CommonJS.
    14:  *
    15:  * A transformação é limitada ao diretório do TOP3. Nenhum arquivo
    16:  * de React, Firebase ou restante do frontend é processado.
    17:  */
    18: function loadTop3PublicApi() {
    19:   if (publicApi) {
    20:     return publicApi;
    21:   }
    22: 
    23:   require("@babel/register")({
    24:     babelrc: false,
    25:     configFile: false,
    26:     cache: true,
    27:     extensions: [".js"],
    28:     only: [
    29:       (filename) => {
    30:         const absolute = path.resolve(filename);
    31: 
    32:         return (
    33:           absolute === TOP3_ROOT ||
    34:           absolute.startsWith(`${TOP3_ROOT}${path.sep}`)
    35:         );
    36:       },
    37:     ],
    38:     plugins: [
    39:       require.resolve(
    40:         "@babel/plugin-transform-modules-commonjs"
    41:       ),
    42:     ],
    43:   });
    44: 
    45:   publicApi = require(
    46:     "../../src/pages/Top3/top3.public-api.js"
    47:   );
    48: 
    49:   return publicApi;
    50: }
    51: 
    52: function assertFunction(api, name) {
    53:   if (!api || typeof api[name] !== "function") {
    54:     throw new Error(
    55:       `Função pública TOP3 indisponível: ${name}`
    56:     );
    57:   }
    58: 
    59:   return api[name];
    60: }
    61: 
    62: function getTop3Capabilities() {
    63:   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
    64: 
    65:   return Object.keys(api)
    66:     .filter((name) => typeof api[name] === "function")
    67:     .sort();
    68: }
    69: 
    70: function computeConditionalNextTop3(input = {}) {
    71:   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
    72: 
    73:   return assertFunction(
    74:     api,
    75:     "computeConditionalNextTop3"
    76:   )(input);
    77: }
    78: 
    79: function computeConditionalNextTop3V2(input = {}) {
    80:   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
    81: 
    82:   return assertFunction(
    83:     api,
    84:     "computeConditionalNextTop3V2"
    85:   )(input);
    86: }
    87: 
    88: function computeStatisticalTop3V3(input = {}) {
    89:   const api = loadTop3PublicApi();
    90: 
    91:   return assertFunction(
    92:     api,
    93:     "computeStatisticalTop3V3"
    94:   )(input);
    95: }
    96: 
    97: module.exports = {
    98:   loadTop3PublicApi,
    99:   getTop3Capabilities,
   100:   computeConditionalNextTop3,
   101:   computeConditionalNextTop3V2,
   102:   computeStatisticalTop3V3,
   103: };

     1: "use strict";
     2: 
     3: const {
     4:   computeStatisticalTop3V3,
     5: } = require("./scoreEngineUnified");

     7: const GROUPS_K = 25;
     8: const LAPLACE_ALPHA = 1;
     9: 
    10: function safeArray(value) {
    11:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
    12: }
    13: 
    14: function normalizeHour(value) {
    15:   const raw = String(value ?? "").trim();
    16: 
    17:   if (!raw) {
    18:     return "";
    19:   }
    20: 
    21:   const match = raw.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?/);
    22: 
    23:   if (!match) {
    24:     return "";
    25:   }
    26: 
    27:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
    28:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
    29: 
    30:   if (
    31:     !Number.isFinite(hour) ||
    32:     hour < 0 ||
    33:     hour > 23 ||
    34:     !Number.isFinite(minute) ||
    35:     minute < 0 ||
    36:     minute > 59
    37:   ) {
    38:     return "";
    39:   }
    40: 
    41:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(minute).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    42: }
    43: 
    44: function normalizeYmd(value) {
    45:   const raw = String(value ?? "").trim();
    46: 
    47:   const isoMatch = raw.match(
    48:     /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/
    49:   );
    50: 
    51:   if (isoMatch) {
    52:     return `${isoMatch[1]}-${isoMatch[2]}-${isoMatch[3]}`;
    53:   }
    54: 
    55:   const brMatch = raw.match(
    56:     /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
    57:   );
    58: 
    59:   if (brMatch) {
    60:     return `${brMatch[3]}-${brMatch[2]}-${brMatch[1]}`;
    61:   }
    62: 
    63:   return "";
    64: }
    65: 
    66: function pickDrawYmd(draw) {
    67:   return normalizeYmd(
    68:     draw?.ymd ??
    69:     draw?.date ??
    70:     draw?.data ??
    71:     draw?.draw_date ??
    72:     draw?.drawDate ??
    73:     draw?.close_date ??
    74:     draw?.closeDate
    75:   );
    76: }
    77: 
    78: function pickDrawHour(draw) {
    79:   return normalizeHour(
    80:     draw?.close_hour ??
    81:     draw?.closeHour ??
    82:     draw?.hour ??
    83:     draw?.hora
    84:   );
    85: }
    86: 
    87: function groupFromDezena(value) {
    88:   const digits = String(value ?? "")
    89:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
    90:     .padStart(2, "0")
    91:     .slice(-2);
    92: 
    93:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(digits)) {
    94:     return null;
    95:   }
    96: 
    97:   const dezenaRaw = Number(digits);
    98: 
    99:   if (!Number.isFinite(dezenaRaw)) {
   100:     return null;
   101:   }
   102: 
   103:   const dezena = dezenaRaw === 0
   104:     ? 100
   105:     : dezenaRaw;
   106: 
   107:   const group = Math.ceil(dezena / 4);
   108: 
   109:   return group >= 1 && group <= GROUPS_K
   110:     ? group
   111:     : null;
   112: }
   113: 
   114: function pickPrizePosition(prize) {
   115:   const candidates = [
   116:     prize?.position,
   117:     prize?.posicao,
   118:     prize?.pos,
   119:     prize?.colocacao,
   120:   ];
   121: 
   122:   for (const value of candidates) {
   123:     const position = Number(value);
   124: 
   125:     if (Number.isFinite(position)) {
   126:       return position;
   127:     }
   128:   }
   129: 
   130:   return null;
   131: }
   132: 
   133: function pickPrizeGroup(prize) {
   134:   const directCandidates = [
   135:     prize?.grupo,
   136:     prize?.group,
   137:     prize?.grupo2,
   138:     prize?.group2,
   139:     prize?.animal_grupo,
   140:     prize?.grupo_animal,
   141:     prize?.grupoAnimal,
   142:     prize?.g,
   143:   ];
   144: 
   145:   for (const value of directCandidates) {
   146:     const group = Number(value);
   147: 
   148:     if (
   149:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
   150:       group >= 1 &&
   151:       group <= GROUPS_K
   152:     ) {
   153:       return group;
   154:     }
   155:   }
   156: 
   157:   const numberCandidates = [
   158:     prize?.milhar,
   159:     prize?.milhar4,
   160:     prize?.numero,
   161:     prize?.number,
   162:     prize?.value,
   163:     prize?.resultado,
   164:     prize?.result,
   165:     prize?.premio,
   166:   ];
   167: 
   168:   for (const value of numberCandidates) {
   169:     const digits = String(value ?? "")
   170:       .replace(/\D+/g, "");
   171: 
   172:     if (!digits) {
   173:       continue;
   174:     }
   175: 
   176:     const group = groupFromDezena(
   177:       digits.slice(-2)
   178:     );
   179: 
   180:     if (group != null) {
   181:       return group;
   182:     }
   183:   }
   184: 
   185:   return null;
   186: }
   187: 
   188: function pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw) {
   189:   const prizes = safeArray(draw?.prizes);
   190: 
   191:   const firstPrize =
   192:     prizes.find(
   193:       (prize) =>
   194:         pickPrizePosition(prize) === 1
   195:     ) || prizes[0] || null;
   196: 
   197:   if (firstPrize) {
   198:     const group = pickPrizeGroup(firstPrize);
   199: 
   200:     if (group != null) {
   201:       return group;
   202:     }
   203:   }
   204: 
   205:   const directCandidates = [
   206:     draw?.grupo1,
   207:     draw?.group1,
   208:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
   209:     draw?.first_grupo,
   210:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
   211:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
   212:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
   213:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
   214:     draw?.grupo_1,
   215:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
   216:     draw?.g1,
   217:   ];
   218: 
   219:   for (const value of directCandidates) {
   220:     const group = Number(value);
   221: 
   222:     if (
   223:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
   224:       group >= 1 &&
   225:       group <= GROUPS_K
   226:     ) {
   227:       return group;
   228:     }
   229:   }
   230: 
   231:   return null;
   232: }
   233: 
   234: function drawTimestamp(draw) {
   235:   const ymd = pickDrawYmd(draw);
   236:   const hour = pickDrawHour(draw);
   237: 
   238:   if (!ymd || !hour) {
   239:     return Number.NaN;
   240:   }
   241: 
   242:   return Date.parse(
   243:     `${ymd}T${hour}:00Z`
   244:   );
   245: }
   246: 
   247: function sortDraws(draws) {
   248:   return safeArray(draws)
   249:     .filter(Boolean)
   250:     .map((draw, index) => ({
   251:       draw,
   252:       index,
   253:       timestamp: drawTimestamp(draw),
   254:     }))
   255:     .filter(
   256:       (item) =>
   257:         Number.isFinite(item.timestamp)
   258:     )
   259:     .sort(
   260:       (a, b) =>
   261:         a.timestamp - b.timestamp ||
   262:         a.index - b.index
   263:     )
   264:     .map((item) => item.draw);
   265: }
   266: 
   267: function createGroupMap() {
   268:   const map = new Map();
   269: 
   270:   for (
   271:     let group = 1;
   272:     group <= GROUPS_K;
   273:     group += 1
   274:   ) {
   275:     map.set(group, 0);
   276:   }
   277: 
   278:   return map;
   279: }
   280: 
   281: function increment(map, group) {
   282:   const value = Number(group);
   283: 
   284:   if (
   285:     !Number.isFinite(value) ||
   286:     value < 1 ||
   287:     value > GROUPS_K
   288:   ) {
   289:     return;
   290:   }
   291: 
   292:   map.set(
   293:     value,
   294:     Number(map.get(value) || 0) + 1
   295:   );
   296: }
   297: 
   298: function probabilityMap(
   299:   frequency,
   300:   samples,
   301:   alpha = LAPLACE_ALPHA
   302: ) {
   303:   const probabilities = new Map();
   304: 
   305:   const safeSamples = Math.max(
   306:     0,
   307:     Number(samples || 0)
   308:   );
   309: 
   310:   const safeAlpha = Math.max(
   311:     0,
   312:     Number(alpha || 0)
   313:   );
   314: 
   315:   const denominator =
   316:     safeSamples +
   317:     (safeAlpha * GROUPS_K);
   318: 
   319:   for (
   320:     let group = 1;
   321:     group <= GROUPS_K;
   322:     group += 1
   323:   ) {
   324:     const count = Number(
   325:       frequency.get(group) || 0
   326:     );
   327: 
   328:     probabilities.set(
   329:       group,
   330:       denominator > 0
   331:         ? (count + safeAlpha) / denominator
   332:         : 1 / GROUPS_K
   333:     );
   334:   }
   335: 
   336:   return probabilities;
   337: }
   338: 
   339: function mapToObject(map) {
   340:   return Object.fromEntries(
   341:     Array.from(map.entries()).map(
   342:       ([group, value]) => [
   343:         String(group).padStart(2, "0"),
   344:         value,
   345:       ]
   346:     )
   347:   );
   348: }
   349: 
   350: function collectTransitionEvidence(input = {}) {
   351:   const draws = sortDraws(
   352:     input.drawsRange
   353:   );
   354: 
   355:   const drawLast =
   356:     input.drawLast || draws[draws.length - 1];
   357: 
   358:   const previousGroup =
   359:     pickFirstPrizeGroup(drawLast);
   360: 
   361:   const previousHour =
   362:     pickDrawHour(drawLast);
   363: 
   364:   const targetHour =
   365:     normalizeHour(
   366:       input.targetHourOverride
   367:     );
   368: 
   369:   const globalFrequency =
   370:     createGroupMap();
   371: 
   372:   const sameHourFrequency =
   373:     createGroupMap();
   374: 
   375:   const transitionFrequency =
   376:     createGroupMap();
   377: 
   378:   let globalSamples = 0;
   379:   let sameHourSamples = 0;
   380:   let transitionSamples = 0;
   381: 
   382:   for (const draw of draws) {
   383:     const group =
   384:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(draw);
   385: 
   386:     const hour =
   387:       pickDrawHour(draw);
   388: 
   389:     if (group == null) {
   390:       continue;
   391:     }
   392: 
   393:     globalSamples += 1;
   394:     increment(
   395:       globalFrequency,
   396:       group
   397:     );
   398: 
   399:     if (
   400:       targetHour &&
   401:       hour === targetHour
   402:     ) {
   403:       sameHourSamples += 1;
   404:       increment(
   405:         sameHourFrequency,
   406:         group
   407:       );
   408:     }
   409:   }
   410: 
   411:   for (
   412:     let index = 0;
   413:     index < draws.length - 1;
   414:     index += 1
   415:   ) {
   416:     const previousDraw =
   417:       draws[index];
   418: 
   419:     const nextDraw =
   420:       draws[index + 1];
   421: 
   422:     const historicalPreviousGroup =
   423:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(
   424:         previousDraw
   425:       );
   426: 
   427:     const historicalPreviousHour =
   428:       pickDrawHour(
   429:         previousDraw
   430:       );
   431: 
   432:     const historicalNextGroup =
   433:       pickFirstPrizeGroup(
   434:         nextDraw
   435:       );
   436: 
   437:     const historicalNextHour =
   438:       pickDrawHour(
   439:         nextDraw
   440:       );
   441: 
   442:     if (
   443:       historicalPreviousGroup == null ||
   444:       historicalNextGroup == null ||
   445:       !historicalPreviousHour ||
   446:       !historicalNextHour
   447:     ) {
   448:       continue;
   449:     }
   450: 
   451:     if (
   452:       Number(historicalPreviousGroup) !==
   453:       Number(previousGroup)
   454:     ) {
   455:       continue;
   456:     }
   457: 
   458:     if (
   459:       historicalPreviousHour !==
   460:       previousHour
   461:     ) {
   462:       continue;
   463:     }
   464: 
   465:     if (
   466:       targetHour &&
   467:       historicalNextHour !==
   468:       targetHour
   469:     ) {
   470:       continue;
   471:     }
   472: 
   473:     transitionSamples += 1;
   474: 
   475:     increment(
   476:       transitionFrequency,
   477:       historicalNextGroup
   478:     );
   479:   }
   480: 
   481:   const globalProbability =
   482:     probabilityMap(
   483:       globalFrequency,
   484:       globalSamples
   485:     );
   486: 
   487:   const sameHourProbability =
   488:     probabilityMap(
   489:       sameHourFrequency,
   490:       sameHourSamples
   491:     );
   492: 
   493:   const transitionProbability =
   494:     probabilityMap(
   495:       transitionFrequency,
   496:       transitionSamples
   497:     );
   498: 
   499:   const source =
   500:     transitionSamples > 0
   501:       ? "TRANSITION"
   502:       : sameHourSamples > 0
   503:         ? "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
   504:         : "GLOBAL_FALLBACK";
   505: 
   506:   const selectedProbability =
   507:     source === "TRANSITION"
   508:       ? transitionProbability
   509:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
   510:         ? sameHourProbability
   511:         : globalProbability;
   512: 
   513:   const selectedFrequency =
   514:     source === "TRANSITION"
   515:       ? transitionFrequency
   516:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
   517:         ? sameHourFrequency
   518:         : globalFrequency;
   519: 
   520:   const selectedSamples =
   521:     source === "TRANSITION"
   522:       ? transitionSamples
   523:       : source === "SAME_HOUR_FALLBACK"
   524:         ? sameHourSamples
   525:         : globalSamples;
   526: 
   527:   const ranking = Array.from(
   528:     {
   529:       length: GROUPS_K,
   530:     },
   531:     (_, index) => {
   532:       const group = index + 1;
   533: 
   534:       return {
   535:         grupo: group,
   536:         scoreProb: Number(
   537:           selectedProbability.get(group) || 0
   538:         ),
   539:         score:
   540:           Number(
   541:             selectedProbability.get(group) || 0
   542:           ) * 1000,
   543:         frequency: Number(
   544:           selectedFrequency.get(group) || 0
   545:         ),
   546:         transitionProbability: Number(
   547:           transitionProbability.get(group) || 0
   548:         ),
   549:         sameHourProbability: Number(
   550:           sameHourProbability.get(group) || 0
   551:         ),
   552:         globalProbability: Number(
   553:           globalProbability.get(group) || 0
   554:         ),
   555:       };
   556:     }
   557:   ).sort((a, b) => {
   558:     if (b.score !== a.score) {
   559:       return b.score - a.score;
   560:     }
   561: 
   562:     if (b.frequency !== a.frequency) {
   563:       return b.frequency - a.frequency;
   564:     }
   565: 
   566:     return a.grupo - b.grupo;
   567:   });
   568: 
   569:   return {
   570:     enabled: true,
   571:     experiment: "E07",
   572:     version: 1,
   573:     source,
   574:     previous: {
   575:       ymd: pickDrawYmd(drawLast),
   576:       hour: previousHour,
   577:       group: previousGroup,
   578:     },
   579:     target: {
   580:       ymd: normalizeYmd(
   581:         input.targetYmdOverride
   582:       ),
   583:       hour: targetHour,
   584:     },
   585:     samples: {
   586:       global: globalSamples,
   587:       sameHour: sameHourSamples,
   588:       transition: transitionSamples,
   589:       selected: selectedSamples,
   590:     },
   591:     frequencies: {
   592:       global:
   593:         mapToObject(globalFrequency),
   594:       sameHour:
   595:         mapToObject(sameHourFrequency),
   596:       transition:
   597:         mapToObject(transitionFrequency),
   598:     },
   599:     probabilities: {
   600:       global:
   601:         mapToObject(globalProbability),
   602:       sameHour:
   603:         mapToObject(sameHourProbability),
   604:       transition:
   605:         mapToObject(transitionProbability),
   606:     },
   607:     ranking,
   608:   };
   609: }
   610: 
   611: function buildExperimentalTop(
   612:   evidence,
   613:   topN = 3
   614: ) {
   615:   return safeArray(
   616:     evidence?.ranking
   617:   )
   618:     .slice(
   619:       0,
   620:       Math.max(
   621:         1,
   622:         Number(topN || 3)
   623:       )
   624:     )
   625:     .map(
   626:       (item, index) => ({
   627:         rank: index + 1,
   628:         title:
   629:           index === 0
   630:             ? "Mais provavel"
   631:             : index === 1
   632:               ? "2o mais provavel"
   633:               : "3o mais provavel",
   634:         grupo: item.grupo,
   635:         scoreProb:
   636:           item.scoreProb,
   637:         rawScoreProb:
   638:           item.scoreProb,
   639:         score:
   640:           item.score,
   641:         probability:
   642:           item.scoreProb,
   643:         probCond:
   644:           item.transitionProbability,
   645:         probBase:
   646:           item.sameHourProbability,
   647:         freq:
   648:           item.frequency,
   649:         freqCond:
   650:           item.frequency,
   651:         freqBase: 0,
   652:         lateBonus: 0,
   653:         reasons: [
   654:           "Motor experimental E07",
   655:           `Fonte: ${evidence.source}`,
   656:           `Transicoes: ${evidence.samples.transition}`,
   657:           `Mesmo horario: ${evidence.samples.sameHour}`,
   658:           `Historico global: ${evidence.samples.global}`,
   659:           `Grupo anterior: ${String(
   660:             evidence.previous.group
   661:           ).padStart(2, "0")}`,
   662:           `Hora anterior: ${evidence.previous.hour}`,
   663:           `Hora alvo: ${evidence.target.hour}`,
   664:         ],
   665:         meta: {
   666:           experiment: "E07",
   667:           source:
   668:             evidence.source,
   669:           samples:
   670:             evidence.samples,
   671:           previous:
   672:             evidence.previous,
   673:           target:
   674:             evidence.target,
   675:         },
   676:       })
   677:     );
   678: }
   679: 
   680: function computeStatisticalTop3V4Experimental(
   681:   input = {}
   682: ) {
   683:   const baseline =
   684:     computeStatisticalTop3V3(
   685:       input
   686:     );
   687: 
   688:   const evidence =
   689:     collectTransitionEvidence(
   690:       input
   691:     );
   692: 
   693:   const experimentalTop =
   694:     buildExperimentalTop(
   695:       evidence,
   696:       input.topN || 3
   697:     );
   698: 
   699:   return {
   700:     ...baseline,
   701:     top:
   702:       experimentalTop.length
   703:         ? experimentalTop
   704:         : safeArray(baseline?.top),
   705:     ranking:
   706:       experimentalTop.length
   707:         ? experimentalTop
   708:         : safeArray(
   709:             baseline?.ranking
   710:           ),
   711:     baselineTop:
   712:       safeArray(baseline?.top),
   713:     experimental: {
   714:       enabled: true,
   715:       version: 4,
   716:       experiment: "E07",
   717:       noProductionInfluence: true,
   718:       evidence,
   719:     },
   720:     meta: {
   721:       ...(baseline?.meta || {}),
   722:       scenario:
   723:         "V4_EXPERIMENTAL_E07",
   724:       experimental: {
   725:         experiment: "E07",
   726:         source:
   727:           evidence.source,
   728:         samples:
   729:           evidence.samples,
   730:       },
   731:     },
   732:   };
   733: }
   734: 
   735: module.exports = {
   736:   collectTransitionEvidence,

    15: function safeArray(value) {
    16:   return Array.isArray(value)
    17:     ? value
    18:     : [];
    19: }
    20: 
    21: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    22:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
    23:     .trim()
    24:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    25: }
    26: 
    27: function validYmd(value) {
    28:   const ymd = String(value || "").trim();
    29: 
    30:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(ymd)
    31:     ? ymd
    32:     : null;
    33: }

    35: function resolveDb(dependencies = {}) {
    36:   return dependencies.db || getDb();
    37: }
    38: 
    39: async function readDrawWithPrizes(
    40:   database,
    41:   drawDoc
    42: ) {
    43:   if (!drawDoc || !drawDoc.exists) {
    44:     return null;
    45:   }
    46: 
    47:   const data = drawDoc.data() || {};
    48: 
    49:   const prizesSnap = await drawDoc.ref
    50:     .collection("prizes")
    51:     .get();
    52: 
    53:   const prizes = prizesSnap.docs.map(
    54:     (doc) => ({
    55:       id: doc.id,
    56:       ...(doc.data() || {}),
    57:     })
    58:   );
    59: 
    60:   return {
    61:     id: drawDoc.id,
    62:     drawId: drawDoc.id,
    63:     ...data,
    64:     prizes,
    65:   };
    66: }
    67: 
    68: async function loadImportedDraws(
    69:   importResult = {},
    70:   dependencies = {}
    71: ) {
    72:   const database = resolveDb(dependencies);
    73: 
    74:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
    75:     importResult.lotteryKey
    76:   );
    77: 
    78:   const date = validYmd(
    79:     importResult.date
    80:   );
    81: 
    82:   const targetDrawIds = safeArray(
    83:     importResult.targetDrawIds
    84:   )
    85:     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim())
    86:     .filter(Boolean);
    87: 
    88:   const docs = [];
    89: 
    90:   if (targetDrawIds.length) {
    91:     for (const drawId of targetDrawIds) {
    92:       const snap = await database
    93:         .collection("draws")
    94:         .doc(drawId)
    95:         .get();
    96: 
    97:       if (snap.exists) {
    98:         docs.push(snap);
    99:       }
   100:     }
   101:   } else if (date) {
   102:     const snap = await database
   103:       .collection("draws")
   104:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lotteryKey)
   105:       .where("ymd", "==", date)
   106:       .get();
   107: 
   108:     docs.push(...snap.docs);
   109:   }
   110: 
   111:   const draws = [];
   112: 
   113:   for (const doc of docs) {
   114:     const draw = await readDrawWithPrizes(
   115:       database,
   116:       doc
   117:     );
   118: 
   119:     if (!draw) {
   120:       continue;
   121:     }
   122: 
   123:     const drawLotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   124:       draw.lottery_key ||
   125:       draw.lotteryKey
   126:     );
   127: 
   128:     if (drawLotteryKey !== lotteryKey) {
   129:       continue;
   130:     }
   131: 
   132:     if (
   133:       date &&
   134:       String(draw.ymd || draw.date || "") !== date
   135:     ) {
   136:       continue;
   137:     }
   138: 
   139:     draws.push(draw);
   140:   }
   141: 
   142:   return deduplicateDraws(draws);
   143: }
   144: 
   145: function summarizeMonths(months = []) {
   146:   const ordered = [...safeArray(months)]
   147:     .sort((a, b) =>
   148:       String(a.yearMonth || a.id)
   149:         .localeCompare(
   150:           String(b.yearMonth || b.id)
   151:         )
   152:     );
   153: 
   154:   const allDraws = deduplicateDraws(
   155:     ordered.flatMap(
   156:       (month) =>
   157:         safeArray(month.draws)
   158:     )
   159:   );
   160: 
   161:   const first = allDraws[0] || null;
   162:   const last =
   163:     allDraws[allDraws.length - 1] || null;
   164: 
   165:   return {
   166:     totalDraws: allDraws.length,
   167:     monthCount: ordered.length,
   168:     months: ordered.map(
   169:       (month) =>
   170:         month.yearMonth || month.id
   171:     ),
   172:     firstYmd: first?.ymd || null,
   173:     lastYmd: last?.ymd || null,
   174:     firstDrawId:
   175:       first?.drawId || null,
   176:     lastDrawId:
   177:       last?.drawId || null,
   178:   };
   179: }
   180: 
   181: async function markHistoryStale(
   182:   lotteryKey,
   183:   error,
   184:   dependencies = {}
   185: ) {
   186:   const saveMetadata =
   187:     dependencies.writeMetadata ||
   188:     writeMetadata;
   189: 
   190:   try {
   191:     await saveMetadata(
   192:       lotteryKey,
   193:       {
   194:         bootstrapStatus: "stale",
   195:         staleReason:
   196:           String(
   197:             error?.message ||
   198:             error ||
   199:             "incremental_sync_failed"
   200:           ),
   201:         staleAt:
   202:           new Date().toISOString(),
   203:       },
   204:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   205:     );
   206: 
   207:     return true;
   208:   } catch (metadataError) {
   209:     console.error(
   210:       "[TOP3-HISTORY] Falha também ao marcar snapshot como stale:",
   211:       metadataError?.message ||
   212:       metadataError
   213:     );
   214: 
   215:     return false;
   216:   }
   217: }

   219: async function syncImportedResultToTop3History(
   220:   importResult = {},
   221:   dependencies = {}
   222: ) {
   223:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   224:     importResult.lotteryKey
   225:   );
   226: 
   227:   const loadMetadata =
   228:     dependencies.readMetadata ||
   229:     readMetadata;
   230: 
   231:   const saveMonth =
   232:     dependencies.upsertHistoryMonth ||
   233:     upsertHistoryMonth;
   234: 
   235:   const loadMonths =
   236:     dependencies.listHistoryMonths ||
   237:     listHistoryMonths;
   238: 
   239:   const saveMetadata =
   240:     dependencies.writeMetadata ||
   241:     writeMetadata;
   242: 
   243:   try {
   244:     if (
   245:       importResult.blocked === true ||
   246:       !validYmd(importResult.date)
   247:     ) {
   248:       return {
   249:         ok: true,
   250:         skipped: true,
   251:         reason:
   252:           importResult.blocked === true
   253:             ? "import_blocked"
   254:             : "invalid_date",
   255:       };
   256:     }
   257: 
   258:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
   259:       lotteryKey,
   260:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   261:     );
   262: 
   263:     const metadata =
   264:       metadataResult?.data || null;
   265: 
   266:     if (
   267:       metadataResult?.exists !== true ||
   268:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus !== "complete"
   269:     ) {
   270:       return {
   271:         ok: true,
   272:         skipped: true,
   273:         reason: "bootstrap_not_complete",
   274:       };
   275:     }
   276: 
   277:     const draws = await (
   278:       dependencies.loadImportedDraws ||
   279:       loadImportedDraws
   280:     )(
   281:       importResult,
   282:       dependencies
   283:     );
   284: 
   285:     if (!draws.length) {
   286:       return {
   287:         ok: true,
   288:         skipped: true,
   289:         reason: "no_draws_found",
   290:       };
   291:     }
   292: 
   293:     const byMonth = new Map();
   294: 
   295:     for (const draw of draws) {
   296:       const yearMonth = draw.ymd.slice(0, 7);
   297: 
   298:       if (!byMonth.has(yearMonth)) {
   299:         byMonth.set(yearMonth, []);
   300:       }
   301: 
   302:       byMonth.get(yearMonth).push(draw);
   303:     }
   304: 
   305:     const updatedMonths = [];
   306: 
   307:     for (
   308:       const [yearMonth, monthDraws]
   309:       of byMonth.entries()
   310:     ) {
   311:       const payload = await saveMonth(
   312:         lotteryKey,
   313:         yearMonth,
   314:         monthDraws,
   315:         dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   316:       );
   317: 
   318:       updatedMonths.push({
   319:         yearMonth,
   320:         drawCount:
   321:           Number(payload?.drawCount || 0),
   322:       });
   323:     }
   324: 
   325:     const months = await loadMonths(
   326:       lotteryKey,
   327:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   328:     );
   329: 
   330:     const summary = summarizeMonths(months);
   331: 
   332:     await saveMetadata(
   333:       lotteryKey,
   334:       {
   335:         bootstrapStatus: "complete",
   336:         incrementalUpdatedAt:
   337:           new Date().toISOString(),
   338:         totalDraws:
   339:           summary.totalDraws,
   340:         monthCount:
   341:           summary.monthCount,
   342:         months:
   343:           summary.months,
   344:         firstYmd:
   345:           summary.firstYmd,
   346:         lastYmd:
   347:           summary.lastYmd,
   348:         firstDrawId:
   349:           summary.firstDrawId,
   350:         lastDrawId:
   351:           summary.lastDrawId,
   352:         lastProcessedDrawId:
   353:           summary.lastDrawId,
   354:         staleReason: null,
   355:         staleAt: null,
   356:         source:
   357:           "bootstrap_plus_incremental",
   358:       },
   359:       dependencies.repositoryDependencies || {}
   360:     );
   361: 
   362:     console.log(
   363:       `[TOP3-HISTORY] Incremental OK | ${lotteryKey} | ` +
   364:       `draws=${draws.length} | meses=${updatedMonths
   365:         .map((item) => item.yearMonth)
   366:         .join(",")}`
   367:     );
   368: 
   369:     return {
   370:       ok: true,
   371:       skipped: false,
   372:       lotteryKey,
   373:       importedDraws: draws.length,
   374:       updatedMonths,
   375:       ...summary,
   376:     };
   377:   } catch (error) {
   378:     console.error(
   379:       "[TOP3-HISTORY] Incremental falhou:",
   380:       error?.stack ||
   381:       error?.message ||
   382:       error
   383:     );
   384: 
   385:     const markedStale = await markHistoryStale(
   386:       lotteryKey,
   387:       error,
   388:       dependencies
   389:     );
   390: 
   391:     return {
   392:       ok: false,
   393:       skipped: false,
   394:       lotteryKey,
   395:       markedStale,
   396:       error:
   397:         String(
   398:           error?.message ||
   399:           error ||
   400:           "incremental_sync_failed"
   401:         ),
   402:     };
   403:   }
   404: }

    74: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    75:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
    76:     .trim()
    77:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    78: }
    79: 
    80: function normalizeYmd(value) {
    81:   const text = String(value || "").trim();
    82: 
    83:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(text)) {
    84:     throw new Error(
    85:       "Data inválida. Utilize o formato YYYY-MM-DD."
    86:     );
    87:   }
    88: 
    89:   return text;
    90: }
    91: 
    92: function normalizeHour(value) {
    93:   const text = String(value || "")
    94:     .trim()
    95:     .replace(/[hH]/g, ":");
    96: 
    97:   const match = text.match(
    98:     /^(\d{1,2})(?::?(\d{2}))?$/
    99:   );
   100: 
   101:   if (!match) {
   102:     throw new Error(
   103:       "Horário inválido. Utilize HH:MM."
   104:     );
   105:   }
   106: 
   107:   const hour = Number(match[1]);
   108:   const minute = Number(match[2] || 0);
   109: 
   110:   if (
   111:     hour < 0 ||
   112:     hour > 23 ||
   113:     minute < 0 ||
   114:     minute > 59
   115:   ) {
   116:     throw new Error("Horário fora do intervalo válido.");
   117:   }
   118: 
   119:   return `${String(hour).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(
   120:     minute
   121:   ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   122: }
   123: 
   124: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
   125:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour)}`;
   126: }
   127: 
   128: function subtractDaysYmd(ymd, days) {
   129:   const safeDays = Math.max(
   130:     1,
   131:     Math.min(1460, Number(days || 180))
   132:   );
   133: 
   134:   const date = new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00Z`);
   135: 
   136:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
   137:     throw new Error(
   138:       "Não foi possível calcular a janela histórica."
   139:     );
   140:   }
   141: 
   142:   date.setUTCDate(
   143:     date.getUTCDate() - safeDays
   144:   );
   145: 
   146:   return date.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   147: }
   148: 
   149: function normalizeHistorySource(value) {
   150:   const source = String(value || "auto")
   151:     .trim()
   152:     .toLowerCase();
   153: 
   154:   if (
   155:     source !== "auto" &&
   156:     source !== "snapshot" &&
   157:     source !== "range"
   158:   ) {
   159:     throw new Error(
   160:       "historySource inválido. Utilize auto, snapshot ou range."
   161:     );
   162:   }
   163: 
   164:   return source;
   165: }
   166: 
   167: async function loadPredictionHistory({
   168:   lotteryKey,
   169:   date,
   170:   input = {},
   171:   dependencies = {},
   172: } = {}) {
   173:   const requestedSource = normalizeHistorySource(
   174:     input.historySource
   175:   );
   176: 
   177:   const loadMetadata =
   178:     dependencies.readHistoryMetadata ||
   179:     readMetadata;
   180: 
   181:   const loadFullHistory =
   182:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
   183:     readFullHistory;
   184: 
   185:   const loadRangeHistory =
   186:     dependencies.fetchDraws ||
   187:     fetchDrawsWithPrizesByRange;
   188: 
   189:   if (
   190:     requestedSource === "auto" ||
   191:     requestedSource === "snapshot"
   192:   ) {
   193:     const metadataResult = await loadMetadata(
   194:       lotteryKey,
   195:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   196:     );
   197: 
   198:     const metadata =
   199:       metadataResult?.data || null;
   200: 
   201:     const snapshotReady =
   202:       metadataResult?.exists === true &&
   203:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus === "complete";
   204: 
   205:     if (snapshotReady) {
   206:       const draws = await loadFullHistory(
   207:         lotteryKey,
   208:         dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   209:       );
   210: 
   211:       if (Array.isArray(draws) && draws.length) {
   212:         const expectedTotal = Number(
   213:           metadata?.totalDraws || 0
   214:         );
   215: 
   216:         if (
   217:           expectedTotal > 0 &&
   218:           draws.length !== expectedTotal
   219:         ) {
   220:           throw new Error(
   221:             "Histórico TOP3 inconsistente: metadata.totalDraws=" +
   222:             `${expectedTotal}, carregados=${draws.length}.`
   223:           );
   224:         }
   225: 
   226:         return {
   227:           source: "snapshot",
   228:           draws,
   229:           metadata,
   230:           lookbackDays: null,
   231:           maxDraws: null,
   232:           startYmd:
   233:             metadata?.firstYmd || null,
   234:         };
   235:       }
   236: 
   237:       throw new Error(
   238:         "Metadata do histórico TOP3 está completo, mas nenhum draw foi carregado."
   239:       );
   240:     }
   241: 
   242:     if (requestedSource === "snapshot") {
   243:       throw new Error(
   244:         "Histórico TOP3 completo ainda não está disponível."
   245:       );
   246:     }
   247:   }
   248: 
   249:   const lookbackDays = Math.max(
   250:     30,
   251:     Math.min(
   252:       1460,
   253:       Number(input.lookbackDays || 180)
   254:     )
   255:   );
   256: 
   257:   const maxDraws = Math.max(
   258:     100,
   259:     Math.min(
   260:       5000,
   261:       Number(input.maxDraws || 1200)
   262:     )
   263:   );
   264: 
   265:   const startYmd = subtractDaysYmd(
   266:     date,
   267:     lookbackDays
   268:   );
   269: 
   270:   const rawDraws = await loadRangeHistory({
   271:     lottery: lotteryKey,
   272:     startYmd,
   273:     endYmd: date,
   274:     pageSize: Number(input.pageSize || 250),
   275:     maxDraws,
   276:     prizeConcurrency: Number(
   277:       input.prizeConcurrency || 24
   278:     ),
   279:   });
   280: 
   281:   return {
   282:     source: "range_fallback",
   283:     draws: extractDraws(rawDraws),
   284:     metadata: null,
   285:     lookbackDays,
   286:     maxDraws,
   287:     startYmd,
   288:   };
   289: }
   290: 
   291: function extractDraws(result) {
   292:   if (Array.isArray(result)) {
   293:     return result;
   294:   }
   295: 
   296:   const candidates = [
   297:     result?.draws,
   298:     result?.items,
   299:     result?.results,
   300:     result?.data,
   301:   ];
   302: 
   303:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
   304:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
   305:       return candidate;
   306:     }
   307:   }
   308: 
   309:   return [];
   310: }
   311: 
   312: function normalizePublicMilhar(value) {
   313:   const digits = String(value || "")
   314:     .replace(/\D+/g, "");
   315: 
   316:   if (!digits) {
   317:     return "";
   318:   }
   319: 
   320:   return digits
   321:     .slice(-4)
   322:     .padStart(4, "0");
   323: }
   324: 
   325: function publicHourCode(value) {
   326:   return normalizeHour(value)
   327:     .replace(/\D+/g, "")
   328:     .slice(0, 2)
   329:     .padStart(2, "0");
   330: }
   331: 
   332: function resolveNextTop3Slot({
   333:   lotteryKey,
   334:   ymd,
   335:   hour,
   336:   publicApi,
   337: }) {
   338:   if (
   339:     !publicApi ||
   340:     typeof publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery !== "function"
   341:   ) {
   342:     throw new Error(
   343:       "API pública TOP3 sem getNextSlotForLottery."
   344:     );
   345:   }
   346: 
   347:   const resolved =
   348:     publicApi.getNextSlotForLottery({
   349:       lotteryKey: normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey),
   350:       ymd: normalizeYmd(ymd),
   351:       hourBucket: normalizeHour(hour),
   352:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   353:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   354:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   355:     });
   356: 
   357:   const rawHour = String(
   358:     resolved?.hour ||
   359:     resolved?.hourBucket ||
   360:     ""
   361:   ).trim();
   362: 
   363:   const hourMatch =
   364:     rawHour.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::00|h)?$/i);
   365: 
   366:   const canonicalHour = hourMatch
   367:     ? `${String(Number(hourMatch[1])).padStart(2, "0")}:00`
   368:     : "";
   369: 
   370:   return {
   371:     ...resolved,
   372:     ymd: normalizeYmd(resolved?.ymd),
   373:     hour: canonicalHour,
   374:     hourBucket: canonicalHour,
   375:   };
   376: }
   377: 
   378: function scheduleForPublicProjection(
   379:   lotteryKey,
   380:   date
   381: ) {
   382:   if (normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey) === "FEDERAL") {
   383:     return [...FEDERAL_SCHEDULE];
   384:   }
   385: 
   386:   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00Z`);
   387:   const dow = parsed.getUTCDay();
   388: 
   389:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) {
   390:     return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT];
   391:   }
   392: 
   393:   return [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL];
   394: }
   395: 
   396: function buildPublicMilharesCols(
   397:   engineOutput,
   398:   expectedCols = 4,
   399:   perCol = 5
   400: ) {
   401:   const dezenas = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.dezenas)
   402:     ? engineOutput.dezenas
   403:     : [];
   404: 
   405:   const slots = Array.isArray(engineOutput?.slots)
   406:     ? engineOutput.slots
   407:     : [];
   408: 
   409:   const cols = [];
   410: 
   411:   for (const dezena of dezenas.slice(0, expectedCols)) {
   412:     const items = slots
   413:       .filter(
   414:         (slot) =>
   415:           String(slot?.dezena || "") ===
   416:           String(dezena || "")
   417:       )
   418:       .map(
   419:         (slot) =>
   420:           normalizePublicMilhar(slot?.milhar)
   421:       )
   422:       .filter(
   423:         (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
   424:       )
   425:       .slice(0, perCol);
   426: 
   427:     while (items.length < perCol) {
   428:       items.push("");
   429:     }
   430: 
   431:     cols.push({
   432:       dezena: String(dezena || ""),
   433:       items,
   434:     });
   435:   }
   436: 
   437:   while (cols.length < expectedCols) {
   438:     cols.push({
   439:       dezena: "",
   440:       items: Array(perCol).fill(""),
   441:     });
   442:   }
   443: 
   444:   return cols.slice(0, expectedCols);
   445: }
   446: 
   447: function buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
   448:   computedTop,
   449:   history,
   450:   lotteryKey,
   451:   date,
   452:   closeHour,
   453:   publicApi,
   454: }) {
   455:   const schedule = scheduleForPublicProjection(
   456:     lotteryKey,
   457:     date
   458:   );
   459: 
   460:   return (Array.isArray(computedTop)
   461:     ? computedTop
   462:     : []
   463:   )
   464:     .slice(0, 3)
   465:     .map((item, index) => {
   466:       const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
   467: 
   468:       if (
   469:         !Number.isFinite(grupo) ||
   470:         grupo < 1 ||
   471:         grupo > 25
   472:       ) {
   473:         return null;
   474:       }
   475: 
   476:       const probability = Number(
   477:         item?.scoreProb ??
   478:         item?.probability ??
   479:         item?.confidence ??
   480:         0
   481:       );
   482: 
   483:       const prob =
   484:         probability > 1
   485:           ? probability / 100
   486:           : probability;
   487: 
   488:       const engineOutput =
   489:         publicApi.build20MilharesForGrupo({
   490:           rangeDraws: history,
   491:           analysisHourBucket: closeHour,
   492:           schedule,
   493:           grupo2: grupo,
   494:           targetYmd: date,
   495:         });
   496: 
   497:       const milharesCols =
   498:         buildPublicMilharesCols(
   499:           engineOutput,
   500:           4,
   501:           5
   502:         );
   503: 
   504:       const milhares20 = milharesCols
   505:         .flatMap((column) => column.items)
   506:         .filter(
   507:           (milhar) => /^\d{4}$/.test(milhar)
   508:         )
   509:         .slice(0, 20);
   510: 
   511:       return {
   512:         rank: index + 1,
   513:         grupo,
   514:         animal:
   515:           item?.animal ||
   516:           ANIMALS[
   517:             String(grupo).padStart(2, "0")
   518:           ] ||
   519:           "",
   520:         prob:
   521:           Number.isFinite(prob)
   522:             ? prob
   523:             : 0,
   524:         probPct:
   525:           Number.isFinite(prob)
   526:             ? Number((prob * 100).toFixed(4))
   527:             : 0,
   528:         milhares20,
   529:         milharesCols,
   530:         meta: item?.meta || null,
   531:       };
   532:     })
   533:     .filter(Boolean);
   534: }
   535: 
   536: async function saveTop3PublicProjection({
   537:   lotteryKey,
   538:   date,
   539:   closeHour,
   540:   snapshot,
   541:   engineVersion = "V3_STATISTICAL",
   542:   source = "backend-top3",
   543: }) {
   544:   const lottery = normalizeLotteryKey(lotteryKey);
   545:   const hour = normalizeHour(closeHour);
   546:   const hourCode = publicHourCode(hour);
   547: 
   548:   const id =
   549:     `${lottery}__${date}__${hourCode}`;
   550: 
   551:   const normalizedSnapshot =
   552:     Array.isArray(snapshot)
   553:       ? snapshot.slice(0, 3)
   554:       : [];
   555: 
   556:   if (!normalizedSnapshot.length) {
   557:     throw new Error(
   558:       "Snapshot público TOP3 vazio."
   559:     );
   560:   }
   561: 
   562:   const picks = normalizedSnapshot
   563:     .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
   564:     .filter(
   565:       (grupo) =>
   566:         Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
   567:         grupo >= 1 &&
   568:         grupo <= 25
   569:     )
   570:     .slice(0, 3);
   571: 
   572:   if (!picks.length) {
   573:     throw new Error(
   574:       "Picks públicos TOP3 inválidos."
   575:     );
   576:   }
   577: 
   578:   const database = getDb();
   579:   const ref = database
   580:     .collection("top3_predictions")
   581:     .doc(id);
   582: 
   583:   const now = Date.now();
   584: 
   585:   const payload = {
   586:     id,
   587:     lotteryKey: lottery,
   588:     targetYmd: date,
   589:     targetHour:
   590:       `${hourCode}h`,
   591:     targetKey:
   592:       `${date}_${hourCode}h`,
   593:     picks,
   594:     snapshot: normalizedSnapshot,
   595:     engineVersion,
   596:     status: "predicted",
   597:     resultGrupo: null,
   598:     resultMilhar: "",
   599:     resultAnimal: "",
   600:     hitType: "",
   601:     hitScore: 0,
   602:     hitPosition: -1,
   603:     matchedValue: "",
   604:     createdAt: now,
   605:     updatedAt: now,
   606:     createdBy: source,
   607:     source,
   608:   };
   609: 
   610:   return database.runTransaction(
   611:     async (transaction) => {
   612:       const current =
   613:         await transaction.get(ref);
   614: 
   615:       if (!current.exists) {
   616:         transaction.set(ref, payload);
   617: 
   618:         return {
   619:           ok: true,
   620:           created: true,
   621:           updated: false,
   622:           existing: false,
   623:           protected: false,
   624:           id,
   625:         };
   626:       }
   627: 
   628:       const currentData =
   629:         current.data() || {};
   630: 
   631:       const currentStatus = String(
   632:         currentData.status || ""
   633:       )
   634:         .trim()
   635:         .toLowerCase();
   636: 
   637:       if (currentStatus === "validated") {
   638:         return {
   639:           ok: true,
   640:           created: false,
   641:           updated: false,
   642:           existing: true,
   643:           protected: true,
   644:           reason: "ALREADY_VALIDATED",
   645:           id,
   646:         };
   647:       }
   648: 
   649:       const updatedPayload = {
   650:         ...payload,
   651:         createdAt:
   652:           currentData.createdAt ||
   653:           payload.createdAt,
   654:         updatedAt: Date.now(),
   655:         resultGrupo:
   656:           currentData.resultGrupo ?? null,
   657:         resultMilhar:
   658:           currentData.resultMilhar || "",
   659:         resultAnimal:
   660:           currentData.resultAnimal || "",
   661:         hitType:
   662:           currentData.hitType || "",
   663:         hitScore:
   664:           Number(currentData.hitScore || 0),
   665:         hitPosition:
   666:           Number.isFinite(
   667:             Number(currentData.hitPosition)
   668:           )
   669:             ? Number(currentData.hitPosition)
   670:             : -1,
   671:         matchedValue:
   672:           currentData.matchedValue || "",
   673:       };
   674: 
   675:       transaction.set(
   676:         ref,
   677:         updatedPayload,
   678:         { merge: true }
   679:       );
   680: 
   681:       return {
   682:         ok: true,
   683:         created: false,
   684:         updated: true,
   685:         existing: true,
   686:         protected: false,
   687:         id,
   688:       };
   689:     }
   690:   );
   691: }
   692: 
   693: function mapTop3ToPredictions(top = []) {
   694:   return (Array.isArray(top) ? top : [])
   695:     .slice(0, 3)
   696:     .map((item, index) => {
   697:       const grupoNumber = Number(item?.grupo);
   698:       const grupo = String(grupoNumber).padStart(2, "0");
   699: 
   700:       const probability = Number(
   701:         item?.scoreProb ??
   702:         item?.probability ??
   703:         item?.confidence ??
   704:         0
   705:       );
   706: 
   707:       const percentage =
   708:         probability > 0 && probability <= 1
   709:           ? probability * 100
   710:           : probability;
   711: 
   712:       return {
   713:         type: "grupo",
   714:         grupo,
   715:         animal:
   716:           item?.animal ||
   717:           ANIMALS[grupo] ||
   718:           null,
   719:         score: Number(
   720:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
   721:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
   722:             : 0
   723:         ),
   724:         confidence: Number(
   725:           Number.isFinite(percentage)
   726:             ? percentage.toFixed(4)
   727:             : 0
   728:         ),
   729:         reasons: Array.isArray(item?.reasons)
   730:           ? item.reasons.filter(Boolean)
   731:           : [],
   732:         signals: {
   733:           engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
   734:           rankPosition: index + 1,
   735:           scoreProb: Number(item?.scoreProb || 0),
   736:           rawScore: Number(item?.score || 0),
   737:           meta: item?.meta || null,
   738:         },
   739:       };
   740:     })
   741:     .filter((item) => /^\d{2}$/.test(item.grupo));
   742: }
   743: 
   744: async function createTop3PredictionRun(
   745:   input = {},
   746:   dependencies = {}
   747: ) {
   748:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   749:     input.lotteryKey
   750:   );
   751: 
   752:   const date = normalizeYmd(input.date);
   753:   const closeHour = normalizeHour(input.closeHour);
   754: 
   755:   const computeTop3 =
   756:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
   757:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
   758: 
   759:   const persistRun =
   760:     dependencies.persistRun ||
   761:     createPredictionRun;
   762: 
   763:   const publicApi =
   764:     dependencies.publicApi ||
   765:     loadTop3PublicApi();
   766: 
   767:   const historyLoad = await loadPredictionHistory({
   768:     lotteryKey,
   769:     date,
   770:     input,
   771:     dependencies,
   772:   });
   773: 
   774:   const allDraws = extractDraws(
   775:     historyLoad.draws
   776:   );
   777: 
   778:   const {
   779:     source: historySource,
   780:     metadata: historyMetadata,
   781:     lookbackDays,
   782:     maxDraws,
   783:     startYmd,
   784:   } = historyLoad;
   785: 
   786:   if (!allDraws.length) {
   787:     throw new Error(
   788:       `Nenhum resultado encontrado para ${lotteryKey}.`
   789:     );
   790:   }
   791: 
   792:   const targetKey = dateHourKey(date, closeHour);
   793: 
   794:   const history = allDraws
   795:     .filter((draw) => {
   796:       const ymd = publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
   797:       const hour = publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
   798: 
   799:       if (!ymd || !hour) {
   800:         return false;
   801:       }
   802: 
   803:       return dateHourKey(ymd, hour) < targetKey;
   804:     })
   805:     .sort((a, b) => {
   806:       const aKey = dateHourKey(
   807:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(a),
   808:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(a)
   809:       );
   810: 
   811:       const bKey = dateHourKey(
   812:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(b),
   813:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(b)
   814:       );
   815: 
   816:       return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
   817:     });
   818: 
   819:   if (!history.length) {
   820:     throw new Error(
   821:       "Não existe histórico anterior ao horário solicitado."
   822:     );
   823:   }
   824: 
   825:   const drawLast = history[history.length - 1];
   826: 
   827:   const drawsToday = history.filter(
   828:     (draw) => publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw) === date
   829:   );
   830: 
   831:   const computed = computeTop3({
   832:     lotteryKey,
   833:     drawsRange: history,
   834:     drawLast,
   835:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   836:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   837:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   838:     topN: 3,
   839:     targetYmdOverride: date,
   840:     targetHourOverride: closeHour,
   841:   });
   842: 
   843:   const predictions = mapTop3ToPredictions(
   844:     computed?.top
   845:   );
   846: 
   847:   const publicSnapshot =
   848:     buildTop3PublicSnapshot({
   849:       computedTop: computed?.top,
   850:       history,
   851:       lotteryKey,
   852:       date,
   853:       closeHour,
   854:       publicApi,
   855:     });
   856: 
   857:   if (!predictions.length) {
   858:     throw new Error(
   859:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu previsões válidas."
   860:     );
   861:   }
   862: 
   863:   if (
   864:     publicSnapshot.length !== 3 ||
   865:     publicSnapshot.some(
   866:       (item) =>
   867:         !Array.isArray(item?.milhares20) ||
   868:         item.milhares20.length !== 20
   869:     )
   870:   ) {
   871:     throw new Error(
   872:       "O motor TOP3 não produziu 20 milhares válidas para cada grupo."
   873:     );
   874:   }
   875: 
   876:   const metadata = {
   877:     ...(input.metadata || {}),
   878:     engine: "top3_statistical_v3",
   879:     historyDraws: history.length,
   880:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
   881:     historySource,
   882:     historyBootstrapStatus:
   883:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
   884:     historyTotalStored:
   885:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
   886:     lookbackDays,
   887:     maxDraws,
   888:     startYmd,
   889:     lastDrawYmd:
   890:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || null,
   891:     lastDrawHour:
   892:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(drawLast) || null,
   893:     targetYmd: date,
   894:     targetHour: closeHour,
   895:     engineMeta: computed?.meta || null,
   896:   };
   897: 
   898:   const engine = {
   899:     name: "top3_statistical_v3",
   900:     historyDraws: history.length,
   901:     drawsToday: drawsToday.length,
   902:     historySource,
   903:     historyBootstrapStatus:
   904:       historyMetadata?.bootstrapStatus || null,
   905:     historyTotalStored:
   906:       Number(historyMetadata?.totalDraws || 0) || null,
   907:     lookbackDays,
   908:     maxDraws,
   909:     startYmd,
   910:     targetYmd: date,
   911:     targetHour: closeHour,
   912:     meta: computed?.meta || null,
   913:   };
   914: 
   915:   if (input.dryRun === true) {
   916:     return {
   917:       run: null,
   918:       predictions,
   919:       publicSnapshot,
   920:       engine,
   921:       dryRun: true,
   922:     };
   923:   }
   924: 
   925:   const source =
   926:     input.source || "backend-top3";
   927: 
   928:   const result = await persistRun({
   929:     lotteryKey,
   930:     date,
   931:     closeHour,
   932:     source,
   933:     algorithm: "top3_statistical_v3",
   934:     metadata,
   935:     predictions,
   936:   });
   937: 
   938:   const publicProjection =
   939:     await saveTop3PublicProjection({
   940:       lotteryKey,
   941:       date,
   942:       closeHour,
   943:       snapshot: publicSnapshot,
   944:       engineVersion: "V3_STATISTICAL",
   945:       source,
   946:     });
   947: 
   948:   return {
   949:     ...result,
   950:     publicSnapshot,
   951:     publicProjection,
   952:     engine,
   953:     dryRun: false,
   954:   };
   955: }
   956: 
   957: module.exports = {
   958:   createTop3PredictionRun,
   959:   mapTop3ToPredictions,
   960:   normalizeHour,
   961:   normalizeHistorySource,
   962:   loadPredictionHistory,
   963:   buildPublicMilharesCols,
   964:   buildTop3PublicSnapshot,
   965:   saveTop3PublicProjection,
   966:   resolveNextTop3Slot,
   967: };

    17:   "09:00",
    18:   "11:00",
    19:   "14:00",
    20:   "16:00",
    21:   "18:00",
    22:   "21:00",
    23: ];
    24: 
    25: const PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT = [
    26:   "09:00",
    27:   "11:00",
    28:   "14:00",
    29:   "16:00",
    30:   "18:00",
    31:   "21:00",

    35:   "19:00",
    36:   "20:00",
