    38: 
    39: function safeArray(value) {
    40:   return Array.isArray(value)
    41:     ? value
    42:     : [];
    43: }
    44: 
    45: function normalizeLotteryKey(value) {
    46:   return String(value || "PT_RIO")
    47:     .trim()
    48:     .toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
    49: }
    50: 
    51: function normalizeHour(value) {
    52:   const raw = String(value || "")
    53:     .trim()
    54:     .replace("h", ":00");
    55: 
    56:   if (/^\d{1,2}$/.test(raw)) {
    57:     return `${raw.padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
    58:   }
    59: 
    60:   if (/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw)) {
    61:     const [hour, minute] = raw.split(":");
    62: 
    63:     return `${hour.padStart(2, "0")}:${minute}`;
    64:   }
    65: 
    66:   return null;
    67: }
    68: 
    69: function dateHourKey(ymd, hour) {
    70:   return `${ymd}T${normalizeHour(hour) || hour}`;
    71: }
    72: 
    73: function parseIntegerFlag(
    74:   args,
    75:   name,
    76:   fallback = null
    77: ) {
    78:   const prefix = `--${name}=`;
    79: 
    80:   const match = args.find(
    81:     (value) =>
    82:       String(value).startsWith(prefix)
    83:   );
    84: 
    85:   if (!match) {
    86:     return fallback;
    87:   }
    88: 
    89:   const number = Number(
    90:     String(match).slice(prefix.length)
    91:   );
    92: 
    93:   if (
    94:     !Number.isInteger(number) ||
    95:     number < 1
    96:   ) {
    97:     throw new Error(
    98:       `Parâmetro --${name} inválido.`
    99:     );
   100:   }
   101: 
   102:   return number;
   103: }
   104: 
   105: function parseBooleanFlag(
   106:   args,
   107:   name,
   108:   fallback = false
   109: ) {
   110:   const exact = `--${name}`;
   111:   const prefix = `--${name}=`;
   112: 
   113:   const match = args.find(
   114:     (value) => {
   115:       const text = String(value);
   116: 
   117:       return (
   118:         text === exact ||
   119:         text.startsWith(prefix)
   120:       );
   121:     }
   122:   );
   123: 
   124:   if (!match) {
   125:     return fallback;
   126:   }
   127: 
   128:   if (String(match) === exact) {
   129:     return true;
   130:   }
   131: 
   132:   const raw = String(match)
   133:     .slice(prefix.length)
   134:     .trim()
   135:     .toLowerCase();
   136: 
   137:   if (
   138:     raw === "1" ||
   139:     raw === "true" ||
   140:     raw === "yes" ||
   141:     raw === "on"
   142:   ) {
   143:     return true;
   144:   }
   145: 
   146:   if (
   147:     raw === "0" ||
   148:     raw === "false" ||
   149:     raw === "no" ||
   150:     raw === "off"
   151:   ) {
   152:     return false;
   153:   }
   154: 
   155:   throw new Error(
   156:     `Parâmetro --${name} inválido.`
   157:   );
   158: }
   159: 
   160: 
   161: function parseStringFlag(
   162:   args,
   163:   name,
   164:   fallback = null
   165: ) {
   166:   const prefix = `--${name}=`;
   167: 
   168:   const match = args.find(
   169:     (value) =>
   170:       String(value).startsWith(prefix)
   171:   );
   172: 
   173:   if (!match) {
   174:     return fallback;
   175:   }
   176: 
   177:   const value = String(match)
   178:     .slice(prefix.length)
   179:     .trim();
   180: 
   181:   return value || fallback;
   182: }
   183: 
   184: function parseCliArgs(argv = []) {
   185:   const lotteryKey = normalizeLotteryKey(
   186:     argv[0] || "PT_RIO"
   187:   );
   188: 
   189:   const flags = argv.slice(1);
   190: 
   191:   return {
   192:     lotteryKey,
   193:     limit: parseIntegerFlag(
   194:       flags,
   195:       "limit",
   196:       null
   197:     ),
   198:     minHistory: parseIntegerFlag(
   199:       flags,
   200:       "min-history",
   201:       100
   202:     ),
   203:     from: parseStringFlag(
   204:       flags,
   205:       "from",
   206:       null
   207:     ),
   208:     to: parseStringFlag(
   209:       flags,
   210:       "to",
   211:       null
   212:     ),
   213:     outputDir: parseStringFlag(
   214:       flags,
   215:       "output-dir",
   216:       "tmp"
   217:     ),
   218:     telemetry: parseBooleanFlag(
   219:       flags,
   220:       "telemetry",
   221:       false
   222:     ),
   223:   };
   224: }
   225: 
   226: function sortDraws(draws = [], publicApi) {
   227:   return [...safeArray(draws)]
   228:     .filter(Boolean)
   229:     .sort((a, b) => {
   230:       const aYmd =
   231:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(a) || "";
   232: 
   233:       const bYmd =
   234:         publicApi.pickDrawYMD(b) || "";
   235: 
   236:       const aHour =
   237:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(a) || "";
   238: 
   239:       const bHour =
   240:         publicApi.pickDrawHour(b) || "";
   241: 
   242:       const aId = String(
   243:         a.drawId || a.id || ""
   244:       );
   245: 
   246:       const bId = String(
   247:         b.drawId || b.id || ""
   248:       );
   249: 
   250:       return dateHourKey(
   251:         aYmd,
   252:         aHour
   253:       ).localeCompare(
   254:         dateHourKey(
   255:           bYmd,
   256:           bHour
   257:         )
   258:       ) || aId.localeCompare(bId);
   259:     });
   260: }
   261: 
   262: function getFirstPrizeGroup(
   263:   draw,
   264:   publicApi
   265: ) {
   266:   const value =
   267:     publicApi.pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(
   268:       draw
   269:     );
   270: 
   271:   const group = Number(value);
   272: 
   273:   if (
   274:     !Number.isFinite(group) ||
   275:     group < 1 ||
   276:     group > 25
   277:   ) {
   278:     return null;
   279:   }
   280: 
   281:   return group;
   282: }
   283: 
   284: function fallbackPrizePosition(prize) {
   285:   const candidates = [
   286:     prize?.position,
   287:     prize?.posicao,
   288:     prize?.pos,
   289:     prize?.colocacao,
   290:   ];
   291: 
   292:   for (const value of candidates) {
   293:     const position = Number(value);
   294: 
   295:     if (Number.isFinite(position)) {
   296:       return position;
   297:     }
   298:   }
   299: 
   300:   return null;
   301: }
   302: 
   303: function fallbackPrizeGroup(prize) {
   304:   const directCandidates = [
   305:     prize?.grupo2,
   306:     prize?.group2,
   307:     prize?.grupo,
   308:     prize?.group,
   309:     prize?.animal_grupo,
   310:     prize?.grupo_animal,
   311:     prize?.grupoAnimal,
   312:     prize?.g,
   313:   ];
   314: 
   315:   for (const value of directCandidates) {
   316:     const group = Number(value);
   317: 
   318:     if (
   319:       Number.isFinite(group) &&
   320:       group >= 1 &&
   321:       group <= 25
   322:     ) {
   323:       return group;
   324:     }
   325:   }
   326: 
   327:   const milharCandidates = [
   328:     prize?.milhar,
   329:     prize?.milhar4,
   330:     prize?.numero,
   331:     prize?.number,
   332:     prize?.value,
   333:     prize?.result,
   334:     prize?.resultado,
   335:     prize?.premio,
   336:   ];
   337: 
   338:   for (const value of milharCandidates) {
   339:     const digits = String(value ?? "")
   340:       .replace(/\D+/g, "");
   341: 
   342:     if (!digits) {
   343:       continue;
   344:     }
   345: 
   346:     const dezenaRaw = Number(
   347:       digits.padStart(2, "0").slice(-2)
   348:     );
   349: 
   350:     if (
   351:       !Number.isFinite(dezenaRaw) ||
   352:       dezenaRaw < 0 ||
   353:       dezenaRaw > 99
   354:     ) {
   355:       continue;
   356:     }
   357: 
   358:     if (dezenaRaw === 0) {
   359:       return 25;
   360:     }
   361: 
   362:     const group = Math.ceil(
   363:       dezenaRaw / 4
   364:     );
   365: 
   366:     if (
   367:       group >= 1 &&
   368:       group <= 25
   369:     ) {
   370:       return group;
   371:     }
   372:   }
   373: 
   374:   return null;
   375: }
   376: 
   377: function getPrizeGroupsByPosition(
   378:   draw,
   379:   publicApi,
   380:   maxPosition = 3
   381: ) {
   382:   const limit = Math.max(
   383:     1,
   384:     Number(maxPosition || 3)
   385:   );
   386: 
   387:   const prizes = safeArray(
   388:     draw?.prizes
   389:   );
   390: 
   391:   const guessPosition =
   392:     typeof publicApi?.guessPrizePos ===
   393:     "function"
   394:       ? publicApi.guessPrizePos
   395:       : fallbackPrizePosition;
   396: 
   397:   const guessGroup =
   398:     typeof publicApi?.guessPrizeGrupo ===
   399:     "function"
   400:       ? publicApi.guessPrizeGrupo
   401:       : fallbackPrizeGroup;
   402: 
   403:   const result = Array.from(
   404:     {
   405:       length: limit,
   406:     },
   407:     () => null
   408:   );
   409: 
   410:   for (const prize of prizes) {
   411:     const position = Number(
   412:       guessPosition(prize)
   413:     );
   414: 
   415:     if (
   416:       !Number.isFinite(position) ||
   417:       position < 1 ||
   418:       position > limit
   419:     ) {
   420:       continue;
   421:     }
   422: 
   423:     const group = Number(
   424:       guessGroup(prize)
   425:     );
   426: 
   427:     if (
   428:       !Number.isFinite(group) ||
   429:       group < 1 ||
   430:       group > 25
   431:     ) {
   432:       continue;
   433:     }
   434: 
   435:     result[position - 1] = group;
   436:   }
   437: 
   438:   if (
   439:     !Number.isFinite(
   440:       Number(result[0])
   441:     )
   442:   ) {
   443:     result[0] = getFirstPrizeGroup(
   444:       draw,
   445:       publicApi
   446:     );
   447:   }
   448: 
   449:   return result;
   450: }
   451: 
   452: function getPredictionGroups(
   453:   computed
   454: ) {
   455:   const candidates =
   456:     safeArray(computed?.top).length
   457:       ? safeArray(computed.top)
   458:       : safeArray(computed?.ranking);
   459: 
   460:   return candidates
   461:     .map((item) =>
   462:       Number(
   463:         item?.grupo ??
   464:         item?.group
   465:       )
   466:     )
   467:     .filter(
   468:       (group) =>
   469:         Number.isFinite(group) &&
   470:         group >= 1 &&
   471:         group <= 25
   472:     )
   473:     .slice(0, 3);
   474: }
   475: 
   476: function ensureBucket(
   477:   map,
   478:   key
   479: ) {
   480:   if (!map[key]) {
   481:     map[key] = {
   482:       evaluated: 0,
   483: 
   484:       top1Hits: 0,
   485:       top3Hits: 0,
   486: 
   487:       prize1Hits: 0,
   488:       prize2Hits: 0,
   489:       prize3Hits: 0,
   490: 
   491:       top3PrizeHits: 0,
   492: 
   493:       matchedPrizePositions: 0,
   494:       matchedPredictions: 0,
   495: 
   496:       errors: 0,
   497:     };
   498:   }
   499: 
   500:   return map[key];
   501: }
   502: 
   503: function finalizeBucket(bucket = {}) {
   504:   const evaluated = Number(
   505:     bucket.evaluated || 0
   506:   );
   507: 
   508:   const top1Hits = Number(
   509:     bucket.top1Hits || 0
   510:   );
   511: 
   512:   const top3Hits = Number(
   513:     bucket.top3Hits || 0
   514:   );
   515: 
   516:   const prize1Hits = Number(
   517:     bucket.prize1Hits || 0
   518:   );
   519: 
   520:   const prize2Hits = Number(
   521:     bucket.prize2Hits || 0
   522:   );
   523: 
   524:   const prize3Hits = Number(
   525:     bucket.prize3Hits || 0
   526:   );
   527: 
   528:   const top3PrizeHits = Number(
   529:     bucket.top3PrizeHits || 0
   530:   );
   531: 
   532:   const matchedPrizePositions = Number(
   533:     bucket.matchedPrizePositions || 0
   534:   );
   535: 
   536:   const matchedPredictions = Number(
   537:     bucket.matchedPredictions || 0
   538:   );
   539: 
   540:   const rate = (hits) =>
   541:     evaluated > 0
   542:       ? Number(
   543:           (
   544:             Number(hits || 0) /
   545:             evaluated *
   546:             100
   547:           ).toFixed(4)
   548:         )
   549:       : 0;
   550: 
   551:   return {
   552:     evaluated,
   553: 
   554:     top1Hits,
   555:     top3Hits,
   556: 
   557:     prize1Hits,
   558:     prize2Hits,
   559:     prize3Hits,
   560: 
   561:     top3PrizeHits,
   562: 
   563:     matchedPrizePositions,
   564:     matchedPredictions,
   565: 
   566:     errors: Number(
   567:       bucket.errors || 0
   568:     ),
   569: 
   570:     top1Rate:
   571:       rate(top1Hits),
   572: 
   573:     top3Rate:
   574:       rate(top3Hits),
   575: 
   576:     prize1Rate:
   577:       rate(prize1Hits),
   578: 
   579:     prize2Rate:
   580:       rate(prize2Hits),
   581: 
   582:     prize3Rate:
   583:       rate(prize3Hits),
   584: 
   585:     top3PrizeRate:
   586:       rate(top3PrizeHits),
   587: 
   588:     averageMatchedPrizePositions:
   589:       evaluated > 0
   590:         ? Number(
   591:             (
   592:               matchedPrizePositions /
   593:               evaluated
   594:             ).toFixed(4)
   595:           )
   596:         : 0,
   597: 
   598:     averageMatchedPredictions:
   599:       evaluated > 0
   600:         ? Number(
   601:             (
   602:               matchedPredictions /
   603:               evaluated
   604:             ).toFixed(4)
   605:           )
   606:         : 0,
   607:   };
   608: }
   609: 
   610: function finalizeMap(map = {}) {
   611:   const OFFICIAL_BASELINE = {
   612:   baselineVersion: "TOP3_STATISTICAL_V3_V1",
   613:   baselineDescription: "Primeira versão oficial do motor estatístico.",
   614:   baselineMetric: "TOP3_PRIZE_ANY_ORDER",
   615:   baselineRate: 31.7411,
   616:   baselineCases: 7432,
   617:   baselineValidatedAt: "2026-07-23"
   618: };
   619: 
   620: const result = {};
   621: 
   622:   for (
   623:     const [key, bucket]
   624:     of Object.entries(map)
   625:   ) {
   626:     result[key] = finalizeBucket(
   627:       bucket
   628:     );
   629:   }
   630: 
   631:   return result;
   632: }
   633: 
   634: function formatPercent(value) {
   635:   return `${Number(value || 0).toFixed(2)}%`;
   636: }
   637: 
   638: function buildTextReport(result) {
   639:   const lines = [];
   640: 
   641:   lines.push(
   642:     "=============================================="
   643:   );
   644: 
   645:   lines.push(
   646:     "TOP3 OFFICIAL BACKTEST"
   647:   );
   648: 
   649:   lines.push(
   650:     "=============================================="
   651:   );
   652: 
   653:   lines.push("");
   654: 
   655:   lines.push(
   656:     `Loteria.............: ${result.lotteryKey}`
   657:   );
   658: 
   659:   lines.push(
   660:     `Engine..............: ${result.engine}`
   661:   );
   662: 
   663:   lines.push(
   664:     `Histórico carregado.: ${result.historyLoaded}`
   665:   );
   666: 
   667:   lines.push(
   668:     `Casos elegíveis.....: ${result.eligibleCases}`
   669:   );
   670: 
   671:   lines.push(
   672:     `Casos avaliados.....: ${result.global.evaluated}`
   673:   );
   674: 
   675:   lines.push(
   676:     `Casos ignorados.....: ${result.skipped}`
   677:   );
   678: 
   679:   lines.push(
   680:     `Erros...............: ${result.global.errors}`
   681:   );
   682: 
   683:   lines.push(
   684:     `Período histórico...: ${result.historyPeriod.from} até ${result.historyPeriod.to}`
   685:   );
   686: 
   687:   lines.push(
   688:     `Período avaliado....: ${result.evaluationPeriod.from || "-"} até ${result.evaluationPeriod.to || "-"}`
   689:   );
   690: 
   691:   lines.push(
   692:     `TOP1................: ${result.global.top1Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.top1Rate)})`
   693:   );
   694: 
   695:   lines.push(
   696:     `TOP3 no 1º prêmio...: ${result.global.top3Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.top3Rate)})`
   697:   );
   698: 
   699:   lines.push(
   700:     `Acerto no 1º prêmio.: ${result.global.prize1Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.prize1Rate)})`
   701:   );
   702: 
   703:   lines.push(
   704:     `Acerto no 2º prêmio.: ${result.global.prize2Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.prize2Rate)})`
   705:   );
   706: 
   707:   lines.push(
   708:     `Acerto no 3º prêmio.: ${result.global.prize3Hits} (${formatPercent(result.global.prize3Rate)})`
   709:   );
   710: 
   711:   lines.push(
   712:     `Algum acerto 1º-3º..: ${result.global.top3PrizeHits} (${formatPercent(result.global.top3PrizeRate)})`
   713:   );
   714: 
   715:   lines.push(
   716:     `Posições atingidas..: ${result.global.matchedPrizePositions}`
   717:   );
   718: 
   719:   lines.push(
   720:     `Média posições/caso.: ${Number(result.global.averageMatchedPrizePositions || 0).toFixed(4)}`
   721:   );
   722: 
   723:   lines.push(
   724:     `Palpites atingidos..: ${result.global.matchedPredictions}`
   725:   );
   726: 
   727:   lines.push(
   728:     `Média palpites/caso.: ${Number(result.global.averageMatchedPredictions || 0).toFixed(4)}`
   729:   );
   730: 
   731:   lines.push(
   732:     `Tempo total.........: ${result.tookMs} ms`
   733:   );
   734: 
   735:   lines.push("");
   736:   lines.push("POR HORÁRIO");
   737: 
   738:   for (
   739:     const [hour, bucket]
   740:     of Object.entries(result.byHour)
   741:   ) {
   742:     lines.push(
   743:       `${hour.padEnd(8)} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | 1º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize1Rate).padStart(8)} | 2º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize2Rate).padStart(8)} | 3º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize3Rate).padStart(8)} | QUALQUER=${formatPercent(bucket.top3PrizeRate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
   744:     );
   745:   }
   746: 
   747:   lines.push("");
   748:   lines.push("POR DIA DA SEMANA");
   749: 
   750:   for (
   751:     const [weekday, bucket]
   752:     of Object.entries(result.byWeekday)
   753:   ) {
   754:     lines.push(
   755:       `${weekday.padEnd(3)} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | 1º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize1Rate).padStart(8)} | 2º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize2Rate).padStart(8)} | 3º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize3Rate).padStart(8)} | QUALQUER=${formatPercent(bucket.top3PrizeRate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
   756:     );
   757:   }
   758: 
   759:   lines.push("");
   760:   lines.push("POR MÊS");
   761: 
   762:   for (
   763:     const [month, bucket]
   764:     of Object.entries(result.byMonth)
   765:   ) {
   766:     lines.push(
   767:       `${month} avaliados=${String(bucket.evaluated).padStart(5)} | TOP1=${formatPercent(bucket.top1Rate).padStart(8)} | 1º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize1Rate).padStart(8)} | 2º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize2Rate).padStart(8)} | 3º=${formatPercent(bucket.prize3Rate).padStart(8)} | QUALQUER=${formatPercent(bucket.top3PrizeRate).padStart(8)} | erros=${bucket.errors}`
   768:     );
   769:   }
   770: 
   771:   return lines.join("\n");
   772: }
   773: 
   774: async function runOfficialBacktest(
   775:   options = {},
   776:   dependencies = {}
   777: ) {
   778:   const startedAt = Date.now();
   779: 
   780:   const lotteryKey =
   781:     normalizeLotteryKey(
   782:       options.lotteryKey ||
   783:       "PT_RIO"
   784:     );
   785: 
   786:   const publicApi =
   787:     dependencies.publicApi ||
   788:     loadTop3PublicApi();
   789: 
   790:   const loadHistory =
   791:     dependencies.readFullHistory ||
   792:     readFullHistory;
   793: 
   794:   const loadMetadata =
   795:     dependencies.readMetadata ||
   796:     readMetadata;
   797: 
   798:   const computeTop3 =
   799:     dependencies.computeTop3 ||
   800:     computeStatisticalTop3V3;
   801: 
   802:   const history = sortDraws(
   803:     await loadHistory(
   804:       lotteryKey,
   805:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   806:     ),
   807:     publicApi
   808:   );
   809: 
   810:   if (!history.length) {
   811:     throw new Error(
   812:       "Histórico TOP3 vazio."
   813:     );
   814:   }
   815: 
   816:   const metadataResult =
   817:     await loadMetadata(
   818:       lotteryKey,
   819:       dependencies.historyDependencies || {}
   820:     );
   821: 
   822:   const metadata =
   823:     metadataResult?.data || null;
   824: 
   825:   if (
   826:     dependencies.allowIncompleteSnapshot !== true &&
   827:     (
   828:       metadataResult?.exists !== true ||
   829:       metadata?.bootstrapStatus !==
   830:         "complete"
   831:     )
   832:   ) {
   833:     throw new Error(
   834:       "Histórico TOP3 não está marcado como completo."
   835:     );
   836:   }
   837: 
   838:   const minHistory = Number(
   839:     options.minHistory || 100
   840:   );
   841: 
   842:   const from =
   843:     options.from || null;
   844: 
   845:   const to =
   846:     options.to || null;
   847: 
   848:   const eligibleIndexes = [];
   849: 
   850:   for (
   851:     let index = minHistory;
   852:     index < history.length;
   853:     index += 1
   854:   ) {
   855:     const draw = history[index];
   856: 
   857:     const ymd =
   858:       publicApi.pickDrawYMD(draw);
   859: 
   860:     const hour =
   861:       publicApi.pickDrawHour(draw);
   862: 
   863:     if (!ymd || !hour) {
   864:       continue;
   865:     }
   866: 
   867:     if (from && ymd < from) {
   868:       continue;
   869:     }
   870: 
   871:     if (to && ymd > to) {
   872:       continue;
   873:     }
   874: 
   875:     if (
   876:       getFirstPrizeGroup(
   877:         draw,
   878:         publicApi
   879:       ) == null
   880:     ) {
   881:       continue;
   882:     }
   883: 
   884:     eligibleIndexes.push(index);
   885:   }
   886: 
   887:   const selectedIndexes =
   888:     options.limit
   889:       ? eligibleIndexes.slice(
   890:           0,
   891:           Number(options.limit)
   892:         )
   893:       : eligibleIndexes;
   894: 
   895:   const globalBucket = {
   896:     evaluated: 0,
   897: 
   898:     top1Hits: 0,
   899:     top3Hits: 0,
   900: 
   901:     prize1Hits: 0,
   902:     prize2Hits: 0,
   903:     prize3Hits: 0,
   904: 
   905:     top3PrizeHits: 0,
   906: 
   907:     matchedPrizePositions: 0,
   908:     matchedPredictions: 0,
   909: 
   910:     errors: 0,
   911:   };
   912: 
   913:   const byHour = {};
   914:   const byWeekday = {};
   915:   const byMonth = {};
   916: 
   917:   const telemetryCases = [];
   918: 
   919:   let skipped = 0;
   920:   let evaluationFrom = null;
   921:   let evaluationTo = null;
   922: 
   923:   for (
   924:     let position = 0;
   925:     position < selectedIndexes.length;
   926:     position += 1
   927:   ) {
   928:     const index =
   929:       selectedIndexes[position];
   930: 
   931:     const targetDraw =
   932:       history[index];

     1: "use strict";

     3: /**
     4:  * importKingApostas.js
     5:  * (topo limpo automaticamente — removido bloco corrompido com \n e \)
     6:  */
     7: const fs = require("fs");
     8: const path = require("path");
     9: const axios = require("axios");
    10: const {
    11:   normalizeFederalRequestedSlot,
    12:   normalizeFederalSourceSlot,
    13: } = require("./federalCalendar");
    14: 
    15: /**
    16:  * =========================
    17:  * ENV helpers
    18:  * =========================
    19:  */
    20: 
    21: /**
    22:  * Carrega variáveis do backend/.env.local (se existir)
    23:  * Observação: só define se a env ainda não estiver definida no processo.
    24:  */
    25: (function loadEnvLocal() {
    26:   try {
    27:     const envPath = path.join(__dirname, "..", ".env.local");
    28:     if (!fs.existsSync(envPath)) return;
    29: 
    30:     let raw = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
    31:     raw = raw.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ""); // remove BOM invisível
    32: 
    33:     raw.split(/\r?\n/).forEach((line) => {
    34:       let s = String(line || "").trim();
    35:       if (!s || s.startsWith("#")) return;
    36: 
    37:       // suporta "export KEY=VAL"
    38:       if (/^export\s+/i.test(s)) s = s.replace(/^export\s+/i, "").trim();
    39: 
    40:       const i = s.indexOf("=");
    41:       if (i <= 0) return;
    42: 
    43:       const k = s.slice(0, i).trim();
    44:       let v = s.slice(i + 1).trim();
    45: 
    46:       // suporta KEY="valor" / KEY='valor'
    47:       if (
    48:         (v.startsWith('"') && v.endsWith('"')) ||
    49:         (v.startsWith("'") && v.endsWith("'"))
    50:       ) {
    51:         v = v.slice(1, -1);
    52:       }
    53: 
    54:       if (k && v && !process.env[k]) process.env[k] = v;
    55:     });
    56:   } catch {
    57:     // silencioso por design
    58:   }
    59: })();
    60: 
    61: /**
    62:  * Resolve automaticamente credenciais se:
    63:  * - GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS estiver ausente, OU
    64:  * - estiver apontando para um arquivo inexistente.
    65:  *
    66:  * Mantém compatível com seu fluxo local (Windows) e com Cloud (ADC).
    67:  */
    68: (function ensureGoogleCredentials() {
    69:   try {
    70:     const current = String(process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS || "").trim();
    71: 
    72:     // se já está OK, não mexe
    73:     if (current) {
    74:       try {
    75:         if (fs.existsSync(current) && fs.lstatSync(current).isFile()) return;
    76:       } catch {
    77:         // segue para fallback
    78:       }
    79:     }
    80: 
    81:     // se existe mas está inválida, remove para permitir fallback (ADC)
    82:     if (current) {
    83:       try {
    84:         delete process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS;
    85:       } catch {
    86:         process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = "";
    87:       }
    88:     }
    89: 
    90:     const tryPickFromDir = (dir) => {
    91:       try {
    92:         if (!dir || !fs.existsSync(dir)) return null;
    93: 
    94:         const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    95:         const hits = entries
    96:           .filter((f) => /^palpitacojb-app-firebase-adminsdk-.*\.json$/i.test(f))
    97:           .map((f) => path.join(dir, f))
    98:           .filter((p) => {
    99:             try {
   100:               return fs.existsSync(p) && fs.lstatSync(p).isFile();
   101:             } catch {
   102:               return false;
   103:             }
   104:           });
   105: 
   106:         if (!hits.length) return null;
   107: 
   108:         hits.sort((a, b) => {
   109:           try {
   110:             return fs.statSync(b).mtimeMs - fs.statSync(a).mtimeMs;
   111:           } catch {
   112:             return 0;
   113:           }
   114:         });
   115: 
   116:         return hits[0] || null;
   117:       } catch {
   118:         return null;
   119:       }
   120:     };
   121: 
   122:     // 1) tenta no CWD
   123:     const cwdPick = tryPickFromDir(process.cwd());
   124:     if (cwdPick) {
   125:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = cwdPick;
   126:       return;
   127:     }
   128: 
   129:     // 2) tenta subir alguns níveis
   130:     const up1 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", ".."); // .../archive_backend
   131:     const up2 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", "..", ".."); // .../palpitaco (provável)
   132:     const pick1 = tryPickFromDir(up2) || tryPickFromDir(up1);
   133: 
   134:     if (pick1) {
   135:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = pick1;
   136:       return;
   137:     }
   138:   } catch {
   139:     // silencioso por design
   140:   }
   141: })();
   142: 
   143: /**
   144:  * =========================
   145:  * Firebase Admin (central)
   146:  * =========================
   147:  *
   148:  * Importante:
   149:  * este require vem DEPOIS do ensureGoogleCredentials/loadEnvLocal
   150:  */
   151: const { admin, db } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
   152: 
   153: const { getPtRioSlotsByDate } = require("./ptRioCalendar");
   154: 
   155: const {

   158:

   163: 
   164: const {
   165:   loadTop3PublicApi,
   166: } = require("../engine/scoreEngineUnified");
   167: 
   168: /**
   169:  * =========================
   170:  * Config / toggles
   171:  * =========================
   172:  */
   173: 
   174: /**
   175:  * Para desempenho/estabilidade:
   176:  * - CHECK_EXISTENCE=1 => faz reads extras para calcular "novos" (lento)
   177:  * - default => não faz reads extras (rápido)
   178:  */
   179: const CHECK_EXISTENCE = String(process.env.CHECK_EXISTENCE || "").trim() === "1";
   180: 
   181: /**
   182:  * Retry HTTP simples (evita falhas intermitentes/429)
   183:  */
   184: const HTTP_RETRIES = Number.isFinite(Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRIES))
   185:   ? Math.max(0, Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRIES))
   186:   : 3;
   187: 
   188: const HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS = Number.isFinite(Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS))
   189:   ? Math.max(50, Number(process.env.HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS))
   190:   : 600;
   191: 
   192: /**
   193:  * ✅ fetch por lottery (robusto contra “cap” da API quando manda muitos lotteries[]).
   194:  * Default: ON
   195:  * - KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY=0 => volta ao modo antigo (1 request com todos lotteries[])
   196:  */
   197: const FETCH_PER_LOTTERY =
   198:   String(process.env.KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY || "").trim() === "0" ? false : true;
   199: 
   200: // ✅ Debug do fetch (log por lotteryId)
   201: // Ative com: set KING_FETCH_DEBUG=1
   202: const KING_FETCH_DEBUG = String(process.env.KING_FETCH_DEBUG || "").trim() === "1";
   203: 
   204: // ✅ Debug leve do override FEDERAL
   205: // Ative com: set KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG=1
   206: const KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG = String(process.env.KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG || "").trim() === "1";
   207: 
   208: /**
   209:  * ✅ Blindagem contra datas futuras (não busca API, não escreve no FS)
   210:  * - ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE=1 => libera (use com MUITO cuidado / apenas para testes)
   211:  */
   212: const ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE = String(process.env.ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE || "").trim() === "1";
   213: 
   214: /**
   215:  * =========================
   216:  * ✅ Regras de negócio: UF x lottery_key
   217:  * =========================
   218:  *
   219:  * IMPORTANTE:
   220:  * - lottery_key identifica a loteria/fonte (ex.: PT_RIO)
   221:  * - uf deve ser o estado (ex.: RJ), pois o frontend consulta por UF ("RJ")
   222:  *
   223:  * Se uf ficar "PT_RIO", o bounds por UF quebra.
   224:  */
   225: const RJ_STATE_CODE = "RJ";
   226: const RJ_LOTTERY_KEY = "PT_RIO";
   227: 
   228: const FEDERAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
   229: const FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "FEDERAL";
   230: 
   231: const LOOK_STATE_CODE = "GO";
   232: const LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY = "LOOK";
   233: 
   234: const NACIONAL_STATE_CODE = "BR";
   235: const NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY = "NACIONAL";
   236: 
   237: function resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lotteryKey) {
   238:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   239:   if (!lk) return null;
   240:   if (lk === RJ_LOTTERY_KEY) return RJ_STATE_CODE;
   241:   if (lk === FEDERAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return FEDERAL_STATE_CODE;
   242:   if (lk === LOOK_LOTTERY_KEY) return LOOK_STATE_CODE;
   243:   if (lk === NACIONAL_LOTTERY_KEY) return NACIONAL_STATE_CODE;
   244: 
   245:   // se for uma UF padrão de 2 letras (SP, MG, DF etc.), preserva
   246:   if (/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(lk)) return lk;
   247: 
   248:   // fallback: não inventa UF
   249:   return null;
   250: }

   252: /**
   253:  * =========================
   254:  * Date helpers (BRT via TZ)
   255:  * =========================
   256:  */
   257: 
   258: function pad2(n) {
   259:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   260: }
   261: 
   262: function todayYMDLocal() {
   263:   try {
   264:     const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
   265:       timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo",
   266:       year: "numeric",
   267:       month: "2-digit",
   268:       day: "2-digit",
   269:     });
   270:     return fmt.format(new Date()); // YYYY-MM-DD
   271:   } catch {
   272:     const d = new Date();
   273:     return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   274:   }
   275: }
   276: 
   277: function isFutureISODate(ymd) {
   278:   // Compare lexicográfico funciona para YYYY-MM-DD
   279:   const t = todayYMDLocal();
   280:   return String(ymd || "").trim() > t;
   281: }

   289: /** Mapeamento oficial Grupo -> Animal */
   290: const GRUPO_TO_ANIMAL = {
   291:   1: "Avestruz",
   292:   2: "Águia",
   293:   3: "Burro",
   294:   4: "Borboleta",
   295:   5: "Cachorro",
   296:   6: "Cabra",
   297:   7: "Carneiro",
   298:   8: "Camelo",
   299:   9: "Cobra",
   300:   10: "Coelho",
   301:   11: "Cavalo",
   302:   12: "Elefante",
   303:   13: "Galo",
   304:   14: "Gato",
   305:   15: "Jacaré",
   306:   16: "Leão",
   307:   17: "Macaco",
   308:   18: "Porco",
   309:   19: "Pavão",
   310:   20: "Peru",
   311:   21: "Touro",
   312:   22: "Tigre",
   313:   23: "Urso",
   314:   24: "Veado",
   315:   25: "Vaca",
   316: };
   317: 
   318: function pad4(s) {
   319:   return String(s ?? "").trim().padStart(4, "0");
   320: }
   321: 
   322: /** Regra do Jogo do Bicho: dezena 00 => grupo 25; senão ceil(dezena/4) */
   323: function dezenaToGrupo(dezena2) {
   324:   const n = Number(dezena2);
   325:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
   326:   if (n === 0) return 25;
   327:   return Math.ceil(n / 4);
   328: }
   329: 
   330: function normalizePrize(raw) {
   331:   const milhar = pad4(raw);
   332:   const centena = milhar.slice(-3);
   333:   const dezena = milhar.slice(-2);
   334:   const grupo = dezenaToGrupo(dezena);
   335:   const animal = grupo ? GRUPO_TO_ANIMAL[grupo] : null;
   336:   return { raw: String(raw), milhar, centena, dezena, grupo, animal };
   337: }
   338: 
   339: function isISODate(s) {
   340:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   341: }
   342: 
   343: function isHHMM(s) {
   344:   return /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   345: }
   346: 
   347: /**
   348:  * Normaliza horário para HH:MM (raw)
   349:  * Aceita: "10", "10h", "10:9", "10:09"
   350:  */
   351: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
   352:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   353:   if (!s) return "";
   354: 
   355:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
   356: 
   357:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   358:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   359: 
   360:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   361:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   362: 
   363:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
   364:   if (m3) {
   365:     const hh = String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0");
   366:     const mm = String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0");
   367:     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
   368:   }
   369: 
   370:   return "";
   371: }
   372: 
   373: /**
   374:  * ✅ Normaliza close_hour para SLOT (campo de negócio)
   375:  * - Para PT_RIO: força minutos = 00 (09:09 -> 09:00)
   376:  * - Para outros: preserva raw
   377:  *
   378:  * Correção crítica:
   379:  * - A API do PT_RIO costuma devolver HH:09 como "marcação" e NÃO um minuto real.
   380:  * - Portanto, para PT_RIO, se raw terminar em ":09", tratamos como "sem minuto real"
   381:  *   e NÃO persistimos close_hour_raw (vira "").
   382:  *
   383:  * Retorna:
   384:  * - raw: HH:MM (normalizado) OU "" quando for "sintético" (PT_RIO HH:09)
   385:  * - slot: HH:MM (normalizado para o que o sistema deve usar em consultas/ID)
   386:  */
   387: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(value, lotteryKey) {
   388:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   389:   const raw0 = normalizeHHMM(value);
   390:   if (!raw0 || !isHHMM(raw0)) return { raw: "", slot: "" };
   391: 
   392:   // Federal: 11h e o novo slot oficial de domingo.
   393:   // A marcacao antecipada de 19h continua pertencendo ao slot 20h.
   394:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   395:     return {
   396:       raw: raw0,
   397:       slot: normalizeFederalRequestedSlot(raw0),
   398:     };
   399:   }
   400: 
   401:   // LOOK:
   402:   // a fonte pode publicar minutos variáveis, por exemplo 07:09, 09:09 e 11:12.
   403:   // Para o sistema, todos pertencem ao slot operacional da hora cheia.
   404:   // Preservamos o horário bruto em close_hour_raw e gravamos HH:00 em close_hour.
   405:   if (lk === "LOOK") {
   406:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
   407:     return { raw: raw0, slot: `${hh}:00` };
   408:   }
   409: 
   410:   if (lk === "PT_RIO") {
   411:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
   412:     const mm = raw0.slice(3, 5);
   413: 
   414:     // ✅ se vier HH:09, é marcação (não minuto real) -> não grava close_hour_raw
   415:     const raw = mm === "09" ? "" : raw0;
   416:     return { raw, slot: `${hh}:00` };
   417:   }
   418: 
   419:   return { raw: raw0, slot: raw0 };
   420: }
   421: 
   422: 
   423: /**
   424:  * NACIONAL:
   425:  * usa lottery_name para obter o horário oficial do sorteio.
   426:  */
   427: function normalizeDrawCloseHour(draw, lotteryKey) {
   428:   const base = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(
   429:     draw?.close_hour || "",
   430:     lotteryKey
   431:   );
   432: 
   433:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   434: 
   435:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   436:     return {
   437:       raw: base.raw,
   438:       slot: normalizeFederalSourceSlot({
   439:         date: draw?.date || draw?.ymd || "",
   440:         rawSlot: base.raw || base.slot,
   441:       }),
   442:     };
   443:   }
   444: 
   445:   if (lk !== "NACIONAL") {
   446:     return base;
   447:   }
   448: 
   449:   const lotteryName = String(
   450:     draw?.lottery_name ||
   451:     draw?.name ||
   452:     ""
   453:   );
   454: 
   455:   const m = lotteryName.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*HS/i);
   456: 
   457:   if (!m) {
   458:     return base;
   459:   }
   460: 
   461:   return {
   462:     raw: base.raw,
   463:     slot: `${String(m[1]).padStart(2,"0")}:00`
   464:   };
   465: }
   466: 
   467: 
   468: /**
   469:  * ✅ Normaliza o parâmetro closeHour (do CLI/scheduler) e evita log enganoso.
   470:  * - Para FEDERAL: qualquer coisa vira slot=20:00, mas mantemos "requested" para log.
   471:  */
   472: function normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lotteryKey) {
   473:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   474:   const requested = closeHour ? normalizeHHMM(closeHour) : null;
   475:   const requestedIsValid = requested ? isHHMM(requested) : false;
   476: 
   477:   if (!requested) {
   478:     return { requested: null, slot: null, note: null };
   479:   }
   480:   if (!requestedIsValid) {
   481:     return { requested, slot: null, note: "invalid" };
   482:   }
   483: 
   484:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
   485:     const slot =
   486:       normalizeFederalRequestedSlot(requested);
   487: 
   488:     const note =
   489:       requested !== slot
   490:         ? `FEDERAL_slot_normalized(${requested}->${slot})`
   491:         : null;
   492: 
   493:     return { requested, slot, note };
   494:   }
   495: 
   496:   // outros: normalização normal por lotteryKey (PT_RIO min->00 etc.)
   497:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(requested, lk).slot;
   498:   return { requested, slot: slot || null, note: null };
   499: }
   500: 
   501: /**
   502:  * Sanitiza string para docId do Firestore.
   503:  */
   504: function safeIdPart(input) {
   505:   const s = String(input ?? "").trim();
   506:   if (!s) return "NA";
   507:   return s
   508:     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
   509:     .replace(/[\/\\?#\[\]]/g, "_")
   510:     .replace(/:+/g, "-")
   511:     .slice(0, 80);
   512: }
   513: 
   514: function pickLotteryId(draw) {
   515:   const candidates = [
   516:     draw?.lottery_id,
   517:     draw?.lotteryId,
   518:     draw?.lottery_uuid,
   519:     draw?.lotteryUuid,
   520:     draw?.lottery,
   521:     draw?.lottery_key,
   522:   ];
   523:   for (const c of candidates) {
   524:     const s = String(c ?? "").trim();
   525:     if (s) return s;
   526:   }
   527:   return null;
   528: }

   574: (function applyLotteryOverridesFromEnv() {
   575:   try {
   576:     const rawRio = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_PT_RIO || "").trim();
   577:     if (rawRio) {
   578:       const arr = rawRio
   579:         .split(",")
   580:         .map((s) => String(s || "").trim())
   581:         .filter(Boolean);
   582:       if (arr.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.PT_RIO = arr;
   583:     }
   584: 
   585:     const rawFed = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_FEDERAL || "").trim();
   586:     if (rawFed) {
   587:       const arrF = rawFed
   588:         .split(",")
   589:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   590:         .filter(Boolean);
   591:       if (arrF.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.FEDERAL = arrF;
   592:     }
   593: 
   594:     const rawLook = String(process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_LOOK || "").trim();
   595:     if (rawLook) {
   596:       const arrLook = rawLook
   597:         .split(",")
   598:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   599:         .filter(Boolean);
   600:       if (arrLook.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.LOOK = arrLook;
   601:     }
   602: 
   603:     const rawNacional = String(
   604:       process.env.KING_LOTTERIES_NACIONAL || ""
   605:     ).trim();
   606:     if (rawNacional) {
   607:       const arrNacional = rawNacional
   608:         .split(",")
   609:         .map((x) => String(x || "").trim())
   610:         .filter(Boolean);
   611:       if (arrNacional.length) LOTTERIES_BY_KEY.NACIONAL = arrNacional;
   612:     }
   613: 
   614:     // debug leve (único, no lugar certo)
   615:     if (KING_OVERRIDE_DEBUG) {
   616:       const counts = Object.fromEntries(
   617:         Object.entries(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY).map(([key, value]) => [
   618:           key,
   619:           Array.isArray(value) ? value.length : 0,
   620:         ])
   621:       );
   622: 
   623:       console.log(
   624:         `[OVERRIDE] PT_RIO lotteries=${counts.PT_RIO} | ` +
   625:           `FEDERAL lotteries=${counts.FEDERAL} | ` +
   626:           `LOOK lotteries=${counts.LOOK} | ` +
   627:           `NACIONAL lotteries=${counts.NACIONAL}`
   628:       );
   629:     }
   630:   } catch {
   631:     // silencioso
   632:   }
   633: })();
   634: 
   635: function buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey, lotteryId = null }) {
   636:   const base = "https://app_services.apionline.cloud/api/results";
   637:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   638:   const lotteries = LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk];
   639: 
   640:   if (!lotteries?.length) {
   641:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY, lk)) {
   642:       throw new Error(
   643:         `${lk} sem lotteryIds. Defina KING_LOTTERIES_${lk}="uuid1,uuid2,...".`
   644:       );
   645:     }
   646: 
   647:     throw new Error(`lotteryKey inválida: ${lk}`);
   648:   }
   649: 
   650:   const params = new URLSearchParams();
   651:   params.append("dates[]", date);
   652: 
   653:   // ✅ se lotteryId for fornecido -> 1 request por lottery
   654:   if (lotteryId) {
   655:     params.append("lotteries[]", lotteryId);
   656:     return `${base}?${params.toString()}`;
   657:   }
   658: 
   659:   // modo antigo (todos lotteries no mesmo request)
   660:   for (const id of lotteries) params.append("lotteries[]", id);
   661:   return `${base}?${params.toString()}`;
   662: }
   663: 
   664: function sleep(ms) {
   665:   return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
   666: }
   667: 
   668: function shouldRetryAxiosError(err) {
   669:   const code = String(err?.code || "").toLowerCase();
   670:   const status = Number(err?.response?.status);
   671: 
   672:   if (
   673:     code.includes("etimedout") ||
   674:     code.includes("econnreset") ||
   675:     code.includes("enotfound")
   676:   ) {
   677:     return true;
   678:   }
   679: 
   680:   // 429 / 5xx
   681:   if (
   682:     Number.isFinite(status) &&
   683:     (status === 429 || (status >= 500 && status <= 599))
   684:   ) {
   685:     return true;
   686:   }
   687: 
   688:   return false;
   689: }
   690: 
   691: async function axiosGetJson(url) {
   692:   let lastErr = null;
   693: 
   694:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= HTTP_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   695:     try {
   696:       const { data } = await axios.get(url, {
   697:         headers: {
   698:           Accept: "application/json, text/plain, */*",
   699:           Origin: "https://app.kingapostas.com",
   700:           Referer: "https://app.kingapostas.com/",
   701:         },
   702:         timeout: 30000,
   703:         validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 300,
   704:       });
   705: 
   706:       if (!data?.success || !Array.isArray(data?.data)) {
   707:         throw new Error("Resposta inesperada da API (success/data).");
   708:       }
   709: 
   710:       return data;
   711:     } catch (e) {
   712:       lastErr = e;
   713: 
   714:       const canRetry = attempt < HTTP_RETRIES && shouldRetryAxiosError(e);
   715:       if (!canRetry) break;
   716: 
   717:       const backoff = HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, attempt);
   718:       console.warn(
   719:         `[HTTP] tentativa ${attempt + 1}/${HTTP_RETRIES + 1} falhou (${
   720:           e?.response?.status || e?.code || "err"
   721:         }). retry em ${backoff}ms...`
   722:       );
   723:       await sleep(backoff);
   724:     }
   725:   }
   726: 
   727:   throw lastErr || new Error("Falha ao buscar API.");
   728: }
   729: 
   730: /* =========================
   731:    DETAILS (HTML) fallback — PT_RIO 18h
   732: ========================= */
   733: function buildDetailsUrl({ date, lotteryKey }) {
   734:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   735: 
   736:   // ✅ bate com o print: lottery=PT+RIO
   737:   const lot = lk === "PT_RIO" ? "PT RIO" : lk.replace(/_/g, " ");
   738: 
   739:   const q =
   740:     "lottery=" +
   741:     encodeURIComponent(lot) +
   742:     "&date=" +
   743:     encodeURIComponent(String(date || "").trim());
   744: 
   745:   return "https://app.kingapostas.com/results/details?" + q;
   746: }
   747: 
   748: async function axiosGetText(url) {
   749:   let lastErr = null;
   750: 
   751:   for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= HTTP_RETRIES; attempt++) {
   752:     try {
   753:       const { data } = await axios.get(url, {
   754:         headers: {
   755:           Accept:
   756:             "text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8",
   757:           Origin: "https://app.kingapostas.com",
   758:           Referer: "https://app.kingapostas.com/",
   759:           "User-Agent":
   760:             "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120 Safari/537.36",
   761:         },
   762:         timeout: 30000,
   763:         responseType: "text",
   764:         validateStatus: (s) => s >= 200 && s < 400,
   765:       });
   766:       return String(data || "");
   767:     } catch (e) {
   768:       lastErr = e;
   769:       const canRetry = attempt < HTTP_RETRIES && shouldRetryAxiosError(e);
   770:       if (!canRetry) break;
   771: 
   772:       const backoff = HTTP_RETRY_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, attempt);
   773:       console.warn(
   774:         `[HTTP] tentativa ${attempt + 1}/${HTTP_RETRIES + 1} (HTML) falhou (${
   775:           e?.response?.status || e?.code || "err"
   776:         }). retry em ${backoff}ms...`
   777:       );
   778:       await sleep(backoff);
   779:     }
   780:   }
   781: 
   782:   throw lastErr || new Error("Falha ao buscar HTML");
   783: }
   784: 
   785: /**
   786:  * Detecta se o HTML é uma página de login/redirect (evita falso positivo).
   787:  */
   788: function detectDetailsHtmlKind(html) {
   789:   const h = String(html || "");
   790:   const low = h.toLowerCase();
   791: 
   792:   // sinais comuns:
   793:   if (low.includes("/login/sign-in") || low.includes("formlogin")) return "login";
   794:   if (low.includes("bailout_to_client_side_rendering")) return "csr_bailout";
   795:   if (low.includes("app/login") || low.includes("sign-in")) return "login_like";
   796: 
   797:   // fallback: parece uma página de results
   798:   if (low.includes("results/details") || low.includes("kingapostas"))
   799:     return "results_like";
   800: 
   801:   return "unknown";
   802: }
   803: 
   804: // Heurística: só diagnosticar se o HTML "parece" ter o slot e algum conteúdo de prêmios
   805: function parseDetailsHtmlForSlot(html, slotHHMM) {
   806:   const out = {
   807:     hasSlot: false,
   808:     hasAnyPrize: false,
   809:     kind: "unknown",
   810:   };
   811:   const h = String(html || "");
   812:   const slot = String(slotHHMM || "").trim();
   813:   if (!h || !slot) return out;
   814: 
   815:   out.kind = detectDetailsHtmlKind(h);
   816: 
   817:   // Se é login/redirect, não confia em nada
   818:   if (out.kind === "login" || out.kind === "login_like") {
   819:     return out;
   820:   }
   821: 
   822:   if (h.toLowerCase().includes(slot.toLowerCase())) out.hasSlot = true;
   823: 
   824:   // mantém heurística, mas só vale quando não for login
   825:   if (/(prize[_\s-]?\d+)|(\b\d{4}\b)/i.test(h)) out.hasAnyPrize = true;
   826: 
   827:   return out;
   828: }
   829: 
   830: async function tryHtmlDetailsFallback({ date, lotteryKey, closeHour }) {
   831:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   832:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
   833:   if (!slot || !isHHMM(slot)) return { ok: false, reason: "bad_slot" };
   834: 
   835:   // por enquanto: só PT_RIO 18:00 (não altera outros horários)
   836:   if (!(lk === "PT_RIO" && slot === "18:00")) {
   837:     return { ok: false, reason: "not_applicable" };
   838:   }
   839: 
   840:   const url = buildDetailsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
   841:   const html = await axiosGetText(url);
   842:   const parsed = parseDetailsHtmlForSlot(html, slot);
   843: 
   844:   return {
   845:     ok: true,
   846:     url,
   847:     slot,
   848:     hasSlot: parsed.hasSlot,
   849:     hasAnyPrize: parsed.hasAnyPrize,
   850:     kind: parsed.kind,
   851:   };
   852: }
   853: 
   854: /**
   855:  * ✅ Merge + dedup de draws vindos de múltiplos requests
   856:  * Dedup key: date + close_hour(SLOT) + lottery_id (se existir)
   857:  */
   858: function mergeAndDedupDraws(arrays, lotteryKey) {
   859:   const map = new Map();
   860: 
   861:   for (const a of arrays) {
   862:     const list = Array.isArray(a) ? a : [];
   863:     for (const d of list) {
   864:       const date = String(d?.date || "").trim();
   865:       const { slot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(d, lotteryKey);
   866: 
   867:       const lid =
   868:         pickLotteryId(d) ||
   869:         String(d?.lottery_name || d?.name || "").trim() ||
   870:         "NA";
   871:       const key = `${date}__${slot}__${lid}`;
   872: 
   873:       if (!map.has(key)) {
   874:         map.set(key, d);
   875:       } else {
   876:         // se duplicado, preferir o que tiver mais prizes preenchidos
   877:         const prev = map.get(key);
   878:         const prevCount = countPrizesInDraw(prev);
   879:         const curCount = countPrizesInDraw(d);
   880:         if (curCount > prevCount) map.set(key, d);
   881:       }
   882:     }
   883:   }
   884: 
   885:   return Array.from(map.values());
   886: }
   887: 
   888: function countPrizesInDraw(draw) {
   889:   let c = 0;
   890:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
   891:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
   892:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
   893:     c++;
   894:   }
   895:   return c;
   896: }
   897: 
   898: function summarizeCloseHours(draws, lotteryKey) {
   899:   const set = new Set();
   900: 
   901:   for (const d of draws) {
   902:     const { slot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
   903:       d,
   904:       lotteryKey
   905:     );
   906: 
   907:     if (slot) {
   908:       set.add(slot);
   909:     }
   910:   }
   911: 
   912:   return Array.from(set).sort();
   913: }
   914: 
   915: /**
   916:  * ✅ Fetch robusto:
   917:  * - Por default faz 1 request por lotteryId e mescla (evita “cap”/consolidação do endpoint)
   918:  * - Se KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY=0 -> faz 1 request único (modo antigo)
   919:  */
   920: async function fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey }) {
   921:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   922:   const lotteries = LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk];
   923:   if (!lotteries?.length) {
   924:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(LOTTERIES_BY_KEY, lk)) {
   925:       throw new Error(
   926:         `${lk} sem lotteryIds. Defina KING_LOTTERIES_${lk}="uuid1,uuid2,..." para habilitar o fetch.`
   927:       );
   928:     }
   929: 
   930:     throw new Error(`lotteryKey inválida: ${lk}`);
   931:   }
   932: 
   933:   if (!FETCH_PER_LOTTERY) {
   934:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
   935:     return await axiosGetJson(url);
   936:   }
   937: 
   938:   // ✅ 1 request por lottery (sequencial: mais estável e respeita rate-limit)
   939:   const parts = [];
   940:   const errors = [];
   941: 
   942:   // helper local: extrai e normaliza closes vindos nesse UUID
   943:   const closeFromDraw = (d) =>
   944:     normalizeHHMM(d?.close_hour ?? d?.closeHour ?? d?.horario ?? d?.close ?? "");
   945: 
   946:   const summarizeCloseHoursPart = (list) => {
   947:     const set = new Set();
   948:     for (const d of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) {
   949:       const c = closeFromDraw(d);
   950:       if (c) set.add(c);
   951:     }
   952:     return Array.from(set).sort();
   953:   };
   954: 
   955:   for (const lotteryId of lotteries) {
   956:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk, lotteryId });
   957: 
   958:     try {
   959:       const data = await axiosGetJson(url);
   960: 
   961:       // ✅ carimba o UUID do request em cada draw (a API nem sempre retorna lottery_id)
   962:       if (data && Array.isArray(data.data)) {
   963:         data.data = data.data.map((d) => ({
   964:           ...d,
   965:           lottery_id: d?.lottery_id || lotteryId,
   966:           lotteryId: d?.lotteryId || lotteryId,
   967:         }));
   968:       }
   969:       const list = Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : [];
   970:       parts.push(list);
   971: 
   972:       if (KING_FETCH_DEBUG) {
   973:         const closesPart = summarizeCloseHoursPart(list);
   974:         console.log(
   975:           `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} draws=${list.length} close_hours=[${closesPart.join(
   976:             ", "
   977:           )}]`
   978:         );
   979:       }
   980:     } catch (e) {
   981:       const msg = e?.response?.status
   982:         ? `HTTP_${e.response.status}`
   983:         : e?.code || e?.message || "ERR";
   984: 
   985:       errors.push({ lotteryId, msg });
   986: 
   987:       console.warn(
   988:         `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} ERROR=${msg} (seguindo...)`
   989:       );
   990: 
   991:       // segue para não travar o dia inteiro por 1 UUID
   992:       continue;
   993:     }
   994:   }
   995: 
   996:   const merged = mergeAndDedupDraws(parts, lk);
   997:   const closes = summarizeCloseHours(merged, lk);
   998:   const errInfo =
   999:     KING_FETCH_DEBUG && errors.length
  1000:       ? ` errors=${errors.length} ids=[${errors.map((x) => x.lotteryId).join(",")}]`
  1001:       : "";
  1002: 
  1003:   console.log(
  1004:     `[FETCH] ${lk} ${date} per-lottery=${lotteries.length} -> merged_draws=${
  1005:       merged.length
  1006:     } close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]${errInfo}`
  1007:   );
  1008:   return { success: true, data: merged };
  1009: }
  1010: 
  1011: /**
  1012:  * Monta prizes do draw (até 15), já filtrando vazios.
  1013:  */
  1014: function extractPrizes(draw) {
  1015:   const prizes = [];
  1016:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
  1017:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
  1018:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
  1019:     prizes.push({ position: i, value: String(v) });
  1020:   }
  1021:   return prizes;
  1022: }
  1023: 
  1024: /**
  1025:  * Gera drawId e drawRef no mesmo padrão do seu projeto.
  1026:  * ✅ Agora usa close_hour SLOT no ID e guarda close_hour_raw (quando fizer sentido).
  1027:  */
  1028: function buildDrawRef({ draw, lotteryKey }) {
  1029:   const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
  1030: 
  1031:   const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
  1032:     draw,
  1033:     lotteryKey
  1034:   );
  1035: 
  1036:   const lotteryName = String(draw?.lottery_name || draw?.name || "SEM_NOME").trim();
  1037: 
  1038:   const lotteryIdFromDraw = pickLotteryId(draw);
  1039:   const lotteryIdPart = safeIdPart(lotteryIdFromDraw || lotteryName);
  1040: 
  1041:   // O ID deve usar sempre o slot oficial.
  1042:   // O horario bruto da fonte pode variar, por exemplo 19:53,
  1043:   // e permanece preservado apenas em close_hour_raw.
  1044:   const drawIdSlot = closeSlot;
  1045: 
  1046:   const drawId = `${safeIdPart(lotteryKey)}__${date}__${safeIdPart(
  1047:     drawIdSlot
  1048:   )}__${lotteryIdPart}`;
  1049:   const drawRef = db.collection("draws").doc(drawId);
  1050: 
  1051:   return {
  1052:     drawId,
  1053:     drawRef,
  1054:     date,
  1055:     closeRaw,
  1056:     closeSlot,
  1057:     lotteryName,
  1058:     lotteryIdFromDraw,
  1059:   };
  1060: }
  1061: 
  1062: /**
  1063:  * Prova forte de “já completo”:
  1064:  * - draw existe
  1065:  * - prizesCount > 0 (ou) existe ao menos 1 doc na subcoleção prizes
  1066:  */
  1067: async function checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef) {
  1068:   try {
  1069:     const dSnap = await drawRef.get();
  1070:     if (!dSnap.exists) return false;
  1071: 
  1072:     const data = dSnap.data() || {};
  1073:     if (Number(data.prizesCount) > 0) return true;
  1074: 
  1075:     // fallback: olha 1 doc de prizes
  1076:     const pSnap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").limit(1).get();
  1077:     return !pSnap.empty;
  1078:   } catch {
  1079:     return false;
  1080:   }
  1081: }
  1082: 
  1083: /**
  1084:  * ✅ Prova forte por SLOT (anti-índice composto)
  1085:  * - Query filtra SOMENTE por date
  1086:  * - close_hour + lottery_key/lotteryKey filtrados em memória
  1087:  *
  1088:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
  1089:  * - Normaliza o close_hour armazenado (pode existir legado "09:09")
  1090:  *   e compara por SLOT (HH:00), para não “perder” doc existente.
  1091:  */
  1092: async function checkSlotCompletion({ date, closeHour, lotteryKey }) {
  1093:   try {
  1094:     const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1095:     const targetSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
  1096:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1097: 
  1098:     // query mínima
  1099:     const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
  1100:     if (snap.empty) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1101: 
  1102:     const refs = [];
  1103:     snap.forEach((doc) => {
  1104:       const d = doc.data() || {};
  1105: 
  1106:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1107:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1108: 
  1109:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;
  1110: 
  1111:       // ✅ compara por SLOT normalizado (pega legado "09:09" como "09:00")
  1112:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;
  1113:       if (storedSlot !== targetSlot) return;
  1114: 
  1115:       refs.push(doc.ref);
  1116:     });
  1117: 
  1118:     if (!refs.length) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1119: 
  1120:     let complete = 0;
  1121:     for (const ref of refs) {
  1122:       const ok = await checkAlreadyComplete(ref);
  1123:       if (ok) complete += 1;
  1124:     }
  1125: 
  1126:     return { docs: refs.length, complete };
  1127:   } catch {
  1128:     return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
  1129:   }
  1130: }
  1131: 
  1132: /**
  1133:  * Importa payload para o Firestore.
  1134:  * closeHour (opcional): se informado, importa SOMENTE draws daquele horário (SLOT).
  1135:  *
  1136:  * Opts extras:
  1137:  * - skipIfAlreadyComplete (boolean): se true, e o draw do closeHour já estiver completo no FS,
  1138:  *   então NÃO escreve nada (evita ruído e falsos positivos).
  1139:  */
  1140: async function importFromPayload({
  1141:   payload,
  1142:   lotteryKey,
  1143:   closeHour = null,
  1144:   skipIfAlreadyComplete = false,
  1145: } = {}) {
  1146:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1147:   const uf = resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lk); // ✅ UF correta (ex.: RJ)
  1148: 
  1149:   let batch = db.batch();
  1150:   let ops = 0;
  1151: 
  1152:   let totalDrawsFromApi = 0;
  1153:   let totalDrawsMatchedClose = 0;
  1154:   let totalDrawsValid = 0;
  1155: 
  1156:   let totalDrawsUpserted = 0;
  1157:   let totalDrawsSaved = 0;
  1158: 
  1159:   let totalPrizesUpserted = 0;
  1160:   let totalPrizesSaved = 0;
  1161: 
  1162:   let skippedInvalid = 0;
  1163:   let skippedEmpty = 0;
  1164:   let skippedCloseHour = 0;
  1165:   let skippedAlreadyComplete = 0;
  1166: 
  1167:   // Provas úteis para o scheduler (quando closeHour é informado)
  1168:   const proof = {
  1169:     filterClose: null,
  1170: 
  1171:     apiHasPrizes: false,
  1172:     apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0, // draws válidos do closeHour com prizes
  1173:     targetDrawIds: [],
  1174:     inferredDate: null, // date do slot (inferido a partir do payload)
  1175: 
  1176:     expectedTargets: 0, // = max(1, apiReturnedTargetDraws)
  1177:     slotDocsFound: 0,
  1178:     alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
  1179:     alreadyCompleteAny: false,
  1180:     alreadyCompleteAll: false,
  1181: 
  1182:     targetWriteCount: 0,
  1183:     targetSavedCount: 0,
  1184:   };
  1185: 
  1186:   // ✅ filtro deve ser SLOT também
  1187:   const filterClose = closeHour
  1188:     ? normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour, lk).slot
  1189:     : null;
  1190:   if (filterClose && !isHHMM(filterClose)) {
  1191:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "closeHour" inválido. Use HH:MM.');
  1192:   }
  1193:   proof.filterClose = filterClose;
  1194: 
  1195:   const apiArr = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
  1196: 
  1197:   for (const draw of apiArr) {
  1198:     totalDrawsFromApi++;
  1199: 
  1200:     const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
  1201:     const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeDrawCloseHour(
  1202:       draw,
  1203:       lk
  1204:     );
  1205: 
  1206:     // filtro por horário (SLOT)
  1207:     if (filterClose) {
  1208:       if (closeSlot !== filterClose) {
  1209:         skippedCloseHour++;
  1210:         continue;
  1211:       }
  1212:     }
  1213: 
  1214:     totalDrawsMatchedClose++;
  1215: 
  1216:     // validações mínimas (slot precisa ser HH:MM)
  1217:     if (!isISODate(date) || !isHHMM(closeSlot)) {
  1218:       skippedInvalid++;
  1219:       continue;
  1220:     }
  1221: 
  1222:     // prizes existentes
  1223:     const prizes = extractPrizes(draw);
  1224: 
  1225:     // Se não tem prize nenhum, NÃO grava
  1226:     if (!prizes.length) {
  1227:       skippedEmpty++;
  1228:       continue;
  1229:     }
  1230: 
  1231:     totalDrawsValid++;
  1232: 
  1233:     if (filterClose) {
  1234:       proof.apiHasPrizes = true;
  1235:       proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws += 1;
  1236:       proof.inferredDate = proof.inferredDate || date;
  1237:     }
  1238: 
  1239:     const { drawId, drawRef, lotteryName, lotteryIdFromDraw } = buildDrawRef({
  1240:       draw,
  1241:       lotteryKey: lk,
  1242:     });
  1243: 
  1244:     if (filterClose) proof.targetDrawIds.push(drawId);
  1245: 
  1246:     // Em closeHour: checa “já completo” e, se configurado, pula escrita (por draw)
  1247:     if (filterClose) {
  1248:       const already = await checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef);
  1249:       if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && already) {
  1250:         skippedAlreadyComplete++;
  1251:         continue;
  1252:       }
  1253:     }
  1254: 
  1255:     // métrica "novo" (opcional — custa reads)
  1256:     let isNewDraw = false;
  1257:     if (CHECK_EXISTENCE) {
  1258:       try {
  1259:         const snap = await drawRef.get();
  1260:         isNewDraw = !snap.exists;
  1261:       } catch {
  1262:         isNewDraw = false;
  1263:       }
  1264:     }
  1265: 
  1266:     const ymd = date;
  1267: 
  1268:     batch.set(
  1269:       drawRef,
  1270:       {
  1271:         source: "kingapostas",
  1272: 
  1273:         uf: uf || null,
  1274:         lottery_key: lk,
  1275: 
  1276:         lottery_name: lotteryName,
  1277:         lottery_id: lotteryIdFromDraw || null,
  1278:         drawId,
  1279: 
  1280:         date,
  1281:         ymd,
  1282: 
  1283:         // ✅ campo de negócio (SLOT)
  1284:         close_hour: closeSlot,
  1285: 
  1286:         // ✅ compat: campos usados no front (evita undefined)
  1287:         hour: closeSlot ? String(closeSlot).slice(0, 2) : null,
  1288:         close: closeSlot || null,
  1289: 
  1290:         // ✅ guarda o que veio da API SOMENTE quando fizer sentido
  1291:         // (PT_RIO HH:09 vira "", então cai em null)
  1292:         close_hour_raw: closeRaw || null,
  1293: 
  1294:         prizesCount: prizes.length,
  1295:         importedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
  1296:       },
  1297:       { merge: true }
  1298:     );
  1299:     ops++;
  1300:     totalDrawsUpserted++;
  1301:     if (isNewDraw) totalDrawsSaved++;
  1302: 
  1303:     if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
  1304: 
  1305:     for (const p of prizes) {
  1306:       const n = normalizePrize(p.value);
  1307:       const prizeId = `p${String(p.position).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  1308:       const prizeRef = drawRef.collection("prizes").doc(prizeId);
  1309: 
  1310:       let isNewPrize = false;
  1311:       if (CHECK_EXISTENCE) {
  1312:         try {
  1313:           const psnap = await prizeRef.get();
  1314:           isNewPrize = !psnap.exists;
  1315:         } catch {
  1316:           isNewPrize = false;
  1317:         }
  1318:       }
  1319: 
  1320:       batch.set(
  1321:         prizeRef,
  1322:         {
  1323:           position: p.position,
  1324:           ...n,
  1325:         },
  1326:         { merge: true }
  1327:       );
  1328:       ops++;
  1329:       totalPrizesUpserted++;
  1330:       if (isNewPrize) totalPrizesSaved++;
  1331: 
  1332:       if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
  1333: 
  1334:       if (ops >= 420) {
  1335:         await batch.commit();
  1336:         batch = db.batch();
  1337:         ops = 0;
  1338:       }
  1339:     }
  1340:   }
  1341: 
  1342:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
  1343: 
  1344:   // ✅ flags finais de complete (por SLOT via query anti-índice)
  1345:   if (proof.filterClose) {
  1346:     const slotDate = proof.inferredDate; // se API trouxe o slot, teremos date
  1347:     proof.expectedTargets = Math.max(1, Number(proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws || 0));
  1348: 
  1349:     if (slotDate) {
  1350:       const { docs, complete } = await checkSlotCompletion({
  1351:         date: slotDate,
  1352:         closeHour: proof.filterClose,
  1353:         lotteryKey: lk,
  1354:       });
  1355: 
  1356:       proof.slotDocsFound = docs;
  1357:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = complete;
  1358:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = complete > 0;
  1359:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = complete >= proof.expectedTargets;
  1360:     } else {
  1361:       proof.slotDocsFound = 0;
  1362:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = 0;
  1363:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = false;
  1364:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = false;
  1365:     }
  1366: 
  1367:     // savedCount “forte” para o scheduler:
  1368:     if (!proof.apiHasPrizes) {
  1369:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
  1370:     } else if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && proof.alreadyCompleteAll) {
  1371:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
  1372:     } else {
  1373:       proof.targetSavedCount = proof.targetWriteCount;
  1374:     }
  1375:   }
  1376: 
  1377:   console.log(
  1378:     `[IMPORT] API draws=${totalDrawsFromApi} | match_close=${totalDrawsMatchedClose} | validos=${totalDrawsValid}` +
  1379:       ` | draws_upserted=${totalDrawsUpserted} | draws_novos=${totalDrawsSaved}` +
  1380:       ` | prizes_upserted=${totalPrizesUpserted} | prizes_novos=${totalPrizesSaved}` +
  1381:       ` | skip_vazio=${skippedEmpty} | skip_invalido=${skippedInvalid} | skip_close=${skippedCloseHour}` +
  1382:       ` | skip_complete=${skippedAlreadyComplete}` +
  1383:       ` | CHECK_EXISTENCE=${CHECK_EXISTENCE ? "ON" : "OFF"}` +
  1384:       ` | uf=${uf || "NULL"} lottery_key=${lk}` +
  1385:       (proof.filterClose
  1386:         ? ` | expected=${proof.expectedTargets} complete=${proof.alreadyCompleteCount} slotDocs=${proof.slotDocsFound} apiTargets=${proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws}`
  1387:         : "")
  1388:   );
  1389: 
  1390:   return {
  1391:     totalDrawsFromApi,
  1392:     totalDrawsMatchedClose,
  1393:     totalDrawsValid,
  1394: 
  1395:     totalDrawsSaved,
  1396:     totalDrawsUpserted,
  1397: 
  1398:     totalPrizesSaved,
  1399:     totalPrizesUpserted,
  1400: 
  1401:     skippedEmpty,
  1402:     skippedInvalid,
  1403:     skippedCloseHour,
  1404:     skippedAlreadyComplete,
  1405: 
  1406:     proof,
  1407:   };
  1408: }
  1409: 
  1410: /**
  1411:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
  1412:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)
  1413:  * - Seguro: só apaga legado se existe UUID no slot
  1414:  */
  1415: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
  1416:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1417:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
  1418:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
  1419: 
  1420:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
  1421:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
  1422: 
  1423:   const mine = rows.filter(
  1424:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
  1425:   );
  1426: 
  1427:   const bySlot = new Map();
  1428:   for (const r of mine) {
  1429:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
  1430:     if (!slot) continue;
  1431:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
  1432:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
  1433:   }
  1434: 
  1435:   let deleted = 0;
  1436:   let slots = 0;
  1437:   let batch = db.batch();
  1438:   let ops = 0;
  1439: 
  1440:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
  1441:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
  1442:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
  1443: 
  1444:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
  1445:     if (!leg.length) continue;
  1446: 
  1447:     for (const d of leg) {
  1448:       batch.delete(d.ref);
  1449:       deleted += 1;
  1450:       ops += 1;
  1451:       if (ops >= 450) {
  1452:         await batch.commit();
  1453:         batch = db.batch();
  1454:         ops = 0;
  1455:       }
  1456:     }
  1457:     slots += 1;
  1458:   }
  1459: 
  1460:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
  1461: 
  1462:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
  1463: }
  1464: 
  1465: async function runImport({ date, lotteryKey = "PT_RIO", closeHour = null } = {}) {
  1466:   if (!date || !isISODate(date)) {
  1467:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "date" inválido. Use YYYY-MM-DD.');
  1468:   }
  1469: 
  1470:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  1471:   if (!lk || !LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk]) {
  1472:     throw new Error(`Parâmetro "lotteryKey" inválida: ${lk}`);
  1473:   }
  1474: 
  1475:   // ✅ BLINDAGEM: nunca importar data futura (evita “sujar” FS por engano)
  1476:   const todayBR = todayYMDLocal();
  1477:   if (!ALLOW_FUTURE_DATE && isFutureISODate(date)) {
  1478:     const norm = normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lk);
  1479:     return {
  1480:       ok: true,
  1481:       lotteryKey: lk,
  1482:       date,
  1483:       closeHour: norm.slot || null,
  1484: 
  1485:       requestedCloseHour: norm.requested || null,
  1486: 
  1487:       blocked: true,

  1489:       todayBR,

  1510:       totalPrizesUpserted: 0,

  1530:   }
  1531: 
  1532:   // ✅ closeHour do scheduler: normaliza SEM log enganoso
  1533:   const norm = normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lk);
  1534:   if (norm.note === "invalid") {
  1535:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "closeHour" inválido. Use HH:MM.');
  1536:   }
  1537:   const normalizedClose = norm.slot || null;
  1538:

  1540: 
  1541:   const performance = {
  1542:     fetchMs: 0,

  1551:     lk === "PT_RIO" &&

  1554:

  1559:

  1569:           );

  1571:       } else {

  1676:

  1790:

  1794:

  1797:   };
  1798: 
  1799:   const historySyncStartedAt = Date.now();
  1800: 
  1801:   const top3HistorySync =
  1802:     await syncImportedResultToTop3History(

  1804:     );
  1805:

  1808: 
  1809:   const top3StartedAt = Date.now();
  1810: 
  1811:   let top3AutoPrediction = {
  1812:     ok: true,
  1813:     skipped: true,
  1814:     reason: "not_attempted",
  1815:   };
  1816: 
  1817:   if (
  1818:     response.ok === true &&
  1819:     response.blocked !== true &&
  1820:     response.captured === true &&
  1821:     normalizedClose
  1822:   ) {
  1823:     try {
  1824:       const publicApi = loadTop3PublicApi();
  1825: 
  1826:       const nextSlot =
  1827:         resolveNextTop3Slot({
  1828:           lotteryKey: lk,
  1829:           ymd: date,

  1833: 
  1834:       const nextYmd =
  1835:         String(nextSlot?.ymd || "").trim();
  1836: 
  1837:       const nextHour =
  1838:         String(nextSlot?.hour || "").trim();
  1839: 
  1840:       if (
  1841:         /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(nextYmd) &&
  1842:         nextHour
  1843:       ) {
  1844:         const generated =
  1845:           await createTop3PredictionRun({
  1846:             lotteryKey: lk,
  1847:             date: nextYmd,
  1848:             closeHour: nextHour,
  1849:             historySource: "auto",
  1850:             source: "auto-import",
  1851:             metadata: {
  1852:               triggeredByImport: true,
  1853:               importedYmd: date,
  1854:               importedHour: normalizedClose,
  1855:             },
  1856:           });
  1857: 
  1858:         top3AutoPrediction = {
  1859:           ok: true,
  1860:           skipped: false,
  1861:           targetYmd: nextYmd,
  1862:           targetHour: nextHour,
  1863:           runId:
  1864:             generated?.run?.id || null,
  1865:           publicProjection:
  1866:             generated?.publicProjection || null,
  1867:         };
  1868: 
  1869:         console.log(
  1870:           `[TOP3 AUTO] OK | ${lk} | ` +
  1871:           `importado=${date} ${normalizedClose} | ` +
  1872:           `próximo=${nextYmd} ${nextHour}`
  1873:         );
  1874:       } else {
  1875:         top3AutoPrediction = {
  1876:           ok: true,
  1877:           skipped: true,
  1878:           reason: "next_slot_not_found",
  1879:         };
  1880: 
  1881:         console.warn(
  1882:           `[TOP3 AUTO] próximo slot não localizado | ` +
  1883:           `${lk} ${date} ${normalizedClose}`
  1884:         );
  1885:       }
  1886:     } catch (error) {
  1887:       top3AutoPrediction = {
  1888:         ok: false,
  1889:         skipped: false,
  1890:         error: String(
  1891:           error?.message ||
  1892:           error ||
  1893:           "top3_auto_failed"
  1894:         ),
  1895:       };
  1896: 
  1897:       console.error(
  1898:         "[TOP3 AUTO] falhou:",
  1899:         error?.stack ||
  1900:         error?.message ||
  1901:         error
  1902:       );
  1903:     }
  1904:   } else {
  1905:     top3AutoPrediction = {
  1906:       ok: true,
  1907:       skipped: true,
  1908:       reason:
  1909:         response.blocked === true
  1910:           ? "import_blocked"
  1911:           : response.captured !== true
  1912:           ? "result_not_captured"
  1913:           : "close_hour_missing",
  1914:     };
  1915:   }
  1916: 
  1917:   performance.top3Ms =
  1918:     Date.now() - top3StartedAt;
  1919: 
  1920:   performance.totalMs =
  1921:     Date.now() - startedAt;
  1922: 
  1923:   console.log(
  1924:     `[PERF:IMPORT] ${lk} ${date}` +
  1925:       `${normalizedClose ? ` ${normalizedClose}` : ""}` +
  1926:       ` | fetch=${performance.fetchMs}ms` +
  1927:       ` | firestore=${performance.importMs}ms` +
  1928:       ` | history=${performance.historySyncMs}ms` +
  1929:       ` | top3=${performance.top3Ms}ms` +
  1930:       ` | total=${performance.totalMs}ms`
  1931:   );
  1932: 
  1933:   return {
  1934:     ...response,
  1935:     top3HistorySync,
  1936:     top3AutoPrediction,
  1937:     performance,
  1938:   };
  1939: }
  1940: 
  1941: /**
  1942:  * CLI wrapper (mantém compatibilidade)
  1943:  */
  1944: async function main() {
  1945:   const date = process.argv[2];
  1946:   const lotteryKey = process.argv[3] || "PT_RIO";
  1947:   const closeHour = process.argv[4] || null;
  1948: 
  1949:   const result = await runImport({
  1950:     date,
  1951:     lotteryKey,
  1952:     closeHour,
  1953:   });
  1954: 
  1955:   console.log("");
  1956:   console.log("===== RESULTADO =====");
  1957:   console.log(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  1958: }
  1959: 
  1960: if (require.main === module) {
  1961:   main().catch((e) => {
  1962:     console.error("ERRO:", e?.message || e);
  1963:     process.exit(1);
  1964:   });
  1965: }
  1966: 
  1967: module.exports = {
  1968:   runImport,
  1969:   fetchKingResults,
  1970:   importFromPayload,
  1971:   buildResultsUrl,
  1972: };
