    27: function drawTs(draw) {
    28:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    29:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    30:   if (!y || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    31: 
    32:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
    33:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
    34:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
    35:   const add = mins >= 0 ? mins * 60 * 1000 : 0;
    36:   return base + add;
    37: }
    38: 
    39: function drawKey(draw) {
    40:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    41:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    42:   if (!y || !h) return "";
    43:   return `${y}|${h}`;
    44: }
    45: 
    46: function dedupeAndSort(draws) {
    47:   const map = new Map();
    48: 
    49:   for (const d of Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []) {
    50:     const key = drawKey(d);
    51:     if (!key) continue;
    52:     if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, d);
    53:   }
    54: 
    55:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawTs(a) - drawTs(b));
    56: }
    57: 
    58: function top5Groups(draw) {
    59:   const out = [];
    60: 
    61:   for (const p of Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : []) {
    62:     const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
    63:     const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(p));
    64: 
    65:     if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 5) continue;
    66:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
    67: 
    68:     out.push({ pos, grupo: g });
    69:   }
    70: 
    71:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
    72: }
    73: 
    74: function ensureRow(map, key) {
    75:   if (!map.has(key)) {
    76:     map.set(key, { total: 0, hitTop5: 0, hitFirst: 0 });
    77:   }
    78: 
    79:   return map.get(key);
    80: }
    81: 
    82: function pct(n, d) {
    83:   return d ? ((n / d) * 100).toFixed(2) : "0.00";
    84: }
    85: 
    86: function assertComputedSlotMatchesNextDraw({ computed, found, nextSlot, drawLast }) {
    87:   const computedNextYmd = computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "";
    88:   const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
    89: 
    90:   const expectedYmd = found?.ymd || nextSlot?.ymd || "";
    91:   const expectedHour = toHourBucket(found?.hour || nextSlot?.hour || "");
    92: 
    93:   if (computedNextYmd !== expectedYmd || computedNextHour !== expectedHour) {
    94:     throw new Error(
    95:       [
    96:         "TOP3 calculou slot diferente do próximo sorteio real.",
    97:         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast))}`,
    98:         `Esperado: ${expectedYmd} ${expectedHour}`,
    99:         `Calculado: ${computedNextYmd} ${computedNextHour}`,
   100:         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
   101:       ].join("\n")
   102:     );
   103:   }
   104: }
   105: 
   106: function assertPredictionIntegrity({ computed, drawLast, nextDraw }) {
   107:   const top = Array.isArray(computed?.top) ? computed.top.slice(0, 3) : [];
   108: 
   109:   if (!top.length) {
   110:     throw new Error(
   111:       [
   112:         "TOP3 vazio para caso com próximo sorteio real.",
   113:         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast))}`,
   114:         `Próximo sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(nextDraw)} ${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(nextDraw))}`,
   115:         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
   116:       ].join("\n")
   117:     );
   118:   }
   119: 
   120:   const seen = new Set();
   121: 
   122:   for (const item of top) {
   123:     const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
   124: 
   125:     if (!Number.isFinite(grupo) || grupo < 1 || grupo > 25) {
   126:       throw new Error(`Grupo inválido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   127:     }
   128: 
   129:     if (seen.has(grupo)) {
   130:       throw new Error(`Grupo repetido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(top)}`);
   131:     }
   132: 
   133:     seen.add(grupo);
   134: 
   135:     const confidence = Number(
   136:       item?.displayConfidence ??
   137:         item?.confidence ??
   138:         item?.scoreProb ??
   139:         item?.prob ??
   140:         0
   141:     );
   142: 
   143:     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence)) {
   144:       throw new Error(`Confiança inválida no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   145:     }
   146: 
   147:     if (confidence < 0) {
   148:       throw new Error(`Confiança negativa no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   149:     }
   150:   }
   151: }

   153: test("backtest TOP3 PT_RIO - camadas e transições", async () => {
   154:   const bounds = await getKingBoundsByUf({ uf: "PT_RIO" });
   155:   const maxDate = bounds?.maxYmd || bounds?.maxDate;
   156:   expect(maxDate).toBeTruthy();
   157: 
   158:   const from = addDaysYMD(maxDate, -179);
   159:   const toExclusive = addDaysYMD(maxDate, 1);
   160: 
   161:   const raw = await getKingResultsByRange({
   162:     uf: "PT_RIO",
   163:     dateFrom: from,
   164:     dateTo: toExclusive,
   165:     mode: "detailed",
   166:     readPolicy: "server",
   167:   });
   168: 
   169:   const allDraws = dedupeAndSort(raw);
   170:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(allDraws);
   171: 
   172:   expect(allDraws.length).toBeGreaterThan(30);
   173: 
   174:   let totalCases = 0;
   175:   let hitTop5 = 0;
   176:   let hitFirst = 0;
   177: 
   178:   const byScenario = new Map();
   179:   const byEdge = new Map();
   180:   const byPrevGrupo = new Map();
   181: 
   182:   for (let i = 0; i < allDraws.length - 1; i += 1) {
   183:     const drawLast = allDraws[i];
   184:     const history = allDraws.slice(0, i + 1);
   185: 
   186:     const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
   187:     const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
   188:     if (!lastY || !lastH) continue;
   189: 
   190:     const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
   191:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   192:       ymd: lastY,
   193:       hourBucket: lastH,
   194:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   195:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   196:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   197:     });
   198: 
   199:     if (!nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
   200: 
   201:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
   202:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   203:       startSlot: { ymd: nextSlot.ymd, hour: nextSlot.hour },
   204:       drawsIndex,
   205:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   206:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   207:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   208:     });
   209: 
   210:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
   211:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
   212: 
   213:     const historyLastKey = drawKey(history[history.length - 1]);
   214:     const nextDrawKey = drawKey(nextDraw);
   215: 
   216:     if (historyLastKey === nextDrawKey) {
   217:       throw new Error(`Vazamento de futuro no histórico: ${nextDrawKey}`);
   218:     }
   219: 
   220:     const computed = computeConditionalNextTop3({
   221:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   222:       drawsRange: history,
   223:       drawLast,
   224:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   225:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   226:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   227:       topN: 3,
   228:     });
   229: 
   230:     assertComputedSlotMatchesNextDraw({
   231:       computed,
   232:       found,
   233:       nextSlot,
   234:       drawLast,
   235:     });
   236: 
   237:     assertPredictionIntegrity({
   238:       computed,
   239:       drawLast,
   240:       nextDraw,
   241:     });
   242: 
   243:     const pred = Array.isArray(computed?.top)
   244:       ? computed.top.slice(0, 3).map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
   245:       : [];
   246: 
   247:     const actualTop5 = top5Groups(nextDraw);
   248:     const actualGroups = actualTop5.map((x) => Number(x.grupo));
   249:     const actualFirst = actualTop5.find((x) => x.pos === 1)?.grupo ?? null;
   250: 
   251:     if (!actualGroups.length || !Number.isFinite(Number(actualFirst))) {
   252:       continue;
   253:     }
   254: 
   255:     const hasTop5 = pred.some((g) => actualGroups.includes(g));
   256:     const hasFirst = pred.includes(Number(actualFirst));
   257: 
   258:     totalCases += 1;
   259:     if (hasTop5) hitTop5 += 1;
   260:     if (hasFirst) hitFirst += 1;
   261: 
   262:     const scenario = computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE";
   263:     const prevHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.trigger?.hour || "") || "";
   264:     const nextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "") || "";
   265:     const prevGrupo = Number(computed?.meta?.trigger?.grupo || 0);
   266:     const edge = `${prevHour}->${nextHour}`;
   267: 
   268:     const rowScenario = ensureRow(byScenario, scenario);
   269:     rowScenario.total += 1;
   270:     if (hasTop5) rowScenario.hitTop5 += 1;
   271:     if (hasFirst) rowScenario.hitFirst += 1;
   272: 
   273:     const rowEdge = ensureRow(byEdge, edge);
   274:     rowEdge.total += 1;
   275:     if (hasTop5) rowEdge.hitTop5 += 1;
   276:     if (hasFirst) rowEdge.hitFirst += 1;
   277: 
   278:     const prevGrupoKey = String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0");
   279:     const rowPrevGrupo = ensureRow(byPrevGrupo, prevGrupoKey);
   280:     rowPrevGrupo.total += 1;
   281:     if (hasTop5) rowPrevGrupo.hitTop5 += 1;
   282:     if (hasFirst) rowPrevGrupo.hitFirst += 1;
   283:   }
   284: 
   285:   console.log("\n================ BACKTEST TOP3 PT_RIO (CAMADAS) ================");
   286:   console.log(`Janela: ${from} até ${maxDate}`);
   287:   console.log(`Casos testados: ${totalCases}`);
   288:   console.log(`TOP5 geral: ${hitTop5}/${totalCases} (${pct(hitTop5, totalCases)}%)`);
   289:   console.log(`1º geral: ${hitFirst}/${totalCases} (${pct(hitFirst, totalCases)}%)`);
   290:   console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------");
   291: 
   292:   console.log("POR CENÁRIO:");
   293:   for (const [key, row] of Array.from(byScenario.entries()).sort(
   294:     (a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total
   295:   )) {
   296:     console.log(
   297:       `${key} | casos=${row.total} | top5=${row.hitTop5}/${row.total} (${pct(
   298:         row.hitTop5,
   299:         row.total
   300:       )}%) | primeiro=${row.hitFirst}/${row.total} (${pct(
   301:         row.hitFirst,
   302:         row.total
   303:       )}%)`
   304:     );
   305:   }
   306: 
   307:   console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------");
   308:   console.log("POR TRANSIÇÃO:");
   309:   for (const [key, row] of Array.from(byEdge.entries()).sort()) {
   310:     console.log(
   311:       `${key} | casos=${row.total} | top5=${row.hitTop5}/${row.total} (${pct(
   312:         row.hitTop5,
   313:         row.total
   314:       )}%) | primeiro=${row.hitFirst}/${row.total} (${pct(
   315:         row.hitFirst,
   316:         row.total
   317:       )}%)`
   318:     );
   319:   }
   320: 
   321:   console.log("----------------------------------------------------------------");
   322:   console.log("TOP 15 GATILHOS ANTERIORES (por volume):");
   323:   for (const [key, row] of Array.from(byPrevGrupo.entries())
   324:     .sort((a, b) => b[1].total - a[1].total)
   325:     .slice(0, 15)) {
   326:     console.log(
   327:       `G${key} | casos=${row.total} | top5=${row.hitTop5}/${row.total} (${pct(
   328:         row.hitTop5,
   329:         row.total
   330:       )}%) | primeiro=${row.hitFirst}/${row.total} (${pct(
   331:         row.hitFirst,
   332:         row.total
   333:       )}%)`
   334:     );
   335:   }
   336: 
   337:   console.log("================================================================\n");
   338: 
   339:   expect(totalCases).toBeGreaterThan(0);
   340: });

   113: function assertPredictionIntegrity({ computed, drawLast, nextDraw }) {
   114:   const top = Array.isArray(computed?.top) ? computed.top.slice(0, 3) : [];
   115: 
   116:   if (!top.length) {
   117:     throw new Error(
   118:       [
   119:         "TOP3 vazio para caso com próximo sorteio real.",
   120:         `Último sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(drawLast)} ${toHourBucket(
   121:           pickDrawHour(drawLast)

   123:         `Próximo sorteio: ${pickDrawYMD(nextDraw)} ${toHourBucket(
   124:           pickDrawHour(nextDraw)
   125:         )}`,
   126:         `Scenario: ${computed?.meta?.scenario || "NONE"}`,
   127:       ].join("\n")
   128:     );
   129:   }
   130: 
   131:   const seen = new Set();
   132: 
   133:   for (const item of top) {
   134:     const grupo = Number(item?.grupo);
   135: 
   136:     if (!Number.isFinite(grupo) || grupo < 1 || grupo > 25) {
   137:       throw new Error(`Grupo inválido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   138:     }
   139: 
   140:     if (seen.has(grupo)) {
   141:       throw new Error(`Grupo repetido no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(top)}`);
   142:     }
   143: 
   144:     seen.add(grupo);
   145: 
   146:     const confidence = Number(
   147:       item?.displayConfidence ??
   148:         item?.confidence ??
   149:         item?.scoreProb ??
   150:         item?.prob ??
   151:         0
   152:     );
   153: 
   154:     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence)) {
   155:       throw new Error(`Confiança inválida no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   156:     }
   157: 
   158:     if (confidence < 0) {
   159:       throw new Error(`Confiança negativa no TOP3: ${JSON.stringify(item)}`);
   160:     }
   161:   }
   162: }
   163: 
   164: test("backtest TOP3 PT_RIO - últimos 180 dias", async () => {
   165:   const bounds = await getKingBoundsByUf({ uf: "PT_RIO" });
   166:   const maxDate = bounds?.maxYmd || bounds?.maxDate;
   167: 
   168:   expect(maxDate).toBeTruthy();
   169: 
   170:   const from = addDaysYMD(maxDate, -179);
   171:   const toExclusive = addDaysYMD(maxDate, 1);
   172: 
   173:   const raw = await getKingResultsByRange({
   174:     uf: "PT_RIO",
   175:     dateFrom: from,
   176:     dateTo: toExclusive,
   177:     mode: "detailed",
   178:     readPolicy: "server",
   179:   });
   180: 
   181:   const allDraws = dedupeAndSort(raw);
   182:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(allDraws);
   183: 
   184:   expect(allDraws.length).toBeGreaterThan(30);
   185: 
   186:   let totalCases = 0;
   187:   let hitTop5 = 0;
   188:   let hitFirst = 0;
   189: 
   190:   const byTargetHour = new Map();
   191: 
   192:   for (let i = 0; i < allDraws.length - 1; i += 1) {
   193:     const drawLast = allDraws[i];
   194:     const history = allDraws.slice(0, i + 1);
   195: 
   196:     const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
   197:     const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
   198: 
   199:     if (!lastY || !lastH) continue;
   200: 
   201:     const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
   202:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   203:       ymd: lastY,
   204:       hourBucket: lastH,
   205:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   206:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   207:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   208:     });
   209: 
   210:     if (!nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
   211: 
   212:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
   213:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   214:       startSlot: { ymd: nextSlot.ymd, hour: nextSlot.hour },
   215:       drawsIndex,
   216:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   217:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   218:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   219:     });
   220: 
   221:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
   222:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
   223: 
   224:     if (drawKey(history[history.length - 1]) === drawKey(nextDraw)) {
   225:       throw new Error(`Vazamento de futuro no histórico: ${drawKey(nextDraw)}`);
   226:     }
   227: 
   228:     const computed = computeConditionalNextTop3({
   229:       lotteryKey: "PT_RIO",
   230:       drawsRange: history,
   231:       drawLast,
   232:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   233:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   234:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   235:       topN: 3,
   236:     });
   237: 
   238:     assertComputedSlotMatchesNextDraw({
   239:       computed,
   240:       found,
   241:       nextSlot,
   242:       drawLast,
   243:     });
   244: 
   245:     assertPredictionIntegrity({
   246:       computed,
   247:       drawLast,
   248:       nextDraw,
   249:     });
   250: 
   251:     const pred = Array.isArray(computed?.top)
   252:       ? computed.top.slice(0, 3).map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
   253:       : [];
   254: 
   255:     const actualTop5 = top5Groups(nextDraw);
   256:     const actualGroups = actualTop5.map((x) => Number(x.grupo));
   257:     const actualFirst = actualTop5.find((x) => x.pos === 1)?.grupo ?? null;
   258: 
   259:     if (!actualGroups.length || !Number.isFinite(Number(actualFirst))) {
   260:       continue;
   261:     }
   262: 
   263:     const hasTop5 = pred.some((g) => actualGroups.includes(g));
   264:     const hasFirst = pred.includes(Number(actualFirst));
   265: 
   266:     totalCases += 1;
   267:     if (hasTop5) hitTop5 += 1;
   268:     if (hasFirst) hitFirst += 1;
   269: 
   270:     const targetHour =
   271:       found?.hour || found?.slot?.hour || pickDrawHour(nextDraw) || "";
   272: 
   273:     const row = ensureHourRow(byTargetHour, targetHour);
   274:     row.total += 1;
   275:     if (hasTop5) row.hitTop5 += 1;
   276:     if (hasFirst) row.hitFirst += 1;
   277:   }
   278: 
   279:   console.log("\n================ BACKTEST TOP3 PT_RIO ================");
   280:   console.log(`Janela: ${from} até ${maxDate}`);
   281:   console.log(`Casos testados: ${totalCases}`);
   282:   console.log(
   283:     `Acerto TOP3 dentro do TOP5: ${hitTop5} / ${totalCases} = ${pct(
   284:       hitTop5,
   285:       totalCases
   286:     )}%`
   287:   );
   288:   console.log(
   289:     `Acerto TOP3 contendo o 1º prêmio: ${hitFirst} / ${totalCases} = ${pct(
   290:       hitFirst,
   291:       totalCases
   292:     )}%`
   293:   );
   294:   console.log("------------------------------------------------------");
   295: 
   296:   for (const [hour, row] of Array.from(byTargetHour.entries()).sort()) {
   297:     console.log(
   298:       `${hour} | casos=${row.total} | top5=${row.hitTop5}/${row.total} (${pct(
   299:         row.hitTop5,
   300:         row.total
   301:       )}%) | primeiro=${row.hitFirst}/${row.total} (${pct(
   302:         row.hitFirst,
   303:         row.total
   304:       )}%)`
   305:     );
   306:   }
   307: 
   308:   console.log("======================================================\n");
   309: 
   310:   expect(totalCases).toBeGreaterThan(0);
   311: });

     1: import { isYMD, toHourBucket } from "../top3.formatters";
     2: 
     3: import {
     4:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
     5:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
     6:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
     7: } from "../top3.constants";
     8: 
     9: import {
    10:   pickDrawHour,
    11:   pickDrawYMD,
    12:   pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw,
    13:   computeStatisticalTop3V3,
    14: } from "../top3.engine";
    15: 
    16: function emptyAnalytics() {
    17:   return { top: [], meta: null };
    18: }
    19: 
    20: function safeCacheRef(ref) {
    21:   if (!ref || typeof ref !== "object") {
    22:     return { current: { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() } };
    23:   }
    24: 
    25:   if (!ref.current || typeof ref.current !== "object") {
    26:     ref.current = { key: "", value: emptyAnalytics() };
    27:   }
    28: 
    29:   return ref;
    30: }
    31: 
    32: function sameSlot(meta, targetYmd, targetHour) {
    33:   const targetY = String(targetYmd || "").trim();
    34:   const targetH = toHourBucket(targetHour || "");
    35: 
    36:   const y = String(meta?.next?.ymd || "").trim();
    37:   const h = toHourBucket(meta?.next?.hour || "");
    38: 
    39:   return isYMD(targetY) && !!targetH && y === targetY && h === targetH;
    40: }
    41: 
    42: function firstFiniteNumber(...values) {
    43:   for (const v of values) {
    44:     const n = Number(v);
    45:     if (Number.isFinite(n)) return n;
    46:   }
    47: 
    48:   return NaN;
    49: }
    50: 
    51: function sanitizeTop3(top) {
    52:   const out = [];
    53:   const seen = new Set();
    54: 
    55:   for (const item of Array.isArray(top) ? top : []) {
    56:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
    57: 
    58:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) continue;
    59: 
    60:     const grupo = Math.trunc(g);
    61:     if (seen.has(grupo)) continue;
    62: 
    63:     const confidence = firstFiniteNumber(
    64:       item?.displayConfidence,
    65:       item?.confidence,
    66:       item?.scoreProb,
    67:       item?.prob,
    68:       0
    69:     );
    70: 
    71:     if (!Number.isFinite(confidence) || confidence < 0) continue;
    72: 
    73:     seen.add(grupo);
    74: 
    75:     out.push({
    76:       ...item,
    77:       grupo,
    78:       displayConfidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.displayConfidence))
    79:         ? Number(item.displayConfidence)
    80:         : confidence,
    81:       confidence: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.confidence))
    82:         ? Number(item.confidence)
    83:         : confidence,
    84:       scoreProb: Number.isFinite(Number(item?.scoreProb))
    85:         ? Number(item.scoreProb)
    86:         : confidence,
    87:     });
    88: 
    89:     if (out.length >= 3) break;
    90:   }
    91: 
    92:   return out;
    93: }
    94: 
    95: export function computeTop3Analytics({
    96:   rangeDraws,
    97:   baseDrawState,
    98:   analyticsCacheRef,
    99:   lotteryKeySafe,
   100:   lookback,
   101:   rangeInfo,
   102:   todayDraws,
   103:   sanitizeHistoricalDraws,
   104:   targetYmd = "",
   105:   targetHourBucket = "",
   106: }) {
   107:   const cacheRef = safeCacheRef(analyticsCacheRef);
   108:   const lotteryKey = String(lotteryKeySafe || "").trim();
   109: 
   110:   const rawList = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
   111:   const drawLast = baseDrawState || null;
   112: 
   113:   if (!rawList.length || !drawLast || !lotteryKey) {
   114:     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
   115:     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
   116:     return empty;
   117:   }
   118: 
   119:   const lastG = Number(pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(drawLast));
   120:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast) || "";
   121:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast)) || "";
   122: 
   123:   const forcedTargetY = isYMD(targetYmd) ? targetYmd : "";
   124:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourBucket);
   125: 
   126:   if (
   127:     !Number.isFinite(lastG) ||
   128:     lastG < 1 ||
   129:     lastG > 25 ||
   130:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
   131:     !lastH ||
   132:     !isYMD(forcedTargetY) ||
   133:     !forcedTargetH
   134:   ) {
   135:     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
   136:     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
   137:     return empty;
   138:   }
   139: 
   140:   const historicalList =
   141:     typeof sanitizeHistoricalDraws === "function"
   142:       ? sanitizeHistoricalDraws({
   143:           draws: rawList,
   144:           lotteryKey,
   145:           baseDraw: drawLast,
   146:         })
   147:       : rawList;
   148: 
   149:   const safeHistoricalList = Array.isArray(historicalList)
   150:     ? historicalList
   151:     : [];
   152: 
   153:   if (!safeHistoricalList.length) {
   154:     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
   155:     cacheRef.current = { key: "", value: empty };
   156:     return empty;
   157:   }
   158: 
   159:   const firstDraw = safeHistoricalList[0] || null;
   160:   const lastDrawInRange =
   161:     safeHistoricalList[safeHistoricalList.length - 1] || null;
   162: 
   163:   const todaySignature = (Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [])
   164:     .map((d) => `${pickDrawYMD(d) || ""}@${toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)) || ""}`)
   165:     .filter(Boolean)
   166:     .join(",");
   167: 
   168:   const cacheKey = [
   169:     "V3",
   170:     lotteryKey,
   171:     lookback,
   172:     rangeInfo?.from || "",
   173:     rangeInfo?.to || "",
   174:     safeHistoricalList.length,
   175:     rawList.length,
   176:     todaySignature,
   177:     firstDraw ? pickDrawYMD(firstDraw) || "" : "",
   178:     firstDraw ? toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(firstDraw)) || "" : "",
   179:     lastDrawInRange ? pickDrawYMD(lastDrawInRange) || "" : "",
   180:     lastDrawInRange ? toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(lastDrawInRange)) || "" : "",
   181:     lastY,
   182:     lastH,
   183:     Math.trunc(lastG),
   184:     forcedTargetY,
   185:     forcedTargetH,
   186:   ].join("|");
   187: 
   188:   if (cacheRef.current?.key === cacheKey) {
   189:     return cacheRef.current.value || emptyAnalytics();
   190:   }
   191: 
   192:   const computed =
   193:     computeStatisticalTop3V3({
   194:       lotteryKey,
   195:       drawsRange: safeHistoricalList,
   196:       drawLast,
   197:       drawsToday: Array.isArray(todayDraws) ? todayDraws : [],
   198:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   199:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   200:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   201:       topN: 3,
   202:       targetYmdOverride: forcedTargetY,
   203:       targetHourOverride: forcedTargetH,
   204:     }) || emptyAnalytics();
   205: 
   206:   if (!sameSlot(computed?.meta, forcedTargetY, forcedTargetH)) {
   207:     const empty = emptyAnalytics();
   208:     cacheRef.current = { key: cacheKey, value: empty };
   209:     return empty;
   210:   }
   211: 
   212:   const value = {
   213:     ...computed,
   214:     top: sanitizeTop3(computed?.top),
   215:   };
   216: 
   217:   cacheRef.current = { key: cacheKey, value };
   218:   return value;
   219: }

    32: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
    33:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
    34:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
    35:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
    36: 
    37:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
    38: 
    39:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
    40: 
    41:   return list
    42:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
    43:     .map((draw) => {
    44:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    45:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    46:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
    47:     })
    48:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
    49:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
    50: }
    51: 
    52: /* =========================
    53:    Draw helpers (robustos)
    54: ========================= */
    55: 
    56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
    57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
    58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
    59: 
    60:   const n = Number(s);
    61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
    62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
    63: 
    64:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
    65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
    66: }
    67: 
    68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
    69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
    70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    72: 
    73:   let score = 0;
    74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
    75:   if (h) score += 20;
    76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
    77: 
    78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
    79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
    80: 
    81:   return score;
    82: }
    83: 
    84: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
    85:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
    86:     ? Number(p.position)
    87:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
    88:       ? Number(p.posicao)
    89:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
    90:         ? Number(p.pos)
    91:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
    92:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
    93:           : null;
    94: 
    95:   return pos;
    96: }
    97: 
    98: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
    99:   const directCandidates = [
   100:     p?.grupo2,
   101:     p?.group2,
   102:     p?.grupo,
   103:     p?.group,
   104:     p?.animal_grupo,
   105:     p?.grupo_animal,
   106:     p?.grupoAnimal,
   107:     p?.g,
   108:   ];
   109: 
   110:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
   111:     const g = Number(v);
   112:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
   113:       return g;
   114:     }
   115:   }
   116: 
   117:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
   118: 
   119:   if (milhar4) {
   120:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
   121:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
   122: 
   123:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
   124:   }
   125: 
   126:   return null;
   127: }
   128: 
   129: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {
   130:   return normalizeHourLike(
   131:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   132:   );
   133: }
   134: 
   135: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
   136:   const y =
   137:     draw?.ymd ||
   138:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
   139:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
   140:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
   141:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
   142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
   143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
   144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
   145:     null;
   146: 
   147:   return y;
   148: }
   149: 
   150: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   151:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   152:   if (prizes.length) {
   153:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   154:     if (p1) {
   155:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   156:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
   157:     }
   158:   }
   159: 
   160:   const candidates = [
   161:     draw?.grupo1,
   162:     draw?.group1,
   163:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
   164:     draw?.first_grupo,
   165:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
   166:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
   167:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
   168:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
   169:     draw?.g1,
   170:     draw?.grupo_1,
   171:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
   172:   ];
   173: 
   174:   for (const v of candidates) {
   175:     if (Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return Number(v);
   176:   }
   177: 
   178:   return null;
   179: }
   180: 
   181: /* =========================
   182:    Helpers internos
   183: ========================= */
   184: 
   185: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
   186:   const n = Number(v);
   187:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
   188: }
   189: 
   190: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
   191:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
   192:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
   193: }
   194: 
   195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
   196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   198: 
   199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   200: 
   201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
   203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   204:   }
   205: 
   206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   208: 
   209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
   210: }
   211: 
   212: /* =========================
   213:    Schedules
   214: ========================= */
   215: 
   216: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
   217:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
   218:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
   219: }
   220: 
   221: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   222:   ymd,
   223:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   224:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   225: ) {
   226:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
   227:   if (dow === 0) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY;
   228:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT;
   229:   return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL;
   230: }
   231: 
   232: export function getScheduleForLottery({
   233:   lotteryKey,
   234:   ymd,
   235:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   236:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   237:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   238: }) {
   239:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   240: 
   241:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   242:     return isFederalDrawDay(ymd) ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE : [];
   243:   }
   244: 
   245:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   246:     ymd,
   247:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   248:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   249:   );
   250: }
   251: 
   252: export function scheduleSet(schedule) {
   253:   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));
   254: }
   255: 
   256: export function isHourInSchedule(schedule, hhmm) {
   257:   const s = scheduleSet(schedule);
   258:   return s.has(toHourBucket(hhmm));
   259: }
   260: 
   261: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
   262:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   263:   if (!list.length) return null;
   264: 
   265:   const sorted = [...list]
   266:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
   267:     .sort((a, b) => {
   268:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
   269:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
   270:       return tb - ta;
   271:     });
   272: 
   273:   return sorted[0] || null;
   274: }
   275: 
   276: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
   277:   draws,
   278:   targetHourBucket,
   279:   schedule
   280: ) {
   281:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   282:   if (!list.length) return null;
   283: 
   284:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
   285:   if (t < 0) return null;
   286: 
   287:   const candidates = list
   288:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
   289:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
   290:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
   291:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
   292: 
   293:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
   294: }
   295: 
   296: export async function getPreviousDrawRobust({
   297:   getKingResultsByDate,
   298:   lotteryKey,
   299:   ymdTarget,
   300:   targetHourBucket,
   301:   todayDraws,
   302:   schedule,
   303:   maxBackDays = 10,
   304:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   305:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   306:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   307: }) {
   308:   const prevSameDay = findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
   309:     todayDraws,
   310:     targetHourBucket,
   311:     schedule
   312:   );
   313: 
   314:   if (prevSameDay) {
   315:     return {
   316:       draw: prevSameDay,
   317:       ymd: pickDrawYMD(prevSameDay) || ymdTarget,
   318:       hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevSameDay)),
   319:       source: "same_day",
   320:     };
   321:   }
   322: 
   323:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxBackDays; i += 1) {
   324:     const day = addDaysYMD(ymdTarget, -i);
   325: 
   326:     const daySchedule = getScheduleForLottery({
   327:       lotteryKey,
   328:       ymd: day,
   329:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   330:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   331:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   332:     });
   333: 
   334:     if (
   335:       safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase() === "FEDERAL" &&
   336:       !daySchedule.length
   337:     ) {
   338:       continue;
   339:     }
   340: 
   341:     const out = await getKingResultsByDate({
   342:       uf: lotteryKey,
   343:       date: day,
   344:       closeHour: null,
   345:       positions: null,
   346:       readPolicy: "server",
   347:     });
   348: 
   349:     const last = findLastDrawInList(out, daySchedule);
   350:     if (last) {
   351:       return {
   352:         draw: last,
   353:         ymd: pickDrawYMD(last) || day,
   354:         hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(last)),
   355:         source: "prev_day",
   356:       };
   357:     }
   358:   }
   359: 
   360:   return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
   361: }
   362: 
   363: /* =========================
   364:    Próximo sorteio (slot válido)
   365: ========================= */
   366: 
   367: export function getNextSlotForLottery({
   368:   lotteryKey,
   369:   ymd,
   370:   hourBucket,
   371:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   372:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   373:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   374:   maxForwardDays = 21,
   375: }) {
   376:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   377:   const y0 = safeStr(ymd);
   378:   const h0 = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   379: 
   380:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !safeStr(h0)) return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   381: 
   382:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   383:     for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
   384:       const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
   385:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   386:         lotteryKey: key,
   387:         ymd: day,
   388:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   389:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   390:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   391:       });
   392:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   393:         return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
   394:       }
   395:     }
   396:     return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   397:   }
   398: 
   399:   const sch0 = getScheduleForLottery({
   400:     lotteryKey: key,
   401:     ymd: y0,
   402:     hourBucket: h0,
   403:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   404:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   405:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   406:   });
   407: 
   408:   const idx = (Array.isArray(sch0) ? sch0 : []).findIndex(
   409:     (x) => toHourBucket(x) === h0
   410:   );
   411: 
   412:   if (idx >= 0 && idx < sch0.length - 1) {
   413:     return { ymd: y0, hour: toHourBucket(sch0[idx + 1]) };
   414:   }
   415: 
   416:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
   417:     const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
   418:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   419:       lotteryKey: key,
   420:       ymd: day,
   421:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   422:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   423:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   424:     });
   425:     if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   426:       return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
   427:     }
   428:   }
   429: 
   430:   return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   431: }
   432: 
   433: /* =========================
   434:    Próximo DRAW REAL (não perde amostra)
   435: ========================= */
   436: 
   437: export function findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
   438:   lotteryKey,
   439:   startSlot,
   440:   drawsIndex,
   441:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   442:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   443:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   444:   maxSteps = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_STEPS,
   445:   maxDays = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_DAYS,
   446: }) {
   447:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   448:   const y0 = safeStr(startSlot?.ymd);
   449:   const h0 = toHourBucket(startSlot?.hour);
   450: 
   451:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !h0 || !(drawsIndex instanceof Map)) {
   452:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
   453:   }
   454: 
   455:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   456:     let curY = y0;
   457:     let steps = 0;
   458:     let daysWalked = 0;
   459: 
   460:     while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
   461:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   462:         lotteryKey: key,
   463:         ymd: curY,
   464:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   465:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   466:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   467:       });
   468: 
   469:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   470:         const hh = toHourBucket(sch[0]);
   471:         const d = drawsIndex.get(`${curY}|${hh}`) || null;
   472:         if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: hh }, draw: d };
   473:       }
   474: 
   475:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
   476:       daysWalked += 1;
   477:       steps += 1;
   478:     }
   479: 
   480:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
   481:   }
   482: 
   483:   let curY = y0;
   484:   let curH = h0;
   485:   let steps = 0;
   486:   let daysWalked = 0;
   487: 
   488:   while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
   489:     const drawKey = `${curY}|${toHourBucket(curH)}`;
   490:     let d = drawsIndex.get(drawKey) || null;
   491: 
   492:     if (!d) {
   493:       const curTs = ymdHourToTs(curY, curH);
   494:       const curYLocked = curY;
   495:       d = Array.from(drawsIndex.entries())
   496:         .map(([k, v]) => {
   497:           const [ky, kh] = String(k).split("|");
   498:           return { ymd: ky, hour: kh, draw: v, ts: ymdHourToTs(ky, kh) };
   499:         })
   500:         .filter((x) => x.ymd === curYLocked)
   501:         .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts >= curTs)
   502:         .sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
   503:     }
   504: 
   505:     if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: toHourBucket(curH) }, draw: d };
   506: 
   507:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   508:       lotteryKey: key,
   509:       ymd: curY,
   510:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   511:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   512:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   513:     });
   514: 
   515:     const arr = Array.isArray(sch) ? sch.map(toHourBucket) : [];
   516:     const curBucket = toHourBucket(curH);
   517:     const idx = arr.indexOf(curBucket);
   518: 
   519:     if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length - 1) {
   520:       curH = arr[idx + 1];
   521:     } else {
   522:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
   523:       daysWalked += 1;
   524: 
   525:       const sch2 = getScheduleForLottery({
   526:         lotteryKey: key,
   527:         ymd: curY,
   528:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   529:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   530:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   531:       });
   532: 
   533:       const arr2 = Array.isArray(sch2) ? sch2.map(toHourBucket) : [];
   534:       if (!arr2.length) break;
   535:       curH = arr2[0];
   536:     }
   537: 
   538:     steps += 1;
   539:   }
   540: 
   541:   return { slot: null, draw: null };
   542: }
   543: 
   544: /* =========================
   545:    Index + utilitários de grupo
   546: ========================= */
   547: 
   548: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
   549:   const map = new Map();
   550:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   551: 
   552:   for (const d of list) {
   553:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   554:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
   555:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
   556: 
   557:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
   558: 
   559:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
   560:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
   561: 
   562:     if (!prev) {
   563:       map.set(drawKey, d);
   564:       continue;
   565:     }
   566: 
   567:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
   568:       map.set(drawKey, d);
   569:     }
   570:   }
   571: 
   572:   return map;
   573: }
   574: 
   575: export function computeLastSeenByGrupo(draws) {
   576:   const last = new Map();
   577:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   578: 
   579:   for (const d of list) {
   580:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   581:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   582:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
   583: 
   584:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
   585:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   586: 
   587:     for (const p of ps) {
   588:       const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   589:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   590:         continue;
   591:       }
   592: 
   593:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   594:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   595: 
   596:       const gg = Number(g);
   597:       const prev = last.get(gg);
   598: 
   599:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
   600:         last.set(gg, ts);
   601:       }
   602:     }
   603:   }
   604: 
   605:   return last;
   606: }
