   608: export function countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(draw) {
   609:   const counts = new Map();
   610:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   611:   for (const p of ps) {
   612:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   613:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   614:       continue;
   615:     }
   616:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   617:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   618:     const gg = Number(g);
   619:     counts.set(gg, (counts.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
   620:   }
   621:   return counts;
   622: }
   623: 
   624: function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   625:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   626:   const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   627:   if (p1) {
   628:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   629:     return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
   630:   }
   631:   return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
   632: }
   633: 
   634: function getAllTop5Groups(draw) {
   635:   const out = [];
   636:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   637:   for (const p of ps) {
   638:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   639:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   640:       continue;
   641:     }
   642:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   643:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   644:     out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
   645:   }
   646:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   647: }
   648: 
   649: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
   650:   const freq = new Map();
   651:   const firstMap = new Map();
   652:   const items = getAllTop5Groups(nextDraw);
   653: 
   654:   for (const it of items) {
   655:     freq.set(it.grupo, (freq.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
   656:     if (it.pos === 1) {
   657:       firstMap.set(it.grupo, (firstMap.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
   658:     }
   659:   }
   660: 
   661:   return { freq, firstMap };
   662: }
   663: 
   664: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
   665:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
   666:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
   667:   }
   668: }
   669: 
   670: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   671:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   672:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
   673:   const out = new Map();
   674: 
   675:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   676:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
   677:     out.set(g, n / denom);
   678:   }
   679: 
   680:   return out;
   681: }
   682: 
   683: function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
   684:   draws,
   685:   lotteryKey,
   686:   targetHour,
   687:   targetDow,
   688:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
   689: ) {
   690:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   691:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   692:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
   693:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   694: 
   695:   const freq = new Map();
   696:   let totalSamples = 0;
   697: 
   698:   for (const d of list) {
   699:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   700:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   701:     if (!h || !y) continue;
   702: 
   703:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   704:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
   705:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
   706:     } else {
   707:       if (h !== target) continue;
   708:     }
   709: 
   710:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
   711:     if (!items.length) continue;
   712: 
   713:     totalSamples += 1;
   714: 
   715:     for (const it of items) {
   716:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   717:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   718:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
   719:     }
   720:   }
   721: 
   722:   const out = new Map();
   723:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
   724: 
   725:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   726:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
   727:     out.set(g, n / denom);
   728:   }
   729: 
   730:   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
   731: }
   732: 
   733: /* =========================
   734:    Recência / pressão estrutural
   735: ========================= */
   736: 
   737: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
   738:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   739:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
   740:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
   741:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
   742:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
   743:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
   744:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
   745:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
   746:     return tsa - tsb;
   747:   });
   748: }
   749: 
   750: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
   751:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
   752:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
   753:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
   754:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
   755: 
   756:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
   757:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   758:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   759:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
   760:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
   761:   });
   762: 
   763:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
   764: }
   765: 
   766: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   767:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   768:   const out = new Map();
   769: 
   770:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   771:     out.set(g, {
   772:       recentTop5: 0,
   773:       recentIndirect: 0,
   774:       recentFirst: 0,
   775:       recentLast1First: 0,
   776:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
   777:     });
   778:   }
   779: 
   780:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
   781:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
   782:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
   783: 
   784:   for (const d of list) {
   785:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
   786: 
   787:     for (const it of items) {
   788:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   789:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
   790:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   791: 
   792:       const row = out.get(g);
   793:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
   794: 
   795:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
   796:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
   797:     }
   798:   }
   799: 
   800:   for (const d of last1) {
   801:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
   802:     for (const it of items) {
   803:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   804:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
   805:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   806:       if (pos === 1) {
   807:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
   808:       }
   809:     }
   810:   }
   811: 
   812:   for (const d of last2) {
   813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
   814:     const seen = new Set();
   815:     for (const it of items) {
   816:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   817:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   818:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
   819:       seen.add(g);
   820:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
   821:     }
   822:   }
   823: 
   824:   return out;
   825: }
   826: 
   827: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
   828:   const v = Number(value || 0);
   829:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
   830:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
   831:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
   832: }
   833: 
   834: function clamp01(v) {
   835:   const n = Number(v);
   836:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
   837:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
   838: }
   839: 
   840: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   841:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   842:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
   843: }
   844: 
   845: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
   846:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
   847:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
   848:   return clamp01(n / lim);
   849: }
   850: 
   851: /* =========================
   852:    Motor condicional puro por camadas
   853: ========================= */
   854: 
   855: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
   856:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
   857:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
   858:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
   859:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
   860:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
   861:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
   862: 
   863:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
   864:   FED_PREVG: 4,
   865:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
   866:   FED_ONLY: 10,
   867: };
   868: 
   869: function buildLayerConfigs({
   870:   lotteryKey,
   871:   targetDayOfMonth,
   872:   targetDow,
   873:   prevHour,
   874:   prevGrupo,
   875:   targetHour,
   876: }) {
   877:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   878: 
   879:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   880:     return [
   881:       {
   882:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
   883:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
   884:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
   885:         match: (ctx) =>
   886:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
   887:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
   888:       },
   889:       {
   890:         key: "FED_PREVG",
   891:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
   892:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
   893:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
   894:       },
   895:       {
   896:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
   897:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
   898:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
   899:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
   900:       },
   901:       {
   902:         key: "FED_ONLY",
   903:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
   904:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
   905:         match: () => true,
   906:       },
   907:     ];
   908:   }
   909: 
   910:   return [
   911:     {
   912:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
   913:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
   914:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
   915:       match: (ctx) =>
   916:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
   917:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
   918:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
   919:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
   920:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   921:     },
   922:     {
   923:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
   924:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
   925:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
   926:       match: (ctx) =>
   927:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
   928:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
   929:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
   930:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   931:     },
   932:     {
   933:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
   934:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
   935:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
   936:       match: (ctx) =>
   937:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
   938:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
   939:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   940:     },
   941:     {
   942:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
   943:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
   944:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
   945:       match: (ctx) =>
   946:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
   947:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   948:     },
   949:     {
   950:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
   951:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
   952:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
   953:       match: (ctx) =>
   954:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
   955:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   956:     },
   957:     {
   958:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
   959:       label: "alvo geral",
   960:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
   961:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
   962:     },
   963:   ];
   964: }
   965: 
   966: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
   967:   lotteryKey,
   968:   drawsRange,
   969:   targetHour,
   970:   targetDow,
   971:   targetDayOfMonth,
   972:   prevHour,
   973:   prevGrupo,
   974:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   975:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   976:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   977: }) {
   978:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
   979:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
   980: 
   981:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
   982:     lotteryKey,
   983:     targetDayOfMonth,
   984:     targetDow,
   985:     prevHour,
   986:     prevGrupo,
   987:     targetHour,
   988:   });
   989: 
   990:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
   991:     key: layer.key,
   992:     label: layer.label,
   993:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
   994:     samples: 0,
   995:     freq: new Map(),
   996:     firstFreq: new Map(),
   997:     matchingDraws: [],
   998:   }));
   999: 
  1000:   for (const d of list) {
  1001:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1002:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1003:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
  1004: 
  1005:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1006:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
  1007: 
  1008:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
  1009:       lotteryKey,
  1010:       ymd: prevY,
  1011:       hourBucket: prevH,
  1012:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1013:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1014:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1015:     });
  1016: 
  1017:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
  1018: 
  1019:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
  1020:       lotteryKey,
  1021:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
  1022:       drawsIndex,
  1023:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1024:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1025:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1026:     });
  1027: 
  1028:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
  1029:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
  1030:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
  1031: 
  1032:     const ctx = {
  1033:       prevYmd: prevY,
  1034:       prevHour: prevH,
  1035:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
  1036:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
  1037:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
  1038:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
  1039:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
  1040:     };
  1041: 
  1042:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
  1043: 
  1044:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
  1045:       const layer = layers[i];
  1046:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
  1047: 
  1048:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
  1049:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
  1050:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
  1051:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
  1052:     }
  1053:   }
  1054: 
  1055:   let chosen =
  1056:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
  1057:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
  1058:     null;
  1059: 
  1060:   if (!chosen) {
  1061:     chosen = {
  1062:       key: "NONE",
  1063:       label: "sem amostra",
  1064:       minSamples: 0,
  1065:       samples: 0,
  1066:       freq: new Map(),
  1067:       firstFreq: new Map(),
  1068:       matchingDraws: [],
  1069:     };
  1070:   }
  1071: 
  1072:   return {
  1073:     chosen,
  1074:     layers: layerResults,
  1075:   };
  1076: }
  1077: 
  1078: /* =========================
  1079:    Motor principal V1
  1080: ========================= */
  1081: 
  1082: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
  1083:   lotteryKey,
  1084:   drawsRange,
  1085:   drawLast,
  1086:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1087:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1088:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1089:   topN = 3,
  1090:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  1091:   targetHourOverride = "",
  1092: }) {
  1093:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  1094:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
  1095:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1096:   }
  1097: 
  1098:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1099: 
  1100:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  1101:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  1102:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  1103: 
  1104:   if (
  1105:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  1106:     !lastH ||
  1107:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  1108:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
  1109:   ) {
  1110:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1111:   }
  1112: 
  1113:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  1114:     lotteryKey,
  1115:     ymd: lastY,
  1116:     hourBucket: lastH,
  1117:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1118:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1119:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1120:   });
  1121: 
  1122:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  1123:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  1124: 
  1125:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  1126:     ? forcedTargetY
  1127:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  1128: 
  1129:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  1130:     ? forcedTargetH
  1131:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  1132:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  1133:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  1134:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
  1135: 
  1136:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
  1137:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
  1138:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
  1139: 
  1140:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
  1141:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1142:   }
  1143: 
  1144:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  1145:     lotteryKey,
  1146:     drawsRange: list,
  1147:     targetHour: targetH,
  1148:     targetDow,
  1149:     targetDayOfMonth,
  1150:     prevHour: lastH,
  1151:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1152:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1153:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1154:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1155:   });
  1156: 
  1157:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
  1158:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
  1159:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
  1160:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
  1161:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  1162: 
  1163:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  1164:     list,
  1165:     key,
  1166:     targetH,
  1167:     targetDow,
  1168:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1169:   );
  1170:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
  1171: 
  1172:   const condWeight =
  1173:     key === "FEDERAL"
  1174:       ? samples >= 8
  1175:         ? 0.55
  1176:         : samples >= 4
  1177:           ? 0.4
  1178:           : 0.25
  1179:       : samples >= 12
  1180:         ? 0.65
  1181:         : samples >= 6
  1182:           ? 0.55
  1183:           : 0.45;
  1184: 
  1185:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
  1186: 
  1187:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
  1188:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  1189: 
  1190:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
  1191:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
  1192:   );
  1193:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
  1194:   const maxGapMsBase =
  1195:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
  1196:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
  1197:       : 1;
  1198: 
  1199:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
  1200:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  1201: 
  1202:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
  1203:     1,
  1204:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
  1205:   );
  1206:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
  1207:     1,
  1208:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
  1209:   );
  1210:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
  1211:     1,
  1212:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
  1213:   );
  1214: 
  1215:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  1216:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  1217:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
  1218:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
  1219:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
  1220:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
  1221:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
  1222: 
  1223:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
  1224:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  1225: 
  1226:     const gapMs =
  1227:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
  1228:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
  1229:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
  1230:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
  1231:         : maxGapMsBase;
  1232: 
  1233:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
  1234:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
  1235:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
  1236: 
  1237:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
  1238:       recentTop5: 0,
  1239:       recentIndirect: 0,
  1240:       recentFirst: 0,
  1241:       recentLast1First: 0,
  1242:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  1243:     };
  1244: 
  1245:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
  1246:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
  1247:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
  1248:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
  1249:     const recentLast2Top5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
  1250: 
  1251:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
  1252:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
  1253:         ? 0.72
  1254:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
  1255:           ? 0.84
  1256:           : 1.0;
  1257: 
  1258:     const smartLateBoost =
  1259:       lateBonus *
  1260:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2Top5Norm * 0.2)) *
  1261:       recencyCoolingFactor;
  1262: 
  1263:     const recentComposite =
  1264:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.45) +
  1265:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
  1266:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
  1267: 
  1268:     const baseScore =
  1269:       key === "FEDERAL"
  1270:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
  1271:           (pBase * 85) +
  1272:           (primeiros * 42) +
  1273:           (aparicoes * 18) +
  1274:           (recentComposite * 26) +
  1275:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
  1276:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
  1277:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
  1278:             (aparicoes * 155) +
  1279:             (primeiros * 70) +
  1280:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
  1281:             (recentComposite * 18) +
  1282:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
  1283:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
  1284:           : (pFinal * 125) +
  1285:             (aparicoes * 135) +
  1286:             (primeiros * 62) +
  1287:             (recentComposite * 24) +
  1288:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
  1289:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
  1290: 
  1291:     return {
  1292:       grupo,
  1293:       aparicoes,
  1294:       primeiros,
  1295:       taxaPrimeiro,
  1296:       scoreProb: pFinal,
  1297:       probCond: pCond,
  1298:       probBase: pBase,
  1299:       score: baseScore,
  1300:       lateBonus,
  1301:       smartLateBoost,
  1302:       recentTop5: rm.recentTop5,
  1303:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
  1304:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
  1305:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
  1306:       recentLast2Top5: rm.recentLast2Top5,
  1307:       recentComposite,
  1308:       lastSeenTs,
  1309:       gapMs,
  1310:     };
  1311:   })
  1312:     .sort((a, b) => {
  1313:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  1314:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
  1315:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
  1316:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
  1317:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  1318:     })
  1319:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  1320: 
  1321:   const reasonsBase = [
  1322:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
  1323:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
  1324:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
  1325:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  1326:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
  1327:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  1328:   ];
  1329: 
  1330:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
  1331:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  1332:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
  1333:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
  1334:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
  1335: 
  1336:     return {
  1337:       rank: idx + 1,
  1338:       title:
  1339:         idx === 0
  1340:           ? "Mais provável"
  1341:           : idx === 1
  1342:             ? "2º mais provável"
  1343:             : "3º mais provável",
  1344:       grupo: x.grupo,
  1345:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
  1346:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
  1347:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
  1348:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
  1349:       freq: x.aparicoes,
  1350:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
  1351:       freqBase: 0,
  1352:       freqZeroWhy:
  1353:         x.aparicoes <= 0
  1354:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
  1355:           : "",
  1356:       reasons: [
  1357:         ...reasonsBase,
  1358:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
  1359:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
  1360:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
  1361:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
  1362:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
  1363:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentTop5}`,
  1364:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  1365:       ],
  1366:       meta: {
  1367:         trigger: {
  1368:           ymd: lastY,
  1369:           hour: lastH,
  1370:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1371:         },
  1372:         next: {
  1373:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  1374:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  1375:         },
  1376:         samples,
  1377:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1378:         explain: {
  1379:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1380:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  1381:           layerSamples: samples,
  1382:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
  1383:           condWeight,
  1384:           baseWeight,
  1385:           targetDow,
  1386:           targetDayOfMonth,
  1387:           prevHour: lastH,
  1388:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1389:           recentTop5: x.recentTop5,
  1390:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
  1391:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
  1392:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
  1393:           recentLast2Top5: x.recentLast2Top5,
  1394:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  1395:             key: layer.key,
  1396:             label: layer.label,
  1397:             samples: layer.samples,
  1398:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  1399:           })),
  1400:         },
  1401:       },
  1402:     };
  1403:   });
  1404: 
  1405:   return {
  1406:     top,
  1407:     meta: {
  1408:       trigger: {
  1409:         ymd: lastY,
  1410:         hour: lastH,
  1411:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1412:       },
  1413:       next: {
  1414:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  1415:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  1416:       },
  1417:       samples,
  1418:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1419:       explain: {
  1420:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1421:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  1422:         layerSamples: samples,
  1423:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
  1424:         condWeight,
  1425:         baseWeight,
  1426:         targetDow,
  1427:         targetDayOfMonth,
  1428:         prevHour: lastH,
  1429:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1430:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  1431:           key: layer.key,
  1432:           label: layer.label,
  1433:           samples: layer.samples,
  1434:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  1435:         })),
  1436:       },
  1437:     },
  1438:   };
  1439: }
  1440: 
  1441: /* =========================
  1442:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
  1443: ========================= */
  1444: 
  1445: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
  1446:   draws,
  1447:   lotteryKey,
  1448:   targetHour,
  1449:   targetDow,
  1450:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1451: ) {
  1452:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1453:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1454:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  1455:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1456: 
  1457:   const firstFreq = new Map();
  1458:   let totalSamples = 0;
  1459: 
  1460:   for (const d of list) {
  1461:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1462:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1463:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  1464: 
  1465:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  1466:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  1467:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  1468:     } else {
  1469:       if (h !== target) continue;
  1470:     }
  1471: 
  1472:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1473:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
  1474: 
  1475:     totalSamples += 1;
  1476:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
  1477:   }
  1478: 
  1479:   const prob = new Map();
  1480:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
  1481: 
  1482:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1483:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1484:   }
  1485: 
  1486:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
  1487: }
  1488: 
  1489: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1490:   draws,
  1491:   lotteryKey,
  1492:   targetHour,
  1493:   targetDow
  1494: ) {
  1495:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  1496:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1497:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  1498: 
  1499:   return list.filter((d) => {
  1500:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1501:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1502:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
  1503: 
  1504:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  1505:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  1506:     }
  1507: 
  1508:     return h === target;
  1509:   });
  1510: }
  1511: 
  1512: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
  1513:   draws,
  1514:   lotteryKey,
  1515:   targetHour,
  1516:   targetDow
  1517: ) {
  1518:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1519:     draws,
  1520:     lotteryKey,
  1521:     targetHour,
  1522:     targetDow
  1523:   )
  1524:     .map((d) => {
  1525:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1526:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1527:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1528:       return {
  1529:         draw: d,
  1530:         ymd: y,
  1531:         hour: h,
  1532:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
  1533:         grupo: Number(grupo),
  1534:       };
  1535:     })
  1536:     .filter((x) =>
  1537:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
  1538:       x.hour &&
  1539:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
  1540:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
  1541:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
  1542:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
  1543:     );
  1544: }
  1545: 
  1546: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
  1547:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
  1548:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
  1549: 
  1550:   return list
  1551:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
  1552:     .slice(-n)
  1553:     .reverse()
  1554:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
  1555:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
  1556: }
  1557: 
  1558: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
  1559:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
  1560:   return list
  1561:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
  1562:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  1563: }
  1564: 
  1565: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
  1566:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
  1567:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
  1568:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
  1569: 
  1570:   let matched = 0;
  1571:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
  1572:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
  1573:     matched += 1;
  1574:   }
  1575:   return matched;
  1576: }
  1577: 
  1578: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
  1579:   drawsRange,
  1580:   lotteryKey,
  1581:   targetHour,
  1582:   targetDow,
  1583:   currentState,
  1584:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  1585: }) {
  1586:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  1587:     drawsRange,
  1588:     lotteryKey,
  1589:     targetHour,
  1590:     targetDow
  1591:   );
  1592: 
  1593:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1594:   const freq = new Map();
  1595: 
  1596:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1597:     freq.set(g, 0);
  1598:   }
  1599: 
  1600:   let totalWeight = 0;
  1601:   let matchedSamples = 0;
  1602: 
  1603:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  1604:     const target = seq[i];
  1605:     const histState = [];
  1606: 
  1607:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
  1608:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
  1609:     }
  1610: 
  1611:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
  1612:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
  1613: 
  1614:     const weight =
  1615:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
  1616:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
  1617:       1.0;
  1618: 
  1619:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
  1620:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1621: 
  1622:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
  1623:     totalWeight += weight;
  1624:     matchedSamples += 1;
  1625:   }
  1626: 
  1627:   const prob = new Map();
  1628:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
  1629: 
  1630:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1631:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1632:   }
  1633: 
  1634:   return {
  1635:     prob,
  1636:     freq,
  1637:     totalWeight,
  1638:     matchedSamples,
  1639:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
  1640:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
  1641:   };
  1642: }
  1643: 
  1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
  1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
  1646:   const groups = list
  1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
  1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
  1649: 
  1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
  1651:     return {
  1652:       regime: "neutral",
  1653:       repeatRate: 0,
  1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
  1655:       samples: groups.length,
  1656:     };
  1657:   }
  1658: 
  1659:   let repeats = 0;
  1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
  1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
  1662:   }
  1663: 
  1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
  1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
  1666: 
  1667:   let regime = "neutral";
  1668: 
  1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
  1670:     regime = "repeat";
  1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
  1672:     regime = "spread";
  1673:   }
  1674: 
  1675:   return {
  1676:     regime,
  1677:     repeatRate,
  1678:     uniqueRate,
  1679:     samples: groups.length,
  1680:   };
  1681: }
  1682: 
  1683: function computeComparableTop5Distribution(
  1684:   draws,
  1685:   lotteryKey,
  1686:   targetHour,
  1687:   targetDow,
  1688:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1689: ) {
  1690:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1691:     draws,
  1692:     lotteryKey,
  1693:     targetHour,
  1694:     targetDow
  1695:   );
  1696: 
  1697:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1698:   const freq = new Map();
  1699:   let totalSamples = 0;
  1700: 
  1701:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1702:     freq.set(g, 0);
  1703:   }
  1704: 
  1705:   for (const d of list) {
  1706:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1707:     if (!items.length) continue;
  1708: 
  1709:     totalSamples += 1;
  1710: 
  1711:     for (const it of items) {
  1712:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1713:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1714:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  1715:     }
  1716:   }
  1717: 
  1718:   const prob = new Map();
  1719:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  1720: 
  1721:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1722:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1723:   }
  1724: 
  1725:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
  1726: }
  1727: 
  1728: function computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
  1729:   draws,
  1730:   lotteryKey,
  1731:   targetHour,
  1732:   targetDow,
  1733:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1734: ) {
  1735:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1736:     draws,
  1737:     lotteryKey,
  1738:     targetHour,
  1739:     targetDow
  1740:   );
  1741: 
  1742:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1743:   const freq = new Map();
  1744:   let totalSamples = 0;
  1745: 
  1746:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1747:     freq.set(g, 0);
  1748:   }
  1749: 
  1750:   for (const d of list) {
  1751:     const counts = countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(d);
  1752:     if (!counts.size) continue;
  1753: 
  1754:     totalSamples += 1;
  1755: 
  1756:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1757:       const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
  1758:       if (c >= 2) {
  1759:         freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  1760:       }
  1761:     }
  1762:   }
  1763: 
  1764:   const prob = new Map();
  1765:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
  1766: 
  1767:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1768:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1769:   }
  1770: 
  1771:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
  1772: }
  1773: 
  1774: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  1775:   draws,
  1776:   lotteryKey,
  1777:   targetHour,
  1778:   targetDow,
  1779:   beforeTs,
  1780:   count = 8
  1781: ) {
  1782:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
  1783:     .filter((d) => {
  1784:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1785:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1786:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  1787:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
  1788:     })
  1789:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
  1790: }
  1791: 
  1792: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  1793:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1794:   const out = new Map();
  1795: 
  1796:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1797:     out.set(g, {
  1798:       recentTop5: 0,
  1799:       recentFirst: 0,
  1800:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  1801:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
  1802:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  1803:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
  1804:     });
  1805:   }
  1806: 
  1807:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1808:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
  1809:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
  1810:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
  1811: 
  1812:   for (const d of list) {
  1813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1814:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
  1815: 
  1816:     for (const it of items) {
  1817:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1818:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1819: 
  1820:       const row = out.get(g);
  1821:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
  1822: 
  1823:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
  1824:         row.recentFirst += 1;
  1825:       }
  1826: 
  1827:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  1828:     }
  1829: 
  1830:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
  1831:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
  1832:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
  1833:       }
  1834:     }
  1835:   }
  1836: 
  1837:   for (const d of last1) {
  1838:     const seen = new Set();
  1839:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1840:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1841:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1842:       seen.add(g);
  1843:       out.get(g).recentLast1Top5 += 1;
  1844:     }
  1845:   }
  1846: 
  1847:   for (const d of last2) {
  1848:     const seen = new Set();
  1849:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1850:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1851:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1852:       seen.add(g);
  1853:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
  1854:     }
  1855:   }
  1856: 
  1857:   for (const d of last3) {
  1858:     const seen = new Set();
  1859:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1860:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1861:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1862:       seen.add(g);
  1863:       out.get(g).recentLast3Top5 += 1;
  1864:     }
  1865:   }
  1866: 
  1867:   return out;
  1868: }
  1869: 
  1870: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
  1871:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
  1872:     return {
  1873:       transition: 0.25,
  1874:       pair: 0.25,
  1875:       memory: 0.2,
  1876:       recent: 0.15,
  1877:       structural: 0.1,
  1878:       late: 0.05,
  1879:     };
  1880:   }
  1881: 
  1882:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
  1883:     return {
  1884:       transition: 0.2,
  1885:       pair: 0.1,
  1886:       memory: 0.1,
  1887:       recent: 0.2,
  1888:       structural: 0.3,
  1889:       late: 0.1,
  1890:     };
  1891:   }
  1892: 
  1893:   return {
  1894:     transition: 0.3,
  1895:     pair: 0.15,
  1896:     memory: 0.15,
  1897:     recent: 0.15,
  1898:     structural: 0.2,
  1899:     late: 0.05,
  1900:   };
  1901: }
  1902: 
  1903: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
  1904:   const safe = {
  1905:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
  1906:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
  1907:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
  1908:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
  1909:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
  1910:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
  1911:   };
  1912: 
  1913:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
  1914:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
  1915:     return {
  1916:       transition: 0.3,
  1917:       pair: 0.15,
  1918:       memory: 0.15,
  1919:       recent: 0.15,
  1920:       structural: 0.2,
  1921:       late: 0.05,
  1922:     };
  1923:   }
  1924: 
  1925:   return {
  1926:     transition: safe.transition / total,
  1927:     pair: safe.pair / total,
  1928:     memory: safe.memory / total,
  1929:     recent: safe.recent / total,
  1930:     structural: safe.structural / total,
  1931:     late: safe.late / total,
  1932:   };
  1933: }
  1934: 
  1935: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  1936:   draws,
  1937:   lotteryKey,
  1938:   targetHour,
  1939:   targetDow,
  1940:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1941: ) {
  1942:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1943:     draws,
  1944:     lotteryKey,
  1945:     targetHour,
  1946:     targetDow
  1947:   );
  1948: 
  1949:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1950:   const last = new Map();
  1951: 
  1952:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1953:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
  1954:   }
  1955: 
  1956:   for (const d of list) {
  1957:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1958:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1959:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  1960:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1961: 
  1962:     for (const it of items) {
  1963:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1964:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1965: 
  1966:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
  1967:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
  1968:         last.set(g, ts);
  1969:       }
  1970:     }
  1971:   }
  1972: 
  1973:   return last;
  1974: }
  1975: 
  1976: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
  1977:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
  1978:   const freq = new Map();
  1979:   const firstFreq = new Map();
  1980:   const seq = [];
  1981: 
  1982:   for (const d of list) {
  1983:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1984:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
  1985: 
  1986:     const gg = Number(g);
  1987:     seq.push(gg);
  1988:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  1989: 
  1990:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1991:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
  1992:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  1993:     }
  1994:   }
  1995: 
  1996:   const total = seq.length;
  1997:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
  1998:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
  1999: 
  2000:   let dominante = null;
  2001:   let dominanteCount = -1;
  2002:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
  2003:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
  2004:       dominante = Number(g);
  2005:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
  2006:     }
  2007:   }
  2008: 
  2009:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
  2010:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
  2011:   const repeatNow =
  2012:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
  2013:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
  2014:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
  2015: 
  2016:   return {
  2017:     total,
  2018:     unique,
  2019:     diversidade,
  2020:     dominante,
  2021:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
  2022:     repeatNow,
  2023:     freq,
  2024:     firstFreq,
  2025:     seq,
  2026:   };
  2027: }
  2028: 
  2029: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2030:   drawsRange,
  2031:   lotteryKey,
  2032:   targetHour,
  2033:   targetDow,
  2034:   targetDayOfMonth,
  2035:   prevPair,
  2036:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2037:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2038:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2039:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2040: }) {
  2041:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2042:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
  2043:     lotteryKey,
  2044:     targetHour,
  2045:     targetDow
  2046:   );
  2047: 
  2048:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2049:   const outFreq = new Map();
  2050: 
  2051:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
  2052: 
  2053:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
  2054:   if (pair.length < 2) {
  2055:     return {
  2056:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
  2057:       samples: 0,
  2058:       weightedSamples: 0,
  2059:       exactDomSamples: 0,
  2060:       freq: outFreq,
  2061:     };
  2062:   }
  2063: 
  2064:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
  2065:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
  2066:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2067: 
  2068:   let matchedSamples = 0;
  2069:   let weightedSamples = 0;
  2070:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
  2071: 
  2072:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2073:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2074:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2075:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2076:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
  2077: 
  2078:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
  2079:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
  2080:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
  2081: 
  2082:     matchedSamples += 1;
  2083: 
  2084:     let weight = 1;
  2085: 
  2086:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
  2087:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
  2088:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
  2089:         weight = 1.35;
  2090:         exactDomSamples += 1;
  2091:       }
  2092:     }
  2093: 
  2094:     weightedSamples += weight;
  2095:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
  2096:   }
  2097: 
  2098:   const prob = new Map();
  2099:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
  2100: 
  2101:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2102:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2103:   }
  2104: 
  2105:   return {
  2106:     prob,
  2107:     samples: matchedSamples,
  2108:     weightedSamples,
  2109:     exactDomSamples,
  2110:     freq: outFreq,
  2111:   };
  2112: }
  2113: 
  2114: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2115:   comparableSeq,
  2116:   currentState,
  2117:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2118: ) {
  2119:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
  2120:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
  2121:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2122: 
  2123:   const freq = new Map();
  2124:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  2125: 
  2126:   if (state.length < 2) {
  2127:     const prob = new Map();
  2128:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
  2129:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
  2130:   }
  2131: 
  2132:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
  2133:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
  2134: 
  2135:   let samples = 0;
  2136: 
  2137:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2138:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2139:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2140:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2141: 
  2142:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
  2143:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
  2144:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
  2145: 
  2146:     samples += 1;
  2147:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
  2148:   }
  2149: 
  2150:   const prob = new Map();
  2151:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
  2152: 
  2153:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2154:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2155:   }
  2156: 
  2157:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
  2158: }
  2159: 
  2160: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2161:   comparableSeq,
  2162:   targetTs,
  2163:   windowSize = 4,
  2164:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2165: ) {
  2166:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
  2167:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
  2168:     targetTs,
  2169:     windowSize
  2170:   );
  2171: 
  2172:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2173:   const counts = new Map();
  2174:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
  2175: 
  2176:   for (const item of seq) {
  2177:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
  2178:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  2179:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  2180:   }
  2181: 
  2182:   const boost = new Map();
  2183:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
  2184: 
  2185:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2186:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
  2187:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
  2188:   }
  2189: 
  2190:   return {
  2191:     boost,
  2192:     counts,
  2193:     windowSize,
  2194:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
  2195:   };
  2196: }
  2197: 
  2198: /* =========================
  2199:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
  2200: ========================= */
  2201: 
  2202: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
  2203:   lotteryKey,
  2204:   drawsRange,
  2205:   drawLast,
  2206:   drawsToday,
  2207:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2208:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2209:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2210:   topN = 3,
  2211:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2212:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2213: }) {
  2214:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  2215:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
  2216:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2217:   }
  2218: 
  2219:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2220: 
  2221:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  2222:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  2223:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  2224: 
  2225:   if (
  2226:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  2227:     !lastH ||
  2228:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  2229:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
  2230:   ) {
  2231:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2232:   }
  2233: 
  2234:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2235:     lotteryKey,
  2236:     ymd: lastY,
  2237:     hourBucket: lastH,
  2238:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2239:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2240:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2241:   });
  2242: 
  2243:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  2244:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  2245: 
  2246:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2247:     ? forcedTargetY
  2248:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  2249: 
  2250:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2251:     ? forcedTargetH
  2252:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  2253:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  2254:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  2255: 
  2256:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  2257:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2258:   }
  2259: 
  2260:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  2261: 
  2262:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
  2263:   const prevPair =
  2264:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
  2265:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
  2266:       : [];
  2267: 
  2268:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2269:     drawsRange: list,
  2270:     lotteryKey: key,
  2271:     targetHour: targetH,
  2272:     targetDow,
  2273:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2274:     prevPair,
  2275:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2276:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2277:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2278:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2279:   });
  2280: 
  2281:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
  2282:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
  2283:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
  2284:   );
  2285: 
  2286:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  2287:     lotteryKey,
  2288:     drawsRange: list,
  2289:     targetHour: targetH,
  2290:     targetDow,
  2291:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2292:     prevHour: lastH,
  2293:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2294:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2295:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2296:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2297:   });
  2298: 
  2299:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
  2300:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
  2301:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
  2302: 
  2303:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
  2304:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
  2305:     pTransitionFirst.set(
  2306:       g,
  2307:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
  2308:     );
  2309:   }
  2310: 
  2311:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
  2312:     list,
  2313:     key,
  2314:     targetH,
  2315:     targetDow,
  2316:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2317:   );
  2318:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
  2319: 
  2320:   const structuralTop5 = computeComparableTop5Distribution(
  2321:     list,
  2322:     key,
  2323:     targetH,
  2324:     targetDow,
  2325:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2326:   );
  2327:   const pStructuralTop5 = structuralTop5?.prob || new Map();
  2328: 
  2329:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
  2330:     list,
  2331:     key,
  2332:     targetH,
  2333:     targetDow,
  2334:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2335:   );
  2336:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
  2337: 
  2338:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2339:     list,
  2340:     key,
  2341:     targetH,
  2342:     targetDow
  2343:   );
  2344: 
  2345:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
  2346:     comparableSeq,
  2347:     targetTs,
  2348:     3
  2349:   );
  2350: 
  2351:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
  2352:     drawsRange: list,
  2353:     lotteryKey: key,
  2354:     targetHour: targetH,
  2355:     targetDow,
  2356:     currentState,
  2357:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2358:   });
  2359: 
  2360:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
  2361: 
  2362:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2363:     comparableSeq,
  2364:     currentState,
  2365:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2366:   );
  2367:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
  2368: 
  2369:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2370:     comparableSeq,
  2371:     targetTs,
  2372:     4,
  2373:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2374:   );
  2375:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
  2376: 
  2377:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
  2378:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
  2379:   );
  2380: 
  2381:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
  2382: 
  2383:   const mode =
  2384:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
  2385:       ? "REPEAT"
  2386:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
  2387:         ? "SPREAD"
  2388:         : "NEUTRAL";
  2389: 
  2390:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
  2391: 
  2392:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  2393:     list,
  2394:     key,
  2395:     targetH,
  2396:     targetDow,
  2397:     targetTs,
  2398:     8
  2399:   );
  2400:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  2401: 
  2402:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
  2403:     1,
  2404:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
  2405:   );
  2406:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
  2407:     1,
  2408:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
  2409:   );
  2410:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
  2411:     1,
  2412:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
  2413:   );
  2414: 
  2415:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  2416:     list,
  2417:     key,
  2418:     targetH,
  2419:     targetDow,
  2420:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2421:   );
  2422: 
  2423:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
  2424:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
  2425:   );
  2426:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
  2427:   const maxGapMsBase =
  2428:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
  2429:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
  2430:       : 1;
  2431: 
  2432:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
  2433:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
  2434: 
  2435:   const confidence =
  2436:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
  2437:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
  2438:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
  2439: 
  2440:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
  2441:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
  2442:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
  2443:     memory: weights.memory,
  2444:     recent: weights.recent,
  2445:     structural: weights.structural,
  2446:     late: weights.late,
  2447:   });
  2448: 
  2449:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  2450:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  2451: 
  2452:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2453:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2454:     const pST = Number(pStructuralTop5.get(grupo) || 0);
  2455:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
  2456:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
  2457:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
  2458:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
  2459:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2460: 
  2461:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
  2462:       recentTop5: 0,
  2463:       recentFirst: 0,
  2464:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  2465:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
  2466:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  2467:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
  2468:     };
  2469: 
  2470:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
  2471:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
  2472:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
  2473:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1Top5, 1);
  2474:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
  2475: 
  2476:     const recentComposite =
  2477:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.34) +
  2478:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.24) +
  2479:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
  2480:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
  2481:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
  2482: 
  2483:     const dominanceScore =
  2484:       (pT * 0.42) +
  2485:       (pSF * 0.26) +
  2486:       (pST * 0.18) +
  2487:       (pD * 0.14);
  2488: 
  2489:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
  2490:     const gapMs =
  2491:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
  2492:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
  2493:         : maxGapMsBase;
  2494: 
  2495:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
  2496:       0,
  2497:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
  2498:     );
  2499: 
  2500:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2501:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2502:     const isDominantToday =
  2503:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
  2504:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
  2505: 
  2506:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
  2507: 
  2508:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
  2509:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
  2510:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
  2511:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
  2512:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
  2513: 
  2514:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
  2515:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
  2516:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
  2517:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
  2518:     }
  2519: 
  2520:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
  2521:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
  2522:     }
  2523: 
  2524:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
  2525: 
  2526:     const pairSequenceScore =
  2527:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
  2528:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
  2529:         : 0;
  2530: 
  2531:     const structuralBlend =
  2532:       (pSF * 0.45) +
  2533:       (pST * 0.35) +
  2534:       (pD * 0.2);
  2535: 
  2536:     const memoryBlend =
  2537:       (pM * 0.75) +
  2538:       (pSeq * 0.25);
  2539: 
  2540:     const recentBlend =
  2541:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
  2542:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
  2543: 
  2544:     const prob =
  2545:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
  2546:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
  2547:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
  2548:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
  2549:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
  2550:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
  2551: 
  2552:     const score =
  2553:       (prob * 1000) +
  2554:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
  2555:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
  2556:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
  2557:       (confidence * 50);
  2558: 
  2559:     return {
  2560:       grupo,
  2561:       prob,
  2562:       scoreProb: prob,
  2563:       score,
  2564:       probTransition: pT,
  2565:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
  2566:       probStructuralTop5: pST,
  2567:       probMemory: pM,
  2568:       probDuplication: pD,
  2569:       probSeq2: pSeq,
  2570:       probPair: pPair,
  2571:       pairProb: pPair,
  2572:       pairConfidence,
  2573:       repeatBoost,
  2574:       dominanceScore,
  2575:       recentComposite,
  2576:       recentTop5: rm.recentTop5,
  2577:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
  2578:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
  2579:       recentLast1Top5: rm.recentLast1Top5,
  2580:       recentLast2Top5: rm.recentLast2Top5,
  2581:       recentLast3Top5: rm.recentLast3Top5,
  2582:       lateNorm,
  2583:       gapMs,
  2584:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
  2585:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2586:       structuralTop5Count: Number(structuralTop5?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2587:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2588:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2589:       dayFreq,
  2590:       dayFirstFreq,
  2591:       isDominantToday,
  2592:       dayFlowConfidence,
  2593:       dayFlowScore,
  2594:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2595:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2596:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2597:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  2598:       pairSequenceScore,
  2599:     };
  2600:   })
  2601:     .sort((a, b) => {
  2602:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  2603:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
  2604:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
  2605:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
  2606:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
  2607:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
  2608:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
  2609:       if (b.probStructuralTop5 !== a.probStructuralTop5) return b.probStructuralTop5 - a.probStructuralTop5;
  2610:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  2611:     })
  2612:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  2613: 
  2614:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  2615:     console.table(
  2616:       ranked.map((x) => ({
  2617:         slot: `${targetY} ${targetH}`,
  2618:         grupo: x.grupo,
  2619:         score: Number(x.score || 0).toFixed(2),
  2620:         final: (Number(x.scoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2621:         transicao: (Number(x.probTransition || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2622:         estrutural1: (Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2623:         estruturalTop5: (Number(x.probStructuralTop5 || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2624:         memoria: (Number(x.probMemory || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2625:         par: (Number(x.probPair || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2626:         duplicacao: (Number(x.probDuplication || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2627:         recencia: (Number(x.recentComposite || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2628:         atraso: (Number(x.lateNorm || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2629:         dayFreq: x.dayFreq,
  2630:         dayFirst: x.dayFirstFreq,
  2631:         dominante: x.isDominantToday,
  2632:         pairSamples: x.pairSamples,
  2633:         condFirstCount: x.condFirstCount,
  2634:         structuralFirstCount: x.structuralFirstCount,
  2635:         structuralTop5Count: x.structuralTop5Count,
  2636:       }))
  2637:     );
  2638: 
  2639:     console.log("[TOP3 V2 CONTEXTO]", {
