  2835: /* =========================
  2836:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
  2837: ========================= */
  2838: 
  2839: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
  2840:   const k = Number(key);
  2841:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
  2842:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
  2843: }
  2844: 
  2845: function emptyGroupMap() {
  2846:   const out = new Map();
  2847:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
  2848:   return out;
  2849: }
  2850: 
  2851: function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
  2852:   return new Set(
  2853:     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
  2854:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
  2855:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
  2856:   );
  2857: }
  2858: 
  2859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
  2860:   const out = new Map();
  2861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
  2862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
  2863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
  2864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
  2865: 
  2866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
  2868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
  2869:   }
  2870: 
  2871:   return out;
  2872: }
  2873: 
  2874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
  2875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
  2876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
  2877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
  2878: }
  2879: 
  2880: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  2881:   lotteryKey,
  2882:   drawsRange,
  2883:   drawLast,
  2884:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2885:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2886:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2887:   topN = 3,
  2888:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2889:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2890: }) {
  2891:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
  2892:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
  2893: 
  2894:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2895: 
  2896:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  2897:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  2898:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  2899: 
  2900:   if (
  2901:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  2902:     !lastH ||
  2903:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  2904:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
  2905:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2906:   ) {
  2907:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2908:   }
  2909: 
  2910:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2911:     lotteryKey: key,
  2912:     ymd: lastY,
  2913:     hourBucket: lastH,
  2914:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2915:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2916:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2917:   });
  2918: 
  2919:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  2920:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  2921: 
  2922:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2923:     ? forcedTargetY
  2924:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  2925: 
  2926:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2927:     ? forcedTargetH
  2928:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  2929:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  2930:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  2931:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  2932: 
  2933:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  2934:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2935:   }
  2936: 
  2937:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
  2938:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  2939:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  2940:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  2941:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
  2942:   });
  2943: 
  2944:   const layers = {
  2945:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
  2946:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
  2947:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
  2948:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
  2949:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
  2950:   };
  2951: 
  2952:   for (const d of history) {
  2953:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  2954:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  2955:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
  2956:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
  2957:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  2958:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
  2959: 
  2960:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
  2961:       layer.samples += 1;
  2962:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
  2963:       for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layer.top5, g, 1);
  2964:     };
  2965: 
  2966:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
  2967:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
  2968:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
  2969:   }
  2970: 
  2971:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
  2972: 
  2973:   for (const prev of history) {
  2974:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
  2975:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
  2976:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
  2977: 
  2978:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
  2979: 
  2980:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2981:       lotteryKey: key,
  2982:       ymd: py,
  2983:       hourBucket: ph,
  2984:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2985:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2986:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2987:     });
  2988: 
  2989:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
  2990:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
  2991: 
  2992:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
  2993:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
  2994: 
  2995:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
  2996:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(nextDraw);
  2997: 
  2998:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
  2999:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
  3000:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.transition.top5, g, 1);
  3001:   }
  3002: 
  3003:   const recentComparable = history
  3004:     .filter((d) => {
  3005:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3006:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3007:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3008:     })
  3009:     .slice(-20);
  3010: 
  3011:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
  3012:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  3013:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
  3014: 
  3015:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
  3016:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
  3017:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.recent.top5, g, 1);
  3018:   }
  3019: 
  3020:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
  3021:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
  3022:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  3023:   const sceneWeight =
  3024:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
  3025:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
  3026: 
  3027: 
  3028:   const activeWeights = {};
  3029:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3030: 
  3031:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3032:     const conf =
  3033:       keyLayer === "transition"
  3034:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
  3035:         : keyLayer === "recent"
  3036:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
  3037:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
  3038: 
  3039:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
  3040:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
  3041:     totalWeight += w;
  3042:   }
  3043: 
  3044:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
  3045:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
  3046:     totalWeight = 1;
  3047:   }
  3048: 
  3049:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
  3050:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
  3051:   }
  3052: 
  3053:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  3054:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  3055: 
  3056:     let scoreProb = 0;
  3057:     const details = {};
  3058: 
  3059:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3060:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3061:       const pTop5 = Number(layerProbability(layer.top5, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3062: 
  3063:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.75) + (pTop5 * 0.25);
  3064:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
  3065: 
  3066:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
  3067: 
  3068:       details[keyLayer] = {
  3069:         label: layer.label,
  3070:         samples: layer.samples,
  3071:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
  3072:         top3Count: Number(layer.top5.get(grupo) || 0),
  3073:         probability: pLayer,
  3074:         weight: w,
  3075:       };
  3076:     }
  3077: 
  3078:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  3079:     const pScene =
  3080:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
  3081:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
  3082: 
  3083:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
  3084:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
  3085:     }
  3086: 
  3087:     details.scene = {
  3088:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
  3089:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3090:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  3091:       top3Count: 0,
  3092:       probability: pScene,
  3093:       weight: sceneWeight,
  3094:     };
  3095: 
  3096:     return {
  3097:       grupo,
  3098:       scoreProb,
  3099:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
  3100:       details,
  3101:     };
  3102:   })
  3103:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3104:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  3105:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  3106:     })
  3107:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  3108: 
  3109:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
  3110:     ranked,
  3111:     {
  3112:       lotteryKey,
  3113:       targetYmd: targetY,
  3114:       targetHour: targetH,
  3115:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
  3116:       totalDraws: history.length,
  3117:     }
  3118:   );
  3119: 
  3120:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
  3121:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  3122:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
  3123: 
  3124:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
  3125:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
  3126:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
  3127:       .slice(0, 3);
  3128: 
  3129:     return {
  3130:       rank: idx + 1,
  3131:       title:
  3132:         idx === 0
  3133:           ? "Mais provável"
  3134:           : idx === 1
  3135:             ? "2º mais provável"
  3136:             : "3º mais provável",
  3137:       grupo: x.grupo,
  3138:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3139:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3140:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
  3141:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
  3142:       lateBonus: 0,
  3143:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3144:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
  3145:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3146:       freqZeroWhy: "",
  3147:       reasons: [
  3148:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
  3149:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  3150:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
  3151:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  3152:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
  3153:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
  3154:         ),
  3155:       ],
  3156:       meta: {
  3157:         trigger: {
  3158:           ymd: lastY,
  3159:           hour: lastH,
  3160:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3161:         },
  3162:         next: {
  3163:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3164:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3165:         },
  3166:         samples: history.length,
  3167:         period: {
  3168:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
  3169:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
  3170:         },
  3171:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3172:         explain: {
  3173:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3174:           targetDow,
  3175:           targetDayOfMonth,
  3176:           prevHour: lastH,
  3177:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3178:           activeWeights,
  3179:           scene: {
  3180:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3181:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
  3182:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
  3183:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
  3184:           },
  3185:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3186:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3187:               k,
  3188:               {
  3189:                 label: v.label,
  3190:                 samples: v.samples,
  3191:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
  3192:               },
  3193:             ])
  3194:           ),
  3195:           details: x.details,
  3196:         },
  3197:       },
  3198:     };
  3199:   });
  3200: 
  3201:   return {
  3202:     top,
  3203:     meta: {
  3204:       trigger: {
  3205:         ymd: lastY,
  3206:         hour: lastH,
  3207:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3208:       },
  3209:       next: {
  3210:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3211:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3212:       },
  3213:       samples: history.length,
  3214:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3215:       explain: {
  3216:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3217:         targetDow,
  3218:         targetDayOfMonth,
  3219:         prevHour: lastH,
  3220:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3221:         activeWeights,
  3222:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3223:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3224:             k,
  3225:             {
  3226:               label: v.label,
  3227:               samples: v.samples,
  3228:               weight: activeWeights[k],
  3229:             },
  3230:           ])
  3231:         ),
  3232:       },
  3233:     },
  3234:   };
  3235: }

  3238: 
  3239: /* =========================
  3240:    TOP3 SCENE READER
  3241: ========================= */
  3242: 
  3243: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
  3244:   const candidates = [
  3245:     prize?.milhar,
  3246:     prize?.milhar4,
  3247:     prize?.numero,
  3248:     prize?.number,
  3249:     prize?.value,
  3250:     prize?.result,
  3251:     prize?.resultado,
  3252:     prize?.premio,
  3253:   ];
  3254: 
  3255:   for (const v of candidates) {
  3256:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
  3257:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
  3258:   }
  3259: 
  3260:   return "";
  3261: }
  3262: 
  3263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
  3264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
  3266: 
  3267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
  3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
  3269: 
  3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
  3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
  3272: 
  3273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
  3274: }
  3275: 
  3276: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
  3277:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  3278: 
  3279:   const rows = prizes
  3280:     .map((p) => {
  3281:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
  3282:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
  3283:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
  3284: 
  3285:       return {
  3286:         pos,
  3287:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
  3288:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
  3289:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
  3290:         grupo,
  3291:       };
  3292:     })
  3293:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
  3294:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
  3295: 
  3296:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
  3297:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
  3298:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
  3299:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
  3300: 
  3301:   const countMap = (arr) => {
  3302:     const m = new Map();
  3303:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
  3304:     return Array.from(m.entries())
  3305:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
  3306:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
  3307:   };
  3308: 
  3309:   return {
  3310:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
  3311:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
  3312:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
  3313:     grupos,
  3314:     dezenas,
  3315:     centenas,
  3316:     milhares,
  3317:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
  3318:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
  3319:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
  3320:     rows,
  3321:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
  3322:   };
  3323: }
  3324: 
  3325: 
  3326: 
  3327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
  3328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
  3329: 
  3330:   let score = 0;
  3331:   const details = {};
  3332: 
  3333:   const add = (key, value) => {
  3334:     if (!value) return;
  3335:     score += value;
  3336:     details[key] = value;
  3337:   };
  3338: 
  3339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
  3340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
  3341: 
  3342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
  3343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
  3344: 
  3345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
  3346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
  3347: 
  3348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
  3349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
  3350: 
  3351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
  3352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
  3353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
  3354:   }
  3355: 
  3356:   add(
  3357:     "groupOverlap",
  3358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
  3359:   );
  3360: 
  3361:   add(
  3362:     "dezenaOverlap",
  3363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
  3364:   );
  3365: 
  3366:   add(
  3367:     "centenaOverlap",
  3368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
  3369:   );
  3370: 
  3371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3373: 
  3374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
  3375:     add("repeatExists",8);
  3376: 
  3377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
  3378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
  3379: 
  3380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
  3381:     add("signature",60);
  3382: 
  3383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
  3384:     add("hour",8);
  3385: 
  3386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
  3387: }
  3388: 
  3389: 
  3390: 
  3391: 
  3392: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
  3393:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  3394:   if (!currentScene) return [];
  3395: 
  3396:   const ordered = list
  3397:     .map((draw) => ({
  3398:       draw,
  3399:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
  3400:     }))
  3401:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
  3402:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3403:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3404:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3405:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
  3406:     });
  3407: 
  3408:   return ordered
  3409:     .map((item, idx) => {
  3410:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
  3411:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
  3412: 
  3413:       return {
  3414:         draw: item.draw,
  3415:         scene: item.scene,
  3416:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
  3417:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
  3418:         score,
  3419:       };
  3420:     })
  3421:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
  3422:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3423:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
  3424:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3425:       }
  3426: 
  3427:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3428:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3429:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
  3430:     })
  3431:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
  3432: }
  3433: 
  3434: 
  3435: 
  3436: 
  3437: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  3438:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
  3439:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  3440: 
  3441:   const freq = new Map();
  3442:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  3443: 
  3444:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3445: 
  3446:   for (const item of ranking) {
  3447:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
  3448:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
  3449: 
  3450:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
  3451:     if (score <= 0) continue;
  3452: 
  3453:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
  3454:     totalWeight += score;
  3455:   }
  3456: 
  3457:   const prob = new Map();
  3458:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  3459:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
  3460:   }
  3461: 
  3462:   return {
  3463:     freq,
  3464:     prob,
  3465:     samples: ranking.length,
  3466:     totalWeight,
  3467:   };
  3468: }
  3469: 
  3470: 
  3471: function logTop3SceneDebug(label, scene, extra = {}) {
  3472:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  3473:   if (!scene) return;
  3474: 
  3475:   console.log(`[TOP3 SCENE] ${label}`, {
  3476:     ...extra,
  3477:     ymd: scene.ymd,
  3478:     hour: scene.hour,
  3479:     firstGrupo: scene.firstGrupo,
  3480:     signature: scene.signature,
  3481:     grupos: scene.grupos,
  3482:     dezenas: scene.dezenas,
  3483:     centenas: scene.centenas,
  3484:     milhares: scene.milhares,
  3485:     repeatedGroups: scene.repeatedGroups,
  3486:     repeatedDezenas: scene.repeatedDezenas,
  3487:     repeatedCentenas: scene.repeatedCentenas,
  3488:     rows: scene.rows,
  3489:   });
  3490: }

  3492: 
  3493: /* =========================
  3494:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
  3495: ========================= */
  3496: 
  3497: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
  3498:   const g = Number(grupo2);
  3499:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
  3500: 
  3501:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
  3502:   const out = [];
  3503: 
  3504:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
  3505:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
  3506:   }
  3507: 
  3508:   return out.filter(Boolean);
  3509: }
  3510: 
  3511: 
  3512: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
  3513:   rangeDraws,
  3514:   analysisHourBucket,
  3515:   schedule,
  3516:   grupo2,
  3517:   count = 20,
  3518:   targetYmd = "",
  3519: }) {
  3520:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  3521:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
  3522:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
  3523:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
  3524:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
  3525: 
  3526:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
  3527:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
  3528:     : 20;
  3529: 
  3530:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
  3531: 
  3532:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
  3533:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3534:   }
  3535: 
  3536:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
  3537:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3538: 
  3539:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
  3540:   const byMilhar = new Map();
  3541: 
  3542:   for (const d of list) {
  3543:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3544:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3545:     if (!h) continue;
  3546: 
  3547:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
  3548:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
  3549:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3550:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3551: 
  3552:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
  3553: 
  3554:     for (const prize of ps) {
  3555:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
  3556:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
  3557: 
  3558:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  3559:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
  3560: 
  3561:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3562:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
  3563: 
  3564:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
  3565:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
  3566: 
  3567:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
  3568: 
  3569:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
  3570:         milhar: m4,
  3571:         dezena: dz,
  3572:         centena: c3,
  3573:         freq: 0,
  3574:         score: 0,
  3575:         targetHits: 0,
  3576:         scheduleHits: 0,
  3577:         sameDowHits: 0,
  3578:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3579:         lastTs: 0,
  3580:       };
  3581: 
  3582:       let inc = 1;
  3583: 
  3584:       if (isTargetHour) {
  3585:         inc += 8;
  3586:         current.targetHits += 1;
  3587:       }
  3588: 
  3589:       if (isScheduleHour) {
  3590:         inc += 3;
  3591:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
  3592:       }
  3593: 
  3594:       if (isSameDow) {
  3595:         inc += 2;
  3596:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
  3597:       }
  3598: 
  3599:       if (pos === 1) {
  3600:         inc += 3;
  3601:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
  3602:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
  3603:         inc += 1;
  3604:       }
  3605: 
  3606:       current.freq += 1;
  3607:       current.score += inc;
  3608: 
  3609:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
  3610:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
  3611:       }
  3612: 
  3613:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
  3614:     }
  3615:   }
  3616: 
  3617:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
  3618:     .map((m) => {
  3619:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
  3620:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
  3621:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
  3622:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
  3623:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3624:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  3625: 
  3626:       return {
  3627:         ...m,
  3628:         score:
  3629:           freq * 100 +
  3630:           targetHits * 70 +
  3631:           scheduleHits * 35 +
  3632:           sameDowHits * 25 +
  3633:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
  3634:           recencyBoost * 5,
  3635:       };
  3636:     })
  3637:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3638:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3639:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3640:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3641:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
  3642:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3643:     });
  3644: 
  3645:   const centenaStats = new Map();
  3646: 
  3647:   for (const item of ranked) {
  3648:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
  3649:     if (!centena) continue;
  3650: 
  3651:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
  3652:       centena,
  3653:       freq: 0,
  3654:       score: 0,
  3655:       targetHits: 0,
  3656:       sameDowHits: 0,
  3657:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3658:       lastTs: 0,
  3659:     };
  3660: 
  3661:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
  3662:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
  3663:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
  3664:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
  3665:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3666:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
  3667: 
  3668:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
  3669:   }
  3670: 
  3671:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
  3672: 
  3673:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
  3674:     centenaScoreMap.set(
  3675:       c.centena,
  3676:       Number(c.score || 0) +
  3677:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
  3678:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
  3679:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
  3680:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
  3681:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
  3682:     );
  3683:   }
  3684: 
  3685:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
  3686:   const slots = [];
  3687: 
  3688:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
  3689:     const candidates = ranked
  3690:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
  3691:       .map((x) => {
  3692:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3693:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
  3694:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
  3695:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
  3696: 
  3697:         return {
  3698:           ...x,
  3699:           milhar,
  3700:           prefix,
  3701:           centena,
  3702:           centenaScore,
  3703:           adjustedScore:
  3704:             centenaScore * 2 +
  3705:             Number(x.score || 0) -
  3706:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
  3707:         };
  3708:       });
  3709: 
  3710:     const byCentena = new Map();
  3711: 
  3712:     for (const item of candidates) {
  3713:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
  3714:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
  3715:     }
  3716: 
  3717:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
  3718:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
  3719:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
  3720:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
  3721:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
  3722:           }
  3723:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3724:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3725:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3726:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3727:         });
  3728: 
  3729:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
  3730: 
  3731:         return {
  3732:           centena,
  3733:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
  3734:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
  3735:           items: sortedItems,
  3736:         };
  3737:       })
  3738:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3739:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
  3740:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
  3741:         }
  3742:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
  3743:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
  3744:         }
  3745:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
  3746:       });
  3747: 
  3748:     const picked = [];
  3749:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
  3750:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
  3751: 
  3752:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3753:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3754:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3755: 
  3756:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
  3757:       if (!item) continue;
  3758: 
  3759:       picked.push(item);
  3760:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3761:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3762:     }
  3763: 
  3764:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
  3765:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3766:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3767:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
  3768: 
  3769:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
  3770:       if (!item) continue;
  3771: 
  3772:       picked.push(item);
  3773:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3774:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3775:     }
  3776: 
  3777:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3778:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3779:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3780: 
  3781:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3782:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3783:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3784: 
  3785:         picked.push(item);
  3786:       }
  3787:     }
  3788: 
  3789:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3790:   }
  3791: 
  3792:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  3793:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  3794: 
  3795:     let pushed = 0;
  3796: 
  3797:     for (const item of items) {
  3798:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  3799:       slots.push({
  3800:         dezena: dz,
  3801:         milhar: item.milhar,
  3802:         score: item.score,
  3803:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  3804:         centena: item.centena,
  3805:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  3806:         freq: item.freq,
  3807:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  3808:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  3809:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  3810:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3811:       });
  3812:       pushed += 1;
  3813:     }
  3814: 
  3815:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3816:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3817:       pushed += 1;
  3818:     }
  3819:   }
  3820: 
  3821:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3822: 
  3823:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  3824: }
  3825: 
  3826: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3827:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
  3828: }
  3829: 
  3830: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3831:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
  3832: }
  3833: 
  3834: /* =========================
  3835:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
  3836: ========================= */
  3837: 
  3838: 
  3839: 
  3840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
  3841:   timeline,
  3842:   lotteryKey = "",
  3843: }) {
  3844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
  3845: 
  3846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
  3847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  3848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
  3849:   });
  3850: 
  3851:   const total = validated.length;
  3852: 
  3853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
  3854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
  3856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
  3857:   }).length;
  3858: 
  3859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
  3860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3862:       .slice(0, 3)
  3863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
  3864:   }).length;
  3865: 
  3866:   function pct(n, d) {
  3867:     if (!d) return 0;
  3868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
  3869:   }
  3870: 
  3871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
  3872:     const map = new Map();
  3873: 
  3874:     for (const slot of validated) {
  3875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
  3876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
  3877:         key,
  3878:         total: 0,
  3879:         top1Hits: 0,
  3880:         top3Hits: 0,
  3881:       };
  3882: 
  3883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
  3885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
  3886: 
  3887:       current.total += 1;
  3888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
  3889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
  3890: 
  3891:       map.set(key, current);
  3892:     }
  3893: 
  3894:     return Array.from(map.values())
  3895:       .map((x) => ({
  3896:         ...x,
  3897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
  3898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
  3899:       }))
  3900:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
  3902:         return b.total - a.total;
  3903:       });
  3904:   }
  3905: 
  3906:   return {
  3907:     lotteryKey,
  3908:     total,
  3909:     top1Hits,
  3910:     top3Hits,
  3911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
  3912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
  3913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
  3914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
  3915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
  3916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
  3917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
  3918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
  3919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
  3920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
  3921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3922:         .slice(0, 3)
  3923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
  3924:       top1Hit:
  3925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
  3926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3927:         .slice(0, 3)
  3928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
  3929:     })),
  3930:   };
  3931: }
  3932: 
  3933: 
  3934: function buildTimelineForDate({
  3935:   ymd,
  3936:   drawsToday,
  3937:   drawsRange,
  3938:   lotteryKey,
  3939:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3940:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3941:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3942: }) {
  3943:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
  3944:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
  3945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
  3946: 
  3947:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
  3948: 
  3949:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
  3950:     lotteryKey,
  3951:     ymd: targetYmd,
  3952:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3953:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3954:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3955:   });
  3956: 
  3957:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
  3958:     .map(toHourBucket)
  3959:     .filter(Boolean);
  3960: 
  3961:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
  3962: 
  3963:   const daySorted = day
  3964:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  3965:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3966:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  3967:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  3968:       return ta - tb;
  3969:     });
  3970: 
  3971:   const rangeSorted = range
  3972:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  3973:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3974:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  3975:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  3976:       return ta - tb;
  3977:     });
  3978: 
  3979:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
  3980: 
  3981:   for (const d of daySorted) {
  3982:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3983:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3984:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  3985:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
  3986:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
  3987:   }
  3988: 
  3989: 
  3990:   const chainContextDraws = [];
  3991:   const timeline = [];
  3992:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
  3993: 
  3994:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
  3995:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
  3996:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
  3997: 
  3998: 
  3999:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
  4000:       timeline.push({
  4001:         targetYmd,
  4002:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4003:         baseYmd: "",
  4004:         baseHour: "",
  4005:         top3: [],
  4006:         resultGrupo: null,
  4007:         hit: null,
  4008:         status: "pending",
  4009:       });
  4010:       continue;
  4011:     }
  4012: 
  4013:     let baseDraw = null;
  4014: 
  4015:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
  4016:       .filter((d) => {
  4017:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4018:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4019:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4020:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4021:       })
  4022:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4023:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4024:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4025:         return tb - ta;
  4026:       })[0] || null;
  4027: 
  4028:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
  4029: 
  4030:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4031:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
  4032:         .filter((d) => {
  4033:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4034:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4035:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4036:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4037:         })
  4038:         .sort((a, b) => {
  4039:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4040:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4041:           return tb - ta;
  4042:         })[0] || null;
  4043: 
  4044:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
  4045:     }
  4046: 
  4047:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4048:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
  4049:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
  4050:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4051:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4052:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4053: 
  4054:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
  4055: 
  4056:         baseDraw = d;
  4057:         break;
  4058:       }
  4059:     }
  4060: 
  4061:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4062:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
  4063:     }
  4064: 
  4065:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4066:       timeline.push({
  4067:         targetYmd,
  4068:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4069:         baseYmd: "",
  4070:         baseHour: "",
  4071:         top3: [],
  4072:         resultGrupo: null,
  4073:         hit: null,
  4074:         status: "pending",
  4075:       });
  4076:       continue;
  4077:     }
  4078: 
  4079:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
  4080:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
  4081:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
  4082:     );
  4083: 
  4084:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
  4085:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4086:         pickDrawYMD(d),
  4087:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4088:       );
  4089:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4090:     });
  4091: 
  4092:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
  4093:       .filter((d) => {
  4094:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4095:           pickDrawYMD(d),
  4096:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4097:         );
  4098:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4099:       })
  4100:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4101:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4102:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4103:         return ta - tb;
  4104:       });
  4105: 
  4106:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  4107:       lotteryKey,
  4108:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
  4109:       drawLast: baseDraw,
  4110:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
  4111:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4112:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4113:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4114:       topN: 3,
  4115:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
  4116:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
  4117:     });
  4118: 
  4119: 
  4120:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
  4121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
  4122:     const computedForSlot =
  4123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
  4124: 
  4125:     const top3 =
  4126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
  4127:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
  4128:         : [];
  4129: 
  4130:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
  4131: 
  4132:     const hit =
  4133:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
  4134:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
  4135:         : null;
  4136: 
  4137:     timeline.push({
  4138:       targetYmd,
  4139:       targetHour: slotHour,
  4140:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
  4141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
  4142:       top3,
  4143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
  4144:       hit,
  4145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
  4146:     });
  4147: 
  4148:     if (currentDraw) {
  4149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
  4150:     } else {
  4151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
  4152:     }
  4153:   }
  4154: 
  4155:   return timeline;
  4156: 
  4157: }
  4158: 
  4159: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
  4160:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
  4161: }
  4162: 
  4163: export function auditTop3Backtest({
  4164:   drawsRange,
  4165:   lotteryKey,
  4166:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4167:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4168:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4169: }) {
  4170:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  4171:   const dates = Array.from(
  4172:     new Set(
  4173:       range
  4174:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
  4175:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
  4176:     )
  4177:   ).sort();
  4178: 
  4179:   const allTimeline = [];
  4180: 
  4181:   for (const ymd of dates) {
  4182:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
  4183: 
  4184:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
  4185:       ymd,
  4186:       drawsToday,
  4187:       drawsRange: range,
  4188:       lotteryKey,
  4189:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4190:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4191:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4192:     });
  4193: 
  4194:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
  4195:   }
  4196: 
  4197:   return auditTop3Timeline({
  4198:     timeline: allTimeline,
  4199:     lotteryKey,
  4200:   });
  4201: }

    32: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
    33:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
    34:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
    35:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
    36: 
    37:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
    38: 
    39:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
    40: 
    41:   return list
    42:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
    43:     .map((draw) => {
    44:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    45:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    46:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
    47:     })
    48:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
    49:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
    50: }

    56: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
    57:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
    58:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
    59: 
    60:   const n = Number(s);
    61:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
    62:   if (n === 0) return 25;
    63: 
    64:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
    65:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
    66: }
    67: 
    68: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
    69:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
    70:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    71:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    72: 
    73:   let score = 0;
    74:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
    75:   if (h) score += 20;
    76:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
    77: 
    78:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
    79:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
    80: 
    81:   return score;
    82: }
    83: 
    84: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
    85:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
    86:     ? Number(p.position)
    87:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
    88:       ? Number(p.posicao)
    89:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
    90:         ? Number(p.pos)
    91:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
    92:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
    93:           : null;
    94: 
    95:   return pos;
    96: }
    97: 
    98: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
    99:   const directCandidates = [
   100:     p?.grupo2,
   101:     p?.group2,
   102:     p?.grupo,
   103:     p?.group,
   104:     p?.animal_grupo,
   105:     p?.grupo_animal,
   106:     p?.grupoAnimal,
   107:     p?.g,
   108:   ];
   109: 
   110:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
   111:     const g = Number(v);
   112:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
   113:       return g;
   114:     }
   115:   }
   116: 
   117:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
   118: 
   119:   if (milhar4) {
   120:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
   121:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
   122: 
   123:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
   124:   }
   125: 
   126:   return null;
   127: }

   129: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {

   132:   );

   135: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
   136:   const y =
   137:     draw?.ymd ||
   138:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
   139:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
   140:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
   141:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
   142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
   143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
   144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
   145:     null;
   146: 
   147:   return y;

   150: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   151:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   152:   if (prizes.length) {
   153:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   154:     if (p1) {
   155:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   156:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
   157:     }
   158:   }
   159: 
   160:   const candidates = [
   161:     draw?.grupo1,
   162:     draw?.group1,
   163:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
   164:     draw?.first_grupo,
   165:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
   166:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
   167:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
   168:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
   169:     draw?.g1,
   170:     draw?.grupo_1,
   171:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
   172:   ];
   173: 
   174:   for (const v of candidates) {
   175:     if (Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return Number(v);
   176:   }
   177: 
   178:   return null;
   179: }

   181: /* =========================

   184: 
   185: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
   186:   const n = Number(v);
   187:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
   188: }
   189: 
   190: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
   191:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
   192:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
   193: }
   194: 
   195: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
   196:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   197:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   198: 
   199:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   200: 
   201:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   202:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
   203:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   204:   }
   205: 
   206:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   207:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   208: 
   209:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
   210: }

   216: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
   217:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
   218:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
   219: }
   220: 
   221: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   222:   ymd,
   223:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   224:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   225: ) {
   226:   const dow = getDowKey(ymd);
   227:   if (dow === 0) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY;
   228:   if (dow === 3 || dow === 6) return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT;
   229:   return PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL;
   230: }

   232: export function getScheduleForLottery({
   233:   lotteryKey,
   234:   ymd,
   235:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   236:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   237:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   238: }) {
   239:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   240: 
   241:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   242:     return isFederalDrawDay(ymd) ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE : [];
   243:   }
   244: 
   245:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   246:     ymd,
   247:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   248:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   249:   );
   250: }
