  1774: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  1775:   draws,
  1776:   lotteryKey,
  1777:   targetHour,
  1778:   targetDow,
  1779:   beforeTs,
  1780:   count = 8
  1781: ) {
  1782:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
  1783:     .filter((d) => {
  1784:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1785:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1786:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  1787:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
  1788:     })
  1789:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
  1790: }
  1791: 
  1792: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  1793:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1794:   const out = new Map();
  1795: 
  1796:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1797:     out.set(g, {
  1798:       recentTop5: 0,
  1799:       recentFirst: 0,
  1800:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  1801:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
  1802:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  1803:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
  1804:     });
  1805:   }
  1806: 
  1807:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1808:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
  1809:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
  1810:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
  1811: 
  1812:   for (const d of list) {
  1813:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1814:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
  1815: 
  1816:     for (const it of items) {
  1817:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1818:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1819: 
  1820:       const row = out.get(g);
  1821:       row.recentTop5 += 1;
  1822: 
  1823:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
  1824:         row.recentFirst += 1;
  1825:       }
  1826: 
  1827:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  1828:     }
  1829: 
  1830:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
  1831:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
  1832:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
  1833:       }
  1834:     }
  1835:   }
  1836: 
  1837:   for (const d of last1) {
  1838:     const seen = new Set();
  1839:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1840:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1841:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1842:       seen.add(g);
  1843:       out.get(g).recentLast1Top5 += 1;
  1844:     }
  1845:   }
  1846: 
  1847:   for (const d of last2) {
  1848:     const seen = new Set();
  1849:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1850:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1851:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1852:       seen.add(g);
  1853:       out.get(g).recentLast2Top5 += 1;
  1854:     }
  1855:   }
  1856: 
  1857:   for (const d of last3) {
  1858:     const seen = new Set();
  1859:     for (const it of getAllTop5Groups(d)) {
  1860:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1861:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1862:       seen.add(g);
  1863:       out.get(g).recentLast3Top5 += 1;
  1864:     }
  1865:   }
  1866: 
  1867:   return out;
  1868: }
  1869: 
  1870: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
  1871:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
  1872:     return {
  1873:       transition: 0.25,
  1874:       pair: 0.25,
  1875:       memory: 0.2,
  1876:       recent: 0.15,
  1877:       structural: 0.1,
  1878:       late: 0.05,
  1879:     };
  1880:   }
  1881: 
  1882:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
  1883:     return {
  1884:       transition: 0.2,
  1885:       pair: 0.1,
  1886:       memory: 0.1,
  1887:       recent: 0.2,
  1888:       structural: 0.3,
  1889:       late: 0.1,
  1890:     };
  1891:   }
  1892: 
  1893:   return {
  1894:     transition: 0.3,
  1895:     pair: 0.15,
  1896:     memory: 0.15,
  1897:     recent: 0.15,
  1898:     structural: 0.2,
  1899:     late: 0.05,
  1900:   };
  1901: }
  1902: 
  1903: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
  1904:   const safe = {
  1905:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
  1906:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
  1907:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
  1908:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
  1909:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
  1910:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
  1911:   };
  1912: 
  1913:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
  1914:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
  1915:     return {
  1916:       transition: 0.3,
  1917:       pair: 0.15,
  1918:       memory: 0.15,
  1919:       recent: 0.15,
  1920:       structural: 0.2,
  1921:       late: 0.05,
  1922:     };
  1923:   }
  1924: 
  1925:   return {
  1926:     transition: safe.transition / total,
  1927:     pair: safe.pair / total,
  1928:     memory: safe.memory / total,
  1929:     recent: safe.recent / total,
  1930:     structural: safe.structural / total,
  1931:     late: safe.late / total,
  1932:   };
  1933: }
  1934: 
  1935: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  1936:   draws,
  1937:   lotteryKey,
  1938:   targetHour,
  1939:   targetDow,
  1940:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1941: ) {
  1942:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1943:     draws,
  1944:     lotteryKey,
  1945:     targetHour,
  1946:     targetDow
  1947:   );
  1948: 
  1949:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1950:   const last = new Map();
  1951: 
  1952:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1953:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
  1954:   }
  1955: 
  1956:   for (const d of list) {
  1957:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1958:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1959:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  1960:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1961: 
  1962:     for (const it of items) {
  1963:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1964:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1965: 
  1966:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
  1967:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
  1968:         last.set(g, ts);
  1969:       }
  1970:     }
  1971:   }
  1972: 
  1973:   return last;
  1974: }
  1975: 
  1976: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
  1977:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
  1978:   const freq = new Map();
  1979:   const firstFreq = new Map();
  1980:   const seq = [];
  1981: 
  1982:   for (const d of list) {
  1983:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1984:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
  1985: 
  1986:     const gg = Number(g);
  1987:     seq.push(gg);
  1988:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  1989: 
  1990:     const items = getAllTop5Groups(d);
  1991:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
  1992:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  1993:     }
  1994:   }
  1995: 
  1996:   const total = seq.length;
  1997:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
  1998:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
  1999: 
  2000:   let dominante = null;
  2001:   let dominanteCount = -1;
  2002:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
  2003:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
  2004:       dominante = Number(g);
  2005:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
  2006:     }
  2007:   }
  2008: 
  2009:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
  2010:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
  2011:   const repeatNow =
  2012:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
  2013:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
  2014:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
  2015: 
  2016:   return {
  2017:     total,
  2018:     unique,
  2019:     diversidade,
  2020:     dominante,
  2021:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
  2022:     repeatNow,
  2023:     freq,
  2024:     firstFreq,
  2025:     seq,
  2026:   };
  2027: }
  2028: 
  2029: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2030:   drawsRange,
  2031:   lotteryKey,
  2032:   targetHour,
  2033:   targetDow,
  2034:   targetDayOfMonth,
  2035:   prevPair,
  2036:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2037:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2038:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2039:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2040: }) {
  2041:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2042:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
  2043:     lotteryKey,
  2044:     targetHour,
  2045:     targetDow
  2046:   );
  2047: 
  2048:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2049:   const outFreq = new Map();
  2050: 
  2051:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
  2052: 
  2053:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
  2054:   if (pair.length < 2) {
  2055:     return {
  2056:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
  2057:       samples: 0,
  2058:       weightedSamples: 0,
  2059:       exactDomSamples: 0,
  2060:       freq: outFreq,
  2061:     };
  2062:   }
  2063: 
  2064:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
  2065:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
  2066:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2067: 
  2068:   let matchedSamples = 0;
  2069:   let weightedSamples = 0;
  2070:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
  2071: 
  2072:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2073:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2074:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2075:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2076:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
  2077: 
  2078:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
  2079:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
  2080:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
  2081: 
  2082:     matchedSamples += 1;
  2083: 
  2084:     let weight = 1;
  2085: 
  2086:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
  2087:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
  2088:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
  2089:         weight = 1.35;
  2090:         exactDomSamples += 1;
  2091:       }
  2092:     }
  2093: 
  2094:     weightedSamples += weight;
  2095:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
  2096:   }
  2097: 
  2098:   const prob = new Map();
  2099:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
  2100: 
  2101:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2102:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2103:   }
  2104: 
  2105:   return {
  2106:     prob,
  2107:     samples: matchedSamples,
  2108:     weightedSamples,
  2109:     exactDomSamples,
  2110:     freq: outFreq,
  2111:   };
  2112: }
  2113: 
  2114: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2115:   comparableSeq,
  2116:   currentState,
  2117:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2118: ) {
  2119:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
  2120:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
  2121:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2122: 
  2123:   const freq = new Map();
  2124:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  2125: 
  2126:   if (state.length < 2) {
  2127:     const prob = new Map();
  2128:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
  2129:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
  2130:   }
  2131: 
  2132:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
  2133:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
  2134: 
  2135:   let samples = 0;
  2136: 
  2137:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2138:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2139:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2140:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2141: 
  2142:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
  2143:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
  2144:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
  2145: 
  2146:     samples += 1;
  2147:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
  2148:   }
  2149: 
  2150:   const prob = new Map();
  2151:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
  2152: 
  2153:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2154:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2155:   }
  2156: 
  2157:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
  2158: }
  2159: 
  2160: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2161:   comparableSeq,
  2162:   targetTs,
  2163:   windowSize = 4,
  2164:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2165: ) {
  2166:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
  2167:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
  2168:     targetTs,
  2169:     windowSize
  2170:   );
  2171: 
  2172:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2173:   const counts = new Map();
  2174:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
  2175: 
  2176:   for (const item of seq) {
  2177:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
  2178:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  2179:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  2180:   }
  2181: 
  2182:   const boost = new Map();
  2183:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
  2184: 
  2185:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2186:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
  2187:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
  2188:   }
  2189: 
  2190:   return {
  2191:     boost,
  2192:     counts,
  2193:     windowSize,
  2194:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
  2195:   };
  2196: }
  2197: 
  2198: /* =========================
  2199:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
  2200: ========================= */
  2201: 
  2202: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
  2203:   lotteryKey,
  2204:   drawsRange,
  2205:   drawLast,
  2206:   drawsToday,
  2207:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2208:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2209:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2210:   topN = 3,
  2211:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2212:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2213: }) {
  2214:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  2215:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
  2216:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2217:   }
  2218: 
  2219:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2220: 
  2221:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  2222:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  2223:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  2224: 
  2225:   if (
  2226:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  2227:     !lastH ||
  2228:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  2229:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
  2230:   ) {
  2231:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2232:   }
  2233: 
  2234:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2235:     lotteryKey,
  2236:     ymd: lastY,
  2237:     hourBucket: lastH,
  2238:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2239:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2240:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2241:   });
  2242: 
  2243:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  2244:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  2245: 
  2246:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2247:     ? forcedTargetY
  2248:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  2249: 
  2250:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2251:     ? forcedTargetH
  2252:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  2253:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  2254:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  2255: 
  2256:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  2257:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2258:   }
  2259: 
  2260:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  2261: 
  2262:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
  2263:   const prevPair =
  2264:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
  2265:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
  2266:       : [];
  2267: 
  2268:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2269:     drawsRange: list,
  2270:     lotteryKey: key,
  2271:     targetHour: targetH,
  2272:     targetDow,
  2273:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2274:     prevPair,
  2275:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2276:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2277:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2278:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2279:   });
  2280: 
  2281:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
  2282:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
  2283:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
  2284:   );
  2285: 
  2286:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  2287:     lotteryKey,
  2288:     drawsRange: list,
  2289:     targetHour: targetH,
  2290:     targetDow,
  2291:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2292:     prevHour: lastH,
  2293:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2294:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2295:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2296:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2297:   });
  2298: 
  2299:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
  2300:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
  2301:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
  2302: 
  2303:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
  2304:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
  2305:     pTransitionFirst.set(
  2306:       g,
  2307:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
  2308:     );
  2309:   }
  2310: 
  2311:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
  2312:     list,
  2313:     key,
  2314:     targetH,
  2315:     targetDow,
  2316:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2317:   );
  2318:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
  2319: 
  2320:   const structuralTop5 = computeComparableTop5Distribution(
  2321:     list,
  2322:     key,
  2323:     targetH,
  2324:     targetDow,
  2325:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2326:   );
  2327:   const pStructuralTop5 = structuralTop5?.prob || new Map();
  2328: 
  2329:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
  2330:     list,
  2331:     key,
  2332:     targetH,
  2333:     targetDow,
  2334:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2335:   );
  2336:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
  2337: 
  2338:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2339:     list,
  2340:     key,
  2341:     targetH,
  2342:     targetDow
  2343:   );
  2344: 
  2345:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
  2346:     comparableSeq,
  2347:     targetTs,
  2348:     3
  2349:   );
  2350: 
  2351:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
  2352:     drawsRange: list,
  2353:     lotteryKey: key,
  2354:     targetHour: targetH,
  2355:     targetDow,
  2356:     currentState,
  2357:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2358:   });
  2359: 
  2360:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
  2361: 
  2362:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2363:     comparableSeq,
  2364:     currentState,
  2365:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2366:   );
  2367:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
  2368: 
  2369:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2370:     comparableSeq,
  2371:     targetTs,
  2372:     4,
  2373:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2374:   );
  2375:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
  2376: 
  2377:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
  2378:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
  2379:   );
  2380: 
  2381:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
  2382: 
  2383:   const mode =
  2384:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
  2385:       ? "REPEAT"
  2386:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
  2387:         ? "SPREAD"
  2388:         : "NEUTRAL";
  2389: 
  2390:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
  2391: 
  2392:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  2393:     list,
  2394:     key,
  2395:     targetH,
  2396:     targetDow,
  2397:     targetTs,
  2398:     8
  2399:   );
  2400:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  2401: 
  2402:   const recentMaxTop5 = Math.max(
  2403:     1,
  2404:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentTop5 || 0))
  2405:   );
  2406:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
  2407:     1,
  2408:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
  2409:   );
  2410:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
  2411:     1,
  2412:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
  2413:   );
  2414: 
  2415:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  2416:     list,
  2417:     key,
  2418:     targetH,
  2419:     targetDow,
  2420:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2421:   );
  2422: 
  2423:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
  2424:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
  2425:   );
  2426:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
  2427:   const maxGapMsBase =
  2428:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
  2429:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
  2430:       : 1;
  2431: 
  2432:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
  2433:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
  2434: 
  2435:   const confidence =
  2436:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
  2437:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
  2438:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
  2439: 
  2440:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
  2441:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
  2442:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
  2443:     memory: weights.memory,
  2444:     recent: weights.recent,
  2445:     structural: weights.structural,
  2446:     late: weights.late,
  2447:   });
  2448: 
  2449:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  2450:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  2451: 
  2452:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2453:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2454:     const pST = Number(pStructuralTop5.get(grupo) || 0);
  2455:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
  2456:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
  2457:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
  2458:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
  2459:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2460: 
  2461:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
  2462:       recentTop5: 0,
  2463:       recentFirst: 0,
  2464:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  2465:       recentLast1Top5: 0,
  2466:       recentLast2Top5: 0,
  2467:       recentLast3Top5: 0,
  2468:     };
  2469: 
  2470:     const recentTop5Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentTop5, recentMaxTop5);
  2471:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
  2472:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
  2473:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1Top5, 1);
  2474:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2Top5, 2);
  2475: 
  2476:     const recentComposite =
  2477:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.34) +
  2478:       (recentTop5Norm * 0.24) +
  2479:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
  2480:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
  2481:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
  2482: 
  2483:     const dominanceScore =
  2484:       (pT * 0.42) +
  2485:       (pSF * 0.26) +
  2486:       (pST * 0.18) +
  2487:       (pD * 0.14);
  2488: 
  2489:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
  2490:     const gapMs =
  2491:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
  2492:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
  2493:         : maxGapMsBase;
  2494: 
  2495:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
  2496:       0,
  2497:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
  2498:     );
  2499: 
  2500:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2501:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2502:     const isDominantToday =
  2503:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
  2504:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
  2505: 
  2506:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
  2507: 
  2508:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
  2509:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
  2510:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
  2511:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
  2512:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
  2513: 
  2514:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
  2515:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
  2516:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
  2517:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
  2518:     }
  2519: 
  2520:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
  2521:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
  2522:     }
  2523: 
  2524:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
  2525: 
  2526:     const pairSequenceScore =
  2527:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
  2528:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
  2529:         : 0;
  2530: 
  2531:     const structuralBlend =
  2532:       (pSF * 0.45) +
  2533:       (pST * 0.35) +
  2534:       (pD * 0.2);
  2535: 
  2536:     const memoryBlend =
  2537:       (pM * 0.75) +
  2538:       (pSeq * 0.25);
  2539: 
  2540:     const recentBlend =
  2541:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
  2542:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
  2543: 
  2544:     const prob =
  2545:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
  2546:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
  2547:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
  2548:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
  2549:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
  2550:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
  2551: 
  2552:     const score =
  2553:       (prob * 1000) +
  2554:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
  2555:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
  2556:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
  2557:       (confidence * 50);
  2558: 
  2559:     return {
  2560:       grupo,
  2561:       prob,
  2562:       scoreProb: prob,
  2563:       score,
  2564:       probTransition: pT,
  2565:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
  2566:       probStructuralTop5: pST,
  2567:       probMemory: pM,
  2568:       probDuplication: pD,
  2569:       probSeq2: pSeq,
  2570:       probPair: pPair,
  2571:       pairProb: pPair,
  2572:       pairConfidence,
  2573:       repeatBoost,
  2574:       dominanceScore,
  2575:       recentComposite,
  2576:       recentTop5: rm.recentTop5,
  2577:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
  2578:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
  2579:       recentLast1Top5: rm.recentLast1Top5,
  2580:       recentLast2Top5: rm.recentLast2Top5,
  2581:       recentLast3Top5: rm.recentLast3Top5,
  2582:       lateNorm,
  2583:       gapMs,
  2584:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
  2585:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2586:       structuralTop5Count: Number(structuralTop5?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2587:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2588:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2589:       dayFreq,
  2590:       dayFirstFreq,
  2591:       isDominantToday,
  2592:       dayFlowConfidence,
  2593:       dayFlowScore,
  2594:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2595:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2596:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2597:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  2598:       pairSequenceScore,
  2599:     };
  2600:   })
  2601:     .sort((a, b) => {
  2602:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  2603:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
  2604:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
  2605:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
  2606:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
  2607:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
  2608:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
  2609:       if (b.probStructuralTop5 !== a.probStructuralTop5) return b.probStructuralTop5 - a.probStructuralTop5;
  2610:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  2611:     })
  2612:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  2613: 
  2614:   if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  2615:     console.table(
  2616:       ranked.map((x) => ({
  2617:         slot: `${targetY} ${targetH}`,
  2618:         grupo: x.grupo,
  2619:         score: Number(x.score || 0).toFixed(2),
  2620:         final: (Number(x.scoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2621:         transicao: (Number(x.probTransition || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2622:         estrutural1: (Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2623:         estruturalTop5: (Number(x.probStructuralTop5 || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2624:         memoria: (Number(x.probMemory || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2625:         par: (Number(x.probPair || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2626:         duplicacao: (Number(x.probDuplication || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2627:         recencia: (Number(x.recentComposite || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2628:         atraso: (Number(x.lateNorm || 0) * 100).toFixed(2),
  2629:         dayFreq: x.dayFreq,
  2630:         dayFirst: x.dayFirstFreq,
  2631:         dominante: x.isDominantToday,
  2632:         pairSamples: x.pairSamples,
  2633:         condFirstCount: x.condFirstCount,
  2634:         structuralFirstCount: x.structuralFirstCount,
  2635:         structuralTop5Count: x.structuralTop5Count,
  2636:       }))
  2637:     );
  2638: 
  2639:     console.log("[TOP3 V2 CONTEXTO]", {
  2640:       targetY,
  2641:       targetH,

  2675:   ];
  2676:

  2679: 
  2680:     return {

  2687:             : "3º mais provável",

  2690:       probCond: Number(x.probTransition || 0),
  2691:       probBase: Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0),
  2692:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateNorm || 0),
  2693:       freq: x.condFirstCount,
  2694:       freqCond: x.condFirstCount,
  2695:       freqBase: x.structuralFirstCount,
  2696:       freqZeroWhy:
  2697:         x.condFirstCount <= 0
  2698:           ? `Sem 1º lugar nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
  2699:           : "",

  2709:         `Grupo G${g2}: memória curta=${(x.probMemory * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2710:         `Grupo G${g2}: recência composta=${(x.recentComposite * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2711:         `Grupo G${g2}: atraso normalizado=${(x.lateNorm * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2712:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs na camada=${x.condFirstCount}`,
  2713:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs estruturais=${x.structuralFirstCount}`,
  2714:         `Grupo G${g2}: TOP3 estruturais=${x.structuralTop5Count}`,
  2715:         `Grupo G${g2}: draws com duplicação=${x.duplicationCount}`,
  2716:         `Grupo G${g2}: peso de memória=${Number(x.memoryWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2717:         `Grupo G${g2}: frequência no dia=${x.dayFreq}`,
  2718:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs no dia=${x.dayFirstFreq}`,
  2719:         `Grupo G${g2}: dominante no dia=${x.isDominantToday ? "sim" : "não"}`,
  2720:         `Grupo G${g2}: score do fluxo do dia=${Number(x.dayFlowScore || 0).toFixed(2)} | confiança=${(Number(x.dayFlowConfidence || 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  2721:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | amostras=${x.pairSamples} | amostras ponderadas=${Number(x.pairWeightedSamples || 0).toFixed(2)} | DOM exato=${x.pairExactDomSamples} | ocorrências=${Number(x.pairCount || 0).toFixed(2)} | prob=${(Number(x.pairProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}% | score=${Number(x.pairSequenceScore || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2722:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2723:       ],
  2724:       meta: {
  2725:         trigger: {
  2726:           ymd: lastY,
  2727:           hour: lastH,
  2728:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2729:         },
  2730:         next: {
  2731:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  2732:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  2733:         },
  2734:         samples: condSamples,
  2735:         scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
  2736:         explain: {
  2737:           engine: "V2_BICHO",
  2738:           mode,
  2739:           regime,
  2740:           repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
  2741:           uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
  2742:           weights: adjustedWeights,
  2743:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  2744:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  2745:           layerSamples: condSamples,
  2746:           structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
  2747:           structuralTop5Samples: Number(structuralTop5?.totalSamples || 0),
  2748:           duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
  2749:           memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
  2750:           memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
  2751:           seq2Samples: Number(seq2Out?.samples || 0),
  2752:           seq2Prev2: Number(seq2Out?.prev2 || 0),
  2753:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),
  2754:           pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2755:           pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2756:           pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2757:           pairConfidence,
  2758:           repeatWindowSize: Number(repeatBoostOut?.windowSize || 0),
  2759:           repeatWindowConsidered: Number(repeatBoostOut?.considered || 0),
  2760:           currentState,
  2761:           targetDow,
  2762:           targetDayOfMonth,
  2763:           prevHour: lastH,
  2764:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2765:           dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
  2766:           dayFlowConfidence,
  2767:           dominanceScore: x.dominanceScore,
  2768:           recentComposite: x.recentComposite,
  2769:           recentTop5: x.recentTop5,
  2770:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
  2771:           recentDupDraws: x.recentDupDraws,
  2772:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  2773:             key: layer.key,
  2774:             label: layer.label,
  2775:             samples: layer.samples,
  2776:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  2777:           })),
  2778:         },
  2779:       },
  2780:     };
  2781:   });
  2782: 
  2783:   return {
  2784:     top,
  2785:     meta: {
  2786:       trigger: {
  2787:         ymd: lastY,
  2788:         hour: lastH,
  2789:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2790:       },
  2791:       next: {
  2792:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  2793:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  2794:       },
  2795:       samples: condSamples,
  2796:       scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
  2797:       explain: {
  2798:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
  2799:         mode,
  2800:         regime,
  2801:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
  2802:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
  2803:         weights: adjustedWeights,
  2804:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  2805:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  2806:         layerSamples: condSamples,
  2807:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
  2808:         structuralTop5Samples: Number(structuralTop5?.totalSamples || 0),
  2809:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
  2810:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
  2811:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
  2812:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2813:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2814:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2815:         pairConfidence,
  2816:         currentState,
  2817:         targetDow,
  2818:         targetDayOfMonth,
  2819:         prevHour: lastH,
  2820:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2821:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
  2822:         dayFlowConfidence,
  2823:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  2824:           key: layer.key,
  2825:           label: layer.label,
  2826:           samples: layer.samples,
  2827:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  2828:         })),
  2829:       },
  2830:     },

  2832: }
  2833: 
  2834: 
  2835: /* =========================
  2836:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
  2837: ========================= */
  2838: 
  2839: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
  2840:   const k = Number(key);
  2841:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
  2842:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
  2843: }
  2844: 
  2845: function emptyGroupMap() {
  2846:   const out = new Map();
  2847:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
  2848:   return out;
  2849: }
  2850: 
  2851: function getTop5GroupSet(draw) {
  2852:   return new Set(
  2853:     getAllTop5Groups(draw)
  2854:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
  2855:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
  2856:   );
  2857: }
  2858: 
  2859: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
  2860:   const out = new Map();
  2861:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
  2862:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
  2863:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
  2864:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
  2865: 
  2866:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2867:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
  2868:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
  2869:   }
  2870: 
  2871:   return out;
  2872: }
  2873: 
  2874: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
  2875:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
  2876:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
  2877:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
  2878: }
  2879: 
  2880: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  2881:   lotteryKey,
  2882:   drawsRange,
  2883:   drawLast,
  2884:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2885:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2886:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2887:   topN = 3,
  2888:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2889:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2890: }) {
  2891:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
  2892:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
  2893: 
  2894:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2895: 
  2896:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  2897:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  2898:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  2899: 
  2900:   if (
  2901:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  2902:     !lastH ||
  2903:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  2904:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
  2905:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2906:   ) {
  2907:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2908:   }
  2909: 
  2910:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2911:     lotteryKey: key,
  2912:     ymd: lastY,
  2913:     hourBucket: lastH,
  2914:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2915:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2916:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2917:   });
  2918: 
  2919:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  2920:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  2921: 
  2922:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2923:     ? forcedTargetY
  2924:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  2925: 
  2926:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2927:     ? forcedTargetH
  2928:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  2929:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  2930:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  2931:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  2932: 
  2933:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  2934:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2935:   }
  2936: 
  2937:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
  2938:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  2939:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  2940:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  2941:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
  2942:   });
  2943: 
  2944:   const layers = {
  2945:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
  2946:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
  2947:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
  2948:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
  2949:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), top5: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
  2950:   };
  2951: 
  2952:   for (const d of history) {
  2953:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  2954:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  2955:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
  2956:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
  2957:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  2958:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
  2959: 
  2960:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
  2961:       layer.samples += 1;
  2962:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
  2963:       for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layer.top5, g, 1);
  2964:     };
  2965: 
  2966:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
  2967:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
  2968:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
  2969:   }
  2970: 
  2971:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
  2972: 
  2973:   for (const prev of history) {
  2974:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
  2975:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
  2976:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
  2977: 
  2978:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
  2979: 
  2980:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2981:       lotteryKey: key,
  2982:       ymd: py,
  2983:       hourBucket: ph,
  2984:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2985:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2986:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2987:     });
  2988: 
  2989:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
  2990:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
  2991: 
  2992:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
  2993:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
  2994: 
  2995:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
  2996:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(nextDraw);
  2997: 
  2998:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
  2999:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
  3000:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.transition.top5, g, 1);
  3001:   }
  3002: 
  3003:   const recentComparable = history
  3004:     .filter((d) => {
  3005:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3006:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3007:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3008:     })
  3009:     .slice(-20);
  3010: 
  3011:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
  3012:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  3013:     const top5Set = getTop5GroupSet(d);
  3014: 
  3015:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
  3016:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
  3017:     for (const g of top5Set) incMap(layers.recent.top5, g, 1);
  3018:   }
  3019: 
  3020:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
  3021:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
  3022:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  3023:   const sceneWeight =
  3024:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
  3025:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
  3026: 
  3027: 
  3028:   const activeWeights = {};
  3029:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3030: 
  3031:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3032:     const conf =
  3033:       keyLayer === "transition"
  3034:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
  3035:         : keyLayer === "recent"
  3036:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
  3037:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
  3038: 
  3039:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
  3040:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
  3041:     totalWeight += w;
  3042:   }
  3043: 
  3044:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
  3045:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
  3046:     totalWeight = 1;
  3047:   }
  3048: 
  3049:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
  3050:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
  3051:   }
  3052: 
  3053:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  3054:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  3055: 
  3056:     let scoreProb = 0;
  3057:     const details = {};
  3058: 
  3059:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3060:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3061:       const pTop5 = Number(layerProbability(layer.top5, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3062: 
  3063:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.75) + (pTop5 * 0.25);
  3064:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
  3065: 
  3066:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
  3067: 
  3068:       details[keyLayer] = {
  3069:         label: layer.label,
  3070:         samples: layer.samples,
  3071:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
  3072:         top3Count: Number(layer.top5.get(grupo) || 0),
  3073:         probability: pLayer,
  3074:         weight: w,
  3075:       };
  3076:     }
  3077: 
  3078:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  3079:     const pScene =
  3080:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
  3081:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
  3082: 
  3083:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
  3084:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
  3085:     }
  3086: 
  3087:     details.scene = {
  3088:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
  3089:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3090:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  3091:       top3Count: 0,
  3092:       probability: pScene,
  3093:       weight: sceneWeight,
  3094:     };
  3095: 
  3096:     return {
  3097:       grupo,
  3098:       scoreProb,
  3099:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
  3100:       details,
  3101:     };
  3102:   })
  3103:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3104:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  3105:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  3106:     })
  3107:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  3108: 
  3109:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
  3110:     ranked,
  3111:     {
  3112:       lotteryKey,
  3113:       targetYmd: targetY,
  3114:       targetHour: targetH,
  3115:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
  3116:       totalDraws: history.length,
  3117:     }
  3118:   );
  3119: 
  3120:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
  3121:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  3122:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
  3123: 
  3124:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
  3125:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
  3126:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
  3127:       .slice(0, 3);
  3128: 
  3129:     return {
  3130:       rank: idx + 1,
  3131:       title:
  3132:         idx === 0
  3133:           ? "Mais provável"
  3134:           : idx === 1
  3135:             ? "2º mais provável"
  3136:             : "3º mais provável",
  3137:       grupo: x.grupo,
  3138:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3139:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3140:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
  3141:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
  3142:       lateBonus: 0,
  3143:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3144:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
  3145:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3146:       freqZeroWhy: "",
  3147:       reasons: [
  3148:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
  3149:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  3150:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
  3151:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  3152:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
  3153:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
  3154:         ),
  3155:       ],
  3156:       meta: {
  3157:         trigger: {
  3158:           ymd: lastY,
  3159:           hour: lastH,
  3160:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3161:         },
  3162:         next: {
  3163:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3164:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3165:         },
  3166:         samples: history.length,
  3167:         period: {
  3168:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
  3169:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
  3170:         },
  3171:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3172:         explain: {
  3173:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3174:           targetDow,
  3175:           targetDayOfMonth,
  3176:           prevHour: lastH,
  3177:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3178:           activeWeights,
  3179:           scene: {
  3180:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3181:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
  3182:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
  3183:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
  3184:           },
  3185:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3186:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3187:               k,
  3188:               {
  3189:                 label: v.label,
  3190:                 samples: v.samples,
  3191:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
  3192:               },
  3193:             ])
  3194:           ),
  3195:           details: x.details,
  3196:         },
  3197:       },
  3198:     };
  3199:   });
  3200: 
  3201:   return {
  3202:     top,
  3203:     meta: {
  3204:       trigger: {
  3205:         ymd: lastY,
  3206:         hour: lastH,
  3207:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3208:       },
  3209:       next: {
  3210:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3211:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3212:       },
  3213:       samples: history.length,
  3214:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3215:       explain: {
  3216:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3217:         targetDow,
  3218:         targetDayOfMonth,
  3219:         prevHour: lastH,
  3220:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3221:         activeWeights,
  3222:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3223:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3224:             k,
  3225:             {
  3226:               label: v.label,
  3227:               samples: v.samples,
  3228:               weight: activeWeights[k],
  3229:             },
  3230:           ])
  3231:         ),
  3232:       },
  3233:     },
  3234:   };
  3235: }
  3236: 
  3237: 
  3238: 
  3239: /* =========================
  3240:    TOP3 SCENE READER
  3241: ========================= */
  3242: 
  3243: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
  3244:   const candidates = [
  3245:     prize?.milhar,
  3246:     prize?.milhar4,
  3247:     prize?.numero,
  3248:     prize?.number,
  3249:     prize?.value,
  3250:     prize?.result,
  3251:     prize?.resultado,
  3252:     prize?.premio,
  3253:   ];
  3254: 
  3255:   for (const v of candidates) {
  3256:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
  3257:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
  3258:   }
  3259: 
  3260:   return "";
  3261: }
  3262: 
  3263: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
  3264:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3265:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
  3266: 
  3267:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
  3268:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
  3269: 
  3270:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
  3271:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
  3272: 
  3273:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
  3274: }
  3275: 
  3276: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
  3277:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  3278: 
  3279:   const rows = prizes
  3280:     .map((p) => {
  3281:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
  3282:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
  3283:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
  3284: 
  3285:       return {
  3286:         pos,
  3287:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
  3288:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
  3289:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
  3290:         grupo,
  3291:       };
  3292:     })
  3293:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
  3294:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
  3295: 
  3296:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
  3297:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
  3298:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
  3299:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
  3300: 
  3301:   const countMap = (arr) => {
  3302:     const m = new Map();
  3303:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
  3304:     return Array.from(m.entries())
  3305:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
  3306:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
  3307:   };
  3308: 
  3309:   return {
  3310:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
  3311:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
  3312:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
  3313:     grupos,
  3314:     dezenas,
  3315:     centenas,
  3316:     milhares,
  3317:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
  3318:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
  3319:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
  3320:     rows,
  3321:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
  3322:   };
  3323: }
  3324: 
  3325: 
  3326: 
  3327: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
  3328:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
  3329: 
  3330:   let score = 0;
  3331:   const details = {};
  3332: 
  3333:   const add = (key, value) => {
  3334:     if (!value) return;
  3335:     score += value;
  3336:     details[key] = value;
  3337:   };
  3338: 
  3339:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
  3340:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
  3341: 
  3342:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
  3343:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
  3344: 
  3345:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
  3346:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
  3347: 
  3348:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
  3349:     add("firstGrupo",45);
  3350: 
  3351:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
  3352:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
  3353:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
  3354:   }
  3355: 
  3356:   add(
  3357:     "groupOverlap",
  3358:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
  3359:   );
  3360: 
  3361:   add(
  3362:     "dezenaOverlap",
  3363:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
  3364:   );
  3365: 
  3366:   add(
  3367:     "centenaOverlap",
  3368:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
  3369:   );
  3370: 
  3371:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3372:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3373: 
  3374:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
  3375:     add("repeatExists",8);
  3376: 
  3377:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
  3378:     add("repeatEqual",6);
  3379: 
  3380:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
  3381:     add("signature",60);
  3382: 
  3383:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
  3384:     add("hour",8);
  3385: 
  3386:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
  3387: }
  3388: 
  3389: 
  3390: 
  3391: 
  3392: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
  3393:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  3394:   if (!currentScene) return [];
  3395: 
  3396:   const ordered = list
  3397:     .map((draw) => ({
  3398:       draw,
  3399:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
  3400:     }))
  3401:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
  3402:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3403:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3404:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3405:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
  3406:     });
  3407: 
  3408:   return ordered
  3409:     .map((item, idx) => {
  3410:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
  3411:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
  3412: 
  3413:       return {
  3414:         draw: item.draw,
  3415:         scene: item.scene,
  3416:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
  3417:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
  3418:         score,
  3419:       };
  3420:     })
  3421:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
  3422:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3423:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
  3424:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3425:       }
  3426: 
  3427:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3428:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3429:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
  3430:     })
  3431:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
  3432: }
  3433: 
  3434: 
  3435: 
  3436: 
  3437: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  3438:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
  3439:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  3440: 
  3441:   const freq = new Map();
  3442:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  3443: 
  3444:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3445: 
  3446:   for (const item of ranking) {
  3447:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
  3448:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
  3449: 
  3450:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
  3451:     if (score <= 0) continue;
  3452: 
  3453:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
  3454:     totalWeight += score;
  3455:   }
  3456: 
  3457:   const prob = new Map();
  3458:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  3459:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
  3460:   }
  3461: 
  3462:   return {
  3463:     freq,
  3464:     prob,
  3465:     samples: ranking.length,
  3466:     totalWeight,
  3467:   };
  3468: }
  3469: 
  3470: 
  3471: function logTop3SceneDebug(label, scene, extra = {}) {
  3472:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  3473:   if (!scene) return;
  3474: 
  3475:   console.log(`[TOP3 SCENE] ${label}`, {
  3476:     ...extra,
  3477:     ymd: scene.ymd,
  3478:     hour: scene.hour,
  3479:     firstGrupo: scene.firstGrupo,
  3480:     signature: scene.signature,
  3481:     grupos: scene.grupos,
  3482:     dezenas: scene.dezenas,
  3483:     centenas: scene.centenas,
  3484:     milhares: scene.milhares,
  3485:     repeatedGroups: scene.repeatedGroups,
  3486:     repeatedDezenas: scene.repeatedDezenas,
  3487:     repeatedCentenas: scene.repeatedCentenas,
  3488:     rows: scene.rows,
  3489:   });
  3490: }
