  3492: 
  3493: /* =========================
  3494:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
  3495: ========================= */
  3496: 
  3497: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
  3498:   const g = Number(grupo2);
  3499:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
  3500: 
  3501:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
  3502:   const out = [];
  3503: 
  3504:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
  3505:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
  3506:   }
  3507: 
  3508:   return out.filter(Boolean);
  3509: }
  3510: 
  3511: 
  3512: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
  3513:   rangeDraws,
  3514:   analysisHourBucket,
  3515:   schedule,
  3516:   grupo2,
  3517:   count = 20,
  3518:   targetYmd = "",
  3519: }) {
  3520:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  3521:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
  3522:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
  3523:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
  3524:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
  3525: 
  3526:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
  3527:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
  3528:     : 20;
  3529: 
  3530:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
  3531: 
  3532:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
  3533:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3534:   }
  3535: 
  3536:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
  3537:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3538: 
  3539:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
  3540:   const byMilhar = new Map();
  3541: 
  3542:   for (const d of list) {
  3543:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3544:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3545:     if (!h) continue;
  3546: 
  3547:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
  3548:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
  3549:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3550:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3551: 
  3552:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
  3553: 
  3554:     for (const prize of ps) {
  3555:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
  3556:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
  3557: 
  3558:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  3559:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
  3560: 
  3561:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3562:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
  3563: 
  3564:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
  3565:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
  3566: 
  3567:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
  3568: 
  3569:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
  3570:         milhar: m4,
  3571:         dezena: dz,
  3572:         centena: c3,
  3573:         freq: 0,
  3574:         score: 0,
  3575:         targetHits: 0,
  3576:         scheduleHits: 0,
  3577:         sameDowHits: 0,
  3578:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3579:         lastTs: 0,
  3580:       };
  3581: 
  3582:       let inc = 1;
  3583: 
  3584:       if (isTargetHour) {
  3585:         inc += 8;
  3586:         current.targetHits += 1;
  3587:       }
  3588: 
  3589:       if (isScheduleHour) {
  3590:         inc += 3;
  3591:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
  3592:       }
  3593: 
  3594:       if (isSameDow) {
  3595:         inc += 2;
  3596:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
  3597:       }
  3598: 
  3599:       if (pos === 1) {
  3600:         inc += 3;
  3601:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
  3602:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
  3603:         inc += 1;
  3604:       }
  3605: 
  3606:       current.freq += 1;
  3607:       current.score += inc;
  3608: 
  3609:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
  3610:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
  3611:       }
  3612: 
  3613:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
  3614:     }
  3615:   }
  3616: 
  3617:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
  3618:     .map((m) => {
  3619:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
  3620:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
  3621:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
  3622:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
  3623:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3624:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  3625: 
  3626:       return {
  3627:         ...m,
  3628:         score:
  3629:           freq * 100 +
  3630:           targetHits * 70 +
  3631:           scheduleHits * 35 +
  3632:           sameDowHits * 25 +
  3633:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
  3634:           recencyBoost * 5,
  3635:       };
  3636:     })
  3637:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3638:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3639:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3640:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3641:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
  3642:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3643:     });
  3644: 
  3645:   const centenaStats = new Map();
  3646: 
  3647:   for (const item of ranked) {
  3648:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
  3649:     if (!centena) continue;
  3650: 
  3651:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
  3652:       centena,
  3653:       freq: 0,
  3654:       score: 0,
  3655:       targetHits: 0,
  3656:       sameDowHits: 0,
  3657:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3658:       lastTs: 0,
  3659:     };
  3660: 
  3661:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
  3662:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
  3663:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
  3664:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
  3665:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3666:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
  3667: 
  3668:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
  3669:   }
  3670: 
  3671:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
  3672: 
  3673:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
  3674:     centenaScoreMap.set(
  3675:       c.centena,
  3676:       Number(c.score || 0) +
  3677:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
  3678:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
  3679:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
  3680:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
  3681:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
  3682:     );
  3683:   }
  3684: 
  3685:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
  3686:   const slots = [];
  3687: 
  3688:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
  3689:     const candidates = ranked
  3690:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
  3691:       .map((x) => {
  3692:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3693:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
  3694:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
  3695:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
  3696: 
  3697:         return {
  3698:           ...x,
  3699:           milhar,
  3700:           prefix,
  3701:           centena,
  3702:           centenaScore,
  3703:           adjustedScore:
  3704:             centenaScore * 2 +
  3705:             Number(x.score || 0) -
  3706:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
  3707:         };
  3708:       });
  3709: 
  3710:     const byCentena = new Map();
  3711: 
  3712:     for (const item of candidates) {
  3713:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
  3714:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
  3715:     }
  3716: 
  3717:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
  3718:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
  3719:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
  3720:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
  3721:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
  3722:           }
  3723:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3724:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3725:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3726:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3727:         });
  3728: 
  3729:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
  3730: 
  3731:         return {
  3732:           centena,
  3733:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
  3734:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
  3735:           items: sortedItems,
  3736:         };
  3737:       })
  3738:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3739:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
  3740:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
  3741:         }
  3742:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
  3743:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
  3744:         }
  3745:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
  3746:       });
  3747: 
  3748:     const picked = [];
  3749:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
  3750:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
  3751: 
  3752:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3753:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3754:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3755: 
  3756:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
  3757:       if (!item) continue;
  3758: 
  3759:       picked.push(item);
  3760:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3761:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3762:     }
  3763: 
  3764:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
  3765:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3766:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3767:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
  3768: 
  3769:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
  3770:       if (!item) continue;
  3771: 
  3772:       picked.push(item);
  3773:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3774:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3775:     }
  3776: 
  3777:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3778:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3779:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3780: 
  3781:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3782:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3783:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3784: 
  3785:         picked.push(item);
  3786:       }
  3787:     }
  3788: 
  3789:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3790:   }
  3791: 
  3792:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  3793:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  3794: 
  3795:     let pushed = 0;
  3796: 
  3797:     for (const item of items) {
  3798:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  3799:       slots.push({
  3800:         dezena: dz,
  3801:         milhar: item.milhar,
  3802:         score: item.score,
  3803:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  3804:         centena: item.centena,
  3805:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  3806:         freq: item.freq,
  3807:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  3808:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  3809:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  3810:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3811:       });
  3812:       pushed += 1;
  3813:     }
  3814: 
  3815:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3816:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3817:       pushed += 1;
  3818:     }
  3819:   }
  3820: 
  3821:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3822: 
  3823:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  3824: }
  3825: 
  3826: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3827:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
  3828: }
  3829: 
  3830: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3831:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
  3832: }
  3833: 
  3834: /* =========================
  3835:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
  3836: ========================= */
  3837: 
  3838: 
  3839: 
  3840: export function auditTop3Timeline({
  3841:   timeline,
  3842:   lotteryKey = "",
  3843: }) {
  3844:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
  3845: 
  3846:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
  3847:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  3848:     return status === "validated" && Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo));
  3849:   });
  3850: 
  3851:   const total = validated.length;
  3852: 
  3853:   const top1Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
  3854:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3855:     const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
  3856:     return Number.isFinite(first) && first === result;
  3857:   }).length;
  3858: 
  3859:   const top3Hits = validated.filter((slot) => {
  3860:     const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3861:     return (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3862:       .slice(0, 3)
  3863:       .some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result);
  3864:   }).length;
  3865: 
  3866:   function pct(n, d) {
  3867:     if (!d) return 0;
  3868:     return Number(((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2));
  3869:   }
  3870: 
  3871:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
  3872:     const map = new Map();
  3873: 
  3874:     for (const slot of validated) {
  3875:       const key = keyFn(slot);
  3876:       const current = map.get(key) || {
  3877:         key,
  3878:         total: 0,
  3879:         top1Hits: 0,
  3880:         top3Hits: 0,
  3881:       };
  3882: 
  3883:       const result = Number(slot.resultGrupo);
  3884:       const first = Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo);
  3885:       const picks = Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3.slice(0, 3) : [];
  3886: 
  3887:       current.total += 1;
  3888:       if (Number.isFinite(first) && first === result) current.top1Hits += 1;
  3889:       if (picks.some((item) => Number(item?.grupo) === result)) current.top3Hits += 1;
  3890: 
  3891:       map.set(key, current);
  3892:     }
  3893: 
  3894:     return Array.from(map.values())
  3895:       .map((x) => ({
  3896:         ...x,
  3897:         top1Rate: pct(x.top1Hits, x.total),
  3898:         top3Rate: pct(x.top3Hits, x.total),
  3899:       }))
  3900:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3901:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
  3902:         return b.total - a.total;
  3903:       });
  3904:   }
  3905: 
  3906:   return {
  3907:     lotteryKey,
  3908:     total,
  3909:     top1Hits,
  3910:     top3Hits,
  3911:     top1Rate: pct(top1Hits, total),
  3912:     top3Rate: pct(top3Hits, total),
  3913:     byHour: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")),
  3914:     byDate: groupBy((slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")),
  3915:     rows: validated.map((slot) => ({
  3916:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
  3917:       hour: slot.targetHour,
  3918:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
  3919:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
  3920:       resultGrupo: slot.resultGrupo,
  3921:       top3: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3922:         .slice(0, 3)
  3923:         .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo)),
  3924:       top1Hit:
  3925:         Number(slot?.top3?.[0]?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo),
  3926:       top3Hit: (Array.isArray(slot.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3927:         .slice(0, 3)
  3928:         .some((x) => Number(x?.grupo) === Number(slot.resultGrupo)),
  3929:     })),
  3930:   };
  3931: }
  3932: 
  3933: 
  3934: function buildTimelineForDate({
  3935:   ymd,
  3936:   drawsToday,
  3937:   drawsRange,
  3938:   lotteryKey,
  3939:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3940:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3941:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3942: }) {
  3943:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
  3944:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
  3945:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
  3946: 
  3947:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
  3948: 
  3949:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
  3950:     lotteryKey,
  3951:     ymd: targetYmd,
  3952:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3953:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3954:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3955:   });
  3956: 
  3957:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
  3958:     .map(toHourBucket)
  3959:     .filter(Boolean);
  3960: 
  3961:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
  3962: 
  3963:   const daySorted = day
  3964:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  3965:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3966:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  3967:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  3968:       return ta - tb;
  3969:     });
  3970: 
  3971:   const rangeSorted = range
  3972:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  3973:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3974:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  3975:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  3976:       return ta - tb;
  3977:     });
  3978: 
  3979:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
  3980: 
  3981:   for (const d of daySorted) {
  3982:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3983:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3984:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  3985:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
  3986:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
  3987:   }
  3988: 
  3989: 
  3990:   const chainContextDraws = [];
  3991:   const timeline = [];
  3992:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
  3993: 
  3994:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
  3995:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
  3996:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
  3997: 
  3998: 
  3999:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
  4000:       timeline.push({
  4001:         targetYmd,
  4002:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4003:         baseYmd: "",
  4004:         baseHour: "",
  4005:         top3: [],
  4006:         resultGrupo: null,
  4007:         hit: null,
  4008:         status: "pending",
  4009:       });
  4010:       continue;
  4011:     }
  4012: 
  4013:     let baseDraw = null;
  4014: 
  4015:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
  4016:       .filter((d) => {
  4017:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4018:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4019:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4020:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4021:       })
  4022:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4023:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4024:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4025:         return tb - ta;
  4026:       })[0] || null;
  4027: 
  4028:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
  4029: 
  4030:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4031:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
  4032:         .filter((d) => {
  4033:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4034:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4035:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4036:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4037:         })
  4038:         .sort((a, b) => {
  4039:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4040:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4041:           return tb - ta;
  4042:         })[0] || null;
  4043: 
  4044:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
  4045:     }
  4046: 
  4047:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4048:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
  4049:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
  4050:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4051:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4052:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4053: 
  4054:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
  4055: 
  4056:         baseDraw = d;
  4057:         break;
  4058:       }
  4059:     }
  4060: 
  4061:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4062:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
  4063:     }
  4064: 
  4065:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4066:       timeline.push({
  4067:         targetYmd,
  4068:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4069:         baseYmd: "",
  4070:         baseHour: "",
  4071:         top3: [],
  4072:         resultGrupo: null,
  4073:         hit: null,
  4074:         status: "pending",
  4075:       });
  4076:       continue;
  4077:     }
  4078: 
  4079:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
  4080:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
  4081:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
  4082:     );
  4083: 
  4084:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
  4085:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4086:         pickDrawYMD(d),
  4087:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4088:       );
  4089:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4090:     });
  4091: 
  4092:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
  4093:       .filter((d) => {
  4094:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4095:           pickDrawYMD(d),
  4096:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4097:         );
  4098:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4099:       })
  4100:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4101:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4102:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4103:         return ta - tb;
  4104:       });
  4105: 
  4106:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  4107:       lotteryKey,
  4108:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
  4109:       drawLast: baseDraw,
  4110:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
  4111:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4112:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4113:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4114:       topN: 3,
  4115:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
  4116:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
  4117:     });
  4118: 
  4119: 
  4120:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
  4121:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
  4122:     const computedForSlot =
  4123:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
  4124: 
  4125:     const top3 =
  4126:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
  4127:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
  4128:         : [];
  4129: 
  4130:     const resultGrupo = currentDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(currentDraw) : null;
  4131: 
  4132:     const hit =
  4133:       Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) && top3.length
  4134:         ? top3.some((t) => Number(t?.grupo) === Number(resultGrupo))
  4135:         : null;
  4136: 
  4137:     timeline.push({
  4138:       targetYmd,
  4139:       targetHour: slotHour,
  4140:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
  4141:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
  4142:       top3,
  4143:       resultGrupo: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? Number(resultGrupo) : null,
  4144:       hit,
  4145:       status: Number.isFinite(Number(resultGrupo)) ? "validated" : "pending",
  4146:     });
  4147: 
  4148:     if (currentDraw) {
  4149:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
  4150:     } else {
  4151:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
  4152:     }
  4153:   }
  4154: 
  4155:   return timeline;
  4156: 
  4157: }
  4158: 
  4159: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
  4160:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
  4161: }
  4162: 
  4163: export function auditTop3Backtest({
  4164:   drawsRange,
  4165:   lotteryKey,
  4166:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4167:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4168:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4169: }) {
  4170:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  4171:   const dates = Array.from(
  4172:     new Set(
  4173:       range
  4174:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
  4175:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
  4176:     )
  4177:   ).sort();
  4178: 
  4179:   const allTimeline = [];
  4180: 
  4181:   for (const ymd of dates) {
  4182:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
  4183: 
  4184:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
  4185:       ymd,
  4186:       drawsToday,
  4187:       drawsRange: range,
  4188:       lotteryKey,
  4189:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4190:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4191:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4192:     });
  4193: 
  4194:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
  4195:   }
  4196: 
  4197:   return auditTop3Timeline({
  4198:     timeline: allTimeline,
  4199:     lotteryKey,
  4200:   });
  4201: }

    34: function findPreviousValidDraw(draws, currentYmd, currentHour) {
    35:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
    36:   const targetY = safeStr(currentYmd);
    37:   const targetH = toHourBucket(currentHour);
    38: 
    39:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH) return null;
    40: 
    41:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
    42: 
    43:   return list
    44:     .filter((draw) => Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) && draw.prizes.length > 0)
    45:     .map((draw) => {
    46:       const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    47:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    48:       return { draw, ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h) };
    49:     })
    50:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts < targetTs)
    51:     .sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
    52: }

    58: function grupoFromDezena2(dezena2) {
    59:   const s = safeStr(dezena2);
    60:   if (!/^\d{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
    61: 
    62:   const n = Number(s);
    63:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 99) return null;
    64:   if (n === 0) return 25;
    65: 
    66:   const g = Math.ceil(n / 4);
    67:   return g >= 1 && g <= 25 ? g : null;
    68: }
    69: 
    70: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
    71:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
    72:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
    73:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
    74: 
    75:   let score = 0;
    76:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
    77:   if (h) score += 20;
    78:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
    79: 
    80:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
    81:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
    82: 
    83:   return score;
    84: }
    85: 
    86: export function guessPrizePos(p) {
    87:   const pos = Number.isFinite(Number(p?.position))
    88:     ? Number(p.position)
    89:     : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.posicao))
    90:       ? Number(p.posicao)
    91:       : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.pos))
    92:         ? Number(p.pos)
    93:         : Number.isFinite(Number(p?.colocacao))
    94:           ? Number(p.colocacao)
    95:           : null;
    96: 
    97:   return pos;
    98: }
    99: 
   100: export function guessPrizeGrupo(p) {
   101:   const directCandidates = [
   102:     p?.grupo2,
   103:     p?.group2,
   104:     p?.grupo,
   105:     p?.group,
   106:     p?.animal_grupo,
   107:     p?.grupo_animal,
   108:     p?.grupoAnimal,
   109:     p?.g,
   110:   ];
   111: 
   112:   for (const v of directCandidates) {
   113:     const g = Number(v);
   114:     if (Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25) {
   115:       return g;
   116:     }
   117:   }
   118: 
   119:   const milhar4 = pickPrizeMilhar4(p);
   120: 
   121:   if (milhar4) {
   122:     const dezena2 = getDezena2(milhar4);
   123:     const derived = grupoFromDezena2(dezena2);
   124: 
   125:     if (Number.isFinite(derived)) return derived;
   126:   }
   127: 
   128:   return null;
   129: }

   131: export function pickDrawHour(draw) {
   132:   return normalizeHourLike(
   133:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   134:   );
   135: }

   137: export function pickDrawYMD(draw) {
   138:   const y =
   139:     draw?.ymd ||
   140:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) ||
   141:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.data) ||
   142:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.dt) ||
   143:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.draw_date) ||
   144:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.drawDate) ||
   145:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.close_date) ||
   146:     normalizeToYMD(draw?.closeDate) ||
   147:     null;
   148: 
   149:   return y;
   150: }

   152: export function pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   153:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   154:   if (prizes.length) {
   155:     const p1 = prizes.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   156:     if (p1) {
   157:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   158:       return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
   159:     }
   160:   }
   161: 
   162:   const candidates = [
   163:     draw?.grupo1,
   164:     draw?.group1,
   165:     draw?.primeiro_grupo,
   166:     draw?.first_grupo,
   167:     draw?.prize1_grupo,
   168:     draw?.prize1Grupo,
   169:     draw?.p1_grupo2,
   170:     draw?.p1Grupo2,
   171:     draw?.g1,
   172:     draw?.grupo_1,
   173:     draw?.grupoPrimeiro,
   174:   ];
   175: 
   176:   for (const v of candidates) {
   177:     if (Number.isFinite(Number(v))) return Number(v);
   178:   }
   179: 
   180:   return null;
   181: }

   183: /* =========================
   184:    Helpers internos

   186: 
   187: function safeInt(v, fallback) {
   188:   const n = Number(v);
   189:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
   190: }
   191: 
   192: function getDayOfMonth(ymd) {
   193:   if (!isYMD(ymd)) return NaN;
   194:   return Number(String(ymd).slice(8, 10));
   195: }
   196: 
   197: function ymdHourToTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
   198:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   199:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   200: 
   201:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   202: 
   203:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   204:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) {
   205:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   206:   }
   207: 
   208:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map((x) => Number(x));
   209:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   210: 
   211:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
   212: }
   213: 
   214: /* =========================
   215:    Schedules
   216: ========================= */
   217: 
   218: const FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD = "2026-07-19";
   219: const PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD = "2026-07-18";
   220: const FEDERAL_20_REMOVES_PT_RIO_18_START_YMD = "2025-11-03";
   221: 
   222: /**
   223:  * Calendário Federal com preservação histórica.
   224:  *
   225:  * Até 18/07/2026:
   226:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
   227:  * - sábado às 20h.
   228:  *
   229:  * A partir de 19/07/2026:
   230:  * - quarta-feira às 20h;
   231:  * - domingo às 11h;
   232:  * - sábado deixa de ter sorteio Federal.
   233:  */
   234: export function isFederalDrawDay(ymd) {
   235:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   236:   if (!y) return false;
   237: 
   238:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   239: 
   240:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD) {
   241:     return dow === 0 || dow === 3;
   242:   }
   243: 
   244:   return dow === 3 || dow === 6;
   245: }
   246: 
   247: function getFederalScheduleForYmd(ymd, FEDERAL_SCHEDULE) {
   248:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   249: 
   250:   if (!y || !isFederalDrawDay(y)) {
   251:     return [];
   252:   }
   253: 
   254:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   255: 
   256:   if (y >= FEDERAL_SUNDAY_START_YMD && dow === 0) {
   257:     return ["11:00"];
   258:   }
   259: 
   260:   const historical = Array.isArray(FEDERAL_SCHEDULE)
   261:     ? FEDERAL_SCHEDULE
   262:     : [];
   263: 
   264:   const normalized = historical
   265:     .map(toHourBucket)
   266:     .filter(Boolean);
   267: 
   268:   if (normalized.includes("20:00")) {
   269:     return ["20:00"];
   270:   }
   271: 
   272:   return normalized.length
   273:     ? normalized
   274:     : ["20:00"];
   275: }
   276: 
   277: /**
   278:  * Determina apenas os slots esperados.
   279:  * Nenhum resultado histórico é removido.
   280:  */
   281: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   282:   ymd,
   283:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   284:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   285: ) {
   286:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   287:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   288: 
   289:   if (dow === 0) {
   290:     const sundaySchedule = Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY)
   291:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY]
   292:       : [];
   293: 
   294:     if (y >= PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD) {
   295:       return sundaySchedule.filter((hour) => {
   296:         const h = normalizeHourLike(hour);
   297: 
   298:         return h === "14:00" || h === "16:00";
   299:       });
   300:     }
   301: 
   302:     return sundaySchedule;
   303:   }
   304: 
   305:   if (dow === 6) {
   306:     const saturdaySchedule = Array.isArray(
   307:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   308:     )
   309:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
   310:       : [];
   311: 
   312:     if (y >= "2026-07-18") {
   313:       const transitionedSchedule = saturdaySchedule
   314:         .map(normalizeHourLike)
   315:         .filter(Boolean)
   316:         .filter((hour) => hour !== "18:00");
   317: 
   318:       if (!transitionedSchedule.includes("19:00")) {
   319:         transitionedSchedule.push("19:00");
   320:       }
   321: 
   322:       return transitionedSchedule
   323:         .filter(
   324:           (hour, index, schedule) =>
   325:             schedule.indexOf(hour) === index
   326:         )
   327:         .sort(
   328:           (hourA, hourB) =>
   329:             hourToInt(hourA) - hourToInt(hourB)
   330:         );
   331:     }
   332: 
   333:     return saturdaySchedule;
   334:   }
   335: 
   336:   if (dow === 3) {
   337:     return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT)
   338:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
   339:       : [];
   340:   }
   341: 
   342:   return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL)
   343:     ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL]
   344:     : [];
   345: }
   346: 
   347: export function getScheduleForLottery({
   348:   lotteryKey,
   349:   ymd,
   350:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   351:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   352:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   353: }) {
   354:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   355: 
   356:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   357:     return getFederalScheduleForYmd(
   358:       ymd,
   359:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE
   360:     );
   361:   }
   362: 
   363:   if (key === "LOOK") {
   364:     return [...LOOK_SCHEDULE];
   365:   }
   366: 
   367:   if (key === "NACIONAL") {
   368:     return [...NACIONAL_SCHEDULE];
   369:   }
   370: 
   371:   return getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   372:     ymd,
   373:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   374:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   375:   );
   376: }
   377: 
   378: export function scheduleSet(schedule) {
   379:   return new Set((Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : []).map(toHourBucket));
   380: }
   381: 
   382: export function isHourInSchedule(schedule, hhmm) {
   383:   const s = scheduleSet(schedule);
   384:   return s.has(toHourBucket(hhmm));
   385: }
   386: 
   387: export function findLastDrawInList(draws, schedule) {
   388:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   389:   if (!list.length) return null;
   390: 
   391:   const sorted = [...list]
   392:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
   393:     .sort((a, b) => {
   394:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
   395:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
   396:       return tb - ta;
   397:     });
   398: 
   399:   return sorted[0] || null;
   400: }
   401: 
   402: export function findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
   403:   draws,
   404:   targetHourBucket,
   405:   schedule
   406: ) {
   407:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   408:   if (!list.length) return null;
   409: 
   410:   const t = hourToInt(targetHourBucket);
   411:   if (t < 0) return null;
   412: 
   413:   const candidates = list
   414:     .filter((d) => isHourInSchedule(schedule, pickDrawHour(d)))
   415:     .map((d) => ({ d, h: hourToInt(toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))) }))
   416:     .filter((x) => x.h >= 0 && x.h < t)
   417:     .sort((a, b) => b.h - a.h);
   418: 
   419:   return candidates.length ? candidates[0].d : null;
   420: }
   421: 
   422: export async function getPreviousDrawRobust({
   423:   getKingResultsByDate,
   424:   lotteryKey,
   425:   ymdTarget,
   426:   targetHourBucket,
   427:   todayDraws,
   428:   schedule,
   429:   maxBackDays = 10,
   430:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   431:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   432:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   433: }) {
   434:   const prevSameDay = findPrevDrawBeforeTargetInSameDay(
   435:     todayDraws,
   436:     targetHourBucket,
   437:     schedule
   438:   );
   439: 
   440:   if (prevSameDay) {
   441:     return {
   442:       draw: prevSameDay,
   443:       ymd: pickDrawYMD(prevSameDay) || ymdTarget,
   444:       hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prevSameDay)),
   445:       source: "same_day",
   446:     };
   447:   }
   448: 
   449:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxBackDays; i += 1) {
   450:     const day = addDaysYMD(ymdTarget, -i);
   451: 
   452:     const daySchedule = getScheduleForLottery({
   453:       lotteryKey,
   454:       ymd: day,
   455:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   456:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   457:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   458:     });
   459: 
   460:     if (
   461:       safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase() === "FEDERAL" &&
   462:       !daySchedule.length
   463:     ) {
   464:       continue;
   465:     }
   466: 
   467:     const out = await getKingResultsByDate({
   468:       uf: lotteryKey,
   469:       date: day,
   470:       closeHour: null,
   471:       positions: null,
   472:       readPolicy: "server",
   473:     });
   474: 
   475:     const last = findLastDrawInList(out, daySchedule);
   476:     if (last) {
   477:       return {
   478:         draw: last,
   479:         ymd: pickDrawYMD(last) || day,
   480:         hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(last)),
   481:         source: "prev_day",
   482:       };
   483:     }
   484:   }
   485: 
   486:   return { draw: null, ymd: "", hour: "", source: "none" };
   487: }
   488: 
   489: /* =========================
   490:    Próximo sorteio (slot válido)
   491: ========================= */
   492: 
   493: export function getNextSlotForLottery({
   494:   lotteryKey,
   495:   ymd,
   496:   hourBucket,
   497:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   498:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   499:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   500:   maxForwardDays = 21,
   501: }) {
   502:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   503:   const y0 = safeStr(ymd);
   504:   const h0 = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   505: 
   506:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !safeStr(h0)) return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   507: 
   508:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   509:     for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
   510:       const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
   511:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   512:         lotteryKey: key,
   513:         ymd: day,
   514:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   515:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   516:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   517:       });
   518:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   519:         return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
   520:       }
   521:     }
   522:     return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   523:   }
   524: 
   525:   const sch0 = getScheduleForLottery({
   526:     lotteryKey: key,
   527:     ymd: y0,
   528:     hourBucket: h0,
   529:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   530:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   531:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   532:   });
   533: 
   534:   const idx = (Array.isArray(sch0) ? sch0 : []).findIndex(
   535:     (x) => toHourBucket(x) === h0
   536:   );
   537: 
   538:   if (idx >= 0 && idx < sch0.length - 1) {
   539:     return { ymd: y0, hour: toHourBucket(sch0[idx + 1]) };
   540:   }
   541: 
   542:   for (let i = 1; i <= maxForwardDays; i += 1) {
   543:     const day = addDaysYMD(y0, i);
   544:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   545:       lotteryKey: key,
   546:       ymd: day,
   547:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   548:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   549:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   550:     });
   551:     if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   552:       return { ymd: day, hour: toHourBucket(sch[0]) };
   553:     }
   554:   }
   555: 
   556:   return { ymd: "", hour: "" };
   557: }
   558: 
   559: /* =========================
   560:    Próximo DRAW REAL (não perde amostra)
   561: ========================= */
   562: 
   563: export function findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
   564:   lotteryKey,
   565:   startSlot,
   566:   drawsIndex,
   567:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   568:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   569:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   570:   maxSteps = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_STEPS,
   571:   maxDays = TOP3_NEXTDRAW_SCAN_MAX_DAYS,
   572: }) {
   573:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   574:   const y0 = safeStr(startSlot?.ymd);
   575:   const h0 = toHourBucket(startSlot?.hour);
   576: 
   577:   if (!isYMD(y0) || !h0 || !(drawsIndex instanceof Map)) {
   578:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
   579:   }
   580: 
   581:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   582:     let curY = y0;
   583:     let steps = 0;
   584:     let daysWalked = 0;
   585: 
   586:     while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
   587:       const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   588:         lotteryKey: key,
   589:         ymd: curY,
   590:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   591:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   592:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   593:       });
   594: 
   595:       if (Array.isArray(sch) && sch.length) {
   596:         const hh = toHourBucket(sch[0]);
   597:         const d = drawsIndex.get(`${curY}|${hh}`) || null;
   598:         if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: hh }, draw: d };
   599:       }
   600: 
   601:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
   602:       daysWalked += 1;
   603:       steps += 1;
   604:     }
   605: 
   606:     return { slot: null, draw: null };
   607:   }
   608: 
   609:   let curY = y0;
   610:   let curH = h0;
   611:   let steps = 0;
   612:   let daysWalked = 0;
   613: 
   614:   while (steps < maxSteps && daysWalked <= maxDays) {
   615:     const drawKey = `${curY}|${toHourBucket(curH)}`;
   616:     let d = drawsIndex.get(drawKey) || null;
   617: 
   618:     if (!d) {
   619:       const curTs = ymdHourToTs(curY, curH);
   620:       const curYLocked = curY;
   621:       d = Array.from(drawsIndex.entries())
   622:         .map(([k, v]) => {
   623:           const [ky, kh] = String(k).split("|");
   624:           return { ymd: ky, hour: kh, draw: v, ts: ymdHourToTs(ky, kh) };
   625:         })
   626:         .filter((x) => x.ymd === curYLocked)
   627:         .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.ts) && x.ts >= curTs)
   628:         .sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts)[0]?.draw || null;
   629:     }
   630: 
   631:     if (d) return { slot: { ymd: curY, hour: toHourBucket(curH) }, draw: d };
   632: 
   633:     const sch = getScheduleForLottery({
   634:       lotteryKey: key,
   635:       ymd: curY,
   636:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   637:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   638:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   639:     });
   640: 
   641:     const arr = Array.isArray(sch) ? sch.map(toHourBucket) : [];
   642:     const curBucket = toHourBucket(curH);
   643:     const idx = arr.indexOf(curBucket);
   644: 
   645:     if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length - 1) {
   646:       curH = arr[idx + 1];
   647:     } else {
   648:       curY = addDaysYMD(curY, 1);
   649:       daysWalked += 1;
   650: 
   651:       const sch2 = getScheduleForLottery({
   652:         lotteryKey: key,
   653:         ymd: curY,
   654:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   655:         PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
   656:         FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
   657:       });
   658: 
   659:       const arr2 = Array.isArray(sch2) ? sch2.map(toHourBucket) : [];
   660:       if (!arr2.length) break;
   661:       curH = arr2[0];
   662:     }
   663: 
   664:     steps += 1;
   665:   }
   666: 
   667:   return { slot: null, draw: null };
   668: }
   669: 
   670: /* =========================
   671:    Index + utilitários de grupo
   672: ========================= */
   673: 
   674: export function indexDrawsByYmdHour(draws) {
   675:   const map = new Map();
   676:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   677: 
   678:   for (const d of list) {
   679:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   680:     const rawH = pickDrawHour(d);
   681:     const h = toHourBucket(rawH);
   682: 
   683:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
   684: 
   685:     const drawKey = `${y}|${h}`;
   686:     const prev = map.get(drawKey);
   687: 
   688:     if (!prev) {
   689:       map.set(drawKey, d);
   690:       continue;
   691:     }
   692: 
   693:     if (drawQualityScore(d) >= drawQualityScore(prev)) {
   694:       map.set(drawKey, d);
   695:     }
   696:   }
   697: 
   698:   return map;
   699: }
   700: 
   701: export function computeLastSeenByGrupo(draws) {
   702:   const last = new Map();
   703:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   704: 
   705:   for (const d of list) {
   706:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   707:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   708:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
   709: 
   710:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
   711:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   712: 
   713:     for (const p of ps) {
   714:       const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   715:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   716:         continue;
   717:       }
   718: 
   719:       const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   720:       if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   721: 
   722:       const gg = Number(g);
   723:       const prev = last.get(gg);
   724: 
   725:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
   726:         last.set(gg, ts);
   727:       }
   728:     }
   729:   }
   730: 
   731:   return last;
   732: }
   733: 
   734: export function countAparicoesByGrupoInDraw(draw) {
   735:   const counts = new Map();
   736:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   737:   for (const p of ps) {
   738:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   739:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   740:       continue;
   741:     }
   742:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   743:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   744:     const gg = Number(g);
   745:     counts.set(gg, (counts.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
   746:   }
   747:   return counts;
   748: }
   749: 
   750: function getFirstGrupoFromDraw(draw) {
   751:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   752:   const p1 = ps.find((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1) || null;
   753:   if (p1) {
   754:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p1);
   755:     return Number.isFinite(Number(g)) ? Number(g) : null;
   756:   }
   757:   return pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(draw);
   758: }
   759: 
   760: function getPrizeGroupsByPosition(draw, maxPosition = 3) {
   761:   const limit = Math.max(1, Number(maxPosition || 3));
   762:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   763: 
   764:   return Array.from({ length: limit }, (_, index) => {
   765:     const position = index + 1;
   766: 
   767:     const prize =
   768:       prizes.find(
   769:         (item) => Number(guessPrizePos(item)) === position
   770:       ) || null;
   771: 
   772:     if (!prize) return null;
   773: 
   774:     const grupo = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
   775: 
   776:     return Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
   777:       grupo >= 1 &&
   778:       grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
   779:       ? grupo
   780:       : null;
   781:   });
   782: }
   783: 
   784: 
   785: function getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw) {
   786:   const out = [];
   787:   const ps = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   788:   for (const p of ps) {
   789:     const pos = guessPrizePos(p);
   790:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(pos)) || Number(pos) < 1 || Number(pos) > 5) {
   791:       continue;
   792:     }
   793:     const g = guessPrizeGrupo(p);
   794:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) <= 0) continue;
   795:     out.push({ pos: Number(pos), grupo: Number(g) });
   796:   }
   797:   return out.sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   798: }
   799: 
   800: function buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw) {
   801:   const freq = new Map();
   802:   const firstMap = new Map();
   803:   const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(nextDraw);
   804: 
   805:   for (const it of items) {
   806:     freq.set(it.grupo, (freq.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
   807:     if (it.pos === 1) {
   808:       firstMap.set(it.grupo, (firstMap.get(it.grupo) || 0) + 1);
   809:     }
   810:   }
   811: 
   812:   return { freq, firstMap };
   813: }
   814: 
   815: function mergeMapsAdd(target, source) {
   816:   for (const [k, v] of source.entries()) {
   817:     target.set(Number(k), Number(target.get(Number(k)) || 0) + Number(v || 0));
   818:   }
   819: }
   820: 
   821: function probFromFreq(freq, samples, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   822:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   823:   const denom = Math.max(1, Number(samples || 0) * 5);
   824:   const out = new Map();
   825: 
   826:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   827:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
   828:     out.set(g, n / denom);
   829:   }
   830: 
   831:   return out;
   832: }
   833: 
   834: function computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
   835:   draws,
   836:   lotteryKey,
   837:   targetHour,
   838:   targetDow,
   839:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
   840: ) {
   841:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   842:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
   843:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
   844:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   845: 
   846:   const freq = new Map();
   847:   let totalSamples = 0;
   848: 
   849:   for (const d of list) {
   850:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   851:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   852:     if (!h || !y) continue;
   853: 
   854:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
   855:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
   856:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
   857:     } else {
   858:       if (h !== target) continue;
   859:     }
   860: 
   861:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
   862:     if (!items.length) continue;
   863: 
   864:     totalSamples += 1;
   865: 
   866:     for (const it of items) {
   867:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   868:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   869:       freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + 1);
   870:     }
   871:   }
   872: 
   873:   const out = new Map();
   874:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
   875: 
   876:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   877:     const n = Number(freq.get(g) || 0);
   878:     out.set(g, n / denom);
   879:   }
   880: 
   881:   return { scoreProb: out, totalSamples };
   882: }
   883: 
   884: /* =========================
   885:    Recência / pressão estrutural
   886: ========================= */
   887: 
   888: function sortDrawsAsc(draws) {
   889:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   890:   return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
   891:     const ya = pickDrawYMD(a);
   892:     const yb = pickDrawYMD(b);
   893:     const ha = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a));
   894:     const hb = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b));
   895:     const tsa = ymdHourToTs(ya, ha);
   896:     const tsb = ymdHourToTs(yb, hb);
   897:     return tsa - tsb;
   898:   });
   899: }
   900: 
   901: function getRecentDrawsBefore(draws, drawLast, count = 6) {
   902:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
   903:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
   904:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
   905:   const lastTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
   906: 
   907:   const prev = list.filter((d) => {
   908:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   909:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   910:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
   911:     return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < lastTs;
   912:   });
   913: 
   914:   return prev.slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
   915: }
   916: 
   917: function computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   918:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   919:   const out = new Map();
   920: 
   921:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
   922:     out.set(g, {
   923:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
   924:       recentIndirect: 0,
   925:       recentFirst: 0,
   926:       recentLast1First: 0,
   927:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
   928:     });
   929:   }
   930: 
   931:   const list = Array.isArray(recentDraws) ? recentDraws : [];
   932:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
   933:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
   934: 
   935:   for (const d of list) {
   936:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
   937: 
   938:     for (const it of items) {
   939:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   940:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
   941:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   942: 
   943:       const row = out.get(g);
   944:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
   945: 
   946:       if (pos >= 2 && pos <= 4) row.recentIndirect += 1;
   947:       if (pos === 1) row.recentFirst += 1;
   948:     }
   949:   }
   950: 
   951:   for (const d of last1) {
   952:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
   953:     for (const it of items) {
   954:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   955:       const pos = Number(it.pos);
   956:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   957:       if (pos === 1) {
   958:         out.get(g).recentLast1First += 1;
   959:       }
   960:     }
   961:   }
   962: 
   963:   for (const d of last2) {
   964:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
   965:     const seen = new Set();
   966:     for (const it of items) {
   967:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
   968:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
   969:       if (seen.has(g)) continue;
   970:       seen.add(g);
   971:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
   972:     }
   973:   }
   974: 
   975:   return out;
   976: }
   977: 
   978: function normalizeMetric(value, maxValue) {
   979:   const v = Number(value || 0);
   980:   const m = Number(maxValue || 0);
   981:   if (!Number.isFinite(v) || !Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return 0;
   982:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v / m));
   983: }
   984: 
   985: function clamp01(v) {
   986:   const n = Number(v);
   987:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 0;
   988:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n));
   989: }
   990: 
   991: function buildZeroProbMap(groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
   992:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
   993:   return new Map(Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => [i + 1, 0]));
   994: }
   995: 
   996: function confidenceFromSamples(samples, fullConfidenceAt = 8) {
   997:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
   998:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullConfidenceAt || 8));
   999:   return clamp01(n / lim);
  1000: }

  1003:    Motor condicional puro por camadas

  1005: 
  1006: const LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES = {
  1007:   DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 3,
  1008:   DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 5,
  1009:   PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET: 8,
  1010:   DOM_TARGET: 8,
  1011:   DOW_TARGET: 12,
  1012:   TARGET_ONLY: 20,
  1013: 
  1014:   FED_DOW_PREVG: 3,
  1015:   FED_PREVG: 4,
  1016:   FED_DOW_ONLY: 6,
  1017:   FED_ONLY: 10,
  1018: };
