  1020: function buildLayerConfigs({
  1021:   lotteryKey,
  1022:   targetDayOfMonth,
  1023:   targetDow,
  1024:   prevHour,
  1025:   prevGrupo,
  1026:   targetHour,
  1027: }) {
  1028:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1029: 
  1030:   if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  1031:     return [
  1032:       {
  1033:         key: "FED_DOW_PREVG",
  1034:         label: "Federal: dia da semana + grupo anterior",
  1035:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_PREVG,
  1036:         match: (ctx) =>
  1037:           ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
  1038:           ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
  1039:       },
  1040:       {
  1041:         key: "FED_PREVG",
  1042:         label: "Federal: grupo anterior",
  1043:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_PREVG,
  1044:         match: (ctx) => ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo,
  1045:       },
  1046:       {
  1047:         key: "FED_DOW_ONLY",
  1048:         label: "Federal: dia da semana",
  1049:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_DOW_ONLY,
  1050:         match: (ctx) => ctx.targetDow === targetDow,
  1051:       },
  1052:       {
  1053:         key: "FED_ONLY",
  1054:         label: "Federal: histórico geral",
  1055:         minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.FED_ONLY,
  1056:         match: () => true,
  1057:       },
  1058:     ];
  1059:   }
  1060: 
  1061:   return [
  1062:     {
  1063:       key: "DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
  1064:       label: "dia do mês + dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
  1065:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
  1066:       match: (ctx) =>
  1067:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
  1068:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
  1069:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
  1070:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
  1071:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1072:     },
  1073:     {
  1074:       key: "DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
  1075:       label: "dia da semana + hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
  1076:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
  1077:       match: (ctx) =>
  1078:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
  1079:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
  1080:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
  1081:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1082:     },
  1083:     {
  1084:       key: "PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET",
  1085:       label: "hora anterior + grupo anterior -> alvo",
  1086:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.PREVH_PREVG_TO_TARGET,
  1087:       match: (ctx) =>
  1088:         ctx.prevHour === prevHour &&
  1089:         ctx.prevGrupo === prevGrupo &&
  1090:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1091:     },
  1092:     {
  1093:       key: "DOM_TARGET",
  1094:       label: "dia do mês + alvo",
  1095:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOM_TARGET,
  1096:       match: (ctx) =>
  1097:         ctx.targetDayOfMonth === targetDayOfMonth &&
  1098:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1099:     },
  1100:     {
  1101:       key: "DOW_TARGET",
  1102:       label: "dia da semana + alvo",
  1103:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.DOW_TARGET,
  1104:       match: (ctx) =>
  1105:         ctx.targetDow === targetDow &&
  1106:         ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1107:     },
  1108:     {
  1109:       key: "TARGET_ONLY",
  1110:       label: "alvo geral",
  1111:       minSamples: LAYER_MIN_SAMPLES.TARGET_ONLY,
  1112:       match: (ctx) => ctx.targetHour === targetHour,
  1113:     },
  1114:   ];
  1115: }
  1116: 
  1117: function buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  1118:   lotteryKey,
  1119:   drawsRange,
  1120:   targetHour,
  1121:   targetDow,
  1122:   targetDayOfMonth,
  1123:   prevHour,
  1124:   prevGrupo,
  1125:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1126:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1127:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1128: }) {
  1129:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  1130:   const drawsIndex = indexDrawsByYmdHour(list);
  1131: 
  1132:   const layers = buildLayerConfigs({
  1133:     lotteryKey,
  1134:     targetDayOfMonth,
  1135:     targetDow,
  1136:     prevHour,
  1137:     prevGrupo,
  1138:     targetHour,
  1139:   });
  1140: 
  1141:   const layerResults = layers.map((layer) => ({
  1142:     key: layer.key,
  1143:     label: layer.label,
  1144:     minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  1145:     samples: 0,
  1146:     freq: new Map(),
  1147:     firstFreq: new Map(),
  1148:     matchingDraws: [],
  1149:   }));
  1150: 
  1151:   for (const d of list) {
  1152:     const prevY = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1153:     const prevH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1154:     if (!isYMD(prevY) || !prevH) continue;
  1155: 
  1156:     const prevG = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1157:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(prevG)) || Number(prevG) <= 0) continue;
  1158: 
  1159:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
  1160:       lotteryKey,
  1161:       ymd: prevY,
  1162:       hourBucket: prevH,
  1163:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1164:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1165:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1166:     });
  1167: 
  1168:     if (!ns?.ymd || !ns?.hour) continue;
  1169: 
  1170:     const found = findNextExistingDrawFromSlot({
  1171:       lotteryKey,
  1172:       startSlot: { ymd: ns.ymd, hour: ns.hour },
  1173:       drawsIndex,
  1174:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1175:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1176:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1177:     });
  1178: 
  1179:     const nextDraw = found?.draw || null;
  1180:     const nextSlot = found?.slot || null;
  1181:     if (!nextDraw || !nextSlot?.ymd || !nextSlot?.hour) continue;
  1182: 
  1183:     const ctx = {
  1184:       prevYmd: prevY,
  1185:       prevHour: prevH,
  1186:       prevGrupo: Number(prevG),
  1187:       targetYmd: safeStr(nextSlot.ymd),
  1188:       targetHour: toHourBucket(nextSlot.hour),
  1189:       targetDow: getDowKey(nextSlot.ymd),
  1190:       targetDayOfMonth: getDayOfMonth(nextSlot.ymd),
  1191:     };
  1192: 
  1193:     const nextMaps = buildFreqAndFirstMaps(nextDraw);
  1194: 
  1195:     for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i += 1) {
  1196:       const layer = layers[i];
  1197:       if (!layer.match(ctx)) continue;
  1198: 
  1199:       layerResults[i].samples += 1;
  1200:       layerResults[i].matchingDraws.push(nextDraw);
  1201:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].freq, nextMaps.freq);
  1202:       mergeMapsAdd(layerResults[i].firstFreq, nextMaps.firstMap);
  1203:     }
  1204:   }
  1205: 
  1206:   let chosen =
  1207:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples >= x.minSamples && x.freq.size > 0) ||
  1208:     layerResults.find((x) => x.samples > 0 && x.freq.size > 0) ||
  1209:     null;
  1210: 
  1211:   if (!chosen) {
  1212:     chosen = {
  1213:       key: "NONE",
  1214:       label: "sem amostra",
  1215:       minSamples: 0,
  1216:       samples: 0,
  1217:       freq: new Map(),
  1218:       firstFreq: new Map(),
  1219:       matchingDraws: [],
  1220:     };
  1221:   }
  1222: 
  1223:   return {
  1224:     chosen,
  1225:     layers: layerResults,
  1226:   };
  1227: }
  1228: 
  1229: /* =========================
  1230:    Motor principal V1
  1231: ========================= */
  1232: 
  1233: export function computeConditionalNextTop3({
  1234:   lotteryKey,
  1235:   drawsRange,
  1236:   drawLast,
  1237:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1238:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1239:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1240:   topN = 3,
  1241:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  1242:   targetHourOverride = "",
  1243: }) {
  1244:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  1245:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
  1246:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1247:   }
  1248: 
  1249:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1250: 
  1251:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  1252:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  1253:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  1254: 
  1255:   if (
  1256:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  1257:     !lastH ||
  1258:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  1259:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
  1260:   ) {
  1261:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1262:   }
  1263: 
  1264:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  1265:     lotteryKey,
  1266:     ymd: lastY,
  1267:     hourBucket: lastH,
  1268:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1269:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1270:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1271:   });
  1272: 
  1273:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  1274:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  1275: 
  1276:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  1277:     ? forcedTargetY
  1278:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  1279: 
  1280:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  1281:     ? forcedTargetH
  1282:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  1283:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  1284:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  1285:   const transition = `${lastH}->${targetH}`;
  1286: 
  1287:   const useFirstFocusedRanking =
  1288:     key !== "FEDERAL" &&
  1289:     (transition === "11h->14h" || transition === "14h->16h");
  1290: 
  1291:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(targetDow)) {
  1292:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  1293:   }
  1294: 
  1295:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  1296:     lotteryKey,
  1297:     drawsRange: list,
  1298:     targetHour: targetH,
  1299:     targetDow,
  1300:     targetDayOfMonth,
  1301:     prevHour: lastH,
  1302:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1303:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  1304:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  1305:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  1306:   });
  1307: 
  1308:   const chosen = layerOut.chosen;
  1309:   const samples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
  1310:   const freq = chosen?.freq || new Map();
  1311:   const firstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
  1312:   const probCond = probFromFreq(freq, samples, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  1313: 
  1314:   const structural = computeStructuralBaseDistribution(
  1315:     list,
  1316:     key,
  1317:     targetH,
  1318:     targetDow,
  1319:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1320:   );
  1321:   const probBase = structural?.scoreProb || new Map();
  1322: 
  1323:   const condWeight =
  1324:     key === "FEDERAL"
  1325:       ? samples >= 8
  1326:         ? 0.55
  1327:         : samples >= 4
  1328:           ? 0.4
  1329:           : 0.25
  1330:       : samples >= 12
  1331:         ? 0.65
  1332:         : samples >= 6
  1333:           ? 0.55
  1334:           : 0.45;
  1335: 
  1336:   const baseWeight = 1 - condWeight;
  1337: 
  1338:   const lastSeen = computeLastSeenByGrupo(list);
  1339:   const nowTs = ymdHourToTs(lastY, lastH);
  1340: 
  1341:   const finiteLastSeen = Array.from(lastSeen.values()).filter((v) =>
  1342:     Number.isFinite(Number(v))
  1343:   );
  1344:   const minLastSeenTs = finiteLastSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteLastSeen) : nowTs;
  1345:   const maxGapMsBase =
  1346:     Number.isFinite(nowTs) && Number.isFinite(minLastSeenTs)
  1347:       ? Math.max(1, nowTs - minLastSeenTs)
  1348:       : 1;
  1349: 
  1350:   const recentDraws = getRecentDrawsBefore(list, drawLast, 6);
  1351:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentPressureMetrics(recentDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  1352: 
  1353:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
  1354:     1,
  1355:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
  1356:   );
  1357:   const recentMaxIndirect = Math.max(
  1358:     1,
  1359:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentIndirect || 0))
  1360:   );
  1361:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
  1362:     1,
  1363:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
  1364:   );
  1365: 
  1366:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  1367:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  1368:     const aparicoes = Number(freq.get(grupo) || 0);
  1369:     const primeiros = Number(firstFreq.get(grupo) || 0);
  1370:     const pCond = Number(probCond.get(grupo) || 0);
  1371:     const pBase = Number(probBase.get(grupo) || 0);
  1372:     const pFinal = (pCond * condWeight) + (pBase * baseWeight);
  1373: 
  1374:     const ls = lastSeen.get(grupo);
  1375:     const lastSeenTs = Number.isFinite(ls) ? ls : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  1376: 
  1377:     const gapMs =
  1378:       Number.isFinite(nowTs) &&
  1379:       Number.isFinite(lastSeenTs) &&
  1380:       lastSeenTs !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
  1381:         ? Math.max(0, nowTs - lastSeenTs)
  1382:         : maxGapMsBase;
  1383: 
  1384:     const taxaPrimeiro = samples > 0 ? primeiros / samples : 0;
  1385:     const lateBonusRaw = Math.max(0, gapMs) / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase);
  1386:     const lateBonus = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lateBonusRaw));
  1387: 
  1388:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
  1389:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
  1390:       recentIndirect: 0,
  1391:       recentFirst: 0,
  1392:       recentLast1First: 0,
  1393:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
  1394:     };
  1395: 
  1396:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
  1397:     const recentIndirectNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentIndirect, recentMaxIndirect);
  1398:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
  1399:     const recentLast1FirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1First, 1);
  1400:     const recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
  1401: 
  1402:     const recencyCoolingFactor =
  1403:       rm.recentLast1First > 0
  1404:         ? 0.72
  1405:         : rm.recentFirst >= 2
  1406:           ? 0.84
  1407:           : 1.0;
  1408: 
  1409:     const smartLateBoost =
  1410:       lateBonus *
  1411:       (0.45 + (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) + (recentLast2PrizePresenceNorm * 0.2)) *
  1412:       recencyCoolingFactor;
  1413: 
  1414:     const recentComposite =
  1415:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.45) +
  1416:       (recentIndirectNorm * 0.35) +
  1417:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.2);
  1418: 
  1419:     const baseScore =
  1420:       key === "FEDERAL"
  1421:         ? (pFinal * 145) +
  1422:           (pBase * 85) +
  1423:           (primeiros * 42) +
  1424:           (aparicoes * 18) +
  1425:           (recentComposite * 26) +
  1426:           (smartLateBoost * 10)
  1427:         : useFirstFocusedRanking
  1428:           ? (pFinal * 125) +
  1429:             (aparicoes * 155) +
  1430:             (primeiros * 70) +
  1431:             (taxaPrimeiro * 34) +
  1432:             (recentComposite * 18) +
  1433:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 10) +
  1434:             (smartLateBoost * 16)
  1435:           : (pFinal * 125) +
  1436:             (aparicoes * 135) +
  1437:             (primeiros * 62) +
  1438:             (recentComposite * 24) +
  1439:             (recentLast1FirstNorm * 8) +
  1440:             (smartLateBoost * 18);
  1441: 
  1442:     return {
  1443:       grupo,
  1444:       aparicoes,
  1445:       primeiros,
  1446:       taxaPrimeiro,
  1447:       scoreProb: pFinal,
  1448:       probCond: pCond,
  1449:       probBase: pBase,
  1450:       score: baseScore,
  1451:       lateBonus,
  1452:       smartLateBoost,
  1453:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
  1454:       recentIndirect: rm.recentIndirect,
  1455:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
  1456:       recentLast1First: rm.recentLast1First,
  1457:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
  1458:       recentComposite,
  1459:       lastSeenTs,
  1460:       gapMs,
  1461:     };
  1462:   })
  1463:     .sort((a, b) => {
  1464:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  1465:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
  1466:       if (b.primeiros !== a.primeiros) return b.primeiros - a.primeiros;
  1467:       if (b.aparicoes !== a.aparicoes) return b.aparicoes - a.aparicoes;
  1468:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  1469:     })
  1470:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  1471: 
  1472:   const reasonsBase = [
  1473:     `Camada usada: ${chosen?.label || "—"}`,
  1474:     `Amostra histórica condicional: ${samples}`,
  1475:     `Amostra estrutural: ${Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
  1476:     `Pesos: condicional=${(condWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(baseWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  1477:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
  1478:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  1479:   ];
  1480: 
  1481:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
  1482:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  1483:     const pct = (x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2);
  1484:     const pctCond = (x.probCond * 100).toFixed(2);
  1485:     const pctBase = (x.probBase * 100).toFixed(2);
  1486: 
  1487:     return {
  1488:       rank: idx + 1,
  1489:       title:
  1490:         idx === 0
  1491:           ? "Mais provável"
  1492:           : idx === 1
  1493:             ? "2º mais provável"
  1494:             : "3º mais provável",
  1495:       grupo: x.grupo,
  1496:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
  1497:       probCond: Number(x.probCond || 0),
  1498:       probBase: Number(x.probBase || 0),
  1499:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateBonus || 0),
  1500:       freq: x.aparicoes,
  1501:       freqCond: x.aparicoes,
  1502:       freqBase: 0,
  1503:       freqZeroWhy:
  1504:         x.aparicoes <= 0
  1505:           ? `Sem ocorrência nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
  1506:           : "",
  1507:       reasons: [
  1508:         ...reasonsBase,
  1509:         `Grupo G${g2}: aparições=${x.aparicoes} em ${samples} amostras do próximo sorteio`,
  1510:         `Grupo G${g2}: primeiros lugares=${x.primeiros}`,
  1511:         `Probabilidade final estimada no TOP3: ${pct}%`,
  1512:         `Composição da probabilidade: condicional=${pctCond}% | estrutural=${pctBase}%`,
  1513:         `Presença indireta recente (2º-4º): ${x.recentIndirect}`,
  1514:         `Persistência curta recente: ${x.recentPrizePresence}`,
  1515:         `Bônus de atraso ajustado: ${(Number(x.smartLateBoost || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  1516:       ],
  1517:       meta: {
  1518:         trigger: {
  1519:           ymd: lastY,
  1520:           hour: lastH,
  1521:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1522:         },
  1523:         next: {
  1524:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  1525:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  1526:         },
  1527:         samples,
  1528:         scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1529:         explain: {
  1530:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1531:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  1532:           layerSamples: samples,
  1533:           baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
  1534:           condWeight,
  1535:           baseWeight,
  1536:           targetDow,
  1537:           targetDayOfMonth,
  1538:           prevHour: lastH,
  1539:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1540:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
  1541:           recentIndirect: x.recentIndirect,
  1542:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
  1543:           recentLast1First: x.recentLast1First,
  1544:           recentLast2PrizePresence: x.recentLast2PrizePresence,
  1545:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  1546:             key: layer.key,
  1547:             label: layer.label,
  1548:             samples: layer.samples,
  1549:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  1550:           })),
  1551:         },
  1552:       },
  1553:     };
  1554:   });
  1555: 
  1556:   return {
  1557:     top,
  1558:     meta: {
  1559:       trigger: {
  1560:         ymd: lastY,
  1561:         hour: lastH,
  1562:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1563:       },
  1564:       next: {
  1565:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  1566:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  1567:       },
  1568:       samples,
  1569:       scenario: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1570:       explain: {
  1571:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  1572:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  1573:         layerSamples: samples,
  1574:         baseSamples: Number(structural?.totalSamples || 0),
  1575:         condWeight,
  1576:         baseWeight,
  1577:         targetDow,
  1578:         targetDayOfMonth,
  1579:         prevHour: lastH,
  1580:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  1581:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  1582:           key: layer.key,
  1583:           label: layer.label,
  1584:           samples: layer.samples,
  1585:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  1586:         })),
  1587:       },
  1588:     },
  1589:   };
  1590: }
  1591: 
  1592: /* =========================
  1593:    V2 — helpers focados em BICHO
  1594: ========================= */
  1595: 
  1596: function computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
  1597:   draws,
  1598:   lotteryKey,
  1599:   targetHour,
  1600:   targetDow,
  1601:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1602: ) {
  1603:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1604:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1605:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  1606:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1607: 
  1608:   const firstFreq = new Map();
  1609:   let totalSamples = 0;
  1610: 
  1611:   for (const d of list) {
  1612:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1613:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1614:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  1615: 
  1616:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  1617:       if (!isFederalDrawDay(y)) continue;
  1618:       if (Number(getDowKey(y)) !== Number(targetDow)) continue;
  1619:     } else {
  1620:       if (h !== target) continue;
  1621:     }
  1622: 
  1623:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1624:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > k) continue;
  1625: 
  1626:     totalSamples += 1;
  1627:     firstFreq.set(Number(g), Number(firstFreq.get(Number(g)) || 0) + 1);
  1628:   }
  1629: 
  1630:   const prob = new Map();
  1631:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples);
  1632: 
  1633:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1634:     prob.set(g, Number(firstFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1635:   }
  1636: 
  1637:   return { prob, firstFreq, totalSamples };
  1638: }
  1639: 
  1640: function getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1641:   draws,
  1642:   lotteryKey,
  1643:   targetHour,
  1644:   targetDow
  1645: ) {
  1646:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(draws);
  1647:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  1648:   const target = toHourBucket(targetHour);
  1649: 
  1650:   return list.filter((d) => {
  1651:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1652:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1653:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return false;
  1654: 
  1655:     if (key === "FEDERAL") {
  1656:       return isFederalDrawDay(y) && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  1657:     }
  1658: 
  1659:     return h === target;
  1660:   });
  1661: }
  1662: 
  1663: function buildComparableFirstSequence(
  1664:   draws,
  1665:   lotteryKey,
  1666:   targetHour,
  1667:   targetDow
  1668: ) {
  1669:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  1670:     draws,
  1671:     lotteryKey,
  1672:     targetHour,
  1673:     targetDow
  1674:   )
  1675:     .map((d) => {
  1676:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1677:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1678:       const grupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  1679:       return {
  1680:         draw: d,
  1681:         ymd: y,
  1682:         hour: h,
  1683:         ts: ymdHourToTs(y, h),
  1684:         grupo: Number(grupo),
  1685:       };
  1686:     })
  1687:     .filter((x) =>
  1688:       isYMD(x.ymd) &&
  1689:       x.hour &&
  1690:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) &&
  1691:       Number.isFinite(Number(x.grupo)) &&
  1692:       Number(x.grupo) >= 1 &&
  1693:       Number(x.grupo) <= 25
  1694:     );
  1695: }
  1696: 
  1697: function getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 3) {
  1698:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
  1699:   const n = Math.max(1, Number(count || 3));
  1700: 
  1701:   return list
  1702:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
  1703:     .slice(-n)
  1704:     .reverse()
  1705:     .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
  1706:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
  1707: }
  1708: 
  1709: function getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(seq, beforeTs, count = 6) {
  1710:   const list = Array.isArray(seq) ? seq : [];
  1711:   return list
  1712:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(Number(x.ts)) && Number(x.ts) < Number(beforeTs))
  1713:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 6)));
  1714: }
  1715: 
  1716: function countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState) {
  1717:   const a = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
  1718:   const b = Array.isArray(histState) ? histState : [];
  1719:   const len = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
  1720: 
  1721:   let matched = 0;
  1722:   for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 1) {
  1723:     if (Number(a[i]) !== Number(b[i])) break;
  1724:     matched += 1;
  1725:   }
  1726:   return matched;
  1727: }
  1728: 
  1729: function buildMemoryStateDistribution({
  1730:   drawsRange,
  1731:   lotteryKey,
  1732:   targetHour,
  1733:   targetDow,
  1734:   currentState,
  1735:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  1736: }) {
  1737:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  1738:     drawsRange,
  1739:     lotteryKey,
  1740:     targetHour,
  1741:     targetDow
  1742:   );
  1743: 
  1744:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1745:   const freq = new Map();
  1746: 
  1747:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1748:     freq.set(g, 0);
  1749:   }
  1750: 
  1751:   let totalWeight = 0;
  1752:   let matchedSamples = 0;
  1753: 
  1754:   for (let i = 0; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  1755:     const target = seq[i];
  1756:     const histState = [];
  1757: 
  1758:     for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && histState.length < 3; j -= 1) {
  1759:       histState.push(Number(seq[j].grupo));
  1760:     }
  1761: 
  1762:     const matchDepth = countLeadingStateMatches(currentState, histState);
  1763:     if (matchDepth <= 0) continue;
  1764: 
  1765:     const weight =
  1766:       matchDepth >= 3 ? 4.0 :
  1767:       matchDepth === 2 ? 2.5 :
  1768:       1.0;
  1769: 
  1770:     const g = Number(target.grupo);
  1771:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1772: 
  1773:     freq.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
  1774:     totalWeight += weight;
  1775:     matchedSamples += 1;
  1776:   }
  1777: 
  1778:   const prob = new Map();
  1779:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalWeight);
  1780: 
  1781:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1782:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1783:   }
  1784: 
  1785:   return {
  1786:     prob,
  1787:     freq,
  1788:     totalWeight,
  1789:     matchedSamples,
  1790:     state: Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [],
  1791:     sequenceSamples: seq.length,
  1792:   };
  1793: }
  1794: 
  1795: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
  1796:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
  1797:   const groups = list
  1798:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
  1799:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
  1800: 
  1801:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
  1802:     return {
  1803:       regime: "neutral",
  1804:       repeatRate: 0,
  1805:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
  1806:       samples: groups.length,
  1807:     };
  1808:   }
  1809: 
  1810:   let repeats = 0;
  1811:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
  1812:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
  1813:   }
  1814: 
  1815:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
  1816:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
  1817: 
  1818:   let regime = "neutral";
  1819: 
  1820:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
  1821:     regime = "repeat";
  1822:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
  1823:     regime = "spread";
  1824:   }
  1825: 
  1826:   return {
  1827:     regime,
  1828:     repeatRate,
  1829:     uniqueRate,
  1830:     samples: groups.length,
  1831:   };
  1832: }

  1834: function computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
  1835:   draws,
  1836:   lotteryKey,
  1837:   targetHour,
  1838:   targetDow,
  1839:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  1840: ) {

  1842:     draws,
  1843:     lotteryKey,
  1844:     targetHour,
  1845:     targetDow
  1846:   );
  1847: 
  1848:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1849:   const freq = new Map();
  1850:   let totalSamples = 0;
  1851: 
  1852:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1853:     freq.set(g, 0);
  1854:   }
  1855: 
  1856:   for (const d of list) {
  1857:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  1858:     if (!items.length) continue;
  1859: 
  1860:     totalSamples += 1;
  1861: 
  1862:     for (const it of items) {
  1863:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1864:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;

  1866:     }
  1867:   }
  1868: 
  1869:   const prob = new Map();
  1870:   const denom = Math.max(1, totalSamples * 5);
  1871: 
  1872:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1873:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  1874:   }
  1875: 
  1876:   return { prob, freq, totalSamples };
  1877: }

  1925: function getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  1926:   draws,
  1927:   lotteryKey,
  1928:   targetHour,
  1929:   targetDow,
  1930:   beforeTs,
  1931:   count = 8
  1932: ) {
  1933:   return getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(draws, lotteryKey, targetHour, targetDow)
  1934:     .filter((d) => {
  1935:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  1936:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  1937:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  1938:       return Number.isFinite(Number(ts)) && ts < Number(beforeTs);
  1939:     })
  1940:     .slice(-Math.max(1, Number(count || 8)));
  1941: }

  1943: function computeRecentBichoMetrics(draws, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  1944:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  1945:   const out = new Map();
  1946: 
  1947:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  1948:     out.set(g, {
  1949:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
  1950:       recentFirst: 0,
  1951:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  1952:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
  1953:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
  1954:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
  1955:     });
  1956:   }
  1957: 
  1958:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  1959:   const last1 = list.length ? [list[list.length - 1]] : [];
  1960:   const last2 = list.slice(-2);
  1961:   const last3 = list.slice(-3);
  1962: 
  1963:   for (const d of list) {
  1964:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  1965:     const perDrawCounts = new Map();
  1966: 
  1967:     for (const it of items) {
  1968:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1969:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  1970: 
  1971:       const row = out.get(g);
  1972:       row.recentPrizePresence += 1;
  1973: 
  1974:       if (Number(it.pos) === 1) {
  1975:         row.recentFirst += 1;
  1976:       }
  1977: 
  1978:       perDrawCounts.set(g, Number(perDrawCounts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  1979:     }
  1980: 
  1981:     for (const [g, c] of perDrawCounts.entries()) {
  1982:       if (Number(c) >= 2) {
  1983:         out.get(Number(g)).recentDupDraws += 1;
  1984:       }
  1985:     }
  1986:   }
  1987: 
  1988:   for (const d of last1) {
  1989:     const seen = new Set();
  1990:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
  1991:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  1992:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  1993:       seen.add(g);
  1994:       out.get(g).recentLast1PrizePresence += 1;
  1995:     }
  1996:   }
  1997: 
  1998:   for (const d of last2) {
  1999:     const seen = new Set();
  2000:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
  2001:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  2002:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  2003:       seen.add(g);
  2004:       out.get(g).recentLast2PrizePresence += 1;
  2005:     }
  2006:   }
  2007: 
  2008:   for (const d of last3) {
  2009:     const seen = new Set();
  2010:     for (const it of getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d)) {
  2011:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  2012:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k || seen.has(g)) continue;
  2013:       seen.add(g);
  2014:       out.get(g).recentLast3PrizePresence += 1;
  2015:     }
  2016:   }
  2017: 
  2018:   return out;
  2019: }

  2021: function getDayDrivenWeights(mode) {
  2022:   if (mode === "REPEAT") {
  2023:     return {
  2024:       transition: 0.25,
  2025:       pair: 0.25,
  2026:       memory: 0.2,
  2027:       recent: 0.15,
  2028:       structural: 0.1,
  2029:       late: 0.05,
  2030:     };
  2031:   }
  2032: 
  2033:   if (mode === "SPREAD") {
  2034:     return {
  2035:       transition: 0.2,
  2036:       pair: 0.1,
  2037:       memory: 0.1,
  2038:       recent: 0.2,
  2039:       structural: 0.3,
  2040:       late: 0.1,
  2041:     };
  2042:   }
  2043: 
  2044:   return {
  2045:     transition: 0.3,
  2046:     pair: 0.15,
  2047:     memory: 0.15,
  2048:     recent: 0.15,
  2049:     structural: 0.2,
  2050:     late: 0.05,
  2051:   };
  2052: }
  2053: 
  2054: function normalizeWeights(weights) {
  2055:   const safe = {
  2056:     transition: Number(weights?.transition || 0),
  2057:     pair: Number(weights?.pair || 0),
  2058:     memory: Number(weights?.memory || 0),
  2059:     recent: Number(weights?.recent || 0),
  2060:     structural: Number(weights?.structural || 0),
  2061:     late: Number(weights?.late || 0),
  2062:   };
  2063: 
  2064:   const total = Object.values(safe).reduce((acc, n) => acc + n, 0);
  2065:   if (!Number.isFinite(total) || total <= 0) {
  2066:     return {
  2067:       transition: 0.3,
  2068:       pair: 0.15,
  2069:       memory: 0.15,
  2070:       recent: 0.15,
  2071:       structural: 0.2,
  2072:       late: 0.05,
  2073:     };
  2074:   }
  2075: 
  2076:   return {
  2077:     transition: safe.transition / total,
  2078:     pair: safe.pair / total,
  2079:     memory: safe.memory / total,
  2080:     recent: safe.recent / total,
  2081:     structural: safe.structural / total,
  2082:     late: safe.late / total,
  2083:   };
  2084: }

  2086: function computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  2087:   draws,
  2088:   lotteryKey,
  2089:   targetHour,
  2090:   targetDow,
  2091:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2092: ) {
  2093:   const list = getComparableDrawsForTargetContext(
  2094:     draws,
  2095:     lotteryKey,
  2096:     targetHour,
  2097:     targetDow
  2098:   );
  2099: 
  2100:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2101:   const last = new Map();
  2102: 
  2103:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2104:     last.set(g, Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY);
  2105:   }
  2106: 
  2107:   for (const d of list) {
  2108:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  2109:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  2110:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  2111:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  2112: 
  2113:     for (const it of items) {
  2114:       const g = Number(it.grupo);
  2115:       if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  2116: 
  2117:       const prev = Number(last.get(g));
  2118:       if (!Number.isFinite(prev) || ts > prev) {
  2119:         last.set(g, ts);
  2120:       }
  2121:     }
  2122:   }
  2123: 
  2124:   return last;
  2125: }

  2127: function buildDayContext(drawsToday = []) {
  2128:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? drawsToday : []);
  2129:   const freq = new Map();
  2130:   const firstFreq = new Map();
  2131:   const seq = [];
  2132: 
  2133:   for (const d of list) {
  2134:     const g = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  2135:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(g)) || Number(g) < 1 || Number(g) > 25) continue;
  2136: 
  2137:     const gg = Number(g);
  2138:     seq.push(gg);
  2139:     freq.set(gg, Number(freq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  2140: 
  2141:     const items = getAllPrizePresenceGroups(d);
  2142:     if (items.some((it) => Number(it.grupo) === gg && Number(it.pos) === 1)) {
  2143:       firstFreq.set(gg, Number(firstFreq.get(gg) || 0) + 1);
  2144:     }
  2145:   }
  2146: 
  2147:   const total = seq.length;
  2148:   const unique = new Set(seq).size;
  2149:   const diversidade = total > 0 ? unique / total : 0;
  2150: 
  2151:   let dominante = null;
  2152:   let dominanteCount = -1;
  2153:   for (const [g, n] of freq.entries()) {
  2154:     if (Number(n) > dominanteCount) {
  2155:       dominante = Number(g);
  2156:       dominanteCount = Number(n);
  2157:     }
  2158:   }
  2159: 
  2160:   const last1 = total >= 1 ? seq[total - 1] : null;
  2161:   const last2 = total >= 2 ? seq[total - 2] : null;
  2162:   const repeatNow =
  2163:     Number.isFinite(Number(last1)) &&
  2164:     Number.isFinite(Number(last2)) &&
  2165:     Number(last1) === Number(last2);
  2166: 
  2167:   return {
  2168:     total,
  2169:     unique,
  2170:     diversidade,
  2171:     dominante,
  2172:     dominanteCount: Math.max(0, dominanteCount),
  2173:     repeatNow,
  2174:     freq,
  2175:     firstFreq,
  2176:     seq,
  2177:   };
  2178: }

  2180: function buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2181:   drawsRange,
  2182:   lotteryKey,
  2183:   targetHour,
  2184:   targetDow,
  2185:   targetDayOfMonth,
  2186:   prevPair,
  2187:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2188:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2189:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2190:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2191: }) {
  2192:   const seq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2193:     Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [],
  2194:     lotteryKey,
  2195:     targetHour,
  2196:     targetDow
  2197:   );
  2198: 
  2199:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2200:   const outFreq = new Map();
  2201: 
  2202:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) outFreq.set(g, 0);
  2203: 
  2204:   const pair = Array.isArray(prevPair) ? prevPair : [];
  2205:   if (pair.length < 2) {
  2206:     return {
  2207:       prob: buildZeroProbMap(k),
  2208:       samples: 0,
  2209:       weightedSamples: 0,
  2210:       exactDomSamples: 0,
  2211:       freq: outFreq,
  2212:     };
  2213:   }
  2214: 
  2215:   const pA = Number(pair[0]);
  2216:   const pB = Number(pair[1]);
  2217:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2218: 
  2219:   let matchedSamples = 0;
  2220:   let weightedSamples = 0;
  2221:   let exactDomSamples = 0;
  2222: 
  2223:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2224:     const a = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2225:     const b = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2226:     const g = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2227:     const y = safeStr(seq[i]?.ymd);
  2228: 
  2229:     if (!Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || !Number.isFinite(g)) continue;
  2230:     if (a !== pA || b !== pB) continue;
  2231:     if (!isYMD(y)) continue;
  2232: 
  2233:     matchedSamples += 1;
  2234: 
  2235:     let weight = 1;
  2236: 
  2237:     if (key !== "FEDERAL") {
  2238:       const dom = Number(getDayOfMonth(y));
  2239:       if (Number.isFinite(dom) && Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) {
  2240:         weight = 1.35;
  2241:         exactDomSamples += 1;
  2242:       }
  2243:     }
  2244: 
  2245:     weightedSamples += weight;
  2246:     outFreq.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) + weight);
  2247:   }
  2248: 
  2249:   const prob = new Map();
  2250:   const denom = Math.max(1, weightedSamples);
  2251: 
  2252:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2253:     prob.set(g, Number(outFreq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2254:   }
  2255: 
  2256:   return {
  2257:     prob,
  2258:     samples: matchedSamples,
  2259:     weightedSamples,
  2260:     exactDomSamples,
  2261:     freq: outFreq,
  2262:   };
  2263: }
  2264: 
  2265: function buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2266:   comparableSeq,
  2267:   currentState,
  2268:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2269: ) {
  2270:   const seq = Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [];
  2271:   const state = Array.isArray(currentState) ? currentState : [];
  2272:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2273: 
  2274:   const freq = new Map();
  2275:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  2276: 
  2277:   if (state.length < 2) {
  2278:     const prob = new Map();
  2279:     for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) prob.set(g, 0);
  2280:     return { prob, freq, samples: 0, prev2: null, prev1: null };
  2281:   }
  2282: 
  2283:   const prev1 = Number(state[0]);
  2284:   const prev2 = Number(state[1]);
  2285: 
  2286:   let samples = 0;
  2287: 
  2288:   for (let i = 2; i < seq.length; i += 1) {
  2289:     const gA = Number(seq[i - 2]?.grupo);
  2290:     const gB = Number(seq[i - 1]?.grupo);
  2291:     const gN = Number(seq[i]?.grupo);
  2292: 
  2293:     if (!Number.isFinite(gA) || !Number.isFinite(gB) || !Number.isFinite(gN)) continue;
  2294:     if (gA !== prev2 || gB !== prev1) continue;
  2295:     if (gN < 1 || gN > k) continue;
  2296: 
  2297:     samples += 1;
  2298:     freq.set(gN, Number(freq.get(gN) || 0) + 1);
  2299:   }
  2300: 
  2301:   const prob = new Map();
  2302:   const denom = Math.max(1, samples);
  2303: 
  2304:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2305:     prob.set(g, Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / denom);
  2306:   }
  2307: 
  2308:   return { prob, freq, samples, prev2, prev1 };
  2309: }

  2311: function buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2312:   comparableSeq,
  2313:   targetTs,
  2314:   windowSize = 4,
  2315:   groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2316: ) {
  2317:   const seq = getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(
  2318:     Array.isArray(comparableSeq) ? comparableSeq : [],
  2319:     targetTs,
  2320:     windowSize
  2321:   );
  2322: 
  2323:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  2324:   const counts = new Map();
  2325:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) counts.set(g, 0);
  2326: 
  2327:   for (const item of seq) {
  2328:     const g = Number(item?.grupo);
  2329:     if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > k) continue;
  2330:     counts.set(g, Number(counts.get(g) || 0) + 1);
  2331:   }
  2332: 
  2333:   const boost = new Map();
  2334:   const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...Array.from(counts.values()).map((n) => Number(n || 0)));
  2335: 
  2336:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2337:     const c = Number(counts.get(g) || 0);
  2338:     boost.set(g, c >= 2 ? c / maxCount : 0);
  2339:   }
  2340: 
  2341:   return {
  2342:     boost,
  2343:     counts,
  2344:     windowSize,
  2345:     considered: Array.isArray(seq) ? seq.length : 0,
  2346:   };
  2347: }
  2348: 
  2349: /* =========================
  2350:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
  2351: ========================= */
  2352: 
  2353: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
  2354:   lotteryKey,
  2355:   drawsRange,
  2356:   drawLast,
  2357:   drawsToday,
  2358:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2359:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2360:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2361:   topN = 3,
  2362:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2363:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2364: }) {
  2365:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  2366:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
  2367:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2368:   }
  2369: 
  2370:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  2371: 
  2372:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  2373:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  2374:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  2375: 
  2376:   if (
  2377:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  2378:     !lastH ||
  2379:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  2380:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
  2381:   ) {
  2382:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2383:   }
  2384: 
  2385:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  2386:     lotteryKey,
  2387:     ymd: lastY,
  2388:     hourBucket: lastH,
  2389:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2390:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2391:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2392:   });
  2393: 
  2394:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  2395:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  2396: 
  2397:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2398:     ? forcedTargetY
  2399:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  2400: 
  2401:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  2402:     ? forcedTargetH
  2403:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  2404:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  2405:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  2406: 
  2407:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  2408:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  2409:   }
  2410: 
  2411:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  2412: 
  2413:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
  2414:   const prevPair =
  2415:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
  2416:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
  2417:       : [];
  2418: 
  2419:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
  2420:     drawsRange: list,
  2421:     lotteryKey: key,
  2422:     targetHour: targetH,
  2423:     targetDow,
  2424:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2425:     prevPair,
  2426:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2427:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2428:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2429:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2430:   });
  2431: 
  2432:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
  2433:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
  2434:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
  2435:   );
  2436: 
  2437:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
  2438:     lotteryKey,
  2439:     drawsRange: list,
  2440:     targetHour: targetH,
  2441:     targetDow,
  2442:     targetDayOfMonth,
  2443:     prevHour: lastH,
  2444:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2445:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2446:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2447:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2448:   });
  2449: 
  2450:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
  2451:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
  2452:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
  2453: 
  2454:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
  2455:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
  2456:     pTransitionFirst.set(
  2457:       g,
  2458:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
  2459:     );
  2460:   }
  2461: 
  2462:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
  2463:     list,
  2464:     key,
  2465:     targetH,
  2466:     targetDow,
  2467:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2468:   );
  2469:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
  2470: 
  2471:   const structuralPrizePresence = computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
  2472:     list,
  2473:     key,
  2474:     targetH,
  2475:     targetDow,
  2476:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2477:   );
  2478:   const pStructuralPrizePresence = structuralPrizePresence?.prob || new Map();
  2479: 
  2480:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
  2481:     list,
  2482:     key,
  2483:     targetH,
  2484:     targetDow,
  2485:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2486:   );
  2487:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
  2488: 
  2489:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
  2490:     list,
  2491:     key,
  2492:     targetH,
  2493:     targetDow
  2494:   );
  2495: 
  2496:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
  2497:     comparableSeq,
  2498:     targetTs,
  2499:     3
  2500:   );
  2501: 
  2502:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
  2503:     drawsRange: list,
  2504:     lotteryKey: key,
  2505:     targetHour: targetH,
  2506:     targetDow,
  2507:     currentState,
  2508:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
  2509:   });
  2510: 
  2511:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
  2512: 
  2513:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
  2514:     comparableSeq,
  2515:     currentState,
  2516:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2517:   );
  2518:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
  2519: 
  2520:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
  2521:     comparableSeq,
  2522:     targetTs,
  2523:     4,
  2524:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2525:   );
  2526:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
  2527: 
  2528:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
  2529:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
  2530:   );
  2531: 
  2532:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
  2533: 
  2534:   const mode =
  2535:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
  2536:       ? "REPEAT"
  2537:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
  2538:         ? "SPREAD"
  2539:         : "NEUTRAL";
  2540: 
  2541:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
  2542: 
  2543:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
  2544:     list,
  2545:     key,
  2546:     targetH,
  2547:     targetDow,
  2548:     targetTs,
  2549:     8
  2550:   );
  2551:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  2552: 
  2553:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
  2554:     1,
  2555:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
  2556:   );
  2557:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
  2558:     1,
  2559:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
  2560:   );
  2561:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
  2562:     1,
  2563:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
  2564:   );
  2565: 
  2566:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
  2567:     list,
  2568:     key,
  2569:     targetH,
  2570:     targetDow,
  2571:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
  2572:   );
  2573: 
  2574:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
  2575:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
  2576:   );
  2577:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
  2578:   const maxGapMsBase =
  2579:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
  2580:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
  2581:       : 1;
  2582: 
  2583:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
  2584:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
  2585: 
  2586:   const confidence =
  2587:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
  2588:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
  2589:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
  2590: 
  2591:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
  2592:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
  2593:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
  2594:     memory: weights.memory,
  2595:     recent: weights.recent,
  2596:     structural: weights.structural,
  2597:     late: weights.late,
  2598:   });
  2599: 
  2600:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  2601:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  2602: 
  2603:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2604:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
  2605:     const pST = Number(pStructuralPrizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
  2606:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
  2607:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
  2608:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
  2609:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
  2610:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2611: 
  2612:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
  2613:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
  2614:       recentFirst: 0,
  2615:       recentDupDraws: 0,
  2616:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
  2617:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
  2618:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
  2619:     };
  2620: 
  2621:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
  2622:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
  2623:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
  2624:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1PrizePresence, 1);
  2625:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
  2626: 
  2627:     const recentComposite =
  2628:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.52) +
  2629:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.08) +
  2630:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
  2631:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
  2632:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
  2633: 
  2634:     const dominanceScore =
  2635:       (pT * 0.42) +
  2636:       (pSF * 0.26) +
  2637:       (pST * 0.18) +
  2638:       (pD * 0.14);
  2639: 
  2640:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
  2641:     const gapMs =
  2642:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
  2643:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
  2644:         : maxGapMsBase;
  2645: 
  2646:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
  2647:       0,
  2648:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
  2649:     );
  2650: 
  2651:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2652:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  2653:     const isDominantToday =
  2654:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
  2655:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
  2656: 
  2657:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
  2658: 
  2659:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
  2660:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
  2661:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
  2662:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
  2663:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
  2664: 
  2665:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
  2666:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
  2667:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
  2668:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
  2669:     }
  2670: 
  2671:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
  2672:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
  2673:     }
  2674: 
  2675:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
  2676: 
  2677:     const pairSequenceScore =
  2678:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
  2679:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
  2680:         : 0;
  2681: 
  2682:     const structuralBlend =
  2683:       (pSF * 0.45) +
  2684:       (pST * 0.35) +
  2685:       (pD * 0.2);
  2686: 
  2687:     const memoryBlend =
  2688:       (pM * 0.75) +
  2689:       (pSeq * 0.25);
  2690: 
  2691:     const recentBlend =
  2692:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
  2693:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
  2694: 
  2695:     const prob =
  2696:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
  2697:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
  2698:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
  2699:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
  2700:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
  2701:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
  2702: 
  2703:     const score =
  2704:       (prob * 1000) +
  2705:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
  2706:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
  2707:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
  2708:       (confidence * 50);
  2709: 
  2710:     return {
  2711:       grupo,
  2712:       prob,
  2713:       scoreProb: prob,
  2714:       score,
  2715:       probTransition: pT,
  2716:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
  2717:       probStructuralPrizePresence: pST,
  2718:       probMemory: pM,
  2719:       probDuplication: pD,
  2720:       probSeq2: pSeq,
  2721:       probPair: pPair,
  2722:       pairProb: pPair,
  2723:       pairConfidence,
  2724:       repeatBoost,
  2725:       dominanceScore,
  2726:       recentComposite,
  2727:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
  2728:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
  2729:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
  2730:       recentLast1PrizePresence: rm.recentLast1PrizePresence,
  2731:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
  2732:       recentLast3PrizePresence: rm.recentLast3PrizePresence,
  2733:       lateNorm,
  2734:       gapMs,
  2735:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
  2736:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2737:       structuralPrizePresenceCount: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2738:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2739:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
  2740:       dayFreq,
  2741:       dayFirstFreq,
  2742:       isDominantToday,
  2743:       dayFlowConfidence,
  2744:       dayFlowScore,
  2745:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2746:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2747:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2748:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  2749:       pairSequenceScore,
  2750:     };
  2751:   })
  2752:     .sort((a, b) => {
  2753:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  2754:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
  2755:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
  2756:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
  2757:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
  2758:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
  2759:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
  2760:       if (b.probStructuralPrizePresence !== a.probStructuralPrizePresence) return b.probStructuralPrizePresence - a.probStructuralPrizePresence;
  2761:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  2762:     })
  2763:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  2764: 
  2765: 
  2766: 
  2767:   const reasonsBase = [
  2768:     `Motor: V2_BICHO`,
  2769:     `Regime detectado: ${regime}`,
  2770:     `RepeatRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0).toFixed(2)} | UniqueRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2771:     `Pesos: mode=${mode} | transição=${(adjustedWeights.transition * 100).toFixed(0)}% | par=${(adjustedWeights.pair * 100).toFixed(0)}% | memória=${(adjustedWeights.memory * 100).toFixed(0)}% | recência=${(adjustedWeights.recent * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(adjustedWeights.structural * 100).toFixed(0)}% | atraso=${(adjustedWeights.late * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  2772:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
  2773:     `Estado curto comparável: [${currentState.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join(", ")}]`,
  2774:     `Camada condicional: ${chosen?.label || "—"} | amostras=${condSamples}`,
  2775:     `Amostra estrutural 1º=${Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0)} | PRIZE_PRESENCE=${Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0)} | duplicação=${Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
  2776:     `Amostra memória: matches=${Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0)} | peso=${Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2777:     `Par do dia: ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | matches=${Number(pairOut?.samples || 0)} | matches DOM=${Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0)} | confiança=${(pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  2778:     `Fluxo do dia: draws=${Number(dayContext?.total || 0)} | confiança=${(dayFlowConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  2779:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  2780:   ];
  2781: 
  2782:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
  2783:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  2784: 
  2785:     return {
  2786:       rank: idx + 1,
  2787:       title:
  2788:         idx === 0
  2789:           ? "Mais provável"
  2790:           : idx === 1
  2791:             ? "2º mais provável"
  2792:             : "3º mais provável",
  2793:       grupo: x.grupo,
  2794:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
  2795:       probCond: Number(x.probTransition || 0),
  2796:       probBase: Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0),
  2797:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateNorm || 0),
  2798:       freq: x.condFirstCount,
  2799:       freqCond: x.condFirstCount,
  2800:       freqBase: x.structuralFirstCount,
  2801:       freqZeroWhy:
  2802:         x.condFirstCount <= 0
  2803:           ? `Sem 1º lugar nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
  2804:           : "",
  2805:       reasons: [
  2806:         ...reasonsBase,
  2807:         `Grupo G${g2}: transição para 1º=${(x.probTransition * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2808:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de 1º=${(x.probStructuralFirst * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2809:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de TOP3=${(x.probStructuralPrizePresence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2810:         `Grupo G${g2}: duplicação histórica=${(x.probDuplication * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2811:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ordem 2=${(x.probSeq2 * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2812:         `Grupo G${g2}: par do dia=${(x.probPair * 100).toFixed(2)}% | confiança=${(x.pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2813:         `Grupo G${g2}: boost de repetição=${(x.repeatBoost * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2814:         `Grupo G${g2}: memória curta=${(x.probMemory * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2815:         `Grupo G${g2}: recência composta=${(x.recentComposite * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2816:         `Grupo G${g2}: atraso normalizado=${(x.lateNorm * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2817:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs na camada=${x.condFirstCount}`,
  2818:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs estruturais=${x.structuralFirstCount}`,
  2819:         `Grupo G${g2}: TOP3 estruturais=${x.structuralPrizePresenceCount}`,
  2820:         `Grupo G${g2}: draws com duplicação=${x.duplicationCount}`,
  2821:         `Grupo G${g2}: peso de memória=${Number(x.memoryWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2822:         `Grupo G${g2}: frequência no dia=${x.dayFreq}`,
  2823:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs no dia=${x.dayFirstFreq}`,
  2824:         `Grupo G${g2}: dominante no dia=${x.isDominantToday ? "sim" : "não"}`,
  2825:         `Grupo G${g2}: score do fluxo do dia=${Number(x.dayFlowScore || 0).toFixed(2)} | confiança=${(Number(x.dayFlowConfidence || 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
  2826:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | amostras=${x.pairSamples} | amostras ponderadas=${Number(x.pairWeightedSamples || 0).toFixed(2)} | DOM exato=${x.pairExactDomSamples} | ocorrências=${Number(x.pairCount || 0).toFixed(2)} | prob=${(Number(x.pairProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}% | score=${Number(x.pairSequenceScore || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
  2827:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  2828:       ],
  2829:       meta: {
  2830:         trigger: {
  2831:           ymd: lastY,
  2832:           hour: lastH,
  2833:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2834:         },
  2835:         next: {
  2836:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  2837:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  2838:         },
  2839:         samples: condSamples,
  2840:         scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
  2841:         explain: {
  2842:           engine: "V2_BICHO",
  2843:           mode,
  2844:           regime,
  2845:           repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
  2846:           uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
  2847:           weights: adjustedWeights,
  2848:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  2849:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  2850:           layerSamples: condSamples,
  2851:           structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
  2852:           structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
  2853:           duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
  2854:           memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
  2855:           memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
  2856:           seq2Samples: Number(seq2Out?.samples || 0),
  2857:           seq2Prev2: Number(seq2Out?.prev2 || 0),
  2858:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),
  2859:           pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2860:           pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2861:           pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2862:           pairConfidence,
  2863:           repeatWindowSize: Number(repeatBoostOut?.windowSize || 0),
  2864:           repeatWindowConsidered: Number(repeatBoostOut?.considered || 0),
  2865:           currentState,
  2866:           targetDow,
  2867:           targetDayOfMonth,
  2868:           prevHour: lastH,
  2869:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2870:           dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
  2871:           dayFlowConfidence,
  2872:           dominanceScore: x.dominanceScore,
  2873:           recentComposite: x.recentComposite,
  2874:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
  2875:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
  2876:           recentDupDraws: x.recentDupDraws,
  2877:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  2878:             key: layer.key,
  2879:             label: layer.label,
  2880:             samples: layer.samples,
  2881:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  2882:           })),
  2883:         },
  2884:       },
  2885:     };
  2886:   });
  2887: 
  2888:   return {
  2889:     top,
  2890:     meta: {
  2891:       trigger: {
  2892:         ymd: lastY,
  2893:         hour: lastH,
  2894:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2895:       },
  2896:       next: {
  2897:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  2898:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  2899:       },
  2900:       samples: condSamples,
  2901:       scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
  2902:       explain: {
  2903:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
  2904:         mode,
  2905:         regime,
  2906:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
  2907:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
  2908:         weights: adjustedWeights,
  2909:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
  2910:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
  2911:         layerSamples: condSamples,
  2912:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
  2913:         structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
  2914:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
  2915:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
  2916:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
  2917:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
  2918:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
  2919:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
  2920:         pairConfidence,
  2921:         currentState,
  2922:         targetDow,
  2923:         targetDayOfMonth,
  2924:         prevHour: lastH,
  2925:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  2926:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
  2927:         dayFlowConfidence,
  2928:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
  2929:           key: layer.key,
  2930:           label: layer.label,
  2931:           samples: layer.samples,
  2932:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
  2933:         })),
  2934:       },
  2935:     },
  2936:   };
  2937: }
  2938: 
  2939: 
  2940: /* =========================
  2941:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
  2942: ========================= */
  2943: 
  2944: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
  2945:   const k = Number(key);
  2946:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
  2947:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
  2948: }
  2949: 
  2950: function emptyGroupMap() {
  2951:   const out = new Map();
  2952:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
  2953:   return out;
  2954: }
  2955: 
  2956: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
  2957:   return new Set(
  2958:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
  2959:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
  2960:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
  2961:   );
  2962: }
  2963: 
  2964: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
  2965:   const out = new Map();
  2966:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
  2967:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
  2968:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
  2969:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
  2970: 
  2971:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  2972:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
  2973:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
  2974:   }
  2975: 
  2976:   return out;
  2977: }
  2978: 
  2979: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
  2980:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
  2981:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
  2982:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
  2983: }
  2984: 
  2985: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  2986:   lotteryKey,
  2987:   drawsRange,
  2988:   drawLast,
  2989:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  2990:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  2991:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  2992:   topN = 3,
  2993:   targetYmdOverride = "",
  2994:   targetHourOverride = "",
  2995: }) {
  2996:   const list = sortDrawsAsc(Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : []);
  2997:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
  2998: 
  2999:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
  3000: 
  3001:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
  3002:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
  3003:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
  3004: 
  3005:   if (
  3006:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
  3007:     !lastH ||
  3008:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
  3009:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
  3010:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3011:   ) {
  3012:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  3013:   }
  3014: 
  3015:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
  3016:     lotteryKey: key,
  3017:     ymd: lastY,
  3018:     hourBucket: lastH,
  3019:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3020:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3021:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3022:   });
  3023: 
  3024:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
  3025:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
  3026: 
  3027:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  3028:     ? forcedTargetY
  3029:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
  3030: 
  3031:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
  3032:     ? forcedTargetH
  3033:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
  3034:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
  3035:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
  3036:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
  3037: 
  3038:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
  3039:     return { top: [], meta: null };
  3040:   }
  3041: 
  3042:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
  3043:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3044:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3045:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3046:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
  3047:   });
  3048: 
  3049:   const layers = {
  3050:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
  3051:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
  3052:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
  3053:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
  3054:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
  3055:   };
  3056: 
  3057:   for (const d of history) {
  3058:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3059:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3060:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
  3061:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
  3062:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  3063:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
  3064: 
  3065:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
  3066:       layer.samples += 1;
  3067:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
  3068:       for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layer.prizePresence, g, 1);
  3069:     };
  3070: 
  3071:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
  3072:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
  3073:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
  3074:   }
  3075: 
  3076:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
  3077: 
  3078:   for (const prev of history) {
  3079:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
  3080:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
  3081:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
  3082: 
  3083:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
  3084: 
  3085:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
  3086:       lotteryKey: key,
  3087:       ymd: py,
  3088:       hourBucket: ph,
  3089:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  3090:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  3091:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  3092:     });
  3093: 
  3094:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
  3095:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
  3096: 
  3097:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
  3098:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
  3099: 
  3100:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
  3101:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(nextDraw);
  3102: 
  3103:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
  3104:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
  3105:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.transition.prizePresence, g, 1);
  3106:   }
  3107: 
  3108:   const recentComparable = history
  3109:     .filter((d) => {
  3110:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3111:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3112:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3113:     })
  3114:     .slice(-20);
  3115: 
  3116:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
  3117:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
  3118:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
  3119: 
  3120:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
  3121:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
  3122:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.recent.prizePresence, g, 1);
  3123:   }
  3124: 
  3125:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
  3126:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
  3127:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
  3128:   const sceneWeight =
  3129:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
  3130:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
  3131: 
  3132: 
  3133:   const activeWeights = {};
  3134:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3135: 
  3136:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3137:     const conf =
  3138:       keyLayer === "transition"
  3139:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
  3140:         : keyLayer === "recent"
  3141:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
  3142:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
  3143: 
  3144:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
  3145:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
  3146:     totalWeight += w;
  3147:   }
  3148: 
  3149:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
  3150:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
  3151:     totalWeight = 1;
  3152:   }
  3153: 
  3154:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
  3155:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
  3156:   }
  3157: 
  3158:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
  3159:     const grupo = idx + 1;
  3160: 
  3161:     let scoreProb = 0;
  3162:     const details = {};
  3163: 
  3164:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
  3165:       const pFirst = Number(layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3166:       const pPrizePresence = Number(layerProbability(layer.prizePresence, layer.samples).get(grupo) || 0);
  3167: 
  3168:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.92) + (pPrizePresence * 0.08);
  3169:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
  3170: 
  3171:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
  3172: 
  3173:       details[keyLayer] = {
  3174:         label: layer.label,
  3175:         samples: layer.samples,
  3176:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
  3177:         top3Count: Number(layer.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0),
  3178:         probability: pLayer,
  3179:         weight: w,
  3180:       };
  3181:     }
  3182: 
  3183:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
  3184:     const pScene =
  3185:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
  3186:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
  3187: 
  3188:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
  3189:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
  3190:     }
  3191: 
  3192:     details.scene = {
  3193:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
  3194:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3195:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
  3196:       top3Count: 0,
  3197:       probability: pScene,
  3198:       weight: sceneWeight,
  3199:     };
  3200: 
  3201:     return {
  3202:       grupo,
  3203:       scoreProb,
  3204:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
  3205:       details,
  3206:     };
  3207:   })
  3208:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3209:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
  3210:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
  3211:     })
  3212:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
  3213: 
  3214:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
  3215:     ranked,
  3216:     {
  3217:       lotteryKey,
  3218:       targetYmd: targetY,
  3219:       targetHour: targetH,
  3220:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
  3221:       totalDraws: history.length,
  3222:     }
