  3223:   );
  3224: 
  3225:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
  3226:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
  3227:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
  3228: 
  3229:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
  3230:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
  3231:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
  3232:       .slice(0, 3);
  3233: 
  3234:     return {
  3235:       rank: idx + 1,
  3236:       title:
  3237:         idx === 0
  3238:           ? "Mais provável"
  3239:           : idx === 1
  3240:             ? "2º mais provável"
  3241:             : "3º mais provável",
  3242:       grupo: x.grupo,
  3243:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3244:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
  3245:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
  3246:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
  3247:       lateBonus: 0,
  3248:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3249:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
  3250:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
  3251:       freqZeroWhy: "",
  3252:       reasons: [
  3253:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
  3254:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  3255:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
  3256:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
  3257:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
  3258:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
  3259:         ),
  3260:       ],
  3261:       meta: {
  3262:         trigger: {
  3263:           ymd: lastY,
  3264:           hour: lastH,
  3265:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3266:         },
  3267:         next: {
  3268:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3269:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3270:         },
  3271:         samples: history.length,
  3272:         period: {
  3273:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
  3274:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
  3275:         },
  3276:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3277:         explain: {
  3278:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3279:           targetDow,
  3280:           targetDayOfMonth,
  3281:           prevHour: lastH,
  3282:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3283:           activeWeights,
  3284:           scene: {
  3285:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
  3286:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
  3287:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
  3288:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
  3289:           },
  3290:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3291:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3292:               k,
  3293:               {
  3294:                 label: v.label,
  3295:                 samples: v.samples,
  3296:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
  3297:               },
  3298:             ])
  3299:           ),
  3300:           details: x.details,
  3301:         },
  3302:       },
  3303:     };
  3304:   });
  3305: 
  3306:   return {
  3307:     top,
  3308:     meta: {
  3309:       trigger: {
  3310:         ymd: lastY,
  3311:         hour: lastH,
  3312:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3313:       },
  3314:       next: {
  3315:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
  3316:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
  3317:       },
  3318:       samples: history.length,
  3319:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3320:       explain: {
  3321:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
  3322:         targetDow,
  3323:         targetDayOfMonth,
  3324:         prevHour: lastH,
  3325:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
  3326:         activeWeights,
  3327:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
  3328:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
  3329:             k,
  3330:             {
  3331:               label: v.label,
  3332:               samples: v.samples,
  3333:               weight: activeWeights[k],
  3334:             },
  3335:           ])
  3336:         ),
  3337:       },
  3338:     },
  3339:   };
  3340: }
  3341: 
  3342: 
  3343: 
  3344: /* =========================
  3345:    TOP3 SCENE READER
  3346: ========================= */
  3347: 
  3348: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
  3349:   const candidates = [
  3350:     prize?.milhar,
  3351:     prize?.milhar4,
  3352:     prize?.numero,
  3353:     prize?.number,
  3354:     prize?.value,
  3355:     prize?.result,
  3356:     prize?.resultado,
  3357:     prize?.premio,
  3358:   ];
  3359: 
  3360:   for (const v of candidates) {
  3361:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
  3362:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
  3363:   }
  3364: 
  3365:   return "";
  3366: }
  3367: 
  3368: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
  3369:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3370:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
  3371: 
  3372:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
  3373:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
  3374: 
  3375:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
  3376:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
  3377: 
  3378:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
  3379: }
  3380: 
  3381: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
  3382:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
  3383: 
  3384:   const rows = prizes
  3385:     .map((p) => {
  3386:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
  3387:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
  3388:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
  3389: 
  3390:       return {
  3391:         pos,
  3392:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
  3393:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
  3394:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
  3395:         grupo,
  3396:       };
  3397:     })
  3398:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
  3399:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
  3400: 
  3401:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
  3402:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
  3403:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
  3404:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
  3405: 
  3406:   const countMap = (arr) => {
  3407:     const m = new Map();
  3408:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
  3409:     return Array.from(m.entries())
  3410:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
  3411:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
  3412:   };
  3413: 
  3414:   return {
  3415:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
  3416:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
  3417:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
  3418:     grupos,
  3419:     dezenas,
  3420:     centenas,
  3421:     milhares,
  3422:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
  3423:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
  3424:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
  3425:     rows,
  3426:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
  3427:   };
  3428: }
  3429: 
  3430: 
  3431: 
  3432: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
  3433:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
  3434: 
  3435:   let score = 0;
  3436:   const details = {};
  3437: 
  3438:   const add = (key, value) => {
  3439:     if (!value) return;
  3440:     score += value;
  3441:     details[key] = value;
  3442:   };
  3443: 
  3444:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
  3445:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
  3446: 
  3447:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
  3448:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
  3449: 
  3450:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
  3451:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
  3452: 
  3453:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
  3454:     add("firstGrupo",45);
  3455: 
  3456:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
  3457:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
  3458:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
  3459:   }
  3460: 
  3461:   add(
  3462:     "groupOverlap",
  3463:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
  3464:   );
  3465: 
  3466:   add(
  3467:     "dezenaOverlap",
  3468:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
  3469:   );
  3470: 
  3471:   add(
  3472:     "centenaOverlap",
  3473:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
  3474:   );
  3475: 
  3476:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3477:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
  3478: 
  3479:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
  3480:     add("repeatExists",8);
  3481: 
  3482:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
  3483:     add("repeatEqual",6);
  3484: 
  3485:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
  3486:     add("signature",60);
  3487: 
  3488:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
  3489:     add("hour",8);
  3490: 
  3491:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
  3492: }
  3493: 
  3494: 
  3495: 
  3496: 
  3497: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
  3498:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  3499:   if (!currentScene) return [];
  3500: 
  3501:   const ordered = list
  3502:     .map((draw) => ({
  3503:       draw,
  3504:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
  3505:     }))
  3506:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
  3507:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3508:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3509:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3510:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
  3511:     });
  3512: 
  3513:   return ordered
  3514:     .map((item, idx) => {
  3515:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
  3516:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
  3517: 
  3518:       return {
  3519:         draw: item.draw,
  3520:         scene: item.scene,
  3521:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
  3522:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
  3523:         score,
  3524:       };
  3525:     })
  3526:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
  3527:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3528:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
  3529:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3530:       }
  3531: 
  3532:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
  3533:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
  3534:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
  3535:     })
  3536:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
  3537: }
  3538: 
  3539: 
  3540: 
  3541: 
  3542: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
  3543:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
  3544:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
  3545: 
  3546:   const freq = new Map();
  3547:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
  3548: 
  3549:   let totalWeight = 0;
  3550: 
  3551:   for (const item of ranking) {
  3552:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
  3553:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
  3554: 
  3555:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
  3556:     if (score <= 0) continue;
  3557: 
  3558:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
  3559:     totalWeight += score;
  3560:   }
  3561: 
  3562:   const prob = new Map();
  3563:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
  3564:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
  3565:   }
  3566: 
  3567:   return {
  3568:     freq,
  3569:     prob,
  3570:     samples: ranking.length,
  3571:     totalWeight,
  3572:   };
  3573: }
  3574: 
  3575: 
  3576: /* =========================
  3577:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
  3578: ========================= */
  3579: 
  3580: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
  3581:   const g = Number(grupo2);
  3582:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
  3583: 
  3584:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
  3585:   const out = [];
  3586: 
  3587:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
  3588:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
  3589:   }
  3590: 
  3591:   return out.filter(Boolean);
  3592: }
  3593: 
  3594: 
  3595: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
  3596:   rangeDraws,
  3597:   analysisHourBucket,
  3598:   schedule,
  3599:   grupo2,
  3600:   count = 20,
  3601:   targetYmd = "",
  3602: }) {
  3603:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
  3604:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
  3605:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
  3606:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
  3607:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
  3608: 
  3609:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
  3610:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
  3611:     : 20;
  3612: 
  3613:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
  3614: 
  3615:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
  3616:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3617:   }
  3618: 
  3619:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
  3620:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
  3621: 
  3622:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
  3623:   const byMilhar = new Map();
  3624: 
  3625:   for (const d of list) {
  3626:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  3627:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  3628:     if (!h) continue;
  3629: 
  3630:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
  3631:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
  3632:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
  3633:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  3634: 
  3635:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
  3636: 
  3637:     for (const prize of ps) {
  3638:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
  3639:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
  3640: 
  3641:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
  3642:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
  3643: 
  3644:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3645:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
  3646: 
  3647:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
  3648:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
  3649: 
  3650:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
  3651: 
  3652:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
  3653:         milhar: m4,
  3654:         dezena: dz,
  3655:         centena: c3,
  3656:         freq: 0,
  3657:         score: 0,
  3658:         targetHits: 0,
  3659:         scheduleHits: 0,
  3660:         sameDowHits: 0,
  3661:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3662:         lastTs: 0,
  3663:       };
  3664: 
  3665:       let inc = 1;
  3666: 
  3667:       if (isTargetHour) {
  3668:         inc += 8;
  3669:         current.targetHits += 1;
  3670:       }
  3671: 
  3672:       if (isScheduleHour) {
  3673:         inc += 3;
  3674:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
  3675:       }
  3676: 
  3677:       if (isSameDow) {
  3678:         inc += 2;
  3679:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
  3680:       }
  3681: 
  3682:       if (pos === 1) {
  3683:         inc += 3;
  3684:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
  3685:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
  3686:         inc += 1;
  3687:       }
  3688: 
  3689:       current.freq += 1;
  3690:       current.score += inc;
  3691: 
  3692:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
  3693:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
  3694:       }
  3695: 
  3696:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
  3697:     }
  3698:   }
  3699: 
  3700:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
  3701:     .map((m) => {
  3702:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
  3703:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
  3704:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
  3705:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
  3706:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3707:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  3708: 
  3709:       return {
  3710:         ...m,
  3711:         score:
  3712:           freq * 100 +
  3713:           targetHits * 70 +
  3714:           scheduleHits * 35 +
  3715:           sameDowHits * 25 +
  3716:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
  3717:           recencyBoost * 5,
  3718:       };
  3719:     })
  3720:     .sort((a, b) => {
  3721:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3722:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3723:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3724:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
  3725:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3726:     });
  3727: 
  3728:   const centenaStats = new Map();
  3729: 
  3730:   for (const item of ranked) {
  3731:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
  3732:     if (!centena) continue;
  3733: 
  3734:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
  3735:       centena,
  3736:       freq: 0,
  3737:       score: 0,
  3738:       targetHits: 0,
  3739:       sameDowHits: 0,
  3740:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
  3741:       lastTs: 0,
  3742:     };
  3743: 
  3744:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
  3745:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
  3746:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
  3747:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
  3748:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
  3749:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
  3750: 
  3751:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
  3752:   }
  3753: 
  3754:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
  3755: 
  3756:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
  3757:     centenaScoreMap.set(
  3758:       c.centena,
  3759:       Number(c.score || 0) +
  3760:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
  3761:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
  3762:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
  3763:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
  3764:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
  3765:     );
  3766:   }
  3767: 
  3768:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
  3769:   const slots = [];
  3770: 
  3771:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
  3772:     const candidates = ranked
  3773:       .filter((x) => x.dezena === dz && !usedMilhares.has(x.milhar))
  3774:       .map((x) => {
  3775:         const milhar = String(x.milhar || "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
  3776:         const prefix = milhar.slice(0, 1);
  3777:         const centena = milhar.slice(1);
  3778:         const centenaScore = Number(centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0);
  3779: 
  3780:         return {
  3781:           ...x,
  3782:           milhar,
  3783:           prefix,
  3784:           centena,
  3785:           centenaScore,
  3786:           adjustedScore:
  3787:             centenaScore * 2 +
  3788:             Number(x.score || 0) -
  3789:             (prefix === "0" ? 80 : 0),
  3790:         };
  3791:       });
  3792: 
  3793:     const byCentena = new Map();
  3794: 
  3795:     for (const item of candidates) {
  3796:       if (!byCentena.has(item.centena)) byCentena.set(item.centena, []);
  3797:       byCentena.get(item.centena).push(item);
  3798:     }
  3799: 
  3800:     const centenaGroups = Array.from(byCentena.entries())
  3801:       .map(([centena, items]) => {
  3802:         const sortedItems = items.slice().sort((a, b) => {
  3803:           if (Number(b.adjustedScore) !== Number(a.adjustedScore)) {
  3804:             return Number(b.adjustedScore) - Number(a.adjustedScore);
  3805:           }
  3806:           if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
  3807:           if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
  3808:           if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
  3809:           return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
  3810:         });
  3811: 
  3812:         const top = sortedItems[0] || {};
  3813: 
  3814:         return {
  3815:           centena,
  3816:           centenaScore: Number(top.centenaScore || 0),
  3817:           bestScore: Number(top.adjustedScore || 0),
  3818:           items: sortedItems,
  3819:         };
  3820:       })
  3821:       .sort((a, b) => {
  3822:         if (Number(b.centenaScore) !== Number(a.centenaScore)) {
  3823:           return Number(b.centenaScore) - Number(a.centenaScore);
  3824:         }
  3825:         if (Number(b.bestScore) !== Number(a.bestScore)) {
  3826:           return Number(b.bestScore) - Number(a.bestScore);
  3827:         }
  3828:         return String(a.centena).localeCompare(String(b.centena));
  3829:       });
  3830: 
  3831:     const picked = [];
  3832:     const usedPrefixes = new Set();
  3833:     const usedCentenas = new Set();
  3834: 
  3835:     // Fase 1: uma melhor milhar por centena forte, evitando repetir prefixo.
  3836:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3837:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3838: 
  3839:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !usedPrefixes.has(x.prefix));
  3840:       if (!item) continue;
  3841: 
  3842:       picked.push(item);
  3843:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3844:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3845:     }
  3846: 
  3847:     // Fase 2: se faltar, pega novas centenas fortes mesmo repetindo prefixo.
  3848:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3849:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3850:       if (usedCentenas.has(group.centena)) continue;
  3851: 
  3852:       const item = group.items.find((x) => !picked.some((p) => p.milhar === x.milhar));
  3853:       if (!item) continue;
  3854: 
  3855:       picked.push(item);
  3856:       usedPrefixes.add(item.prefix);
  3857:       usedCentenas.add(item.centena);
  3858:     }
  3859: 
  3860:     // Fase 3: se ainda faltar, permite mais de uma milhar da mesma centena.
  3861:     for (const group of centenaGroups) {
  3862:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3863: 
  3864:       for (const item of group.items) {
  3865:         if (picked.length >= limit) break;
  3866:         if (picked.some((p) => p.milhar === item.milhar)) continue;
  3867: 
  3868:         picked.push(item);
  3869:       }
  3870:     }
  3871: 
  3872:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
  3873:   }
  3874: 
  3875:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
  3876:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
  3877: 
  3878:     let pushed = 0;
  3879: 
  3880:     for (const item of items) {
  3881:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
  3882:       slots.push({
  3883:         dezena: dz,
  3884:         milhar: item.milhar,
  3885:         score: item.score,
  3886:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
  3887:         centena: item.centena,
  3888:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
  3889:         freq: item.freq,
  3890:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
  3891:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
  3892:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
  3893:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
  3894:       });
  3895:       pushed += 1;
  3896:     }
  3897: 
  3898:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
  3899:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
  3900:       pushed += 1;
  3901:     }
  3902:   }
  3903: 
  3904:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
  3905: 
  3906:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
  3907: }
  3908: 
  3909: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3910:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
  3911: }
  3912: 
  3913: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
  3914:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
  3915: }
  3916: 
  3917: /* =========================
  3918:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
  3919: ========================= */
  3920: 
  3921: 
  3922: 
  3923: export function auditTop3Timeline({
  3924:   timeline,
  3925:   lotteryKey = "",
  3926: }) {
  3927:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
  3928: 
  3929:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
  3930:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  3931: 
  3932:     return (
  3933:       status === "validated" &&
  3934:       Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo))
  3935:     );
  3936:   });
  3937: 
  3938:   function pct(n, d) {
  3939:     if (!d) return 0;
  3940: 
  3941:     return Number(
  3942:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
  3943:     );
  3944:   }
  3945: 
  3946:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
  3947:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
  3948:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
  3949:       : [];
  3950: 
  3951:     const normalized = source
  3952:       .slice(0, 3)
  3953:       .map((grupo) => {
  3954:         const value = Number(grupo);
  3955: 
  3956:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
  3957:           value >= 1 &&
  3958:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3959:           ? value
  3960:           : null;
  3961:       });
  3962: 
  3963:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
  3964:       normalized.push(null);
  3965:     }
  3966: 
  3967:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
  3968:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
  3969: 
  3970:       normalized[0] =
  3971:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
  3972:         fallback >= 1 &&
  3973:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3974:           ? fallback
  3975:           : null;
  3976:     }
  3977: 
  3978:     return normalized;
  3979:   }
  3980: 
  3981:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
  3982:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
  3983:       .slice(0, 3)
  3984:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
  3985:       .filter(
  3986:         (grupo) =>
  3987:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
  3988:           grupo >= 1 &&
  3989:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  3990:       );
  3991: 
  3992:     const resultTop3 = normalizeResultTop3(slot);
  3993:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3[0]);
  3994: 
  3995:     const top1Hit =
  3996:       picks.length > 0 &&
  3997:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  3998:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
  3999: 
  4000:     const top3Hit =
  4001:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  4002:       picks.some(
  4003:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
  4004:       );
  4005: 
  4006:     const prizePositionHits = resultTop3.map(
  4007:       (grupo) =>
  4008:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&
  4009:         picks.some(
  4010:           (pick) => Number(pick) === Number(grupo)
  4011:         )
  4012:     );
  4013: 
  4014:     const predictionHits = picks.map((grupo) =>
  4015:       resultTop3.some(
  4016:         (result) =>
  4017:           Number.isFinite(Number(result)) &&
  4018:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
  4019:       )
  4020:     );
  4021: 
  4022:     const matchedPrizePositions =
  4023:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4024: 
  4025:     const matchedPredictions =
  4026:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
  4027: 
  4028:     return {
  4029:       picks,
  4030:       resultTop3,
  4031:       resultGrupo,
  4032: 
  4033:       top1Hit,
  4034:       top3Hit,
  4035: 
  4036:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
  4037:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
  4038:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
  4039: 
  4040:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
  4041: 
  4042:       matchedPrizePositions,
  4043:       matchedPredictions,
  4044: 
  4045:       prizePositionHits,
  4046:       predictionHits,
  4047:     };
  4048:   }
  4049: 
  4050:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
  4051:     slot,
  4052:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
  4053:   }));
  4054: 
  4055:   function summarize(items) {
  4056:     const summary = {
  4057:       total: items.length,
  4058: 
  4059:       top1Hits: 0,
  4060:       top3Hits: 0,
  4061: 
  4062:       prize1Hits: 0,
  4063:       prize2Hits: 0,
  4064:       prize3Hits: 0,
  4065: 
  4066:       top3PrizeHits: 0,
  4067: 
  4068:       matchedPrizePositions: 0,
  4069:       matchedPredictions: 0,
  4070:     };
  4071: 
  4072:     for (const item of items) {
  4073:       const metrics = item.metrics;
  4074: 
  4075:       if (metrics.top1Hit) summary.top1Hits += 1;
  4076:       if (metrics.top3Hit) summary.top3Hits += 1;
  4077: 
  4078:       if (metrics.prize1Hit) summary.prize1Hits += 1;
  4079:       if (metrics.prize2Hit) summary.prize2Hits += 1;
  4080:       if (metrics.prize3Hit) summary.prize3Hits += 1;
  4081: 
  4082:       if (metrics.top3PrizeHit) {
  4083:         summary.top3PrizeHits += 1;
  4084:       }
  4085: 
  4086:       summary.matchedPrizePositions +=
  4087:         Number(metrics.matchedPrizePositions || 0);
  4088: 
  4089:       summary.matchedPredictions +=
  4090:         Number(metrics.matchedPredictions || 0);
  4091:     }
  4092: 
  4093:     return {
  4094:       ...summary,
  4095: 
  4096:       top1Rate: pct(summary.top1Hits, summary.total),
  4097:       top3Rate: pct(summary.top3Hits, summary.total),
  4098: 
  4099:       prize1Rate: pct(summary.prize1Hits, summary.total),
  4100:       prize2Rate: pct(summary.prize2Hits, summary.total),
  4101:       prize3Rate: pct(summary.prize3Hits, summary.total),
  4102: 
  4103:       top3PrizeRate: pct(
  4104:         summary.top3PrizeHits,
  4105:         summary.total
  4106:       ),
  4107: 
  4108:       averageMatchedPrizePositions: summary.total
  4109:         ? Number(
  4110:             (
  4111:               summary.matchedPrizePositions /
  4112:               summary.total
  4113:             ).toFixed(4)
  4114:           )
  4115:         : 0,
  4116: 
  4117:       averageMatchedPredictions: summary.total
  4118:         ? Number(
  4119:             (
  4120:               summary.matchedPredictions /
  4121:               summary.total
  4122:             ).toFixed(4)
  4123:           )
  4124:         : 0,
  4125:     };
  4126:   }
  4127: 
  4128:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
  4129:     const map = new Map();
  4130: 
  4131:     for (const item of evaluated) {
  4132:       const key = keyFn(item.slot);
  4133: 
  4134:       if (!map.has(key)) {
  4135:         map.set(key, []);
  4136:       }
  4137: 
  4138:       map.get(key).push(item);
  4139:     }
  4140: 
  4141:     return Array.from(map.entries())
  4142:       .map(([key, items]) => ({
  4143:         key,
  4144:         ...summarize(items),
  4145:       }))
  4146:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4147:         if (b.top3PrizeRate !== a.top3PrizeRate) {
  4148:           return b.top3PrizeRate - a.top3PrizeRate;
  4149:         }
  4150: 
  4151:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) {
  4152:           return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
  4153:         }
  4154: 
  4155:         return b.total - a.total;
  4156:       });
  4157:   }
  4158: 
  4159:   const summary = summarize(evaluated);
  4160: 
  4161:   return {
  4162:     lotteryKey,
  4163:     ...summary,
  4164: 
  4165:     byHour: groupBy(
  4166:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")
  4167:     ),
  4168: 
  4169:     byDate: groupBy(
  4170:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")
  4171:     ),
  4172: 
  4173:     rows: evaluated.map(({ slot, metrics }) => ({
  4174:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
  4175:       hour: slot.targetHour,
  4176: 
  4177:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
  4178:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
  4179: 
  4180:       resultGrupo: metrics.resultGrupo,
  4181:       resultTop3: metrics.resultTop3,
  4182: 
  4183:       top3: metrics.picks,
  4184: 
  4185:       top1Hit: metrics.top1Hit,
  4186:       top3Hit: metrics.top3Hit,
  4187: 
  4188:       prize1Hit: metrics.prize1Hit,
  4189:       prize2Hit: metrics.prize2Hit,
  4190:       prize3Hit: metrics.prize3Hit,
  4191: 
  4192:       top3PrizeHit: metrics.top3PrizeHit,
  4193: 
  4194:       matchedPrizePositions:
  4195:         metrics.matchedPrizePositions,
  4196: 
  4197:       matchedPredictions:
  4198:         metrics.matchedPredictions,
  4199: 
  4200:       prizePositionHits:
  4201:         metrics.prizePositionHits,
  4202: 
  4203:       predictionHits:
  4204:         metrics.predictionHits,
  4205: 
  4206:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
  4207:     })),
  4208:   };
  4209: }
  4210: 
  4211: 
  4212: function buildTimelineForDate({
  4213:   ymd,
  4214:   drawsToday,
  4215:   drawsRange,
  4216:   lotteryKey,
  4217:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4218:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4219:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4220: }) {
  4221:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
  4222:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
  4223:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
  4224: 
  4225:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
  4226: 
  4227:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
  4228:     lotteryKey,
  4229:     ymd: targetYmd,
  4230:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4231:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4232:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4233:   });
  4234: 
  4235:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
  4236:     .map(toHourBucket)
  4237:     .filter(Boolean);
  4238: 
  4239:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
  4240: 
  4241:   const daySorted = day
  4242:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  4243:     .sort((a, b) => {
  4244:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4245:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4246:       return ta - tb;
  4247:     });
  4248: 
  4249:   const rangeSorted = range
  4250:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
  4251:     .sort((a, b) => {
  4252:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4253:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4254:       return ta - tb;
  4255:     });
  4256: 
  4257:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
  4258: 
  4259:   for (const d of daySorted) {
  4260:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4261:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4262:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
  4263:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
  4264:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
  4265:   }
  4266: 
  4267: 
  4268:   const chainContextDraws = [];
  4269:   const timeline = [];
  4270:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
  4271: 
  4272:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
  4273:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
  4274:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
  4275: 
  4276: 
  4277:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
  4278:       timeline.push({
  4279:         targetYmd,
  4280:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4281:         baseYmd: "",
  4282:         baseHour: "",
  4283:         top3: [],
  4284:         resultGrupo: null,
  4285:         hit: null,
  4286:         status: "pending",
  4287:       });
  4288:       continue;
  4289:     }
  4290: 
  4291:     let baseDraw = null;
  4292: 
  4293:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
  4294:       .filter((d) => {
  4295:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4296:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4297:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4298:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4299:       })
  4300:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4301:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4302:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4303:         return tb - ta;
  4304:       })[0] || null;
  4305: 
  4306:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
  4307: 
  4308:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4309:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
  4310:         .filter((d) => {
  4311:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4312:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4313:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4314:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
  4315:         })
  4316:         .sort((a, b) => {
  4317:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4318:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4319:           return tb - ta;
  4320:         })[0] || null;
  4321: 
  4322:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
  4323:     }
  4324: 
  4325:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4326:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
  4327:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
  4328:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
  4329:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
  4330:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
  4331: 
  4332:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
  4333: 
  4334:         baseDraw = d;
  4335:         break;
  4336:       }
  4337:     }
  4338: 
  4339:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4340:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
  4341:     }
  4342: 
  4343:     if (!baseDraw) {
  4344:       timeline.push({
  4345:         targetYmd,
  4346:         targetHour: slotHour,
  4347:         baseYmd: "",
  4348:         baseHour: "",
  4349:         top3: [],
  4350:         resultGrupo: null,
  4351:         hit: null,
  4352:         status: "pending",
  4353:       });
  4354:       continue;
  4355:     }
  4356: 
  4357:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
  4358:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
  4359:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
  4360:     );
  4361: 
  4362:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
  4363:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4364:         pickDrawYMD(d),
  4365:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4366:       );
  4367:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4368:     });
  4369: 
  4370:     const historyStats = (() => {
  4371:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  4372: 
  4373:       let d30 = 0;
  4374:       let d90 = 0;
  4375:       let d180 = 0;
  4376:       let d365 = 0;
  4377: 
  4378:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
  4379:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
  4380:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
  4381:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
  4382:         );
  4383: 
  4384:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
  4385: 
  4386:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
  4387: 
  4388:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
  4389:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
  4390:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
  4391:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
  4392:       }
  4393: 
  4394:       return {
  4395:         total: usableHistory.length,
  4396:         d30,
  4397:         d90,
  4398:         d180,
  4399:         d365,
  4400:       };
  4401:     })();
  4402: 
  4403:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
  4404:       .filter((d) => {
  4405:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
  4406:           pickDrawYMD(d),
  4407:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
  4408:         );
  4409:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
  4410:       })
  4411:       .sort((a, b) => {
  4412:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
  4413:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
  4414:         return ta - tb;
  4415:       });
  4416: 
  4417:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
  4418:       lotteryKey,
  4419:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
  4420:       drawLast: baseDraw,
  4421:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
  4422:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4423:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4424:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4425:       topN: 3,
  4426:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
  4427:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
  4428:     });
  4429: 
  4430: 
  4431:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
  4432:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
  4433:     const computedForSlot =
  4434:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
  4435: 
  4436:     const top3 =
  4437:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
  4438:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
  4439:         : [];
  4440: 
  4441:     const resultTop3Groups = currentDraw
  4442:       ? getPrizeGroupsByPosition(currentDraw, 3)
  4443:       : [null, null, null];
  4444: 
  4445:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3Groups[0]);
  4446: 
  4447:     const normalizedResultGrupo =
  4448:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
  4449:       resultGrupo >= 1 &&
  4450:       resultGrupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
  4451:         ? resultGrupo
  4452:         : null;
  4453: 
  4454:     const hitPrizePositions = resultTop3Groups.map(
  4455:       (resultGroup) =>
  4456:         Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup)) &&
  4457:         top3.some(
  4458:           (item) =>
  4459:             Number(item?.grupo) ===
  4460:             Number(resultGroup)
  4461:         )
  4462:     );
  4463: 
  4464:     const matchedPrizePositions =
  4465:       hitPrizePositions.filter(Boolean).length;
  4466: 
  4467:     const hit =
  4468:       top3.length > 0 &&
  4469:       resultTop3Groups.some(
  4470:         (resultGroup) =>
  4471:           Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup))
  4472:       )
  4473:         ? matchedPrizePositions > 0
  4474:         : null;
  4475: 
  4476:     timeline.push({
  4477:       targetYmd,
  4478:       targetHour: slotHour,
  4479: 
  4480:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
  4481:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
  4482: 
  4483:       top3,
  4484: 
  4485:       resultGrupo: normalizedResultGrupo,
  4486:       resultTop3Groups,
  4487: 
  4488:       hit,
  4489:       top3PrizeHit: hit,
  4490:       hitPrizePositions,
  4491:       matchedPrizePositions,
  4492: 
  4493:       status: Number.isFinite(
  4494:         Number(normalizedResultGrupo)
  4495:       )
  4496:         ? "validated"
  4497:         : "pending",
  4498: 
  4499:       historyStats,
  4500:     });
  4501: 
  4502:     if (currentDraw) {
  4503:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
  4504:     } else {
  4505:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
  4506:     }
  4507:   }
  4508: 
  4509:   return timeline;
  4510: 
  4511: }
  4512: 
  4513: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
  4514:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
  4515: }
  4516: 
  4517: export function auditTop3Backtest({
  4518:   drawsRange,
  4519:   lotteryKey,
  4520:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4521:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4522:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4523: }) {
  4524:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
  4525:   const dates = Array.from(
  4526:     new Set(
  4527:       range
  4528:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
  4529:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
  4530:     )
  4531:   ).sort();
  4532: 
  4533:   const allTimeline = [];
  4534: 
  4535:   for (const ymd of dates) {
  4536:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
  4537: 
  4538:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
  4539:       ymd,
  4540:       drawsToday,
  4541:       drawsRange: range,
  4542:       lotteryKey,
  4543:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
  4544:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
  4545:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
  4546:     });
  4547: 
  4548:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
  4549:   }
  4550: 
  4551:   return auditTop3Timeline({
  4552:     timeline: allTimeline,
  4553:     lotteryKey,
  4554:   });
  4555: }
